`README.md`:

```md
# izzys-quest-decomp

**Izzy's Quest for Olympic Gold, rebuilt from its machine code as portable C, and playable again on modern Windows.**

This repository holds a source-level decompilation of the 1995 Windows 3.1 CD-ROM game *Izzy's Quest for Olympic Gold*. It also holds a native Windows 10/11 version built on it (`IzzyQuest.exe`), and every tool used to take the original apart and check the result.

> **No game files are included.** The pictures, sounds, music, videos, animations and game scripts belong to their copyright holders. To build the tests or play, you need **your own copy of the original game CD** (see [Getting the game files](#getting-the-game-files)).

---

## Contents

- [The game](#the-game)
- [What this project is](#what-this-project-is)
- [What was done](#what-was-done)
- [Bugs fixed](#bugs-fixed)
- [Repository layout](#repository-layout)
- [Getting the game files](#getting-the-game-files)
- [Setting up the tools](#setting-up-the-tools)
- [Compiling](#compiling)
- [Playing](#playing)
- [Tool reference](#tool-reference)
- [How the engine is organised](#how-the-engine-is-organised)
- [Known limitations](#known-limitations)
- [Legal and third-party notices](#legal-and-third-party-notices)

---

## The game

*Izzy's Quest for Olympic Gold* is a children's adventure game for Windows 3.1, released on CD-ROM in 1995. It stars Izzy, the mascot of the 1996 Atlanta Summer Olympics.

- **Players:** up to four players take turns guiding Izzy around an illustrated world map. Each player types their name at the start.
- **Searching:** clicking places on the map searches them. Gold, silver and bronze medals, compete cards, morph cards and other items are hidden around the world when a game starts. What you find depends on where you click.
- **Medals:** finding a medal plays a short narrated scene about a real moment of Olympic history. There are 30 different scenes for each medal type, dealt like a shuffled deck, so you see every scene before any repeats. Izzy then pops up to announce the medal.
- **Competitions:** these show real Olympic footage, with 40 video clips covering tennis, judo, handball, football, archery, boxing, gymnastics, athletics and more. A rival, Lanse, can also challenge the player for a medal.
- **Scoring:** the game keeps a high-score table, can be saved and loaded (`.SGE` files), and ends with the credits.

The original runs on the game's own "StoryGame Engine": a 16-bit Windows program (`IZZY.EXE`) that plays scene scripts called *takes*, driven by a compiled game-logic script (`IZZY.VET`). **64-bit Windows can't run 16-bit programs at all**, so the original no longer works on today's PCs.

## What this project is

1. **A decompilation.** Every game function in `IZZY.EXE` has been accounted for: either rewritten as readable C99 that does the same thing, or identified as Windows 3.1 plumbing or dead code.
   - Each reconstructed function names the original it came from, for example `/* orig: 1018:4d7a vet_Step */`. That makes it traceable back to the machine code.
2. **A modern Windows version.** `IzzyQuest.exe` runs that engine in a normal window, with picture, sound, MIDI music, video, menus and dialogs.
3. **A toolkit.** It includes the asset extractors, a script compiler and decompiler, the video decoder, test and stress-test programs, and the Ghidra-based decompilation workspace.

The engine is plain, portable C99 with no dependencies; only the front end in `src/win/` is Windows-specific. Everything is checked against the real game data by a self-test of 85 checks.

## What was done

**File formats.** All the game's formats were decoded:

- `IZZY.DIR` and `IZZYCD1.DAT`: the asset archive of 677 groups.
- `.DEF`: compiled TAKE scene scripts.
- `.ANI`: "CELS" animation packs, compressed with "MMP1". There are 2,582 packs holding 29,341 cels, and all of them decode.
- The `MONO` / `STER` streamed audio.
- `IZZY.VET`: the compiled logic script, with 1,315 nodes including 1,134 script nodes.
- The `.SGE` save format, `izzy.ini`, and the EXE's own resources (bitmaps, fonts, cursors, menu, strings).

**The engine, rebuilt function by function:**

- **TAKE player:** the scene-script interpreter and its 60 Hz clock.
- **Stage:** 104 sprite slots, the view and perspective math, palette fades, shadows and text.
- **Renderer:** 8-bit pictures and MMP1 sprites at any scale.
- **VET machine:** the script interpreter for expressions, `IF`, `GOTO`, `GOSUB`, `RETURN` and `LOOP`, the busy flags, and the narration pages.
- **Game rules:** the 22 built-in functions (hiding the medals, the medal and competition decks, fusing medals and so on).
- **UI:** hotspots, the 196-state control panel, the HUD, name entry and the high scores.
- **Sound:** the 3-channel wave mixer, the MIDI command layer, saved games, the menus and option handling, and the quit-through-the-credits sequence.

**Verification.** These checks compare against the original's own data:

- A scene rendered by the engine matches the static composition pixel for pixel (0 of 307,200 pixels differ).
- A save survives a round trip with all 1,315 variables intact.
- `IZZY.VET` was decompiled back into script source, and compiling that source reproduces all **176,729 bytes of the original file exactly**.
- The game plays from the intro to the name screen, the main map, medals, competitions, saving and loading, and the credits.

**Coverage.** Of the game code in `IZZY.EXE` (segments 2–5):

| Status | Share of code bytes |
|---|---|
| Rewritten directly | 77.2% |
| Equivalent (same output, different algorithm) | 10.5% |
| Platform code (Windows 3.1, GDI, MCI) replaced by the front end | 11.7% |
| Dead code | 0.5% |
| Left to do | 0 |

Segment 1 is the Microsoft C runtime and iostream library, replaced by the standard C library.

**Modern Windows version.**

- **Picture:** scaled to the window, with a full-screen mode.
- **Cursors:** the original's own cursors, loaded from its EXE.
- **Sound:** through `waveOut`; MIDI through the Windows MCI sequencer, as the original did.
- **Pausing:** the game pauses when its window loses focus, as the original did.
- **Video:** every one of the 40 videos plays through a **built-in Indeo 3.2 decoder**. Windows' own Indeo codec has been switched off by a Microsoft security update, and the old Intel decoder behind it crashes. The decoder handles all 7,187 video frames without errors.
- **Script tools:** the developers' script compiler (for `.VEP` / `.VP*` / `.VW*` source files, which didn't ship) was reconstructed. A matching decompiler turns `IZZY.VET` back into readable script.

The full technical notes are in [`tools/decomp/TECHNICAL_NOTES.md`](tools/decomp/TECHNICAL_NOTES.md). They cover every format, every reconstructed system, the addresses, and how each was verified.

## Bugs fixed

### Bugs in the original game

These are mistakes in the shipped game. Each fix is marked "not in the original" in the code.

| Bug | Fix |
|---|---|
| **Izzy never announces a bronze medal.** His bronze announcement (take `SIZ00013`, with voice and fanfare) is on the CD, but the game script never plays it. Gold ends with `GOTO SIZ00011` and silver with `GOTO SIZ00012`, but bronze ends with `RETURN`. Winning bronze in a competition skips it the same way. | When the script loads, two statements are corrected in memory (`game_fix_script` in `src/game.c`). They're applied only if the bytes are exactly the shipped ones; `IZZY.VET` on disk is never changed. |
| **New Game or Load from the credits screen.** The "credits are running" flag was never cleared, so the next click quit the game. | The flag is cleared on New Game and on Load. |
| **A failed Load damaged the current game.** Loading writes into the running game as it reads, so a damaged save left a half-loaded game behind after the "error opening your file" message. | The file is first loaded into a scratch copy of the game. Only a file that loads completely touches the real game. |

### Problems with modern Windows

| Problem | Fix |
|---|---|
| 64-bit Windows can't run 16-bit programs. | A native 32-bit Windows front end (`IzzyQuest.exe`). |
| The videos need the Indeo 3.2 codec, which Windows has disabled. | A built-in AVI reader and Indeo 3.2 decoder (`src/avi.c`, `src/indeo3.c`). |
| The game writes `izzy.ini` and saves next to itself, which fails in read-only folders such as Program Files. | It falls back to `%APPDATA%\IzzysQuest`. |
| High-resolution screens. | DPI-aware scaling with the 4:3 shape kept, plus full screen (F11). |

### Bugs found and fixed in this project along the way

- **Medal scenes returning at once.** A music-only narration page was treated as spoken narration, which ended the following scene immediately.
- **Hardening:**
  - the video decoder's bit reader now stops safely at the end of damaged data;
  - `izzy.ini` gets a line break if its last line had none;
  - a video pauses whenever the game pauses (focus loss, dialogs).

**Stress testing.** `stress.exe` played nine unattended games, about 4.7 hours of game time. It made around 13,000 clicks, played 9,000 scenes, ran 111 save/reload comparisons and 21 New Games, and played 12 competition videos. It found no script errors, missing files, stalls or save mismatches. Damaged save files never crash the game.

**Left as in the original:** one competition script asks for `VIDHAN1a.AVI`, which isn't on the CD. That video is skipped, exactly as the original skipped it.

## Repository layout

```
izzys-quest-decomp/
├─ README.md
├─ .gitignore              keeps game files, build output and downloaded tools out of git
├─ src/                    the source code
│  ├─ *.c, *.h             the engine (portable C99, see "How the engine is organised")
│  ├─ ui_controls.inc      the 196 control-panel states, generated from IZZY.EXE's code
│  ├─ win/izzy_win.c       IzzyQuest.exe, the Windows front end
│  ├─ tests/               the self-test and the command-line tools (see "Tool reference")
│  └─ third_party/         FFmpeg's Indeo 3 codebook tables (LGPL 2.1) and their notice
├─ tools/                  every script and tool
│  ├─ build.cmd            builds the command-line tools and runs the self-test
│  ├─ build_win.cmd        builds IzzyQuest.exe
│  ├─ Extract-IzzyDat.ps1, Convert-IzzyAssets.ps1, IzzyConvert.cs,
│  │  Compare-Images.ps1, Get-NEInfo.ps1            asset and EXE inspection scripts
│  ├─ decomp/              the Ghidra decompilation workspace (scripts, names.csv, types,
│  │                       coverage list, Ghidra scripts, TECHNICAL_NOTES.md)
│  └─ tcc/, ghidra_*/, jdk-*/   downloaded tools (not in git - see "Setting up the tools")
└─ build/                  created by the build scripts (not in git)
```

## Getting the game files

You need the files from the original *Izzy's Quest for Olympic Gold* CD-ROM. Copy the **whole CD** into a folder on your PC, for example `C:\Games\Izzy`. The engine needs these files from the CD's top folder:

| File | What it is |
|---|---|
| `IZZY.EXE` | the original program. The engine reads its bitmaps, fonts, cursors and the control-panel data from it, and the decompilation tools analyse it. |
| `IZZY.DIR`, `IZZYCD1.DAT` | the asset archive (pictures, animations, sounds, scene scripts) |
| `IZZY.VET` | the compiled game-logic script |
| `VID*.AVI` (40 files) | the competition videos |
| `*.MID` (14 files) | the music |

Nothing is installed or changed in that folder, apart from `izzy.ini` (options and high scores), your saved games, and `IzzyQuest.exe` if you choose to copy it there. You can point the tools at the folder each time, or set it once:

```powershell
setx IZZY_GAME "C:\Games\Izzy"
```

## Setting up the tools

**To build and play you only need TinyCC.** Ghidra and Java are only needed to redo the decompilation itself.

1. **TinyCC 0.9.27 for Windows** (required). Download `tcc-0.9.27-win64-bin.zip` from the TinyCC download area (https://download.savannah.gnu.org/releases/tinycc/) and extract its `tcc` folder to `tools\tcc`. You should then have `tools\tcc\tcc.exe` and `tools\tcc\i386-win32-tcc.exe`; the second one builds `IzzyQuest.exe`. The archive used during development had SHA-256 `34A721949A2583FDFF725312DA092FA0F5F1F284B702E6F811C6954714FAABB2`.
   - Instead of TinyCC, `tools\build.cmd` also accepts `gcc` or `clang` on your PATH for the command-line tools.
2. **Ghidra 12.2** (optional): https://github.com/NationalSecurityAgency/ghidra/releases. Extract it to `tools\ghidra_12.2_PUBLIC`.
3. **A Java runtime for Ghidra** (optional): Eclipse Temurin 25 from https://adoptium.net. Extract it to `tools\jdk-25`.

## Compiling

Open a command prompt or PowerShell in the repository folder.

**The command-line tools and the self-test:**

```powershell
tools\build.cmd "C:\Games\Izzy"
```

This builds `selftest`, `scene_render`, `game_run`, `vep_tool`, `avi_dump` and `stress` into `build\`, then runs the self-test against your game files. It should end with `ALL PASSED (0 failures)`. The self-test writes its pictures, saves and the decompiled script into `build\test-output\`, never into the game folder. Without a game folder it just builds.

**The Windows version:**

```powershell
tools\build_win.cmd "C:\Games\Izzy"
```

This builds `build\IzzyQuest.exe` and copies it into your game folder. Without a game folder it only builds.

> The program is called `IzzyQuest.exe` and not `izzy.exe` on purpose. Windows ignores upper and lower case in file names, so `izzy.exe` would overwrite the original `IZZY.EXE`, which the engine still reads its pictures and cursors from.

## Playing

Double-click `IzzyQuest.exe` in your game folder. You can also run it from anywhere with the folder as its argument: `IzzyQuest.exe "C:\Games\Izzy"`.

**Controls:**

| | |
|---|---|
| Mouse | everything, as in the original: click places on the map, the stone control panel, SKIP and so on |
| Keyboard | typing player names on the name screen |
| F11 or Alt+Enter | full screen on and off; Esc also leaves full screen |
| Clicking during a video | skips it, unless the `INTRO` option is on (see below) |
| Clicking during the credits | ends them and closes the game |

**Menus.** These are the original's:

- **Game:** New Game, Save Game, Load Game, Exit. New Game and Load Game offer to save first.
- **Sound:** Volume High, Medium and Low, and Music Playing on/off.
- **About.**

Exit asks first, then plays the credits, as the original did.

**Options (`izzy.ini`).** The file lives in the game folder, or in `%APPDATA%\IzzysQuest` if that folder is read-only:

| Section and key | Values |
|---|---|
| `[OPTIONS] VOLUME` | `0` high, `-1` medium, `-2` low (set from the Sound menu) |
| `[OPTIONS] MUSIC` | `1` or `0` (Sound → Music Playing) |
| `[OPTIONS] INTRO` | `1` (default): a click can't skip a video; `0`: it can |
| `[Options] Override8Bit` | `true` or `FALSE`, as in the original |
| `[SCORE] NAMEn`, `SCOREn` | the high-score table |

**Saved games** are `.SGE` files, saved wherever you choose in the Save dialog. By default that's the game folder (or `%APPDATA%\IzzysQuest`).

**Pausing.** The game pauses, music and videos included, whenever its window loses focus, and carries on when you click back into it.

## Tool reference

All C programs are built by `tools\build.cmd` into `build\`. `<game>` is your game folder.

### Programs

| Program | What it does |
|---|---|
| `IzzyQuest.exe [game]` | the Windows version of the game (see [Playing](#playing)) |
| `selftest <game> [output folder]` | the 85-check self-test against the real game files: formats, renderer, stage, scripts, rules, saves, videos, menus, medals |
| `game_run <game> <ticks> [out.bmp] [every]` | runs the whole game without a window for `<ticks>` 60 Hz ticks, printing every script node and take. It saves the last frame, or one every `every` ticks. |
| `scene_render <game> <TAKE> <ticks> <out.bmp> [every]` | plays a single take (for example `SBC00010`) and saves frames |
| `vep_tool decompile <IZZY.VET> <out.VP0>` | writes the compiled game script as readable programmer source |
| `vep_tool build <in.VP0> <out.VET>` | compiles one programmer script into a `.VET` |
| `vep_tool compile <base>` | the original's load-or-compile: `<base>.VEP`, or `<base>.VP0`–`9` plus `<base>.VW0`–`9`, into `<base>.VET` when the sources are newer |
| `avi_dump <file.avi> [prefix frame...]` | decodes a game video with the built-in Indeo 3.2 decoder, prints its details, and saves the listed frames as `prefix_NNN.bmp` |
| `stress <game> [games] [minutes]` | plays unattended to find bugs: it clicks, types, saves and reloads, and starts New Games. It reports script errors, missing files, stalls, medal scenes and videos. Set `STRESS_FIRST=n` to start at game seed `n` (useful for running several in parallel). Scratch files go to the current folder. |

**`game_run` options (environment variables):**

| Variable | Effect |
|---|---|
| `GAME_AUTOPLAY=<seed>` | after the intro, clicks something clickable every 2 seconds |
| `GAME_TYPE=<text>` with `GAME_TYPE_AT=<tick>` | types text at a tick (`\|` is Enter) |
| `GAME_CLICK=x,y` with `GAME_CLICK_AT=<tick>` | one click |
| `GAME_CLICKS=tick:x,y;tick:x,y;...` | a list of clicks |
| `GAME_WAV=<file>` | records the game's sound to a file |

**`IzzyQuest.exe` test mode (environment variables):**

| Variable | Effect |
|---|---|
| `IZZY_TEST_LOG=<file>` | logs what Windows reported: the sound device, MIDI, videos, cursors |
| `IZZY_TEST_SECONDS=<n>` with `IZZY_TEST_SHOT=<file.bmp>` | quits after `n` seconds, saving a picture of the window first. While this is set, the game doesn't pause on focus loss. |
| `IZZY_TEST_AVI=<NAME.AVI>`, `IZZY_TEST_MIDI=<NAME.MID>` | plays that file at start |

### Scripts in `tools\` (PowerShell)

| Script | What it does |
|---|---|
| `Extract-IzzyDat.ps1 [-Source <game>] [-Out <folder>]` | unpacks `IZZYCD1.DAT` into one folder per group (default `build\extracted\IZZYCD1`) |
| `Convert-IzzyAssets.ps1 [-In ...] [-Out ...] [-Group name...]` | turns the extracted files into viewable ones: animations as PNG plus JSON, sounds as WAV, scene scripts as readable `.take.txt` with a preview picture. Default output is `build\converted`. Uses `IzzyConvert.cs`. |
| `Compare-Images.ps1 -A <img> -B <img> [-Rows n]` | counts the pixels that differ between two images |
| `Get-NEInfo.ps1 -Path <IZZY.EXE>` | dumps a 16-bit Windows program's header: segments, imports, exports |

### The decompilation workspace (`tools\decomp\`)

You need Ghidra and Java for the first two scripts.

| Script | What it does |
|---|---|
| `Run-Decomp.ps1 [-Exe <IZZY.EXE>] [-Gui]` | imports `IZZY.EXE` into a Ghidra project (`tools\decomp\ghidra_project`), analyses it, and exports decompiled C and assembly listings to `tools\decomp\out\IZZY\`. `-Gui` opens Ghidra instead. By default it reads `%IZZY_GAME%\IZZY.EXE`. |
| `Update-Decomp.ps1 [-NoExport]` | reapplies the function names from `names.csv` and the structure types from `types\`, re-exports, and prints progress |
| `Show-Func.ps1 <address or name> [-Asm]` | prints one function's decompiled C, or its assembly with `-Asm` |
| `Set-Names.ps1 -Rows "addr,name,area,comment"` | adds or updates function names in `names.csv` (708 of the 781 functions in IZZY.EXE are named) |
| `Get-SourceCoverage.ps1 [-List] [-Area x]` | how much of the game code is reconstructed in `src\`. It counts `orig:` markers plus `coverage.manual.csv`. |
| `Get-DecompProgress.ps1` | naming progress per segment |
| `Classify-Library.ps1` | labels C-runtime library functions in bulk |
| `Extract-ControlTable.ps1` | regenerates `src\ui_controls.inc` from the control-panel code in `IZZY.EXE` |
| `types\*.ps1` | build the structure definitions Ghidra uses (Stage, VetVM, TakePlayer, ...) from observed field offsets |
| `ghidra_scripts\*.java` | Ghidra scripts: apply names and types, export C, assembly and an index, find functions Ghidra missed (`FindHiddenFunctions`), fix 16-bit decoding (`FixSeg16`), and more |

The Ghidra project and its exports are generated from your copy of `IZZY.EXE`, so they're not in git. Run `Run-Decomp.ps1` once to create them; after that, `Show-Func.ps1` and `Get-SourceCoverage.ps1` work.

## How the engine is organised

| File | Reconstructs |
|---|---|
| `dat.c` | the `IZZY.DIR` / `IZZYCD1.DAT` archive |
| `cels.c` | `.ANI` animation packs and MMP1 cel decompression |
| `render.c` | 8-bit pictures, sprites, shadows |
| `stage.c` | the 104-slot stage: animation, motion paths, perspective, palette fades, text |
| `take.c`, `scene.c` | TAKE scene scripts: the parser, and running them on the stage |
| `vet.c` | the VET game-logic interpreter and the narration pages |
| `builtins.c` | the 22 game-rule functions |
| `vep.c`, `vepc.c` | the VEP expression compiler, the full source compiler, and a VET → VEP decompiler |
| `savegame.c` | `.SGE` saved games |
| `ui.c`, `ui_controls.inc` | hotspots, the control panel, the HUD, names, high scores |
| `audio.c` | the 3-channel wave mixer |
| `game.c` | the game object: the take player, narration, MIDI commands, pausing, script fixes |
| `app.c` | the menus, options, saving and loading, quitting through the credits |
| `resource.c`, `config.c` | resources inside `IZZY.EXE`; `izzy.ini` |
| `avi.c`, `indeo3.c` | the AVI reader and the Indeo 3.2 decoder |
| `win/izzy_win.c` | the Windows front end: window, input, sound, MIDI, video, menus, dialogs |

The engine never talks to the operating system directly. Pictures come out of `Game.screen` (640×480, 8-bit, with a palette), and sound comes from `game_audio_mix()`. MIDI, video and dialogs go through small callback structures (`GameAudio`, `AppHost`). That makes a front end for another system (for example SDL) straightforward.

## Known limitations

- **One original glitch kept:** `VIDHAN1a.AVI` doesn't exist on the CD, so that one competition plays without its video, as in the original.
- **Windows only for now:** `IzzyQuest.exe` is the only front end. The engine itself is portable C99.
- **Memory checking:** TinyCC's bounds checker doesn't work on Windows, so memory safety was checked by long stress runs and code review rather than by instrumentation.

## Legal and third-party notices

- *Izzy's Quest for Olympic Gold*, its characters and the "Izzy" mascot are the property of their respective owners. This project contains **no game assets**: you must own the original game to use it. It's a clean reimplementation of the engine's behaviour for preservation and interoperability. It's not affiliated with or endorsed by the game's owners or the Olympic organisations.
- `src/third_party/indeo3data.h`: the Indeo Video 3 codebook tables from FFmpeg, © 2009–2011 Maxim Poliakovski, under the **GNU LGPL 2.1 or later**. See [`src/third_party/README.md`](src/third_party/README.md). The decoder code in `src/indeo3.c` was written for this project.
- These tools aren't redistributed here; download them as described in [Setting up the tools](#setting-up-the-tools):
  - TinyCC (LGPL);
  - Ghidra (Apache 2.0);
  - Eclipse Temurin (GPL 2.0 with the Classpath Exception).

```

`build/test-output/_c_app.ini`:

```ini
[SCORE]
name1=Coriba
score1=100,000
name2=Rocko
score2=90,000
name3=Flo
score3=80,000
name4=Owen
score4=70,000
name5=Trudy
score5=60,000
name6=Lockerbee

 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_MORPH ) GOTO USE_MORPH_CARD
 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_CORIBA ) GOTO USE_CORIBA_CARD
 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_COMPETE ) GOTO USE_COMPETE_CARD
 GOTO SUI00050

@SCORE_SCREEN_60
SET:
 SHOW_MESSAGE ( 0 )
 CONTROLS = BACK
LOOP:
 GOSUB SCORE_SCREEN_SUB
 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_MORPH ) GOTO USE_MORPH_CARD
 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_CORIBA ) GOTO USE_CORIBA_CARD
 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_COMPETE ) GOTO USE_COMPETE_CARD
 GOTO SUI00060

@SCORE_SCREEN_SUB
SHOW: GO.MID
SET:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_NONE
LOOP:
 IF ( PLAYER = 1 ) GOTO SCORE_SCREEN_1
 IF ( PLAYER = 2 ) GOTO SCORE_SCREEN_2
 IF ( PLAYER = 3 ) GOTO SCORE_SCREEN_3
 GOTO SCORE_SCREEN_4
TAKE: MIDI

@SCORE_SCREEN_1
SHOW: UI010181.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_SCORE )
 HOTSPOTS = 0
 IF ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 0 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_1
 IF ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_2
 IF ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 2 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_3
 IF ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] > 0 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_4
 IF ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_5
 IF ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] > 2 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_6
 IF ( BRONZE [ PLAYER ] > 0 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_7
 IF ( BRONZE [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_8
 IF ( BRONZE [ PLAYER ] > 2 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_9
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( HOTSPOTS )
 SET_CONTROLS ( CONTROLS + SCORE )
 TIME = 0
LOOP:
 IF ( BRONZE [ PLAYER ] > 2 ) GOTO SCORE_3_BRONZE_1
 IF ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] > 2 ) GOTO SCORE_3_SILVER_1
 IF ( HELP_GOLD [ PLAYER ] & ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 2 ) ) GOTO SCORE_3_GOLD_1
 IF ( CONTROL ( SCORE ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_HIGH_SCORES
 IF ( CONTROL ( BACK ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_BACK
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_MORPH
 IF ( CONTROL ( CORIBA_C ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_CORIBA
 IF ( CONTROL ( COMPETE ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_COMPETE
 IF ( CONTROL ( ACCEPT ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_END_TURN
 V = 1
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_1 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_GOLD
 V = 2
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_2 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_GOLD
 V = 3
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_3 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_GOLD
 V = 1
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_4 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_SILVER
 V = 2
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_5 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_SILVER
 V = 3
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_6 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_SILVER
 V = 1
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_7 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_BRONZE
 V = 2
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_8 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_BRONZE
 V = 3
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_9 ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_BRONZE
 LOOP WHILE ( TIME < 100 )
 IF ( HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] & ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] = 2 ) ) GOTO SCORE_2_GOLD
 IF ( HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] & ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] = 2 ) ) GOTO SCORE_2_SILVER
 IF ( HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] & ( BRONZE [ PLAYER ] = 2 ) ) GOTO SCORE_2_BRONZE
 LOOP
TAKE: SUI00081

 GOTO SCORE_VIEW_DENIED_3

 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
TAKE: SUI00083

@SCORE_3_BRONZE_3_A
SHOW: UI010183.BMP
LOOP:
 V = SILVER [ PLAYER ]
 R = NEXT_MEDAL ( 2 )
 SHOW_SILVER_MEDAL = R
 VIEW_SILVER [ ( V * 4 ) + PLAYER ] = R
 HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] = TRUE
 GOTO SCORE_SCREEN_SUB
TAKE: SUI00083

@SCORE_3_BRONZE_4
SHOW: UI010184.BMP
SET:
 TIME = 0
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE ( TIME < 30 )
 GOSUB SHE00044
 BRONZE [ PLAYER ] -= 3
 SILVER [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
TAKE: SUI00084

@SCORE_3_BRONZE_4_A
SHOW: UI010184.BMP
LOOP:
 V = SILVER [ PLAYER ]
 R = NEXT_MEDAL ( 2 )
 SHOW_SILVER_MEDAL = R
 VIEW_SILVER [ ( V * 4 ) + PLAYER ] = R
 HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] = TRUE
 GOTO SCORE_SCREEN_SUB
TAKE: SUI00084

@SCORE_VIEW_HIGH_SCORES
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_HIGH_SCORE )
 SET_CONTROLS ( BACK )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( BACK ) ) GOTO SCORE_SCREEN_SUB
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_MORPH
 IF ( CONTROL ( CORIBA_C ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_CORIBA
 IF ( CONTROL ( COMPETE ) ) GOTO SCORE_VIEW_COMPETE
 LOOP
TAKE: SHS00080

@COMPETITION
SET:
 SHOW_MESSAGE ( 0 )
 HOTSPOTS = 0
 IF 100 > ( ( PLAYER = 1 ) | ( HAS_MEDALS ( 1 ) = 0 ) ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_1
 IF 100 > ( ( PLAYER = 2 ) | ( HAS_MEDALS ( 2 ) = 0 ) ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_2
 IF 100 > ( ( PLAYER = 3 ) | ( HAS_MEDALS ( 3 ) = 0 ) ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_3
 IF 100 > ( ( PLAYER = 4 ) | ( HAS_MEDALS ( 4 ) = 0 ) ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_4
 CHALLENGE = 0
LOOP:
 IF ( HOTSPOTS = 0 ) GOTO COMP_ABORT
 EXIT_TO_NEXT_PLAYER = FALSE
 COMPETE_CARD [ PLAYER ] -= 1
 IF ( HOTSPOTS = HOTSPOT_1 ) THEN CHALLENGE = 1
 IF ( HOTSPOTS = HOTSPOT_2 ) THEN CHALLENGE = 2
 IF ( HOTSPOTS = HOTSPOT_3 ) THEN CHALLENGE = 3
 IF ( HOTSPOTS = HOTSPOT_4 ) THEN CHALLENGE = 4
 IF ( CHALLENGE > 0 ) GOTO COMP_AUTO_SELECT
 GOTO COMP_SELECT

@COMP_ABORT
LOOP:
 IF ( EXIT_TO_NEXT_PLAYER ) GOTO NEXT_PLAYER
 GOTO MAIN_MENU

@COMP_SELECT
SHOW: DCM00010.WAV
SHOW: BCM00010.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_CHALLENGE )
 SET_CONTROLS ( 0 )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( HOTSPOTS )
 SHOW_MESSAGE ( 0 )
LOOP:
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_1 ) ) THEN CHALLENGE = 1
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_2 ) ) THEN CHALLENGE = 2
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_3 ) ) THEN CHALLENGE = 3
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_4 ) ) THEN CHALLENGE = 4
 IF ( CHALLENGE > 0 ) GOTO COMP_CHOOSE_SET
 LOOP
TAKE: SCM00010

@COMP_AUTO_SELECT
SHOW: DCM00013.WAV
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
TAKE: SCM00013

@COMP_CHOOSE_SET
SET:
 PLAYER_LEFT = 0
 PLAYER_RIGHT = 0
 LEFT_COMP_FLAG = 0
 RIGHT_COMP_FLAG = 0
 LEFT_LIGHT = 0
 RIGHT_LIGHT = 0
 COMP_WINNER = 0
 COMP_FLAG1 = 0
 COMP_FLAG2 = 0
 COMP_FLAG3 = 0
 COMP_JUDGES = 1
LOOP:
 COMP_SET = NEXT_COMPETITION ( )

@COMP_PICK_FLAG
SET:
 SET_CONTROLS ( 0 )
 HOTSPOTS = 0
 MEDAL_TO_LOSE = MEDAL_TO_LOSE ( PLAYER )
 MEDAL_TO_WIN = MEDAL_TO_WIN ( CHALLENGE )
LOOP:
 IF ( MEDAL_TO_WIN = 1 ) GOTO COMP_PICK_FLAG_1
 IF ( MEDAL_TO_WIN = 2 ) GOTO COMP_PICK_FLAG_2
 GOTO COMP_PICK_FLAG_3

@COMP_PICK_FLAG_1
LOOP:
 GOSUB COMP_FLAG
 GOSUB SCM00023
 GOTO COMP_PICK_FLAG_4

@COMP_PICK_FLAG_2
LOOP:
 GOSUB COMP_FLAG
 GOSUB SCM00022
 GOTO COMP_PICK_FLAG_4

@COMP_PICK_FLAG_3
LOOP:
 GOSUB COMP_FLAG
 GOSUB SCM00021
 GOTO COMP_PICK_FLAG_4

@COMP_PICK_FLAG_4
LOOP:
 GOTO COMP_BEGIN
 IF ( MEDAL_TO_LOSE <= MEDAL_TO_WIN ) GOTO COMP_BEGIN
 IF ( MEDAL_TO_LOSE = 2 ) GOTO COMP_PICK_FLAG_5
 GOTO COMP_PICK_FLAG_6

@COMP_PICK_FLAG_5
LOOP:
 GOSUB SCM00025
 GOTO COMP_BEGIN

@COMP_PICK_FLAG_6
LOOP:
 GOSUB SCM00026
 GOTO COMP_BEGIN

@COMP_BEGIN
SET:
 COMP_CHOICE = 0
 HOTSPOTS = HOTSPOT_1 + HOTSPOT_2
LOOP:
 GOSUB COMP_FLAG

@COMP_BEGIN_0
LOOP:
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_1 ) ) GOTO COMP_BEGIN_1
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_2 ) ) GOTO COMP_BEGIN_2
 LOOP

@COMP_BEGIN_1
SET:
 COMP_CHOICE = 1
 PLAYER_LEFT = PLAYER
 PLAYER_RIGHT = CHALLENGE
LOOP:
 GOTO COMP_BEGIN_3

@COMP_BEGIN_2
SET:
 COMP_CHOICE = 2
 PLAYER_LEFT = CHALLENGE
 PLAYER_RIGHT = PLAYER
LOOP:
 GOTO COMP_BEGIN_3

@COMP_BEGIN_3
LOOP:
 GOSUB COMP_VIDEO
 IF ( COMP_WINNER = 1 ) GOTO COMP_JUDGE_1
 GOTO COMP_JUDGE_2

@COMP_JUDGE_1
SET:
 COMP_FLAG1 = LEFT_COMP_FLAG
 COMP_FLAG2 = LEFT_COMP_FLAG
 COMP_FLAG3 = LEFT_COMP_FLAG
 LEFT_LIGHT = 1
 RIGHT_LIGHT = 0
LOOP:
 GOTO COMP_JUDGE_3

@COMP_JUDGE_2
SET:
 COMP_FLAG1 = RIGHT_COMP_FLAG
 COMP_FLAG2 = RIGHT_COMP_FLAG
 COMP_FLAG3 = RIGHT_COMP_FLAG
 LEFT_LIGHT = 0
 RIGHT_LIGHT = 1
LOOP:
 GOTO COMP_JUDGE_3

@COMP_JUDGE_3
SET:
 COMP_JUDGES = 2
LOOP:
 GOSUB COMP_WRAPUP
 GOTO COMP_CONSEQUENCE

@COMP_CONSEQUENCE
LOOP:
 IF ( COMP_CHOICE = COMP_WINNER ) GOTO COMP_WIN

@COMP_LOSE
SET:
 PLAYER_CHANGE = TRUE
 COMP_NEW_PLAYER = CHALLENGE
 COMP_NEW_CHALLENGE = PLAYER
 TAM_MEDAL = MEDAL_TO_LOSE
LOOP:
 IF ( MEDAL_TO_LOSE = 1 ) GOTO COMP_LOSE_1
 IF ( MEDAL_TO_LOSE = 2 ) GOTO COMP_LOSE_2
 GOTO COMP_LOSE_3

@COMP_LOSE_1
SHOW: DCM00075.WAV
SHOW: CM010175.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO COMP_TAKE_MEDAL
TAKE: SCM00075

@COMP_LOSE_2
SHOW: DCM00074.WAV
SHOW: CM010174.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO COMP_TAKE_MEDAL
TAKE: SCM00074

@COMP_LOSE_3
SHOW: DCM00073.WAV
SHOW: CM010173.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO COMP_TAKE_MEDAL
TAKE: SCM00073

@COMP_WIN
SET:
 PLAYER_CHANGE = FALSE
 COMP_NEW_PLAYER = PLAYER
 COMP_NEW_CHALLENGE = CHALLENGE
 MORPH_TO_MAIN_MENU = TRUE
 TAM_MEDAL = MEDAL_TO_WIN
LOOP:
 IF ( MEDAL_TO_WIN = 1 ) GOTO COMP_WIN_1
 IF ( MEDAL_TO_WIN = 2 ) GOTO COMP_WIN_2
 GOTO COMP_WIN_3

@COMP_WIN_1
SHOW: DCM00070.WAV
SHOW: CM010170.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO COMP_TAKE_MEDAL
TAKE: SCM00070

@COMP_WIN_2
SHOW: DCM00071.WAV
SHOW: CM010171.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO COMP_TAKE_MEDAL
TAKE: SCM00071

@COMP_WIN_3
SHOW: DCM00072.WAV
SHOW: CM010172.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO COMP_TAKE_MEDAL
TAKE: SCM00072

@COMP_TAKE_MEDAL
LOOP:
 IF ( TAM_MEDAL = 0 ) GOTO COMP_RETURN

@COMP_SHOW_SCORE
SHOW: DUI00081.WAV
SHOW: UI010181.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_SCORE )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
 SET_CONTROLS ( 0 )
 PLAYER = COMP_NEW_PLAYER
 CHALLENGE = COMP_NEW_CHALLENGE
 TAKE_FROM = CHALLENGE
 GIVE_TO = PLAYER
 IF ( PLAYER_CHANGE ) THEN SHOW_MESSAGE ( 1 )
 TIME = 0
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE ( TIME < 40 )
 IF ( TAM_MEDAL = 1 ) GOTO COMP_BRONZE
 IF ( TAM_MEDAL = 2 ) GOTO COMP_SILVER
TAKE: SUI00081

@COMP_GOLD
LOOP:
 R = GOLD [ TAKE_FROM ]
 V = VIEW_GOLD [ ( R * 4 ) + TAKE_FROM ]
 GOLD [ TAKE_FROM ] -= 1
 IF ( GOLD [ GIVE_TO ] > 5 ) GOTO COMP_GOLD_BONUS
 GOLD [ GIVE_TO ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 R = GOLD [ GIVE_TO ]
 VIEW_GOLD [ ( R * 4 ) + GIVE_TO ] = V
 GOTO COMP_SHOW_RESULT

@COMP_GOLD_BONUS
LOOP:
 BONUS [ GIVE_TO ] += 10
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 R = GOLD [ GIVE_TO ]
 VIEW_GOLD [ ( R * 4 ) + GIVE_TO ] = V
 GOTO COMP_SHOW_RESULT

@COMP_SILVER
SET:
 R = SILVER [ TAKE_FROM ]
 V = VIEW_SILVER [ ( R * 4 ) + TAKE_FROM ]
 SILVER [ TAKE_FROM ] -= 1
 SILVER [ GIVE_TO ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 R = SILVER [ GIVE_TO ]
 VIEW_SILVER [ ( R * 4 ) + GIVE_TO ] = V
LOOP:
 GOTO COMP_SHOW_RESULT

@COMP_BRONZE
SET:
 R = BRONZE [ TAKE_FROM ]
 V = VIEW_BRONZE [ ( R * 4 ) + TAKE_FROM ]
 BRONZE [ TAKE_FROM ] -= 1
 BRONZE [ GIVE_TO ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 R = BRONZE [ GIVE_TO ]
 VIEW_BRONZE [ ( R * 4 ) + GIVE_TO ] = V
LOOP:
 GOTO COMP_SHOW_RESULT

@COMP_SHOW_RESULT
SET:
 CONTROLS = ACCEPT
 EXIT_TO_NEXT_PLAYER = FALSE
LOOP:
 GOSUB SCORE_SCREEN_SUB
 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_MORPH ) GOTO USE_MORPH_CARD
 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_CORIBA ) GOTO USE_CORIBA_CARD
 IF ( RESPONSE = RESPONSE_COMPETE ) GOTO USE_COMPETE_CARD

@COMP_RETURN
SET:
 EXIT_TO_NEXT_PLAYER = FALSE
 PLACES = 0
 TURNS = 0
LOOP:
 IF ( PLAYER_CHANGE ) GOTO NEW_PLAYER
 GOTO NEW_TURN

@FINALE
SHOW: STOP.MID
SET:
 IF ( N_PLAYERS = 1 ) THEN BONUS [ PLAYER ] += 10 * N_TURNS
TAKE: MIDI

@FINALE_1
SHOW: DFI00010.WAV
SHOW: FI010110.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_TURNS )
 SET_DEFAULT_CONTROLS ( 0 )
 SET_CONTROLS ( 0 )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
 SHOW_MESSAGE ( 0 )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
TAKE: SFI00010

@FINALE_2
SHOW: DFI00020.WAV
SHOW: FI010120.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
TAKE: SFI00020

@FINALE_4
SHOW: DFI00040.WAV
SHOW: FI010140.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
TAKE: SFI00040

@FINALE_SCORE
SET:
 CONTROLS = ACCEPT
LOOP:
 GOSUB SCORE_SCREEN_SUB
 GOTO CREDITS

@CREDITS
SHOW: MUIZZY.MID
TAKE: MIDI

@CREDITS_HIGH_SCORES
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_HIGH_SCORE )
 SET_CONTROLS ( ACCEPT )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
 SHOW_MESSAGE ( 0 )
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( FALSE )
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( ACCEPT ) ) GOTO END_CREDITS
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO END_CREDITS
 IF ( CONTROL ( COMPETE ) ) GOTO END_CREDITS
 IF ( CONTROL ( CORIBA_C ) ) GOTO END_CREDITS
 LOOP
TAKE: SHS00080

@END_CREDITS
SHOW: CREDITS.WAV
SHOW: CREDITS.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( 0 )
 SET_DEFAULT_CONTROLS ( 0 )
 SET_CONTROLS ( 0 )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO END_CREDITS
TAKE: CREDITS

 IF ( INTRO [ LOCATION ] = R ) THEN R = RND ( 3 )
 INTRO [ LOCATION ] = R
LOOP:

 IF ( ( RND ( 4 ) + 2 ) < PLACES ) GOTO LANSE_CAUGHT_IZZY
 IF ( LOCATION = CORIBA_LOCATION ) GOTO SFE00050
 CORIBA = 0
 IF ( SETS_READY ( PLAYER ) = 0 ) GOTO SFE00024
 HELP_FIND_CORIBA = HELP_FIND_CORIBA [ PLAYER ]
 IF ( INTRO [ LOCATION ] = 1 ) GOTO SFE00021

 GOTO SFE00023

 R = RND ( 20 )
 IF ( R = 1 ) GOTO STR00024_MORPH
 IF ( ( R = 2 ) & ( N_PLAYERS > 1 ) ) GOTO STR00024_COMPETE
 IF ( SETS_READY ( PLAYER ) = 0 ) GOTO STR00030
 IF ( HELP_SETS [ PLAYER ] ) GOTO STR00024_CORIBA_HELP
 GOTO STR00030

 IF ( INTRO [ LOCATION ] = 2 ) GOTO SSH00022
 GOTO SSH00023

@LOGIC_SUBROUTINES

@CHOOSE_ITEM
SET:
 HOTSPOTS = FOUR_HOTSPOTS
 IF ( STATE5 > 0 ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_5
 IF ( WHATIZIT & SETS_READY ( PLAYER ) ) THEN HOTSPOTS += HOTSPOT_6
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( HOTSPOTS )
 SET_CONTROLS ( BACK )
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_NONE
 ITEM = 0
 TIME = 0
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( BACK ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_BACK
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_MORPH
 IF ( CONTROL ( CORIBA_C ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_CORIBA
 IF ( CONTROL ( COMPETE ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_COMPETE
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_1 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_1
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_2 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_2
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_3 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_3
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_4 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_4
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_5 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_5
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_6 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_WHATIZIT
 LOOP WHILE ( TIME < 100 )
 IF ( HELP_FIND_CORIBA ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_FIND_CORIBA
 LOOP WHILE ( LOCATION_HELP = 0 )
 LOOP WHILE ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 2 )
 LOCATION_HELP = 0
 IF ( ( ITEM_1 [ LOCATION ] = 0 ) & ( ITEM_2 [ LOCATION ] = 0 ) & ( ITEM_3 [ LOCATION ] = 0 ) & ( ITEM_4 [ LOCATION ] = 0 ) & ( ITEM_5 [ LOCATION ] = 0 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_EMPTY
 R = RND ( 20 )
 IF ( ( R = 1 ) & ( BRONZE [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_BRONZE_1
 IF ( ( R = 2 ) & ( BRONZE [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_BRONZE_2
 IF ( ( R = 3 ) & ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_SILVER_1
 IF ( ( R = 4 ) & ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_SILVER_2
 IF ( ( R = 5 ) & ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_SILVER_3
 IF ( ( R = 6 ) & ( SILVER [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_SILVER_4
 IF ( R = 7 ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_GOLD_1
 IF ( R = 8 ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_GOLD_2
 IF ( R = 9 ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_GOLD_3
 IF ( R = 10 ) GOTO CHOOSE_HELP_GOLD_4
 LOOP

@CHOOSE_HELP_FIND_CORIBA
SHOW: DHE00074
SET:
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
 LOCATION_HELP = FALSE
 HELP_FIND_CORIBA = FALSE
 HELP_FIND_CORIBA [ PLAYER ] = FALSE
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( BACK ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_BACK
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_MORPH
 IF ( CONTROL ( CORIBA_C ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_CORIBA
 IF ( CONTROL ( COMPETE ) ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_COMPETE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 RETURN
TAKE: SHE00074

@CHOOSE_ITEM_1
SET:
 MEDAL = ITEM_1 [ LOCATION ]
 IF ( MEDAL = 0 ) THEN TURNS += 1
 IF ( ( MEDAL >= ITEM_GOLD_MEDAL ) & ( MEDAL <= ITEM_BRONZE_MEDAL ) ) THEN TURNS += 1
LOOP:
 ITEM = 1
 STATE1 = MEDAL
 STATE2 = 0
 STATE3 = 0
 STATE4 = 0
 RETURN IF ( ( MEDAL = ITEM_COMPETE_CARD ) | ( MEDAL >= ITEM_MORPH_CARD ) )
 IF ( ( RND ( 4 ) + 2 ) < TURNS ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_1_LANSE
 RETURN IF ( MEDAL > 0 )
 ITEM = 0
 STATE1 = ITEM_EMPTY
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_1_LANSE
LOOP:
 LANSE1 = 1
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_LANSE
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_2
SET:
 MEDAL = ITEM_2 [ LOCATION ]
 IF ( MEDAL = 0 ) THEN TURNS += 1
 IF ( ( MEDAL >= ITEM_GOLD_MEDAL ) & ( MEDAL <= ITEM_BRONZE_MEDAL ) ) THEN TURNS += 1
LOOP:
 ITEM = 2
 STATE1 = 0
 STATE2 = MEDAL
 STATE3 = 0
 STATE4 = 0
 RETURN IF ( ( MEDAL = ITEM_COMPETE_CARD ) | ( MEDAL >= ITEM_MORPH_CARD ) )
 IF ( ( RND ( 4 ) + 2 ) < TURNS ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_2_LANSE
 RETURN IF ( MEDAL > 0 )
 ITEM = 0
 STATE2 = ITEM_EMPTY
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_2_LANSE
LOOP:
 LANSE2 = 1
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_LANSE
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_3
SET:
 MEDAL = ITEM_3 [ LOCATION ]
 IF ( MEDAL = 0 ) THEN TURNS += 1
 IF ( ( MEDAL >= ITEM_GOLD_MEDAL ) & ( MEDAL <= ITEM_BRONZE_MEDAL ) ) THEN TURNS += 1
LOOP:
 ITEM = 3
 STATE1 = 0
 STATE2 = 0
 STATE3 = MEDAL
 STATE4 = 0
 RETURN IF ( ( MEDAL = ITEM_COMPETE_CARD ) | ( MEDAL >= ITEM_MORPH_CARD ) )
 IF ( ( RND ( 4 ) + 2 ) < TURNS ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_3_LANSE
 RETURN IF ( MEDAL > 0 )
 ITEM = 0
 STATE3 = ITEM_EMPTY
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_3_LANSE
LOOP:
 LANSE3 = 1
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_LANSE
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_4
SET:
 MEDAL = ITEM_4 [ LOCATION ]
 IF ( MEDAL = 0 ) THEN TURNS += 1
 IF ( ( MEDAL >= ITEM_GOLD_MEDAL ) & ( MEDAL <= ITEM_BRONZE_MEDAL ) ) THEN TURNS += 1
LOOP:
 ITEM = 4
 STATE1 = 0
 STATE2 = 0
 STATE3 = 0
 STATE4 = MEDAL
 RETURN IF ( ( MEDAL = ITEM_COMPETE_CARD ) | ( MEDAL >= ITEM_MORPH_CARD ) )
 IF ( ( RND ( 4 ) + 2 ) < TURNS ) GOTO CHOOSE_ITEM_4_LANSE
 RETURN IF ( MEDAL > 0 )
 ITEM = 0
 STATE4 = ITEM_EMPTY
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_4_LANSE
LOOP:
 LANSE4 = 1
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_LANSE
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_5
LOOP:
 ITEM = 5
 MEDAL = ITEM_BRONZE_MEDAL
 STATE1 = 0
 STATE2 = 0
 STATE3 = 0
 STATE4 = 0
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_BACK
LOOP:
 ITEM =- 1
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_WHATIZIT
LOOP:
 ITEM =- 2
 STATE1 = 0
 STATE2 = 0
 STATE3 = 0
 STATE4 = 0
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_MORPH
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_MORPH
 ITEM = 0
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_CORIBA
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_CORIBA
 ITEM = 0
 RETURN

@CHOOSE_ITEM_COMPETE
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_COMPETE
 ITEM = 0
 RETURN

@ACCEPT_ITEM
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_NONE
 IF ( ( ITEM < 1 ) | ( ITEM > 4 ) ) GOTO ACCEPT_YES
 IF ( ( MEDAL < ITEM_GOLD_MEDAL ) | ( MEDAL > ITEM_BRONZE_MEDAL ) ) GOTO ACCEPT_YES_DELAY

@ACCEPT_ITEM_YES_NO
SET:
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
 SET_CONTROLS ( ACCEPT + DECLINE )
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( ACCEPT ) ) GOTO ACCEPT_YES
 IF ( CONTROL ( DECLINE ) ) GOTO ACCEPT_NO
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO ACCEPT_USE_MORPH
 IF ( CONTROL ( CORIBA_C ) ) GOTO ACCEPT_USE_CORIBA
 IF ( CONTROL ( COMPETE ) ) GOTO ACCEPT_USE_COMPETE
 LOOP

@ACCEPT_NO
LOOP:
 STATE1 = 0
 STATE2 = 0
 STATE3 = 0
 STATE4 = 0
 RETURN

@ACCEPT_YES_DELAY
SET:
 TIME = 0
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE ( TIME < 20 )

@ACCEPT_YES
LOOP:
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_COMPETE_CARD ) GOSUB ACCEPT_COMPETE_CARD
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_MORPH_CARD ) GOSUB ACCEPT_MORPH_CARD
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_GOLD_MEDAL ) GOSUB ACCEPT_GOLD
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_SILVER_MEDAL ) GOSUB ACCEPT_SILVER
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_BRONZE_MEDAL ) GOSUB ACCEPT_BRONZE
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_CORIBA_CARD ) GOSUB ACCEPT_CORIBA_CARD
 IF ( ITEM = 1 ) GOTO ACCEPT_ITEM_1
 IF ( ITEM = 2 ) GOTO ACCEPT_ITEM_2
 IF ( ITEM = 3 ) GOTO ACCEPT_ITEM_3
 IF ( ITEM = 4 ) GOTO ACCEPT_ITEM_4
 IF ( ITEM = 5 ) GOTO ACCEPT_ITEM_5
 GOTO ACCEPT_ITEM_GIFT

@ACCEPT_COMPETE_CARD
LOOP:
 COMPETE_CARD [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 GOSUB SIZ00023
 GOTO SHE00020

@ACCEPT_MORPH_CARD
LOOP:
 MORPH_CARD [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 GOSUB SIZ00021
 GOTO SHE00030

@ACCEPT_CORIBA_CARD
LOOP:
 CORIBA_CARD [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 GOSUB SIZ00024
 GOTO SHE00032

@ACCEPT_GOLD
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_END_TURN
 IF ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 5 ) GOTO ACCEPT_GOLD_BONUS
 GOLD [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 V = GOLD [ PLAYER ]
 GOSUB ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD
 HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] = TRUE
 GOTO SIZ00011

@ACCEPT_GOLD_BONUS
LOOP:
 BONUS [ PLAYER ] += 10
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 GOSUB ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD
 GOTO SIZ00011

@ACCEPT_SILVER
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_END_TURN
 SILVER [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 V = SILVER [ PLAYER ]
 GOSUB ACCEPT_SHOW_SILVER
 HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] = TRUE
 GOTO SIZ00012

@ACCEPT_BRONZE
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_END_TURN
 BRONZE [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 V = BRONZE [ PLAYER ]
 GOSUB ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE
 HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] = TRUE
 RETURN

@ACCEPT_ITEM_1
LOOP:
 ITEM_1 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 STATE1 = ITEM_EMPTY
 RETURN

@ACCEPT_ITEM_2
LOOP:
 ITEM_2 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 STATE2 = ITEM_EMPTY
 RETURN

@ACCEPT_ITEM_3
LOOP:
 ITEM_3 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 STATE3 = ITEM_EMPTY
 RETURN

@ACCEPT_ITEM_4

 ITEM_4 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 STATE4 = ITEM_EMPTY
 RETURN

@ACCEPT_ITEM_5
LOOP:
 ITEM_5 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 STATE5 = 0
 RETURN

@ACCEPT_ITEM_GIFT
LOOP:
 RETURN

 RETURN

 RETURN

@ACCEPT_USE_COMPETE

 RETURN

@FIND_CORIBA
SET:
 CONTROLS = 0
 IF ( SETS_READY ( PLAYER ) = 0 ) THEN CONTROLS = SKIP
 SET_CONTROLS ( CONTROLS )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
LOOP:
 IF ( ( SETS_READY ( PLAYER ) & ( SUNCATCHER >= 4 ) ) GOTO FC_LAST_SET
 IF ( SETS_READY ( PLAYER ) ) GOTO FC_ONE_SET
 GOTO FC_NO_SETS

@FC_ONE_SET
SHOW: DCR00053.WAV
SHOW: CR010153.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
TAKE: SCR00053

@FC_READY_TO_FUSE
SHOW: DCR00020.WAV
SHOW: CR010120.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO FC_FUSE_SETS
TAKE: SCR00020

@FC_LAST_SET
SHOW: DCR00051.WAV
SHOW: CR010151.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
TAKE: SCR00051

@FC_LAST_SET_CORIBA
SHOW: DCR00060.WAV
SHOW: CR010160.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO FC_FUSE_SETS
TAKE: SCR00060

@FC_NO_SETS
SHOW: DCR00054.WAV
SHOW: CR010154.BMP
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( SKIP ) ) GOTO FC_NONE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 ENCOURAGE = RND ( 3 )
 IF ( ENCOURAGE = 1 ) GOTO FC_NO_SETS_1
 IF ( ENCOURAGE = 2 ) GOTO FC_NO_SETS_2
 GOTO FC_NO_SETS_3
TAKE: SCR00054

@FC_NO_SETS_1
SHOW: DCR00011.WAV
SHOW: CR010111.BMP
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( SKIP ) ) GOTO FC_NONE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO FC_NONE
TAKE: SCR00011

@FC_NO_SETS_2
SHOW: DCR00012.WAV
SHOW: CR010112.BMP
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( SKIP ) ) GOTO FC_NONE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO FC_NONE
TAKE: SCR00012

@FC_NO_SETS_3
SHOW: DCR00013.WAV
SHOW: CR010113.BMP
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( SKIP ) ) GOTO FC_NONE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO FC_NONE
TAKE: SCR00013

@FC_FUSE_SETS
SHOW: DCR00030.WAV
SHOW: CR010130.BMP
SET:
 HELP_SETS [ 1 ] = FALSE
 HELP_SETS [ 2 ] = FALSE
 HELP_SETS [ 3 ] = FALSE
 HELP_SETS [ 4 ] = FALSE
 HELP_GOLD [ 1 ] = FALSE
 HELP_GOLD [ 2 ] = FALSE
 HELP_GOLD [ 3 ] = FALSE
 HELP_GOLD [ 4 ] = FALSE
 HELP_MEDAL [ 1 ] = FALSE
 HELP_MEDAL [ 2 ] = FALSE
 HELP_MEDAL [ 3 ] = FALSE
 HELP_MEDAL [ 4 ] = FALSE
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 FUSE_SETS ( PLAYER )
 BONUS [ PLAYER ] += 30
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 IF ( SUNCATCHER = 1 ) GOTO FC_FUSE_SETS_1
 IF ( SUNCATCHER = 2 ) GOTO FC_FUSE_SETS_2
 IF ( SUNCATCHER = 3 ) GOTO FC_FUSE_SETS_3
 IF ( SUNCATCHER = 4 ) GOTO FC_FUSE_SETS_4
 GOTO FC_FUSE_SETS_5
TAKE: SCR00030

@FC_FUSE_SETS_1
SHOW: DCR00041.WAV
SHOW: CR010141.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOSUB SHE00047
 GOTO FC_END_ROUND
TAKE: SCR00041

@FC_FUSE_SETS_2
SHOW: DCR00042.WAV
SHOW: CR010142.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOSUB SHE00048
 GOTO FC_END_ROUND
TAKE: SCR00042

@FC_FUSE_SETS_3
SHOW: DCR00043.WAV
SHOW: CR010143.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOSUB SHE00049
 GOTO FC_END_ROUND
TAKE: SCR00043

@FC_FUSE_SETS_4
SHOW: DCR00044.WAV
SHOW: CR010144.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOSUB SHE00050
 GOTO FC_END_ROUND
TAKE: SCR00044

@FC_FUSE_SETS_5
SHOW: DCR00045.WAV
SHOW: CR010145.BMP
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO FC_WIN_GAME
TAKE: SCR00045

@FC_NONE
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_NONE
 GOTO MOVE_CORIBA

@FC_WIN_GAME
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_WIN_GAME
 GOTO MOVE_CORIBA

@FC_END_TURN
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_END_TURN
 GOTO MOVE_CORIBA

@FC_END_ROUND
LOOP:
 RESPONSE = RESPONSE_END_ROUND
 GOTO MOVE_CORIBA

@MOVE_CORIBA
LOOP:
 R = RND ( 12 ) + 1
 CORIBA_LOCATION = CORIBA_LOCATION [ R ]
 LOOP WHILE ( CORIBA_LOCATION = LAST_CORIBA_LOCATION )
 LAST_CORIBA_LOCATION = CORIBA_LOCATION
 RETURN

@LANSE_APPEARS
SHOW: STOP.MID
SET:
 SET_CONTROLS ( 0 )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
LOOP:
 IF ( ( MEDAL = 0 ) & ( LANSE_TAKE [ PLAYER ] ) & ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 1 ) ) GOTO LANSE_TAKE_MEDAL
 IF ( ( MEDAL = 0 ) & ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 3 ) ) GOTO LANSE_TAKE_MEDAL
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_GOLD_MEDAL ) GOTO LANSE_COMPETITION_GOLD
 IF ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] < 2 ) GOTO LANSE_LOSE_TURN
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_SILVER_MEDAL ) GOTO LANSE_COMPETITION_SILVER
 GOTO LANSE_LOSE_TURN
TAKE: MIDI

@LANSE_COMPETITION_GOLD
SHOW: DLA00013.WAV
SHOW: LA010113.BMP
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO USE_MORPH_CARD_ON_LANSE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_BEGIN
TAKE: SLA00013

@LANSE_COMPETITION_SILVER
SHOW: DLA00012.WAV
SHOW: LA010112.BMP
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO USE_MORPH_CARD_ON_LANSE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_BEGIN
TAKE: SLA00012

@LANSE_COMPETITION_BRONZE

SHOW: LA010111.BMP
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO USE_MORPH_CARD_ON_LANSE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_BEGIN
TAKE: SLA00011

LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO USE_MORPH_CARD_ON_LANSE
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )

@LANSE_COMP_BEGIN
SET:
 PLAYER_LEFT = 0
 PLAYER_RIGHT = 0
 LEFT_COMP_FLAG = 0
 RIGHT_COMP_FLAG = 0
 LEFT_LIGHT = 0
 RIGHT_LIGHT = 0
 COMP_WINNER = 0
 COMP_FLAG1 = 0
 COMP_FLAG2 = 0
 COMP_FLAG3 = 0
 COMP_JUDGES = 1
LOOP:
 COMP_SET = NEXT_COMPETITION ( )
 GOSUB COMP_FLAG
 COMP_CHOICE = RND ( 2 ) + 1
 IF ( COMP_CHOICE = 1 ) GOTO LANSE_COMP_BEGIN_1
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_BEGIN_2

@LANSE_COMP_BEGIN_1
LOOP:
 GOSUB SLA00031
 PLAYER_LEFT = 5
 PLAYER_RIGHT = PLAYER
 LEFT_COMP_FLAG = 25
 GOSUB COMP_FLAG
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_BEGIN_3

@LANSE_COMP_BEGIN_2
LOOP:
 GOSUB SLA00031
 PLAYER_LEFT = PLAYER
 PLAYER_RIGHT = 5
 RIGHT_COMP_FLAG = 25
 GOSUB COMP_FLAG
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_BEGIN_3

@LANSE_COMP_BEGIN_3
LOOP:
 GOSUB COMP_VIDEO
 IF ( COMP_WINNER = 1 ) GOTO LANSE_COMP_JUDGE_1
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_JUDGE_2

@LANSE_COMP_JUDGE_1
SET:
 COMP_FLAG1 = LEFT_COMP_FLAG
 COMP_FLAG2 = LEFT_COMP_FLAG
 COMP_FLAG3 = LEFT_COMP_FLAG
 LEFT_LIGHT = 1
 RIGHT_LIGHT = 0
LOOP:
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_CONSEQUENCE

@LANSE_COMP_JUDGE_2
SET:
 COMP_FLAG1 = RIGHT_COMP_FLAG
 COMP_FLAG2 = RIGHT_COMP_FLAG
 COMP_FLAG3 = RIGHT_COMP_FLAG
 LEFT_LIGHT = 0
 RIGHT_LIGHT = 1
LOOP:
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_CONSEQUENCE

@LANSE_COMP_CONSEQUENCE
LOOP:
 IF ( COMP_CHOICE = COMP_WINNER ) GOTO LANSE_WINS

@LANSE_LOSES
SET:
 LOST_TO_LANSE = FALSE
 COMP_JUDGES = 2
LOOP:
 GOSUB COMP_WRAPUP

@LANSE_LOSES_BADLY
SHOW: DLA00042.WAV
SHOW: LA010142.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL )
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE BUSY ( )
 GOTO LANSE_GIVES_UP_MEDAL
TAKE: SLA00042

 GOSUB LANSE_COMP_WRAPUP

@LANSE_GIVES_UP_MEDAL
SET:
 PLACES = 0
 TURNS = 0
LOOP:
 GOSUB LANSE_COMP_ITEM
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_GOLD_MEDAL ) GOTO LANSE_COMP_GOLD
 IF ( MEDAL = ITEM_SILVER_MEDAL ) GOTO LANSE_COMP_SILVER
 GOTO LANSE_COMP_BRONZE

@LANSE_COMP_GOLD
LOOP:
 IF ( GOLD [ PLAYER ] > 5 ) GOTO LANSE_COMP_GOLD_BONUS
 GOLD [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 GOSUB ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD
 HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] = TRUE
 GOSUB SIZ00011
 GOTO END_OF_TURN

@LANSE_COMP_GOLD_BONUS
LOOP:
 BONUS [ PLAYER ] += 10
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 GOSUB ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD
 GOSUB SIZ00011
 GOTO END_OF_TURN

@LANSE_COMP_SILVER
LOOP:
 SILVER [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 GOSUB ACCEPT_SHOW_SILVER
 HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] = TRUE
 GOSUB SIZ00012
 GOTO END_OF_TURN

@LANSE_COMP_BRONZE
LOOP:
 BRONZE [ PLAYER ] += 1
 SAVE_GAME_OK ( TRUE )
 GOSUB ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE
 HELP_MEDAL [ PLAYER ] = TRUE
 GOTO END_OF_TURN

@LANSE_COMP_ITEM
LOOP:
 IF ( ITEM = 1 ) THEN ITEM_1 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 IF ( ITEM = 2 ) THEN ITEM_2 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 IF ( ITEM = 3 ) THEN ITEM_3 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 IF ( ITEM = 4 ) THEN ITEM_4 [ LOCATION ] = 0
 RETURN

 GOTO SLA00061

 IF ( CONTROL ( MORPH ) ) GOTO USE_MORPH_CARD_ON_LANSE
 LOOP

TAKE: SCM00089

@LANSE_TAKE_SHOW_SCORE
SHOW: DUI00081.WAV
SHOW: UI010181.BMP
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_SCORE )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( 0 )
 SET_CONTROLS ( 0 )
 TIME = 0
LOOP:
 LOOP WHILE ( TIME < 40 )
TAKE: SUI00081

@LANSE_TAKE_REMOVE_MEDAL
LOOP:
 GOLD [ PLAYER ] -= 1
 GOTO END_OF_TURN

@ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD

 R = NEXT_MEDAL ( 3 )

@ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD_R

SET:
 SHOW_GOLD_MEDAL = R
 VIEW_GOLD [ ( V * 4 ) + PLAYER ] = R
LOOP:
 IF ( R = 1 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD_01
 IF ( R = 2 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD_02

@ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE
LOOP:
 R = NEXT_MEDAL ( 1 )

@ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_R
SHOW: STOP.MID
SET:
 SHOW_BRONZE_MEDAL = R
 VIEW_BRONZE [ ( V * 4 ) + PLAYER ] = R
LOOP:
 IF ( R = 1 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_01
 IF ( R = 2 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_02
 IF ( R = 3 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_03
 IF ( R = 4 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_04
 IF ( R = 5 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_05
 IF ( R = 6 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_06
 IF ( R = 7 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_07
 IF ( R = 8 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_08
 IF ( R = 9 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_09
 IF ( R = 10 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_10
 IF ( R = 11 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_11
 IF ( R = 12 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_12
 IF ( R = 13 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_13
 IF ( R = 14 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_14
 IF ( R = 15 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_15
 IF ( R = 16 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_16
 IF ( R = 17 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_17
 IF ( R = 18 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_18
 IF ( R = 19 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_19
 IF ( R = 20 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_20
 IF ( R = 21 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_21
 IF ( R = 22 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_22
 IF ( R = 23 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_23
 IF ( R = 24 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_24
 IF ( R = 25 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_25
 IF ( R = 26 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_26
 IF ( R = 27 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_27
 IF ( R = 28 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_28
 IF ( R = 29 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_29
 IF ( R = 30 ) GOTO ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_30
 RETURN
TAKE: MIDI

@THE_MAGICAL_HISTORY_TOUR

@HISTORY_TOUR
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_SUNDIAL )
 SET_CONTROLS ( BACK )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( ELEVEN_HOTSPOTS )
 SHOW_MESSAGE ( 0 )
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( BACK ) ) GOTO MAIN_MENU
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_1 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1896
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_2 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1900
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_3 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1912
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_4 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1920
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_5 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1932
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_6 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1948
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_7 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1952
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_8 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1960
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_9 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1972
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_10 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1980
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_11 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1992
 LOOP
TAKE: SHE00075

@HISTORY_SUNDIAL
SET:
 SET_CONTROL_PANEL ( CPANEL_SUNDIAL )
 SET_CONTROLS ( BACK )
 SET_HOTSPOTS ( ELEVEN_HOTSPOTS )
 SHOW_MESSAGE ( 0 )
LOOP:
 IF ( CONTROL ( BACK ) ) GOTO MAIN_MENU
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_1 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1896
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_2 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1900
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_3 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1912
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_4 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1920
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_5 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1932
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_6 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1948
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_7 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1952
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_8 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1960
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_9 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1972
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_10 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1980
 IF ( HOTSPOT ( HOTSPOT_11 ) ) GOTO HISTORY_1992
 LOOP
TAKE: SHIS0000
