`src/app.c`:

```c
/*
 * app.c - the application shell (see app.h).
 */
#include "app.h"
#include "config.h"
#include "izzy_common.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ strings */

static const char *const k_strings[] = {   /* STRINGTABLE 0x1b59.. (blocks 0x1b6 / 0x1b7) */
    "Do you really want to quit?",
    "Do you want to save your game?",
    "AVI video support not found.\nPlease install Pagemaster again.",
    "The AVI version installed on your system is outdated.\nPlease install Pagemaster again.",
    "WAV audio support not found.\nCheck your sound card installation.",
    "Unable to access WaveMapper audio support.\nCheck your sound card installation.",
    "Your audio card or drivers do not support 16 bit stereo sound.",
    "System is not in 256 color mode.\nPlease check your Windows setup.",
    "You're the new champion!\nPlease enter your name in the high score list.",
    "An error occurred while saving your file.\nCheck the disk and try again.",
    "Unable to save file.\nCheck the disk and try again.",
    "An error occurred while opening your file.\nCheck the disk and try again.",
    "Unable to open file.",
    "Do you really want to start over?",
    "Unable to run the StoryGame Engine..\nTry closing some other applications.",
    "Unable to find your CD-ROM drive.\nCheck your CD-ROM Installation Manual.",
    "Please put the Izzy's Adventure CD in the CD-ROM drive.",
    "Please close the CD-ROM drive door.\nTo exit press CANCEL.",
    "Please put the Izzy's Adventure CD in the CD-ROM drive.",
    "Please put the Izzy's Adventure CD in the CD-ROM drive.",
    "Please type your name in the highlighted box."
};

const char *app_string(int id)
{   /* orig: 1008:5bce app_LoadString (LoadString into a buffer of the Game) */
    if (id < APP_STR_QUIT || id > APP_STR_TYPE_NAME) return NULL;
    return k_strings[id - APP_STR_QUIT];
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ settings */

static int ini_int(const char *ini, const char *section, const char *key, const char *def)
{   /* GetPrivateProfileString + atoi (1000:3848) */
    char buf[16];
    ini_get(ini, section, key, def, buf, sizeof buf);
    return atoi(buf);
}

void app_load_settings(AppSettings *s, const char *ini)
{
    char buf[16];
    s->volume = ini_int(ini, "OPTIONS", "VOLUME", "0");           /* 1008:1d2c (app_InitInstance) */
    if (s->volume != -2 && s->volume != -1) s->volume = 0;
    s->music = ini_int(ini, "OPTIONS", "MUSIC", "1");
    s->intro = ini_int(ini, "OPTIONS", "INTRO", "1");             /* ui_ctor */
    ini_get(ini, "Options", "Override8Bit", "FALSE", buf, sizeof buf);   /* app_LoadSettings: _fstricmp with "true" */
    s->override8Bit = izzy_stricmp(buf, "true") == 0;
}

void app_save_settings(const AppSettings *s, const char *ini)
{   /* WM_CLOSE: itoa (1000:7ff0) + WritePrivateProfileString */
    char buf[16];
    sprintf(buf, "%d", s->volume); ini_set(ini, "OPTIONS", "VOLUME", buf);
    sprintf(buf, "%d", s->music);  ini_set(ini, "OPTIONS", "MUSIC", buf);
}

void app_apply_settings(Game *g, const AppSettings *s)
{
    g->wave.masterVolume = s->volume;                             /* Game +0x30 */
    g->musicOn = s->music != 0;                                   /* Game +0x32 */
    g->noSkipAvi = s->intro;                                      /* UI +0xc */
    g->override8Bit = s->override8Bit;                            /* Game +0x34 */
}

static void check(App *a, int cmd, int on) { if (a->host.check_menu) a->host.check_menu(a->host.ctx, cmd, on); }

static void show_volume(App *a)
{
    check(a, APP_CMD_VOLUME_HIGH, a->settings.volume == 0);
    check(a, APP_CMD_VOLUME_MED, a->settings.volume == -1);
    check(a, APP_CMD_VOLUME_LOW, a->settings.volume == -2);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ open / close */

int app_open(App *a, const char *gameDir, unsigned seed)
{   /* app_InitInstance: the window, the menu checks from [OPTIONS], then the engine (app_LoadSettings) */
    AppHost host = a->host;
    memset(a, 0, sizeof *a);
    a->host = host;
    if (game_open(&a->game, gameDir, seed) != 0) return -1;
    app_load_settings(&a->settings, a->game.iniPath);
    app_apply_settings(&a->game, &a->settings);
    show_volume(a);
    check(a, APP_CMD_MUSIC, a->settings.music != 0);
    return 0;
}

void app_close(App *a) { game_close(&a->game); }

static int ask(App *a, int id, int style)
{
    if (!a->host.message) return style == APP_MB_YESNO || style == APP_MB_YESNO_WARN ? APP_ID_NO : APP_ID_CANCEL;
    return a->host.message(a->host.ctx, id, app_string(id), style);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ save / load */

int app_save_to(App *a, const char *path)
{   /* ofstream (1000:2868, mode 0x82 binary|out); vet_SaveGame + UI +0xcc */
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "wb");
    int ok;
    if (!f) return 0;
    ok = game_save(&a->game, f);
    if (fclose(f) != 0) ok = 0;
    return ok;
}

static int load_checked(App *a, FILE *f)
{   /* Not in the original: vet_LoadState writes into the running game as it reads, so a damaged file left a
     * half-loaded game behind after "An error occurred while opening your file". Read it into a scratch game
     * first; only a file that loads completely is then loaded into the real one. */
    static Game probe;
    int ok = 0;
    long start = ftell(f);
    if (game_open(&probe, a->game.gameDir, 1) == 0) {
        probe.ui.iniPath = a->game.ui.iniPath;
        ok = game_load(&probe, f);
        game_close(&probe);
    }
    if (!ok || fseek(f, start, SEEK_SET) != 0) return 0;
    a->closed = 0;
    return game_load(&a->game, f);
}

int app_load_from(App *a, const char *path)
{   /* ifstream (1000:23fe, mode 0xa1); the UI resets, vet_LoadState, UI +0xd4 / +0xd0 */
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "rb");
    int ok;
    if (!f) return 0;
    ok = load_checked(a, f);
    fclose(f);
    return ok;
}

static int choose(App *a, int save)
{   /* GetSaveFileName / GetOpenFileName with "*.SGE" (1008:387e), flags 2 (overwrite prompt) / 0x1800 (must exist).
     * With no extension typed, ".SGE" is appended (strchr '.', 1000:08ce). */
    a->savePath[0] = 0;
    if (!a->host.choose_file || !a->host.choose_file(a->host.ctx, save, a->savePath, sizeof a->savePath - 5)) return 0;
    if (save && !strchr(a->savePath, '.')) strcat(a->savePath, ".SGE");
    return 1;
}

static void save_loop(App *a)
{   /* "Do you want to save your game?" -> save until it works, the dialog is cancelled or Cancel is pressed */
    int done;
    do {
        done = 1;
        if (!choose(a, 1)) break;
        {
            FILE *f = fopen(a->savePath, "wb");
            if (!f) { ask(a, APP_STR_SAVE_OPEN, APP_MB_OK); continue; }
            if (!game_save(&a->game, f) | (fclose(f) != 0))
                if (ask(a, APP_STR_SAVE_ERROR, APP_MB_RETRYCANCEL) == APP_ID_RETRY) done = 0;
        }
    } while (!done);
    a->game.rules.ui.saveGameOk = 0;
}

static void offer_save(App *a)
{
    if (a->game.rules.ui.saveGameOk && ask(a, APP_STR_SAVE_FIRST, APP_MB_YESNO) == APP_ID_YES) save_loop(a);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ commands */

int app_command(App *a, int cmd)
{   /* orig: MAINWNDPROC WM_COMMAND (1008:0ad1 switch) */
    Game *g = &a->game;
    switch (cmd) {
    case APP_CMD_NEW_GAME:
        game_pause(g);
        if (ask(a, APP_STR_START_OVER, APP_MB_YESNO) != APP_ID_NO) {
            a->closed = 0;
            offer_save(a);
            game_resume(g);
            game_new_game(g);
        } else game_resume(g);
        return 1;
    case APP_CMD_SAVE_GAME:
        game_pause(g);
        if (choose(a, 1)) {
            FILE *f = fopen(a->savePath, "wb");
            if (!f) ask(a, APP_STR_SAVE_OPEN, APP_MB_OK);
            else {
                int ok = game_save(g, f);
                if (fclose(f) != 0) ok = 0;
                if (!ok) ask(a, APP_STR_SAVE_ERROR, 0);
                g->rules.ui.saveGameOk = 0;                          /* UI +0x192 = 0 */
            }
        }
        game_resume(g);
        return 1;
    case APP_CMD_LOAD_GAME:
        game_pause(g);
        offer_save(a);
        if (choose(a, 0)) {
            FILE *f = fopen(a->savePath, "rb");
            if (!f) ask(a, APP_STR_LOAD_OPEN, APP_MB_OK);
            else {
                if (!load_checked(a, f)) ask(a, APP_STR_LOAD_ERROR, 0);   /* the current game is left as it was */
                else g->rules.ui.saveGameOk = 0;
                fclose(f);
            }
        }
        game_resume(g);
        return 1;
    case APP_CMD_EXIT:
        app_request_quit(a);                                         /* PostMessage(WM_CLOSE) */
        return 1;
    case APP_CMD_ABOUT:
        if (a->host.about) a->host.about(a->host.ctx);               /* DialogBox 0x67, ABOUTDLG */
        return 1;
    case APP_CMD_VOLUME_LOW: case APP_CMD_VOLUME_MED: case APP_CMD_VOLUME_HIGH:
        a->settings.volume = cmd == APP_CMD_VOLUME_LOW ? -2 : cmd == APP_CMD_VOLUME_MED ? -1 : 0;
        show_volume(a);
        g->wave.masterVolume = a->settings.volume;                  /* Game +0x30 */
        return 1;
    case APP_CMD_MUSIC:
        a->settings.music = !a->settings.music;
        check(a, APP_CMD_MUSIC, a->settings.music);
        g->musicOn = a->settings.music;                             /* Game +0x32: the next MIDI tick stops it */
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

int app_request_quit(App *a)
{   /* WM_CLOSE (1008:0787) */
    Game *g = &a->game;
    if (a->closed) { if (a->host.quit) a->host.quit(a->host.ctx); return 1; }
    if (ask(a, APP_STR_QUIT, APP_MB_YESNO_WARN) != APP_ID_YES) return 0;
    if (g->aviPlaying && g->audio.stopAvi) g->audio.stopAvi(g->audio.ctx);   /* UI +0xb0 */
    offer_save(a);
    /* orig: 1008:76f0 (UI +0xd8): close the open panels, pause, stop the MIDI, remember the node (UI +0x496) and run
     * the CREDITS node; UI +0x50 = 1; resume. The window stays until the credits post 0x401 (game.quitRequested). */
    game_pause(g);
    game_midi(g, NULL, MIDI_CMD_STOP);
    vet_jump_to(&g->vm, "CREDITS");
    game_resume(g);
    app_save_settings(&a->settings, g->iniPath);
    a->closed = 1;                                                  /* a second WM_CLOSE closes at once */
    return 1;
}

int app_idle(App *a, int32_t now)
{
    game_idle(&a->game, now);
    if (a->game.quitRequested) {                                    /* WM 0x401: DestroyWindow */
        if (a->host.quit) a->host.quit(a->host.ctx);
        return 0;
    }
    return 1;
}

```

`src/app.h`:

```h
/*
 * app.h - the application shell around the Game: izzy.ini options, the main menu commands, quitting.
 *
 * orig: MAINWNDPROC 1008:0446 (WM_COMMAND / WM_CLOSE), app_InitInstance 1008:1a20 (the menu check marks from
 * [OPTIONS]), app_LoadSettings 1008:574e ([Options] Override8Bit), ui_ctor 1008:5fce ([OPTIONS] INTRO), and the
 * string table 0x1b59..0x1b6d (app_LoadString 1008:5bce).
 *
 * The Windows dialogs (MessageBox, GetOpenFileName / GetSaveFileName, the About box) are host callbacks.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_APP_H
#define IZZY_APP_H

#include "game.h"

/* the menu resource (MENU 0x7c): Game / Sound / About */
enum {
    APP_CMD_NEW_GAME   = 0x65,  /* &New Game */
    APP_CMD_SAVE_GAME  = 0x67,  /* &Save Game */
    APP_CMD_LOAD_GAME  = 0x68,  /* &Load Game */
    APP_CMD_EXIT       = 0x6a,  /* E&xit (posts WM_CLOSE) */
    APP_CMD_ABOUT      = 0x6b,  /* &About (dialog 0x67) */
    APP_CMD_VOLUME_LOW = 0x6d,  /* Volume Low    -> VOLUME = -2 */
    APP_CMD_VOLUME_MED = 0x6e,  /* Volume Medium -> VOLUME = -1 */
    APP_CMD_VOLUME_HIGH= 0x6f,  /* Volume High   -> VOLUME = 0 */
    APP_CMD_MUSIC      = 0x70   /* Music Playing (toggle) */
};

/* string table ids */
enum {
    APP_STR_QUIT = 0x1b59, APP_STR_SAVE_FIRST, APP_STR_NO_AVI, APP_STR_OLD_AVI, APP_STR_NO_WAV, APP_STR_NO_WAVEMAPPER,
    APP_STR_NO_16BIT, APP_STR_NOT_256, APP_STR_CHAMPION, APP_STR_SAVE_ERROR, APP_STR_SAVE_OPEN, APP_STR_LOAD_ERROR,
    APP_STR_LOAD_OPEN, APP_STR_START_OVER, APP_STR_NO_ENGINE, APP_STR_NO_CDROM, APP_STR_INSERT_CD, APP_STR_CLOSE_DOOR,
    APP_STR_INSERT_CD2, APP_STR_INSERT_CD3, APP_STR_TYPE_NAME
};
const char *app_string(int id);     /* NULL for an unknown id */

/* MessageBox styles used (the answers are IDYES 6 / IDNO 7 / IDRETRY 4 / IDCANCEL 2 / IDOK 1) */
enum { APP_MB_OK = 0x30, APP_MB_YESNO = 0x24, APP_MB_YESNO_WARN = 0x34, APP_MB_RETRYCANCEL = 5 };
enum { APP_ID_OK = 1, APP_ID_CANCEL = 2, APP_ID_RETRY = 4, APP_ID_YES = 6, APP_ID_NO = 7 };

typedef struct AppSettings {
    int volume;        /* [OPTIONS] VOLUME, default "0": 0 high, -1 medium, -2 low (Game +0x30 / DS:1a32) */
    int music;         /* [OPTIONS] MUSIC, default "1" (Game +0x32 / DS:1a34) */
    int intro;         /* [OPTIONS] INTRO, default "1" (UI +0xc: AVIs cannot be skipped) */
    int override8Bit;  /* [Options] Override8Bit = "true" (default "FALSE"; Game +0x34) */
} AppSettings;

void app_load_settings(AppSettings *s, const char *iniPath);
void app_save_settings(const AppSettings *s, const char *iniPath);   /* VOLUME and MUSIC, written on quit */
void app_apply_settings(Game *g, const AppSettings *s);

typedef struct AppHost {
    void *ctx;
    int  (*message)(void *ctx, int strId, const char *text, int style);         /* MessageBox -> APP_ID_* */
    int  (*choose_file)(void *ctx, int save, char *path, size_t cap);          /* "*.SGE" dialog; 0 = cancelled */
    void (*about)(void *ctx);
    void (*check_menu)(void *ctx, int cmd, int checked);                       /* CHECKMENUITEM */
    void (*quit)(void *ctx);                                                   /* DestroyWindow / PostQuitMessage */
} AppHost;

typedef struct App {
    Game        game;
    AppSettings settings;
    AppHost     host;
    char        savePath[600];   /* DS:0062: the last file name (".SGE" appended when it has no extension) */
    int         closed;          /* DS:002c */
} App;

/* game_open + the settings (and the menu check marks) */
int  app_open(App *a, const char *gameDir, unsigned seed);
void app_close(App *a);
/* a WM_COMMAND; returns 1 if handled */
int  app_command(App *a, int cmd);
/* WM_CLOSE: "Do you really want to quit?", offer to save, write the options; returns 1 if the app quits */
int  app_request_quit(App *a);
/* game_idle; returns 0 once the game asked to close (after the credits) and host.quit was called */
int  app_idle(App *a, int32_t now);
/* save / load to a path with no dialogs (0 on failure) */
int  app_save_to(App *a, const char *path);
int  app_load_from(App *a, const char *path);

#endif

```

`src/audio.c`:

```c
/*
 * audio.c - the wave mixer (see audio.h).
 */
#include "audio.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static void part_free(WavePart *p) { free(p->data); p->data = NULL; p->len = 0; }

static WavePart part_copy(const uint8_t *data, uint32_t len)
{   /* skip the header: RIFF -> 0x2c bytes, "MONO"/"STER" -> 8 bytes (1010:12e4 case A1) */
    WavePart p = { NULL, 0 };
    uint32_t skip = (len >= 4 && (data[0] == 'R' || data[0] == 'r')) ? 0x2c : 8;
    if (len <= skip) return p;
    p.len = len - skip;
    p.data = (uint8_t *)malloc(p.len);
    if (!p.data) { p.len = 0; return p; }
    memcpy(p.data, data + skip, p.len);
    return p;
}

void wave_init(WaveMixer *m, int speechVolume, int musicVolume, int masterVolume)
{
    memset(m, 0, sizeof *m);
    m->speechVolume = speechVolume; m->musicVolume = musicVolume; m->masterVolume = masterVolume;
}

static void channel_clear(WaveChannel *c)
{   /* (orig: audio_ReleaseChannel releases the assets) */
    int i;
    part_free(&c->part);
    for (i = c->next; i < c->queued; i++) part_free(&c->queue[i]);
    c->queued = c->next = 0; c->pos = 0; c->active = 0;
}

void wave_shutdown(WaveMixer *m) { wave_stop(m, -1); }

void wave_play(WaveMixer *m, int channel, const uint8_t *data, uint32_t len, int volume, unsigned mode)
{   /* orig: 1010:8e80 wave_PlayStream (1010:5a6a sfx_Register / 1010:5b18 sfx_Lookup feed it from game.c) */
    WaveChannel *c;
    int v;
    if (channel < 0 || channel >= WAVE_CHANNELS || !data) return;
    c = &m->ch[channel];
    if (volume < 1) volume = 1; else if (volume > 8) volume = 8;
    v = volume + ((mode & 4) ? m->musicVolume : m->speechVolume);
    if (v > 8) v = 8;
    v -= 1;
    if (v < 1) v = 1;
    if ((mode & 8) && c->active) {                       /* append a part */
        if (c->queued < WAVE_QUEUE) c->queue[c->queued++] = part_copy(data, len);
        return;
    }
    if (c->active) channel_clear(c);
    c->part = part_copy(data, len);
    c->pos = 0;
    c->level = v;
    c->mono = (mode & 1) ? 1 : 2;
    c->loop = (mode & 4) ? 1 : 0;
    c->queued = c->next = 0;
    c->active = c->part.len > 0;
}

void wave_stop(WaveMixer *m, int channel)
{   /* orig: 1010:919e audio_StopChannel / 1010:8ac8 wave_Stop */
    int i;
    if (channel == -1) { for (i = 0; i < WAVE_CHANNELS; i++) channel_clear(&m->ch[i]); return; }
    if (channel >= 0 && channel < WAVE_CHANNELS) channel_clear(&m->ch[channel]);
}

int wave_busy(const WaveMixer *m, int channel) { return channel >= 0 && channel < WAVE_CHANNELS && m->ch[channel].active; }

void wave_mix(WaveMixer *m, int16_t *out, int frames)
{   /* orig: 1010:924a, 1010:0e54 - the mixer pass (one waveOut buffer of 1838 frames per call there; any size here) */
    int ci, f;
    memset(out, 0, sizeof(int16_t) * 2 * (size_t)frames);          /* 1000:0010 clear */
    for (ci = 0; ci < WAVE_CHANNELS; ci++) {
        WaveChannel *c = &m->ch[ci];
        int level, shift;
        if (!c->active) continue;
        level = c->level + m->masterVolume;
        if (level > 8) level = 8;
        if (level < 1) level = 1;
        shift = 8 - level;
        for (f = 0; f < frames && c->active; f++) {
            uint32_t need = c->mono == 1 ? 1 : 2;
            if (c->pos + need > c->part.len) {                       /* part finished */
                if (c->loop) c->pos = 0;
                else if (c->next < c->queued) { part_free(&c->part); c->part = c->queue[c->next]; c->queue[c->next].data = NULL; c->next++; c->pos = 0; }
                else { channel_clear(c); break; }
                if (c->pos + need > c->part.len) { channel_clear(c); break; }
            }
            if (c->mono == 1) {
                int16_t s = (int16_t)(((uint16_t)c->part.data[c->pos] << 8) ^ 0x8000);
                int v = s >> shift;
                out[2 * f] = (int16_t)(out[2 * f] + v);
                out[2 * f + 1] = (int16_t)(out[2 * f + 1] + v);
            } else {
                int16_t l = (int16_t)(((uint16_t)c->part.data[c->pos] << 8) ^ 0x8000);
                int16_t r = (int16_t)(((uint16_t)c->part.data[c->pos + 1] << 8) ^ 0x8000);
                out[2 * f] = (int16_t)(out[2 * f] + (l >> shift));
                out[2 * f + 1] = (int16_t)(out[2 * f + 1] + (r >> shift));
            }
            c->pos += need;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/audio.h`:

```h
/*
 * audio.h - the game's 3-channel software wave mixer (orig: the WavePlayer at Game+0x1c).
 *
 * Sources are raw 8-bit unsigned PCM at 22050 Hz: "MONO" (1 byte per frame) or "STER" (2 bytes, left then
 * right) streams, or the data of a RIFF WAVE file. Output is 16-bit signed stereo at 22050 Hz (the original's
 * normal mode; its 8-bit mono fallback for old cards is not reproduced).
 * Each source sample becomes ((s << 8) ^ 0x8000) >> (8 - level) and is added into the output without
 * clamping (orig: 1000:0038 audio_MixU8, 1000:0170 audio_MixS16 - the "S16" one mixes 8-bit stereo pairs).
 * Play modes (wave_PlayStream's flags): 1 mono (else stereo), 4 loop (and use the music volume), 8 append to
 * the channel's queue of parts (up to 150) instead of restarting it.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_AUDIO_H
#define IZZY_AUDIO_H

#include "izzy_common.h"

#define WAVE_CHANNELS 3
#define WAVE_QUEUE 150
#define WAVE_RATE 22050

typedef struct WavePart { uint8_t *data; uint32_t len; } WavePart;

typedef struct WaveChannel {
    int active;                  /* +0x0e */
    int mono;                    /* +0x26: 1 mono, 2 stereo */
    int loop;                    /* +0x32 */
    int level;                   /* +0x2c: 1..8 */
    WavePart part;               /* the part playing (start / length) */
    uint32_t pos;                /* byte position in it */
    WavePart queue[WAVE_QUEUE];  /* +0xe6 / +0x7ee */
    int queued, next;            /* +0xef6 / +0xefc */
} WaveChannel;

typedef struct WaveMixer {
    WaveChannel ch[WAVE_CHANNELS];
    int speechVolume, musicVolume;   /* DS:05be / DS:05c0 added to a part's volume */
    int masterVolume;                /* the AudioMgr's +0x30 added at mix time */
} WaveMixer;

void wave_init(WaveMixer *m, int speechVolume, int musicVolume, int masterVolume);
void wave_shutdown(WaveMixer *m);
/* orig: 1010:8e80 wave_PlayStream - data: a RIFF WAVE file, or a "MONO"/"STER" stream (len includes the
 * header); the mixer keeps its own copy. volume 1..8; mode as above. */
void wave_play(WaveMixer *m, int channel, const uint8_t *data, uint32_t len, int volume, unsigned mode);
/* orig: 1010:919e audio_StopChannel (channel -1 = all, 1010:8ac8 wave_Stop) */
void wave_stop(WaveMixer *m, int channel);
int  wave_busy(const WaveMixer *m, int channel);
/* orig: 1010:924a - mix `frames` stereo frames into out (interleaved L/R) */
void wave_mix(WaveMixer *m, int16_t *out, int frames);

#endif

```

`src/avi.c`:

```c
/*
 * avi.c - the AVI reader (see avi.h).
 */
#include "avi.h"
#include "izzy_common.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static uint32_t u32(const uint8_t *p) { return (uint32_t)p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24); }
static uint16_t u16(const uint8_t *p) { return (uint16_t)(p[0] | (p[1] << 8)); }
#define FCC(a, b, c, d) ((uint32_t)(a) | ((uint32_t)(b) << 8) | ((uint32_t)(c) << 16) | ((uint32_t)(d) << 24))

typedef struct Ctx { Avi *a; int stream, vidStream, audStream, nstreams; size_t audioCap; int fcap; } Ctx;

static int add_frame(Ctx *c, uint32_t off, uint32_t size)
{
    Avi *a = c->a;
    if (a->nframes == c->fcap) {
        c->fcap = c->fcap ? c->fcap * 2 : 256;
        a->frames = (AviFrame *)realloc(a->frames, sizeof *a->frames * (size_t)c->fcap);
        if (!a->frames) return -1;
    }
    a->frames[a->nframes].offset = off;
    a->frames[a->nframes].size = size;
    a->nframes++;
    return 0;
}

static int add_audio(Ctx *c, const uint8_t *p, uint32_t size)
{
    Avi *a = c->a;
    if (a->audioLen + size > c->audioCap) {
        c->audioCap = (a->audioLen + size) * 2 + 65536;
        a->audio = (uint8_t *)realloc(a->audio, c->audioCap);
        if (!a->audio) return -1;
    }
    memcpy(a->audio + a->audioLen, p, size);
    a->audioLen += size;
    return 0;
}

static int walk(Ctx *c, size_t pos, size_t end)
{   /* chunks: id, size, data (padded to even); LISTs nest */
    Avi *a = c->a;
    while (pos + 8 <= end) {
        uint32_t id = u32(a->data + pos), size = u32(a->data + pos + 4);
        size_t body = pos + 8, next = body + size + (size & 1);
        if (body + size > end) size = (uint32_t)(end - body);
        if ((id == FCC('L','I','S','T') || id == FCC('R','I','F','F')) && size >= 4) {
            uint32_t type = u32(a->data + body);
            if (type == FCC('s','t','r','l')) c->stream = c->nstreams++;
            if (walk(c, body + 4, body + size) < 0) return -1;
        } else if (id == FCC('a','v','i','h') && size >= 40) {
            a->width = u32(a->data + body + 32);
            a->height = u32(a->data + body + 36);
        } else if (id == FCC('s','t','r','h') && size >= 32) {
            uint32_t type = u32(a->data + body);
            if (type == FCC('v','i','d','s') && c->vidStream < 0) {
                c->vidStream = c->stream;
                a->handler = u32(a->data + body + 4);
                a->scale = u32(a->data + body + 20);
                a->rate = u32(a->data + body + 24);
            } else if (type == FCC('a','u','d','s') && c->audStream < 0) c->audStream = c->stream;
        } else if (id == FCC('s','t','r','f')) {
            if (c->stream == c->audStream && size >= 16 && u16(a->data + body) == 1) {   /* WAVE_FORMAT_PCM */
                a->channels = u16(a->data + body + 2);
                a->sampleRate = (int)u32(a->data + body + 4);
                a->bits = u16(a->data + body + 14);
            } else if (c->stream == c->vidStream && size >= 40) {
                uint32_t comp = u32(a->data + body + 16);
                if (comp) a->handler = comp;
            }
        } else if (id != FCC('i','d','x','1')) {
            /* stream chunks "NNdc" / "NNdb" / "NNwb" */
            uint8_t d0 = (uint8_t)id, d1 = (uint8_t)(id >> 8);
            if (d0 >= '0' && d0 <= '9' && d1 >= '0' && d1 <= '9') {
                int s = (d0 - '0') * 10 + (d1 - '0');
                uint16_t kind = (uint16_t)(id >> 16);
                /* video: "dc" / "db", or - as in these files - the codec's two characters ("0032" for IV32) */
                if (s == c->vidStream && kind != ('w' | ('b' << 8)) && kind != ('p' | ('c' << 8))) {
                    if (add_frame(c, (uint32_t)body, size) < 0) return -1;
                } else if (s == c->audStream && kind == ('w' | ('b' << 8))) {
                    if (add_audio(c, a->data + body, size) < 0) return -1;
                }
            }
        }
        if (next <= pos) break;
        pos = next;
    }
    return 0;
}

int avi_open_memory(Avi *a, uint8_t *data, size_t len)
{
    Ctx c;
    memset(a, 0, sizeof *a);
    a->data = data; a->len = len;
    if (!data || len < 12 || u32(data) != FCC('R','I','F','F') || u32(data + 8) != FCC('A','V','I',' ')) return -1;
    memset(&c, 0, sizeof c);
    c.a = a; c.stream = -1; c.vidStream = -1; c.audStream = -1;
    if (walk(&c, 0, len) < 0 || c.vidStream < 0 || !a->width || !a->height) return -1;
    if (!a->rate || !a->scale) { a->rate = 15; a->scale = 1; }
    return 0;
}

int avi_open(Avi *a, const char *path)
{
    size_t len;
    uint8_t *d = izzy_read_file(path, &len);
    if (!d) { memset(a, 0, sizeof *a); return -1; }
    if (avi_open_memory(a, d, len) < 0) { avi_close(a); return -1; }
    return 0;
}

void avi_close(Avi *a)
{
    free(a->data); free(a->frames); free(a->audio);
    memset(a, 0, sizeof *a);
}

int avi_frame_at(const Avi *a, uint32_t ms)
{
    uint64_t f = (uint64_t)ms * a->rate / ((uint64_t)a->scale * 1000);
    if (a->nframes <= 0) return -1;
    return f >= (uint64_t)a->nframes ? a->nframes - 1 : (int)f;
}

uint32_t avi_duration_ms(const Avi *a)
{
    uint32_t video = a->rate ? (uint32_t)((uint64_t)a->nframes * a->scale * 1000 / a->rate) : 0, audio = 0;
    if (a->sampleRate > 0 && a->channels > 0 && a->bits > 0)
        audio = (uint32_t)((uint64_t)a->audioLen * 1000 / ((uint64_t)a->sampleRate * a->channels * (a->bits / 8)));
    return video > audio ? video : audio;
}

```

`src/avi.h`:

```h
/*
 * avi.h - a small AVI (RIFF) reader for the game's videos: one video stream and one PCM audio stream.
 * The original played them with MCI "avivideo" (1008:7bae); this reads the frames and the sound for the port.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_AVI_H
#define IZZY_AVI_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

typedef struct AviFrame { uint32_t offset, size; } AviFrame;   /* into Avi.data */

typedef struct Avi {
    uint8_t  *data; size_t len;     /* the whole file */
    uint32_t  width, height, handler;   /* handler: the video FOURCC (e.g. 'IV32') */
    uint32_t  rate, scale;          /* frames per second = rate / scale */
    AviFrame *frames; int nframes;
    /* audio: PCM, concatenated */
    uint8_t  *audio; size_t audioLen;
    int       channels, sampleRate, bits;
} Avi;

int  avi_open(Avi *a, const char *path);         /* 0 on success */
int  avi_open_memory(Avi *a, uint8_t *data, size_t len);   /* takes ownership of data */
void avi_close(Avi *a);
/* the frame to show at time t (milliseconds from the start), clamped to the last frame */
int  avi_frame_at(const Avi *a, uint32_t ms);
uint32_t avi_duration_ms(const Avi *a);

#endif

```

`src/builtins.c`:

```c
#include "builtins.h"

#include <string.h>

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- data segment tables */

/* DS:05da, 20 bytes per entry; words 0..2 (the rest are copies of the winner id and two far pointers) */
const IzzyCompetition izzy_competitions[50] = {
    {1, 1, 2}, {2, 1, 2}, {1, 3, 4}, {2, 3, 4}, {0, 5, 6}, {0, 5, 6}, {1, 7, 8}, {2, 7, 8},
    {1, 21, 19}, {2, 21, 19}, {1, 13, 6}, {1, 14, 1}, {2, 14, 1}, {1, 1, 18}, {2, 1, 18},
    {1, 14, 16}, {2, 14, 16}, {1, 1, 11}, {2, 1, 11}, {1, 12, 15}, {2, 12, 15}, {1, 13, 17},
    {2, 13, 17}, {2, 16, 7}, {1, 16, 7}, {2, 10, 1}, {1, 10, 1}, {1, 16, 19}, {2, 16, 19},
    {1, 1, 24}, {2, 1, 24}, {1, 19, 10}, {2, 19, 10}, {1, 17, 8}, {2, 17, 8}, {1, 9, 19},
    {2, 9, 19}, {1, 1, 5}, {1, 1, 22}, {2, 1, 22}, {2, 23, 1}, {2, 23, 1}, {2, 23, 5},
    {2, 23, 5}, {2, 1, 5}, {2, 1, 5}, {1, 16, 15}, {2, 16, 15}, {1, 19, 24}, {2, 19, 24},
};

/* HIDE_EASTER_EGGS counts per player count 1..4 (DS:09ec, 09f6, 0a00, 0a0a, 0a14, 0a1e; index 0 unused) */
static const int16_t eggType2[5] = {0, 10, 10, 10, 10};
static const int16_t eggType3[5] = {0, 20, 20, 20, 20};
static const int16_t eggType4[5] = {0, 35, 35, 35, 35};
static const int16_t eggBonus[5] = {0, 10, 10, 10, 10};
static const int16_t eggType1[5] = {0, 10, 10, 10, 10};
static const int16_t eggType5[5] = {0, 10, 10, 10, 10};

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- VM helpers (orig: 1018:3be6 1018:5d4e 1018:5db2 1018:5b44 1018:5b64) */

static int16_t lookup(VetVM *vm, const char *name)
{   /* orig: 1018:3be6 vet_LookupIndex - binary search by name; nodeCount+1 when missing */
    int i = vet_find(vm, name);
    return (int16_t)(i >= 0 ? i : vm->nodeCount + 1);
}

static int16_t cached(VetVM *vm, int16_t *slot, const char *name)
{   /* the built-ins look a name up only while its cached index is still 0 */
    if (*slot == 0) { int i = vet_find(vm, name); if (i >= 0) *slot = (int16_t)i; }
    return *slot;
}

static int16_t read_elem(VetVM *vm, int16_t node, int index)
{   /* orig: 1018:5d4e, 1018:33fa - vet_ReadElement (and its variant that also returns found) - value + jitter noise */
    int16_t v = 0, j = 0;
    if (vet_get_variable(vm, node, index, &v, &j) && j > 0) {
        v = (int16_t)(v + vm->rand() % (j + 1));
        v = (int16_t)(v - vm->rand() % (j + 1));
    }
    return v;
}

static void write_elem(VetVM *vm, int16_t node, int index, int16_t value)
{   /* orig: 1018:5b44 vet_WriteElement / 1018:5b64 vet_WriteScalar (jitter 0) */
    vet_store_variable(vm, node, index, value, 0);
}

static void clear_input(IzzyUi *u) { u->controlPressed = 0; u->hotspotClicked = 0; }

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- the 22 built-ins */
#define R ((IzzyRules *)ctx)
#define FN(name) static int16_t name(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, int node, int16_t arg)
#define UNUSED (void)vm; (void)node; (void)arg; (void)ctx

FN(bi_BUSY)          { UNUSED; return (vm->busyFlags & 3) ? 100 : 0; }                           /* orig: 1010:ba3a */

FN(bi_CONTROL)       { UNUSED;                                                                 /* orig: 1010:ba64 */
    if (R->ui.controlPressed & (uint16_t)arg) { clear_input(&R->ui); R->ui.hotspotsEnabled = 0; return 100; }
    return 0; }

FN(bi_HOTSPOT)       { UNUSED;                                                                 /* orig: 1010:bacc */
    if (R->ui.hotspotClicked & (uint16_t)arg) { clear_input(&R->ui); R->ui.hotspotsEnabled = 0; return 100; }
    return 0; }

FN(bi_INIT_IZZY_GAME) {                                                                       /* orig: 1010:bb34 */
    IzzyRules *r = R; int i;
    (void)node; (void)arg;
    if (r->ui.setPanel) r->ui.setPanel(r->ui.ctx, 0);
    if (r->ui.setDefaultControls) r->ui.setDefaultControls(r->ui.ctx, 0);
    if (r->ui.setControls) r->ui.setControls(r->ui.ctx, 0);
    clear_input(&r->ui); r->ui.hotspotsEnabled = 0;
    r->gold = lookup(vm, "GOLD"); r->silver = lookup(vm, "SILVER"); r->bronze = lookup(vm, "BRONZE");
    r->bonus = lookup(vm, "BONUS"); r->suncatcher = lookup(vm, "SUNCATCHER");
    r->competeCard = lookup(vm, "COMPETE_CARD"); r->morphCard = lookup(vm, "MORPH_CARD");
    r->coribaCard = lookup(vm, "CORIBA_CARD");
    r->viewGold = lookup(vm, "VIEW_GOLD"); r->viewSilver = lookup(vm, "VIEW_SILVER");
    r->viewBronze = lookup(vm, "VIEW_BRONZE"); r->leftComp = lookup(vm, "LEFT_COMP_FLAG");
    r->rightComp = lookup(vm, "RIGHT_COMP_FLAG"); r->compWinner = lookup(vm, "COMP_WINNER");
    for (i = 0; i < 36; i++) {
        write_elem(vm, r->viewGold, i, 0); write_elem(vm, r->viewSilver, i, 0); write_elem(vm, r->viewBronze, i, 0);
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        write_elem(vm, r->gold, i, 0); write_elem(vm, r->silver, i, 0); write_elem(vm, r->bronze, i, 0);
        write_elem(vm, r->bonus, i, 0); write_elem(vm, r->suncatcher, i, 0); write_elem(vm, r->competeCard, i, 0);
        write_elem(vm, r->morphCard, i, 0); write_elem(vm, r->coribaCard, i, 0);
    }
    r->compNext = 50;                       /* DS:09e2 = 0x32: reshuffle the competition deck on next use */
    return 100;
}

FN(bi_PLAYER_NAME_OK) { UNUSED;                                                               /* orig: 1010:bf1c */
    return (arg >= 1 && arg <= 4 && R->ui.nameLen[arg] >= 1) ? 100 : 0; }

FN(bi_HIDE_EASTER_EGGS) {                                                                     /* orig: 1010:bf48 */
    IzzyRules *r = R;
    int16_t cell[100], perm[100], bonusCell[25], perm2[25];
    int a, e, t4, bon, t1, t5, k = 0, i, n;
    static const char *names[5] = {"ITEM_1", "ITEM_2", "ITEM_3", "ITEM_4", "ITEM_5"};
    (void)node;
    if (arg < 1 || arg > 4) return 0;
    a = eggType2[arg]; e = eggType3[arg]; t4 = eggType4[arg]; bon = eggBonus[arg]; t5 = eggType5[arg];
    if (arg < 2) { t1 = 0; t4 += eggType1[arg]; } else t1 = eggType1[arg];
    memset(cell, 0, sizeof cell);
    for (i = 0; i < 100; i++) perm[i] = (int16_t)i;
    for (i = 0; i < 100; i++) {                /* swap with (rand() & 0x7fff) * 100 >> 15 */
        int j = (int)(((long)(vm->rand() & 0x7FFF) * 100) >> 15);
        int16_t t = perm[i]; perm[i] = perm[j]; perm[j] = t;
    }
    cell[perm[k++]] = 6;                                       /* one type-6 item */
    for (n = 0; n < a && k < 100; n++) cell[perm[k++]] = 2;
    for (n = 0; n < e && k < 100; n++) cell[perm[k++]] = 3;
    for (n = 0; n < t1 && k < 100; n++) cell[perm[k++]] = 1;
    for (n = 0; n < t5 && k < 100; n++) cell[perm[k++]] = 5;
    for (n = 0; n < t4 && k + n < 100; n++) cell[perm[k + n]] = 4;
    memset(bonusCell, 0, sizeof bonusCell);
    for (i = 0; i < 25; i++) perm2[i] = (int16_t)i;
    for (i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
        int j = (int)(((long)(vm->rand() & 0x7FFF) * 25) >> 15);
        int16_t t = perm2[i]; perm2[i] = perm2[j]; perm2[j] = t;
    }
    for (n = 0; n < bon && n < 25; n++) bonusCell[perm2[n]] = 1;
    for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) r->item[i] = lookup(vm, names[i]);
    for (i = 1; i <= 25; i++) {                               /* ITEM_1..4[location] = 4 cells; ITEM_5 = bonus */
        write_elem(vm, r->item[0], i, cell[4 * (i - 1) + 0]);
        write_elem(vm, r->item[1], i, cell[4 * (i - 1) + 1]);
        write_elem(vm, r->item[2], i, cell[4 * (i - 1) + 2]);
        write_elem(vm, r->item[3], i, cell[4 * (i - 1) + 3]);
        write_elem(vm, r->item[4], i, bonusCell[i - 1]);
    }
    return 100;
}

static int16_t panel_code(int16_t p)
{   /* SET_CONTROL_PANEL argument -> control object panel id */
    static const int16_t map[11] = {0, 2, 3, 4, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13};
    return (p >= 1 && p <= 10) ? map[p] : 0;
}

FN(bi_SET_CONTROL_PANEL) { UNUSED;                                                            /* orig: 1010:c3a8 */
    if (R->ui.setPanel) R->ui.setPanel(R->ui.ctx, panel_code(arg));         /* UI vtable +0x28 (1008:7fe2) */
    R->ui.controlPressed = 0; return 100; }

FN(bi_SET_CONTROLS) { UNUSED;                                                                 /* orig: 1010:c460 */
    if (R->ui.setControls) R->ui.setControls(R->ui.ctx, arg);               /* UI vtable +0x48 (1008:95ac) */
    clear_input(&R->ui); return 100; }

FN(bi_SET_DEFAULT_CONTROLS) { UNUSED;                                                         /* orig: 1010:c4bc */
    if (R->ui.setDefaultControls) R->ui.setDefaultControls(R->ui.ctx, arg);   /* UI vtable +0x44 (1008:955a) */
    clear_input(&R->ui); return 100; }

FN(bi_SET_HOTSPOTS) { UNUSED;                                                                 /* orig: 1010:c518 */
    R->ui.hotspotsEnabled = (uint16_t)arg; R->ui.hotspotClicked = 0; return 100; }

FN(bi_SHOW_MESSAGE) { UNUSED; R->ui.message = arg; return 100; }                             /* orig: 1010:c556 */

FN(bi_SETS_READY) { UNUSED;                                                                   /* orig: 1010:c578 */
    if (arg < 1 || arg > 4) return 0;
    return read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &R->gold, "GOLD"), arg) > 2 ? 100 : 0; }

FN(bi_FUSE_SETS) {                                                                            /* orig: 1010:c5ec */
    IzzyRules *r = R; int16_t fused, owned, golds;
    (void)node;
    if (arg < 1 || arg > 4) return 0;
    cached(vm, &r->gold, "GOLD"); cached(vm, &r->suncatcher, "SUNCATCHER");
    fused = read_elem(vm, r->suncatcher, 0);         /* scalar: suncatchers fused so far */
    owned = read_elem(vm, r->suncatcher, arg);
    golds = read_elem(vm, r->gold, arg);
    if (golds > 2) {                                  /* three golds make one suncatcher */
        write_elem(vm, r->gold, arg, (int16_t)(golds - 3));
        fused++;
        write_elem(vm, r->suncatcher, 0, fused);
        write_elem(vm, r->suncatcher, arg, (int16_t)(fused + owned));
    }
    return fused;
}

FN(bi_HAS_MEDALS) {                                                                           /* orig: 1010:c742 */
    IzzyRules *r = R; (void)node;
    if (arg < 1 || arg > 4) return 0;
    if (read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->gold, "GOLD"), arg) || read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->silver, "SILVER"), arg) ||
        read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->bronze, "BRONZE"), arg)) return 100;
    return 0;
}

FN(bi_CAN_COMPETE) {                                                                          /* orig: 1010:c86e */
    IzzyRules *r = R; int p, mine = 0, others = 0;
    if (read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->competeCard, "COMPETE_CARD"), arg) == 0) return 0;
    for (p = 1; p < 5; p++) {
        if (p == arg) mine += bi_HAS_MEDALS(ctx, vm, node, (int16_t)p);
        else others += bi_HAS_MEDALS(ctx, vm, node, (int16_t)p);
    }
    return (mine && others) ? 100 : 0;
}

FN(bi_MEDAL_TO_WIN) {                                                                         /* orig: 1010:c91a: best medal held */
    IzzyRules *r = R; (void)node;
    if (arg < 1 || arg > 4) return 0;
    if (read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->gold, "GOLD"), arg)) return 3;
    if (read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->silver, "SILVER"), arg)) return 2;
    return read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->bronze, "BRONZE"), arg) ? 1 : 0;
}

FN(bi_MEDAL_TO_LOSE) {                                                                        /* orig: 1010:ca52: lowest medal held */
    IzzyRules *r = R; (void)node;
    if (arg < 1 || arg > 4) return 0;
    if (read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->bronze, "BRONZE"), arg)) return 1;
    if (read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->silver, "SILVER"), arg)) return 2;
    return read_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->gold, "GOLD"), arg) ? 3 : 0;
}

FN(bi_NEXT_MEDAL) {                                                                           /* orig: 1010:cb88: 30-card deck per type */
    IzzyRules *r = R; int k, i, v; (void)node;
    if (arg < 1 || arg > 3) return 0;
    k = arg - 1;
    if (r->medalNext[k] > 29) {
        for (i = 0; i < 30; i++) r->medalDeck[k][i] = (int16_t)i;
        for (i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            int j = (int)(((long)(vm->rand() & 0x7FFF) * 30) >> 15);
            int16_t t = r->medalDeck[k][i]; r->medalDeck[k][i] = r->medalDeck[k][j]; r->medalDeck[k][j] = t;
        }
        r->medalNext[k] = 0;
    }
    v = r->medalDeck[k][r->medalNext[k]++];
    return (int16_t)(v + 1);
}

FN(bi_NEXT_COMPETITION) {                                                                     /* orig: 1010:cc6a: 50-card deck */
    IzzyRules *r = R; int i, c; (void)node; (void)arg;
    do {
        if (r->compNext > 49) {
            for (i = 0; i < 50; i++) r->compDeck[i] = (int16_t)i;
            for (i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
                int j = (int)(((long)(vm->rand() & 0x7FFF) * 50) >> 15);
                int16_t t = r->compDeck[i]; r->compDeck[i] = r->compDeck[j]; r->compDeck[j] = t;
            }
            r->compNext = 0;
        }
        c = r->compDeck[r->compNext++];
    } while (izzy_competitions[c].winner < 1);
    write_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->leftComp, "LEFT_COMP_FLAG"), 0, izzy_competitions[c].left);
    write_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->rightComp, "RIGHT_COMP_FLAG"), 0, izzy_competitions[c].right);
    write_elem(vm, cached(vm, &r->compWinner, "COMP_WINNER"), 0, izzy_competitions[c].winner);
    return (int16_t)(c + 1);
}

FN(bi_PLAYED_INTRO) { UNUSED; if (arg > 0) R->playedIntro = 100; return R->playedIntro; }    /* orig: 1010:cd82 */

FN(bi_SAVE_GAME_OK) { UNUSED; R->ui.saveGameOk = arg > 0; return 100; }                      /* orig: 1010:cd98 */

FN(bi_RND) { UNUSED;                                                                          /* orig: 1010:cdc4: [0, n) */
    if (arg < 2) return 0;
    return (int16_t)(((long)(vm->rand() & 0x7FFF) * (long)(arg & 0x7FFF)) >> 15); }

#undef FN
#undef R
#undef UNUSED

void izzy_rules_init(IzzyRules *r, VetVM *vm)
{
    static const struct { const char *name; VetBuiltinFn fn; } table[22] = {
        {"BUSY", bi_BUSY}, {"CONTROL", bi_CONTROL}, {"HOTSPOT", bi_HOTSPOT}, {"INIT_IZZY_GAME", bi_INIT_IZZY_GAME},
        {"PLAYER_NAME_OK", bi_PLAYER_NAME_OK}, {"HIDE_EASTER_EGGS", bi_HIDE_EASTER_EGGS},
        {"SET_CONTROL_PANEL", bi_SET_CONTROL_PANEL}, {"SET_CONTROLS", bi_SET_CONTROLS},
        {"SET_DEFAULT_CONTROLS", bi_SET_DEFAULT_CONTROLS}, {"SET_HOTSPOTS", bi_SET_HOTSPOTS},
        {"SHOW_MESSAGE", bi_SHOW_MESSAGE}, {"SETS_READY", bi_SETS_READY}, {"FUSE_SETS", bi_FUSE_SETS},
        {"HAS_MEDALS", bi_HAS_MEDALS}, {"CAN_COMPETE", bi_CAN_COMPETE}, {"MEDAL_TO_WIN", bi_MEDAL_TO_WIN},
        {"MEDAL_TO_LOSE", bi_MEDAL_TO_LOSE}, {"NEXT_MEDAL", bi_NEXT_MEDAL},
        {"NEXT_COMPETITION", bi_NEXT_COMPETITION}, {"PLAYED_INTRO", bi_PLAYED_INTRO},
        {"SAVE_GAME_OK", bi_SAVE_GAME_OK}, {"RND", bi_RND} };
    int i;
    memset(r, 0, sizeof *r);
    r->vm = vm;
    r->compNext = 50;                                   /* DS:09e2 initial value */
    r->medalNext[0] = r->medalNext[1] = r->medalNext[2] = 30;   /* DS:09e4..09e8 */
    for (i = 0; i < 22; i++) vet_bind_builtin(vm, table[i].name, table[i].fn, r);   /* orig: RegisterVepBuiltins */
}
```

```h
/*
 * builtins.h - the 22 VEP built-in functions (the game rules), callable from IZZY.VET as NAME(arg).
 * orig: registered by 1008:cbd8 RegisterVepBuiltins; handlers 1010:ba3a..cdc4 (vepfn_*).
 * Their data tables come from the data segment (DS:05da competitions, DS:09bc..0a2a counters/tables).
 */
#ifndef IZZY_BUILTINS_H
#define IZZY_BUILTINS_H

#include "vet.h"

/* UI/controller state the built-ins read and write (orig: the hotspot/control object at Game+0x14). */
typedef struct IzzyUi {
    uint16_t controlPressed;   /* +0x17e: bits of control-panel buttons pressed */
    uint16_t hotspotClicked;   /* +0x180: bits of hotspots clicked */
    uint16_t hotspotsEnabled;  /* +0x184: set by SET_HOTSPOTS */
    int16_t  message;          /* +0x18a: set by SHOW_MESSAGE */
    int16_t  saveGameOk;       /* +0x192: set by SAVE_GAME_OK */
    int16_t  nameLen[5];       /* DS:2096: player name lengths, 1..4 (PLAYER_NAME_OK) */
    /* host hooks (optional): the control object's virtuals +0x28 / +0x44 / +0x48 */
    void (*setPanel)(void *ctx, int panelId);
    void (*setDefaultControls)(void *ctx, int mask);
    void (*setControls)(void *ctx, int controls);
    void *ctx;
} IzzyUi;

typedef struct IzzyRules {
    VetVM  *vm;
    IzzyUi  ui;
    /* cached node indexes (orig DS:09bc..09e0) */
    int16_t gold, silver, bronze, bonus, suncatcher, competeCard, morphCard, coribaCard;
    int16_t item[5];                       /* ITEM_1..ITEM_5 */
    int16_t viewGold, viewSilver, viewBronze, leftComp, rightComp, compWinner;
    /* decks (orig DS:2470 competitions, DS:24d4 medals) and their counters (DS:09e2, DS:09e4..09e8) */
    int16_t compDeck[50], compNext;
    int16_t medalDeck[3][30], medalNext[3];
    int16_t playedIntro;                   /* DS:09ea */
} IzzyRules;

/* Initialise the rule state with the data segment's initial values and bind all 22 built-ins to the VM. */
void izzy_rules_init(IzzyRules *r, VetVM *vm);

/* Competition table (DS:05da, 50 entries): winner (1 left / 2 right / 0 never drawn), left id, right id. */
typedef struct IzzyCompetition { int16_t winner, left, right; } IzzyCompetition;
extern const IzzyCompetition izzy_competitions[50];

#endif
```

`src/cels.c`:

```c
#include "cels.h"

#include <string.h>

static int32_t even(int32_t n) { return (n & 1) ? n + 1 : n; }

int cels_open(CelPack *p, const uint8_t *data, size_t len)
{
    /* orig: 1018:82aa anim_LoadCelPack */
    size_t pos;
    int32_t l12, l16, i, bad = 0;
    memset(p, 0, sizeof *p);
    if (len < 0x28 || memcmp(data, "CELS", 4) != 0) return CELS_ERR_HEADER;   /* _fstrncmp(pack, "CELS", 4) */
    p->data = data; p->len = len;
    p->nCels = rd_i32(data + 0x18);
    p->nSeq = rd_i32(data + 0x1c);
    p->nDir = rd_i32(data + 0x20);
    p->nFrames = rd_i32(data + 0x24);
    l12 = p->nFrames * p->nSeq;          /* local_12 = [9]*[7] */
    l16 = l12 * p->nDir;                 /* local_16 = local_12*[8] */
    pos = 0x28;
    p->tblNext = data + pos;   pos += 2 * (size_t)even(l12);
    p->tblDelay = data + pos;  pos += 2 * (size_t)even(l12);
    p->tblFrame = data + pos;  pos += 2 * (size_t)even(l16);
    p->tblDx = data + pos;     pos += 2 * (size_t)even(p->nCels);
    p->tblDy = data + pos;     pos += 2 * (size_t)even(p->nCels);
    p->tblHotX = data + pos;   pos += 2 * (size_t)even(p->nCels);
    p->tblHotY = data + pos;   pos += 2 * (size_t)even(p->nCels);
    p->tblFlags = data + pos;  pos += 2 * (size_t)even(p->nCels);
    p->tblOffset = data + pos; pos += 4 * (size_t)p->nCels;
    /* "unexpected end of header": the tables must end inside the pack */
    if (p->nCels < 0 || pos > len || (int64_t)pos >= (int64_t)rd_u32(data + 4)) return CELS_ERR_TRUNCATED;

    /* per-cel scan: offset must be inside the pack ("unexpected end of data").
     * A BITMAPINFOHEADER cel with width < 1 or height <= 0 only counts as a warning; a cel that is not a
     * BITMAPINFOHEADER at all fails the load (the original returns -1 only when that second counter is non-zero). */
    for (i = 0; i < p->nCels; i++) {
        uint32_t off = rd_u32(p->tblOffset + 4 * i);
        if ((int32_t)off < 0 || off >= rd_u32(data + 4) || off >= len) return CELS_ERR_TRUNCATED;
        if (off == 0) continue;
        if (off + 12 > len) return CELS_ERR_TRUNCATED;
        if (rd_u32(data + off) != 0x28) bad++;
        /* else: rd_i32(+4) < 1 || rd_i32(+8) < 1 -> warning only ("Cel pack %d -- %d bad cels") */
    }
    return bad ? CELS_ERR_BADCELS : CELS_OK;
}

int cels_frame_cel(const CelPack *p, int dir, int seq, int frame)
{
    /* orig: 1020:069c anim_Step - cel = frame[(dir*nSeq + seq)*nFrames + frame], 0 if out of range */
    int32_t idx, cel;
    if (seq == 0) return 0;              /* sequence 0 is the hidden sequence */
    if (dir < 0 || dir >= p->nDir || seq < 0 || seq >= p->nSeq || frame < 0 || frame >= p->nFrames) return 0;
    idx = ((int32_t)dir * p->nSeq + seq) * p->nFrames + frame;
    cel = rd_i16(p->tblFrame + 2 * idx);
    return (cel < 0 || cel >= p->nCels) ? 0 : (int)cel;
}

int cels_next_frame(const CelPack *p, int dir, int seq, int frame)
{
    /* orig: 1020:0596 anim_NextFrame - step forward, wrap to 0, skip frames whose cel is 0 */
    int f = frame, cel;
    if (seq == 0) return 0;
    do {
        f++;
        if (f < 0 || f >= p->nFrames) f = 0;
        cel = (f == 0) ? 0 : cels_frame_cel(p, dir, seq, f);
    } while (cel == 0 && f != 0);
    return f;
}

int cels_frame_delay(const CelPack *p, int seq, int frame)
{
    if (seq < 0 || seq >= p->nSeq || frame < 0 || frame >= p->nFrames) return 0;
    return rd_i16(p->tblDelay + 2 * ((int32_t)seq * p->nFrames + frame));
}

int cels_get(const CelPack *p, int i, Cel *out)
{
    uint32_t off, hdr, size;
    memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    if (i <= 0 || i >= p->nCels) return -1;
    off = rd_u32(p->tblOffset + 4 * i);
    if (off == 0 || off + 40 > p->len) return -1;
    hdr = rd_u32(p->data + off);                /* biSize (40) */
    out->width = rd_i32(p->data + off + 4);
    out->height = rd_i32(p->data + off + 8);
    out->compression = rd_u32(p->data + off + 16);
    size = rd_u32(p->data + off + 20);
    out->bits = p->data + off + hdr;
    out->bitsLen = (size && off + hdr + size <= p->len) ? size : p->len - (off + hdr);
    out->dx = rd_i16(p->tblDx + 2 * i);
    out->dy = rd_i16(p->tblDy + 2 * i);
    out->hotX = rd_i16(p->tblHotX + 2 * i);
    out->hotY = rd_i16(p->tblHotY + 2 * i);
    out->flags = rd_i16(p->tblFlags + 2 * i);
    return 0;
}

void mmp1_decode(const Cel *c, uint8_t *pixels)
{
    /* orig: 1018:cfa8 sprite_DrawMMP1 - per row, top to bottom:
     *   b <  0x80 : b+1 literal pixels follow            (advance scaleTable[0]    = 1 pixel each)
     *   b <  0xFF : (b - 0x7E) copies of the next byte   (advance scaleTable[b-0x7F] = (b-0x7F+1) pixels)
     *   b == 0xFF : end of row
     * (Verified: with b-0x7E every row of the opaque cels CACOBO42 / CCMJGR4 ends exactly at the cel width.)
     * pixel 0 is transparent; 0xF6..0xFE select background remap tables (shadow/highlight). */
    const uint8_t *s = c->bits, *end = c->bits + c->bitsLen;
    int32_t y;
    memset(pixels, 0, (size_t)c->width * (size_t)c->height);
    for (y = 0; y < c->height && s < end; y++) {
        uint8_t *row = pixels + (size_t)y * (size_t)c->width;
        int32_t x = 0;
        while (s < end) {
            unsigned b = *s++;
            if (b == 0xFF) break;
            if (b < 0x80) {
                unsigned n = b + 1;
                while (n-- && s < end) { uint8_t v = *s++; if (x < c->width) row[x] = v; x++; }
            } else {
                unsigned n = b - 0x7E;
                uint8_t v = s < end ? *s++ : 0;
                while (n--) { if (x < c->width) row[x] = v; x++; }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/cels.h`:

```h
/*
 * cels.h - CELS animation packs (.ANI) and MMP1 sprite cels.
 *
 * Layout (all little-endian), orig: 1018:82aa anim_LoadCelPack:
 *   "CELS" u32 totalLen; char name[16]
 *   @0x18 i32 nCels, nSeq, nDir, nFrames
 *   @0x28 i16 nextFrame[nSeq*nFrames]   (stored, never read by the engine)
 *         i16 delay    [nSeq*nFrames]
 *         i16 frame    [nDir*nSeq*nFrames]   cel index at ((dir*nSeq + seq)*nFrames + f)
 *         i16 dx[nCels], dy[nCels], hotX[nCels], hotY[nCels], flags[nCels]
 *         u32 celOffset[nCels]               from file start; cel 0 is the null cel
 *   every table is padded to an even element count.
 *   cel = BITMAPINFOHEADER (biSize 40, 8 bpp, biCompression 'MMP1', no palette) + data.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_CELS_H
#define IZZY_CELS_H

#include "izzy_common.h"

#define CELS_MAX_SLOT 0x67   /* slots 1..0x67; slot 0x65 is the mouse cursor */

typedef struct CelPack {
    const uint8_t *data; size_t len;
    int32_t nCels, nSeq, nDir, nFrames;
    const uint8_t *tblNext, *tblDelay, *tblFrame;
    const uint8_t *tblDx, *tblDy, *tblHotX, *tblHotY, *tblFlags;
    const uint8_t *tblOffset;
} CelPack;

typedef struct Cel {
    int32_t width, height;
    uint32_t compression;           /* 0 = raw bottom-up DIB, 'MMP1' = row RLE */
    const uint8_t *bits; size_t bitsLen;
    int16_t hotX, hotY, dx, dy, flags;
} Cel;

enum { CELS_OK = 0, CELS_ERR_HEADER = -1, CELS_ERR_TRUNCATED = -2, CELS_ERR_BADCELS = -3 };

/* Parse and validate a pack in memory (data must stay alive). Mirrors anim_LoadCelPack:
 * header check, table layout, and the per-cel scan that rejects packs with bad cels. */
int cels_open(CelPack *p, const uint8_t *data, size_t len);

/* Cel index for (dir, seq, frame); 0 if out of range (orig: anim_Step / anim_NextFrame bounds checks). */
int cels_frame_cel(const CelPack *p, int dir, int seq, int frame);

/* Next frame whose cel is non-empty after `frame`, wrapping to 0 (orig: 1020:0596 anim_NextFrame). */
int cels_next_frame(const CelPack *p, int dir, int seq, int frame);

/* Frame delay in ticks for (seq, frame) (orig: tblDelay lookup in 1020:069c anim_Step). */
int cels_frame_delay(const CelPack *p, int seq, int frame);

/* Fetch cel i (1..nCels-1). Returns 0 on success. */
int cels_get(const CelPack *p, int i, Cel *out);

/* Decode an MMP1 cel into width*height 8-bit pixels (0 = transparent; 0xF6..0xFE = remap effects).
 * orig: 1018:cfa8 sprite_DrawMMP1 (without its scaling/clipping/remap, which belong to the blitter). */
void mmp1_decode(const Cel *c, uint8_t *pixels);

#endif

```

`src/config.c`:

```c
/*
 * config.c - a minimal Windows-style INI reader / writer (see config.h).
 */
#include "config.h"
#include "izzy_common.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static int is_section(const char *line, const char *section)
{
    size_t n = strlen(section);
    char buf[128];
    if (line[0] != '[') return 0;
    if (n >= sizeof buf) return 0;
    memcpy(buf, line + 1, n); buf[n] = 0;
    return izzy_stricmp(buf, section) == 0 && line[n + 1] == ']';
}

static int key_value(const char *line, const char *key, const char **value)
{   /* "key=value" -> 1 */
    const char *eq = strchr(line, '=');
    size_t n;
    char buf[128];
    if (!eq) return 0;
    n = (size_t)(eq - line);
    while (n && (line[n - 1] == ' ' || line[n - 1] == '\t')) n--;
    if (n >= sizeof buf) return 0;
    memcpy(buf, line, n); buf[n] = 0;
    if (izzy_stricmp(buf, key) != 0) return 0;
    *value = eq + 1;
    while (**value == ' ' || **value == '\t') (*value)++;
    return 1;
}

size_t ini_get(const char *file, const char *section, const char *key, const char *def, char *out, size_t cap)
{
    FILE *f = fopen(file, "r");
    char line[512];
    int in = 0;
    if (f) {
        while (fgets(line, sizeof line, f)) {
            const char *v;
            line[strcspn(line, "\r\n")] = 0;
            if (line[0] == '[') { in = is_section(line, section); continue; }
            if (in && key_value(line, key, &v)) {
                strncpy(out, v, cap - 1); out[cap - 1] = 0;
                fclose(f);
                return strlen(out);
            }
        }
        fclose(f);
    }
    strncpy(out, def ? def : "", cap - 1); out[cap - 1] = 0;
    return strlen(out);
}

int ini_set(const char *file, const char *section, const char *key, const char *value)
{
    size_t len = 0, cap = 0, i;
    char *text = NULL, *outbuf;
    FILE *f = fopen(file, "rb");
    int in = 0, done = 0, sawSection = 0;
    size_t outLen = 0, outCap;
    if (f) {
        int c;
        while ((c = fgetc(f)) != EOF) {
            if (len + 1 >= cap) { cap = cap ? cap * 2 : 4096; text = (char *)realloc(text, cap); if (!text) { fclose(f); return -1; } }
            text[len++] = (char)c;
        }
        fclose(f);
    }
    outCap = len + strlen(section) + strlen(key) + strlen(value) + 16;
    outbuf = (char *)malloc(outCap);
    if (!outbuf) { free(text); return -1; }
#define PUT(s, n) do { memcpy(outbuf + outLen, (s), (n)); outLen += (n); } while (0)
    for (i = 0; i < len;) {
        size_t e = i, ll;
        char line[512];
        const char *v;
        while (e < len && text[e] != '\n') e++;
        ll = e - i; if (ll >= sizeof line) ll = sizeof line - 1;
        memcpy(line, text + i, ll); line[ll] = 0;
        if (ll && line[ll - 1] == '\r') line[ll - 1] = 0;
        if (line[0] == '[') {
            if (in && !done) { PUT(key, strlen(key)); PUT("=", 1); PUT(value, strlen(value)); PUT("\r\n", 2); done = 1; }
            in = is_section(line, section);
            if (in) sawSection = 1;
        } else if (in && !done && key_value(line, key, &v)) {
            PUT(key, strlen(key)); PUT("=", 1); PUT(value, strlen(value)); PUT("\r\n", 2); done = 1;
            i = e < len ? e + 1 : e;
            continue;
        }
        PUT(text + i, (e < len ? e + 1 : e) - i);
        i = e < len ? e + 1 : e;
    }
    if (!done) {
        if (outLen && outbuf[outLen - 1] != '\n') PUT("\r\n", 2);   /* the file's last line had no line break */
        if (!sawSection) { PUT("[", 1); PUT(section, strlen(section)); PUT("]\r\n", 3); }
        PUT(key, strlen(key)); PUT("=", 1); PUT(value, strlen(value)); PUT("\r\n", 2);
    }
#undef PUT
    free(text);
    f = fopen(file, "wb");
    if (!f) { free(outbuf); return -1; }
    fwrite(outbuf, 1, outLen, f);
    fclose(f);
    free(outbuf);
    return 0;
}
```

```h
/*
 * config.h - izzy.ini (orig: GetPrivateProfileString / WritePrivateProfileString on "izzy.ini";
 * sections [OPTIONS] and [SCORE]). Keys and sections compare case-insensitively, as in Windows.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_CONFIG_H
#define IZZY_CONFIG_H

#include <stddef.h>

/* Read `key` from `section`; copies `def` when the file, section or key is missing. Returns the length. */
size_t ini_get(const char *file, const char *section, const char *key, const char *def, char *out, size_t cap);
/* Write (or add) `key = value` in `section`. 0 on success. */
int    ini_set(const char *file, const char *section, const char *key, const char *value);

#endif
```
