`src/dat.c`:

```c
#include "dat.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

int dat_dir_load(DatDir *dir, const char *dirPath)
{
    /* orig: 1010:798e dat_LookupTake (first call): _lopen, count = _filelength / 0x12, GlobalAlloc, _hread */
    size_t len, i;
    uint8_t *raw = izzy_read_file(dirPath, &len);
    dir->entries = NULL; dir->count = 0;
    if (!raw) return 3;
    dir->count = len / DAT_DIR_RECORD;
    dir->entries = (DatDirEntry *)calloc(dir->count ? dir->count : 1, sizeof(DatDirEntry));
    if (!dir->entries) { free(raw); return 2; }
    for (i = 0; i < dir->count; i++) {
        const uint8_t *r = raw + i * DAT_DIR_RECORD;
        memcpy(dir->entries[i].name, r, 13);
        dir->entries[i].name[13] = 0;
        dir->entries[i].disc = r[13];
        dir->entries[i].offset = rd_u32(r + 14);
    }
    free(raw);
    return 0;
}

void dat_dir_free(DatDir *dir)
{
    free(dir->entries);
    dir->entries = NULL; dir->count = 0;
}

const DatDirEntry *dat_dir_lookup(const DatDir *dir, const char *takeName)
{
    /* orig: 1010:798e dat_LookupTake - binary search over the sorted records with _fstricmp */
    long lo = 0, hi = (long)dir->count - 1;
    while (lo <= hi) {
        long mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
        int c = izzy_stricmp(takeName, dir->entries[mid].name);
        if (c == 0) return &dir->entries[mid];
        if (c < 0) hi = mid - 1; else lo = mid + 1;
    }
    return NULL;
}

int dat_group_open(DatGroup *g, void *datFile, uint32_t offset)
{
    /* orig: 1010:9c06 dat_OpenGroup - seek to the group, read 24-byte entries until an empty name */
    FILE *f = (FILE *)datFile;
    uint8_t e[DAT_GROUP_ENTRY];
    size_t cap = 16;
    g->base = offset; g->count = 0;
    g->files = (DatFileEntry *)malloc(cap * sizeof(DatFileEntry));
    if (!g->files) return 2;
    if (fseek(f, (long)offset, SEEK_SET) != 0) return 3;
    for (;;) {
        if (fread(e, 1, sizeof e, f) != sizeof e) return 3;
        if (e[0] == 0) break;
        if (g->count == cap) {
            DatFileEntry *n = (DatFileEntry *)realloc(g->files, (cap *= 2) * sizeof(DatFileEntry));
            if (!n) return 2;
            g->files = n;
        }
        memcpy(g->files[g->count].name, e, 16);
        g->files[g->count].name[16] = 0;
        g->files[g->count].size = rd_u32(e + 16);
        g->files[g->count].offset = rd_u32(e + 20);
        g->count++;
    }
    return 0;
}

void dat_group_free(DatGroup *g)
{
    free(g->files);
    g->files = NULL; g->count = 0;
}

const DatFileEntry *dat_group_find(const DatGroup *g, const char *fileName)
{
    /* orig: 1010:a15c dat_ReadFile - linear _fstricmp scan of the group's entries */
    size_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < g->count; i++)
        if (izzy_stricmp(g->files[i].name, fileName) == 0) return &g->files[i];
    return NULL;
}

uint8_t *dat_read(void *datFile, const DatGroup *g, const DatFileEntry *e)
{
    /* orig: 1010:a15c dat_ReadFile - _lseek to group base + offset, _hread in 0x1000-byte chunks */
    FILE *f = (FILE *)datFile;
    uint8_t *buf = (uint8_t *)malloc(e->size ? e->size : 1);
    size_t done = 0;
    if (!buf) return NULL;
    if (fseek(f, (long)(g->base + e->offset), SEEK_SET) != 0) { free(buf); return NULL; }
    while (done < e->size) {
        size_t chunk = e->size - done > 0x1000 ? 0x1000 : e->size - done;
        if (fread(buf + done, 1, chunk, f) != chunk) { free(buf); return NULL; }
        done += chunk;
    }
    return buf;
}

```

`src/dat.h`:

```h
/*
 * dat.h - the IZZY.DIR / IZZYCDn.DAT asset archive.
 *
 * IZZY.DIR  : sorted array of 18-byte records { char name[13]; u8 disc; u32 offset; }  (count = size / 18)
 *             orig: 1010:798e dat_LookupTake loads it whole and binary-searches it with _fstricmp.
 * IZZYCDn.DAT: groups ("takes"), each 0x800-aligned, starting with 24-byte entries
 *             { char name[16]; u32 size; u32 offset_from_group; } terminated by an empty name.
 *             orig: 1010:9c06 dat_OpenGroup, 1010:a15c dat_ReadFile (reads in 0x1000-byte chunks).
 */
#ifndef IZZY_DAT_H
#define IZZY_DAT_H

#include "izzy_common.h"

#define DAT_DIR_RECORD 18
#define DAT_GROUP_ENTRY 24

typedef struct DatDirEntry {
    char     name[14];   /* 13 bytes on disk + terminator */
    uint8_t  disc;       /* 1 = on IZZYCD1.DAT; 0 = not on this disc */
    uint32_t offset;     /* byte offset of the group in the .DAT */
} DatDirEntry;

typedef struct DatDir {
    DatDirEntry *entries;
    size_t       count;
} DatDir;

typedef struct DatFileEntry {
    char     name[17];
    uint32_t size;
    uint32_t offset;     /* from the start of the group */
} DatFileEntry;

typedef struct DatGroup {
    uint32_t      base;  /* group offset in the .DAT */
    DatFileEntry *files;
    size_t        count;
} DatGroup;

/* Load IZZY.DIR. Returns 0 on success. (orig return codes of dat_LookupTake: 2 = alloc, 3 = read) */
int  dat_dir_load(DatDir *dir, const char *dirPath);
void dat_dir_free(DatDir *dir);

/* Binary search by take name, case-insensitive. Returns the entry or NULL (orig: dat_LookupTake returns 1). */
const DatDirEntry *dat_dir_lookup(const DatDir *dir, const char *takeName);

/* Read the file table of the group at `offset` in an open .DAT stream. Returns 0 on success. */
int  dat_group_open(DatGroup *g, void *datFile /* FILE* */, uint32_t offset);
void dat_group_free(DatGroup *g);

/* Find a file in a group (case-insensitive, like the original _fstricmp scan). */
const DatFileEntry *dat_group_find(const DatGroup *g, const char *fileName);

/* Read a file's bytes from the .DAT into a malloc'd buffer. Returns NULL on failure. */
uint8_t *dat_read(void *datFile, const DatGroup *g, const DatFileEntry *e);

#endif

```

`src/game.c`:

```c
/*
 * game.c - the game object (see game.h).
 */
#include "game.h"
#include "savegame.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* orig: 1010:85e0 path_Resolve - file type from the first two extension letters (a 3-digit extension is a
 * numbered audio part): 0 sound (WA WV AB SX), 1 BM, 2 AN, 3 DE, 4 TK, 5 SM, 6 AV, 7 MI, 8 other */
int game_file_type(const char *name)
{
    static const char *ext[] = { "WA", "WV", "AB", "SX", "BM", "AN", "DE", "TK", "SM", "AV", "MI" };
    static const int type[] = { 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7 };
    const char *d = strrchr(name, '.');
    size_t i;
    if (!d) return 8;
    if (d[1] >= '0' && d[1] <= '9' && d[2] >= '0' && d[2] <= '9' && d[3] >= '0' && d[3] <= '9') return 0;
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof ext / sizeof ext[0]; i++)
        if (d[1] && d[2] && izzy_stricmp((char[3]){ d[1], d[2], 0 }, ext[i]) == 0) return type[i];
    return 8;
}

/* a file from the current take's groups, else the shared MIA group (orig: the asset manager's lookups) */
static uint8_t *game_read(Game *g, const char *name, size_t *len)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < g->scene.ngroups; i++) {
        const DatFileEntry *e = dat_group_find(&g->scene.groups[i], name);
        if (e) { *len = e->size; return dat_read(g->dat, &g->scene.groups[i], e); }
    }
    {
        const DatDirEntry *d = dat_dir_lookup(&g->dir, "MIA.TKE");
        DatGroup grp;
        uint8_t *r = NULL;
        if (d && dat_group_open(&grp, g->dat, d->offset) == 0) {
            const DatFileEntry *e = dat_group_find(&grp, name);
            if (e) { *len = e->size; r = dat_read(g->dat, &grp, e); }
            dat_group_free(&grp);
        }
        return r;
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ takes */

static int stage_uses(const Stage *st, const uint8_t *d)
{
    int s;
    if (st->bgPending == d) return 1;
    for (s = 0; s < STAGE_SLOTS; s++) {
        const StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
        if (p->pendPack == d || p->pendDib == d || p->dib == d || (p->havePack && p->pack.data == d)) return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static void game_purge_cache(Game *g)
{   /* orig: asset_Release reference counts (1010:...) - free what no slot holds any more */
    int i, n = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < g->cache.nfiles; i++) {
        if (stage_uses(&g->stage, g->cache.files[i].data)) g->cache.files[n++] = g->cache.files[i];
        else free(g->cache.files[i].data);
    }
    g->cache.nfiles = n;
}

int game_jump_to_take(Game *g, const char *name)
{   /* orig: 1010:1c8c take_JumpToTake; 1010:3afe take_LoadTake: a missing take plays "MIA" ("Missing Take") */
    char base[16];
    const char *dot;
    size_t n;
    {   /* UI +0xa4 (1008:c2d6): the first take of the CREDITS node arms the quit, the next one quits */
        const char *s2 = (g->vm.currentNode >= 0 && g->vm.currentNode < g->vm.nodeCount) ? g->vm.nodes[g->vm.currentNode].s2 : NULL;
        if (s2 && izzy_stricmp(s2, "CREDITS") == 0) {
            if (g->creditsRunning) {
                wave_stop(&g->wave, -1);
                game_pause(g);
                g->quitRequested = 1;                         /* PostMessage(0x401) */
                return 1;
            }
            g->creditsRunning = 1;
        }
    }
    g->queued = 0;
    g->more = 0;
    dot = strrchr(name, '.');
    n = dot ? (size_t)(dot - name) : strlen(name);
    if (n > 12) n = 12;
    memcpy(base, name, n); base[n] = 0;
    strncpy(g->takeName, base, sizeof g->takeName - 1);
    if (g->haveScene) { scene_close(&g->scene); g->haveScene = 0; }
    game_purge_cache(g);
    if (scene_open(&g->scene, &g->stage, &g->vm, &g->dir, g->dat, base) != 0) {
        g->takesMissing++;
        if (scene_open(&g->scene, &g->stage, &g->vm, &g->dir, g->dat, "MIA") != 0) {
            g->takeMode = 0; g->more = 1;                     /* take_LoadTake status 6 */
            return 0;
        }
    }
    g->haveScene = 1;
    g->takeMode = 1;
    /* orig: 1010:1290 take_SetMusicMode(1) once the take's files are in (immediately here): default view, re-sync armed */
    take_SetDefaultView(&g->stage);
    g->musicPending = 1;
    g->loadState = 5;
    g->takesStarted++;
    return 1;
}

static int game_execute_next_vep_cmnd(Game *g);

static int game_process_step(Game *g)
{   /* orig: 1010:2486 game_ProcessStep */
    int r = 1;
    if (g->loadState == 5) {                                 /* first step of a loaded take */
        g->more = 0;
        g->takeClock = 0; g->takeLast = g->now;              /* ref_SetSecond */
        g->loadState = 2;
        return 1;
    }
    if (g->more) return 0;
    if (g->takeMode == 0) return game_execute_next_vep_cmnd(g);
    if (g->takeMode == 1) {
        if (!g->haveScene) return 0;
        g->takeClock += g->now - g->takeLast; g->takeLast = g->now;    /* ref_SwapSecond */
        if (g->playMode == 1 && vet_get_busy(&g->vm) == 0) return 1;
        if (!g->ui.active) { g->ui.active = 1; ui_set_panel(&g->ui, 5); }   /* 1010:12e4: the panel wakes with the take */
        r = scene_tick(&g->scene, g->takeClock);
        if (r <= 0) {                                         /* FF: take over (1010:12e4 tail) */
            vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 0, 0xd);
            g->more = 1;
            r = r < 0 ? 0 : 1;
        }
        return r;
    }
    return 1;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ narration */

static int game_execute_next_vep_cmnd(Game *g)
{   /* orig: 1010:0f0c ExecuteNextVepCmnd - the picture (3 s) and the voice of a narration page */
    int32_t ms = g->now * 1000 / 60;
    if (g->queued & 1) {
        if (g->picture[0] == 0) { g->picState = 0; vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 0, 8); }
        else g->picState = 1;
    }
    if (g->picState == 1) {
        size_t len;
        uint8_t *bmp = game_read(g, g->picture, &len);
        if (!bmp) bmp = game_read(g, "MISSING.BMP", &len);
        if (bmp) {
            if (g->cache.nfiles < (int)(sizeof g->cache.files / sizeof g->cache.files[0])) {   /* keep it in the cache */
                SceneFile *f = &g->cache.files[g->cache.nfiles++];
                strncpy(f->name, g->picture, sizeof f->name - 1); f->data = bmp; f->len = len;
            }
            if (!g->ui.active) { g->ui.active = 1; ui_set_panel(&g->ui, 5); }
            take_ShowPicture(&g->stage, bmp);
            take_SetAnimRunning(&g->stage, 1);
            g->picState = 2;
            g->picStart = ms;
        }
    }
    if (g->picState == 2 && (uint32_t)(ms - g->picStart) > 3000) { g->picState = 0; vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 0, 8); }
    if (g->queued & 2) {
        if (g->voice[0] == 0) { g->voiceState = 0; vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 0, 4); }
        else g->voiceState = 1;
    }
    if (g->voiceState == 1) {
        size_t len;
        uint8_t *wav = game_read(g, g->voice, &len);
        if (wav) wave_play(&g->wave, 0, wav, (uint32_t)len, 8, (len > 0x18 && rd_i16(wav + 0x16) < 2) ? 1 : 0);
        free(wav);
        g->voiceState = 2;
    }
    if (g->voiceState == 2 && !wave_busy(&g->wave, 0)) {
        g->voiceState = 0; vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 0, 4);
    }
    if (g->voiceState == 0 && g->picState == 0) {
        g->more = 1;
        if (g->playMode == 1) vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 0, 0xc);
    }
    g->queued = 0;
    return 1;
}

static int game_queue_page(Game *g, const char *page)
{   /* orig: 1010:1eac, 1010:1dc8 (line i of the page) - one line per file: BMP -> picture, WAV -> voice, MID -> the MIDI player */
    int any = 0;
    const char *p = page;
    while (*p) {
        char line[64]; size_t n = strcspn(p, "\n");
        int t;
        if (n > sizeof line - 1) n = sizeof line - 1;
        memcpy(line, p, n); line[n] = 0;
        p += strcspn(p, "\n"); if (*p) p++;
        t = game_file_type(line);
        if (t == 7) {                                           /* MID: UI +0x20 (name, 2), then +0x24(6): narration music loops */
            game_midi(g, line, MIDI_CMD_PLAY);
            game_midi(g, NULL, MIDI_CMD_LOOP_ON);
        } else if (t == 1) {
            strncpy(g->picture, line, sizeof g->picture - 1);
            g->more = 0; g->queued |= 1; vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 1, 8); any = 1;
        } else if (t == 0) {
            strncpy(g->voice, line, sizeof g->voice - 1);
            g->more = 0; g->queued |= 2; vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 1, 4); any = 1;
        } else {
            g->more = 1;                                        /* "Unknown file type from vep" */
            return any;
        }
    }
    if (any && !(g->takeMode == 1 && !g->more)) g->takeMode = 0;
    return any;
}

/* VM callbacks (installed by 1010:2d1a take_Player_ctor). orig: 1010:1c26 is the body of cb_interrupt */
static void cb_interrupt(void *c, VetVM *vm, const char *s2)
{   /* orig: 1010:b8d6 -> 1010:1c26 - a new description interrupts the narration still playing */
    Game *g = c; (void)s2;
    g->ui.active = 0;                                     /* UI +0x190 */
    if (vet_get_busy(vm) & 1) {
        wave_stop(&g->wave, 0); wave_stop(&g->wave, 1);
    }
}
static int cb_jump_take(void *c, VetVM *vm, const char *s2)
{   /* orig: 1010:b8ee vepfn_JumpTake */
    vet_set_busy(vm, 0, 1);
    return game_jump_to_take((Game *)c, s2);
}
static int cb_narrate(void *c, VetVM *vm, const char *page, int first)
{   /* orig: 1010:b928 */
    Game *g = c;
    if (first) {
        wave_stop(&g->wave, -1);
        vet_set_busy(vm, 0, 0xd);
        take_Wait(&g->stage, 0);
    }
    return game_queue_page(g, page);
}
static int cb_music(void *c, VetVM *vm, const char *page, int first)
{   /* orig: 1010:b996 - a page holding only a MID */
    (void)vm; (void)first;
    return game_queue_page((Game *)c, page);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ input */

static void hk_spot(void *c, int add, const TakeFields *f)
{   /* Spot (B3): add -> UI vtable +0x18 (1008:bc0a), else +0x1c (1008:bcbc); Clear removes all */
    Game *g = c;
    if (add) {
        int16_t xy[8]; int k;
        static const int off[8] = { TF_44, TF_S0, TF_S2, TF_S4, TF_S6, TF_4E, TF_50, TF_52 };
        for (k = 0; k < 8; k++) xy[k] = take_field(f, off[k]);
        ui_spot_add(&g->ui, take_field(f, TF_ARG1), take_field(f, TF_ARG2HI), xy);
    } else ui_spot_remove(&g->ui, take_field(f, TF_ARG1));
}

static void hk_sfx(void *c, int id, const char *file, int volume)
{   /* Sfx (A2): register the sound for channel id (1010:12e4 case A2; sfx_Register) */
    Game *g = c;
    size_t len; uint8_t *d;
    if (id < 1 || id > 16) return;
    d = game_read(g, file, &len);
    free(g->sfx[id].data);
    g->sfx[id].data = d; g->sfx[id].len = d ? len : 0; g->sfx[id].volume = volume;
}

static void hk_audio(void *c, int channel, const char *file, int volume, unsigned flags)
{   /* Audio (A1), orig: 1010:12e4: channels 1..3 are wave streams, higher ones MIDI */
    Game *g = c;
    size_t len; uint8_t *d;
    if (channel >= 1 && channel <= 3) {
        unsigned mode;
        if (!file || !*file) { wave_stop(&g->wave, channel - 1); return; }
        d = game_read(g, file, &len);
        if (!d) return;
        if (flags & 2) mode = 6;                                   /* looping stereo */
        else mode = ((flags & 1) ? 8 : 0) | ((flags & 4) ? 2 : 1); /* 8 = queue the part, 1 = mono */
        wave_play(&g->wave, channel - 1, d, (uint32_t)len, volume, mode);
        free(d);
    } else if (file && *file) {                                     /* UI +0x20 (name, 2); flag 2 -> +0x24(6) loop */
        game_midi(g, file, MIDI_CMD_PLAY);
        if (flags & 2) game_midi(g, NULL, MIDI_CMD_LOOP_ON);
    }
}

void game_audio_mix(Game *g, int16_t *out, int frames)
{   /* game_Pause -> wave_Pause: the waveOut device stops, the channels keep their place */
    if (g->paused) { memset(out, 0, sizeof(int16_t) * 2 * (size_t)frames); return; }
    wave_mix(&g->wave, out, frames);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ MIDI */

static void midi_host(Game *g, int op, const char *name, const uint8_t *data, size_t len)
{
    if (g->audio.midi) g->audio.midi(g->audio.ctx, op, name, data, len);
}

void game_midi(Game *g, const char *name, int cmd)
{   /* orig: 1008:9358 ui_MidiOpen + 1008:8e34 ui_MidiCommand. The original first asks MCI for a sequencer
     * ("sysinfo sequencer quantity wait", DS:0292); here the host decides. */
    if (name && cmd == MIDI_CMD_PLAY) { strncpy(g->midiNext, name, sizeof g->midiNext - 1); g->midiNext[sizeof g->midiNext - 1] = 0; }
    if (!g->musicOn) cmd = MIDI_CMD_STOP;                      /* Game +0x32 == 0: everything stops the music */
    else if (cmd == MIDI_CMD_PLAY) {                           /* the special names */
        if (izzy_stricmp(g->midiNext, "OFF.MID") == 0) cmd = MIDI_CMD_STOP;
        else if (izzy_stricmp(g->midiNext, "STOP.MID") == 0) cmd = MIDI_CMD_PAUSE;
        else if (izzy_stricmp(g->midiNext, "GO.MID") == 0) cmd = MIDI_CMD_RESUME;
        if (cmd != MIDI_CMD_PLAY) g->midiNext[0] = 0;
    }
    switch (cmd) {
    case MIDI_CMD_POLL:                                        /* looping: "status music mode" == stopped -> play from 0 (the host loops) */
        break;
    case MIDI_CMD_PLAY:
        if (!g->midiNext[0]) break;
        if (g->midiOpen && izzy_stricmp(g->midiNext, g->midiFile) == 0) {   /* the same file: resume if paused */
            if (g->midiPaused) { midi_host(g, GAME_MIDI_RESUME, g->midiFile, NULL, 0); g->midiPaused = 0; }
            g->midiNext[0] = 0;
            break;
        }
        if (g->midiOpen) midi_host(g, GAME_MIDI_STOP, g->midiFile, NULL, 0);
        g->midiOpen = g->midiLoop = g->midiPaused = 0;
        {   /* the .MID files are loose in the game folder: MCI opens "<dir>\NAME.MID" (1010:3152 builds the path);
             * with DS:0276 == 0 a file in IZZYCD1.DAT is used */
            size_t len = 0; uint8_t *d = NULL;
            char p[700]; FILE *mf;
            sprintf(p, "%s/%s", g->gameDir, g->midiNext);
            mf = fopen(p, "rb");
            if (mf) {
                long n; fseek(mf, 0, SEEK_END); n = ftell(mf); fseek(mf, 0, SEEK_SET);
                if (n > 0 && (d = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)n)) != NULL && fread(d, 1, (size_t)n, mf) != (size_t)n) { free(d); d = NULL; }
                len = n > 0 ? (size_t)n : 0;
                fclose(mf);
            }
            if (!d) d = game_read(g, g->midiNext, &len);
            if (d) {
                strcpy(g->midiFile, g->midiNext);
                midi_host(g, GAME_MIDI_PLAY, g->midiFile, d, len);
                g->midiOpen = 1;
                free(d);
            }
        }
        g->midiNext[0] = 0;
        break;
    case MIDI_CMD_STOP:
        if (g->midiOpen) {
            midi_host(g, GAME_MIDI_STOP, g->midiFile, NULL, 0);
            g->midiFile[0] = 0; g->midiPaused = 0; g->midiOpen = 0;
        }
        break;
    case MIDI_CMD_PAUSE:
        if (g->midiOpen && !g->midiPaused) { midi_host(g, GAME_MIDI_PAUSE, g->midiFile, NULL, 0); g->midiPaused = 1; }
        break;
    case MIDI_CMD_RESUME:
        if (g->midiOpen && g->midiPaused) { midi_host(g, GAME_MIDI_RESUME, g->midiFile, NULL, 0); g->midiPaused = 0; }
        break;
    case MIDI_CMD_LOOP_ON:
        g->midiLoop = 1; if (g->midiOpen) midi_host(g, GAME_MIDI_LOOP_ON, g->midiFile, NULL, 0);
        break;
    case MIDI_CMD_LOOP_OFF:
        g->midiLoop = 0; if (g->midiOpen) midi_host(g, GAME_MIDI_LOOP_OFF, g->midiFile, NULL, 0);
        break;
    }
}

void game_pause(Game *g)
{   /* orig: 1008:5e14 game_Pause */
    g->paused = 1;
    stage_SetRunning(&g->stage, 0);
    game_midi(g, NULL, MIDI_CMD_PAUSE);
}

void game_resume(Game *g)
{   /* orig: 1008:5dac timer_Resume */
    g->paused = 0;
    stage_SetRunning(&g->stage, 1);
    game_midi(g, NULL, MIDI_CMD_RESUME);
    g->rebase = 1;
}

void game_new_game(Game *g)
{   /* orig: MAINWNDPROC case 0x65 after the prompts: vet_Restart, UI +0xd4 (1008:6e14), UI +0x5a / +0xc6 / +0x18e = 0,
     * UI +0x44 SetDefaultControls(0), UI +0x48 SetControls(0) */
    vet_restart(&g->vm);
    g->creditsRunning = 0;
    g->ui.u46a = 0; g->ui.u4e = 0; g->ui.ds[0x282] = 0;           /* 1008:6e14 */
    g->rules.ui.controlPressed = 0;
    g->ui.editCur = 0;
    ui_set_default_controls(&g->ui, 0);
    ui_set_controls(&g->ui, 0);
}

static void hk_avi(void *c, const TakeFields *f)
{   /* Avi (B7). orig: 1008:7bae, 1010:821a, 1010:7c88 - pause, open "<dir>\NAME.AVI" with MCI, play in the
     * rectangle; busy |= 2 until it ends. With no host player it behaves like a failed MCI open: nothing. */
    Game *g = c;
    char path[700];
    if (g->aviPlaying || !g->audio.playAvi) return;
    sprintf(path, "%s/%s", g->gameDir, take_field_str(f, TF_S0));
    if (g->audio.playAvi(g->audio.ctx, path, take_field(f, TF_ARG1), take_field(f, TF_ARG2),
                         take_field(f, TF_ARG2HI), take_field(f, TF_44))) {
        vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 1, 2);
        g->aviPlaying = 1;
    }
}

static void w16f(FILE *f, int v) { fputc(v & 0xff, f); fputc((v >> 8) & 0xff, f); }
static int r16f(FILE *f) { int a = fgetc(f), b = fgetc(f); return (a == EOF || b == EOF) ? 0 : (int16_t)(a | (b << 8)); }

int game_save(Game *g, FILE *f)
{   /* the VM block, then UI +0xcc (1008:7290) */
    static VetSaveExtras x;
    char names[SAVE_PLAYERS][32];
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < SAVE_PLAYERS; i++) {
        int n = g->rules.ui.nameLen[i];
        if (n < 0 || n > 31) n = 0;
        memcpy(names[i], g->ui.ds + 0x1ff6 + 0x20 * i, 32); names[i][n] = 0;
    }
    if (!vet_save_game(&g->vm, &x, (const char (*)[32])names, f)) return 0;
    w16f(f, g->rules.ui.hotspotsEnabled); w16f(f, g->ui.mask188); w16f(f, g->ui.mask186);
    w16f(f, g->ui.panel); w16f(f, g->ui.u46a); w16f(f, g->ui.u4e);
    fwrite(g->ui.ds + 0x282, 1, 15, f);
    ui_scores_save(&g->ui);                                      /* UI +0xc8 */
    return ferror(f) ? 0 : 1;
}

int game_load(Game *g, FILE *f)
{   /* MAINWNDPROC Open: reset the edit state, vet_LoadState, UI +0xd4 (1008:6e14), UI +0xd0 (1008:7334) */
    static VetSaveExtras x;
    char names[SAVE_PLAYERS][32];
    int i, desc = 0, mask;
    g->ui.editWant = g->ui.editCur = 0;
    g->creditsRunning = 0;                                       /* not in the original (UI +0x52 stays set): a click would quit */
    if (!vet_load_game(&g->vm, &x, names, f, &desc)) return 0;
    for (i = 0; i < SAVE_PLAYERS; i++) {
        size_t n = strlen(names[i]);
        memset(g->ui.ds + 0x1ff6 + 0x20 * i, 0, 32);
        memcpy(g->ui.ds + 0x1ff6 + 0x20 * i, names[i], n);
        g->rules.ui.nameLen[i] = (int16_t)n;
    }
    g->ui.u46a = 0; g->ui.u4e = 0; g->ui.ds[0x282] = 0;           /* 1008:6e14 */
    g->rules.ui.controlPressed = 0;
    (void)r16f(f);                                               /* hotspots (read and dropped) */
    mask = r16f(f); ui_set_default_controls(&g->ui, (unsigned)mask);
    (void)r16f(f); (void)r16f(f);                                /* mask186, panel (dropped) */
    g->ui.u46a = (int16_t)r16f(f); g->ui.u4e = (int16_t)r16f(f);
    if (fread(g->ui.ds + 0x282, 1, 15, f) != 15) return 0;
    ui_update_hud(&g->ui, 1);
    return 1;
}

void game_avi_finished(Game *g)
{   /* orig: 1008:7ea6, 1008:7b42, 1008:7f4a, 1008:7f9e - close the device, clear busy bit 2, resume the wave player */
    if (!g->aviPlaying) return;
    g->aviPlaying = 0;
    vet_set_busy(&g->vm, 0, 2);
}

static void hk_sync(void *c, int32_t time)
{   /* orig: 1010:87ba ref_SetFirst(ref154, statement time) - the first channel-1 Audio / Show re-bases the take clock */
    Game *g = c;
    if (g->musicPending) { g->takeClock = time; g->musicPending = 0; }
}

static void hk_done(void *c) { vet_set_busy(&((Game *)c)->vm, 0, 0xd); }   /* Done (A3) */

static int game_try_skip_avi(Game *g)
{   /* orig: 1008:76b6 (UI +0xe0) - an AVI is playing, no modal (+0x58) and skipping allowed (+0xc == 0): UI +0xb0 stops it */
    if (g->aviPlaying && !g->noSkipAvi) {
        if (g->audio.stopAvi) g->audio.stopAvi(g->audio.ctx);
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

int game_click(Game *g, int x, int y)
{   /* orig: 1008:75a2 (button down: during an AVI that may not be skipped nothing happens, else UI +0xe0) and
     * 1008:94a0 game_HandleInput; a hotspot plays its channel's sound (1010:0e54 take_IsChannelReady) */
    int r;
    if (g->aviPlaying && g->noSkipAvi) return 0;
    if (game_try_skip_avi(g)) return 0;
    if (g->creditsRunning) { g->quitRequested = 1; return 0; }  /* +0x52: a click during the credits quits (0x401) */
    r = ui_click(&g->ui, &g->stage, x, y);
    if (r < 0 && -r <= 16 && g->sfx[-r].data)                 /* channel 2, mono, the registered volume - 1 */
        wave_play(&g->wave, 2, g->sfx[-r].data, (uint32_t)g->sfx[-r].len, g->sfx[-r].volume - 1, 1);
    return r;
}

void game_mouse_move(Game *g, int x, int y)
{   /* orig: 1008:7a60 ui_SetCursor - entering the top 20 rows tries to skip the AVI once */
    if (y < 20) { if (!g->aviSkipped) { g->aviSkipped = 1; game_try_skip_avi(g); } }
    else g->aviSkipped = 0;
    ui_mouse_move(&g->ui, &g->stage, x, y);
}

static void hk_set_panel(void *c, int panel) { ui_set_panel(&((Game *)c)->ui, panel); }
static void hk_set_controls(void *c, int mask) { ui_set_controls(&((Game *)c)->ui, (unsigned)mask); }
static void hk_set_default_controls(void *c, int mask) { ui_set_default_controls(&((Game *)c)->ui, (unsigned)mask); }

void game_char(Game *g, int ch) { ui_char(&g->ui, ch); }

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ the game */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ script fixes */

static int fix_last_statement(VetVM *vm, const char *nodeName, const uint8_t *expect, int expectLen,
                              const uint8_t *with, int withLen, int keepOld)
{   /* replace (keepOld = 0) or insert before (keepOld = 1) the last statement of a node's LOOP block, if it is `expect` */
    int node = vet_find(vm, nodeName), starts[64], n, at;
    const VetCode *c;
    if (node < 0 || vm->nodes[node].type != 1) return 0;
    c = &vm->nodes[node].code2;
    n = vet_statements(c, starts, 64);
    if (n < 1 || n > 64) return 0;
    at = starts[n - 1];
    if (c->len - at != expectLen || memcmp(c->data + at, expect, (size_t)expectLen) != 0) return 0;
    return vet_replace_code(vm, node, 2, at, keepOld ? 0 : expectLen, with, withLen);
}

int game_fix_script(Game *g)
{   /* Not in the original. IZZY.VET never plays Izzy's bronze-medal announcement: take SIZ00013 (DIZ00013.WAV,
     * MUPOWER.MID) and its node exist, but nothing goes there - gold and silver end with GOTO SIZ00011 / SIZ00012
     * (or GOSUB after a competition), bronze with RETURN. These two edits give bronze the same ending.
     * Each applies only when the bytes are exactly the shipped ones. Returns how many were applied. */
    VetVM *vm = &g->vm;
    int siz13 = vet_find(vm, "SIZ00013"), eot = vet_find(vm, "END_OF_TURN"), done = 0;
    uint8_t ret[4] = { 0x81, 0x40, 0x00, 0x88 };                              /* RETURN */
    uint8_t go13[7] = { 0x81, 0x12, 0x00, 0x86, 0, 0, 0x88 };                 /* GOTO SIZ00013 */
    uint8_t goEot[7] = { 0x81, 0x12, 0x00, 0x86, 0, 0, 0x88 };                /* GOTO END_OF_TURN */
    uint8_t sub13[7] = { 0x81, 0x22, 0x00, 0x86, 0, 0, 0x88 };                /* GOSUB SIZ00013 */
    if (siz13 < 0 || eot < 0) return 0;
    go13[4] = sub13[4] = (uint8_t)siz13; go13[5] = sub13[5] = (uint8_t)(siz13 >> 8);
    goEot[4] = (uint8_t)eot; goEot[5] = (uint8_t)(eot >> 8);
    /* a bronze found on the map: ACCEPT_BRONZE ... RETURN  ->  ... GOTO SIZ00013 (which RETURNs to ACCEPT_YES) */
    done += fix_last_statement(vm, "ACCEPT_BRONZE", ret, 4, go13, 7, 0);
    /* a bronze from Lanse's competition: ... GOTO END_OF_TURN  ->  ... GOSUB SIZ00013, GOTO END_OF_TURN */
    done += fix_last_statement(vm, "LANSE_COMP_BRONZE", goEot, 7, sub13, 7, 1);
    return done;
}

int game_open(Game *g, const char *dir, unsigned seed)
{
    char p[1024];
    memset(g, 0, sizeof *g);
    strncpy(g->gameDir, dir, sizeof g->gameDir - 1);
    sprintf(p, "%s/IZZY.DIR", dir);
    if (dat_dir_load(&g->dir, p) != 0) return -1;
    sprintf(p, "%s/IZZYCD1.DAT", dir);
    g->dat = fopen(p, "rb");
    if (!g->dat) return -1;
    sprintf(p, "%s/IZZY.VET", dir);
    if (vet_load(&g->vm, p) != 0) return -1;
    g->scriptFixes = game_fix_script(g);
    stage_init(&g->stage, &g->screen);
    g->scene.cache = &g->cache;
    izzy_rules_init(&g->rules, &g->vm);
    sprintf(p, "%s/IZZY.EXE", dir);
    if (res_open(&g->exe, p) == 0) {
        /* the data segment image (NE segment 7, the DGROUP) - its strings and tables back the control texts */
        size_t ne = rd_u32(g->exe.exe + 0x3c), segtab = ne + (uint16_t)rd_i16(g->exe.exe + ne + 0x22);
        int shift = (uint16_t)rd_i16(g->exe.exe + ne + 0x32), dgrp = (uint16_t)rd_i16(g->exe.exe + ne + 0x0e);
        size_t e = segtab + (size_t)(dgrp - 1) * 8, off = (size_t)(uint16_t)rd_i16(g->exe.exe + e) << shift;
        size_t len = (uint16_t)rd_i16(g->exe.exe + e + 2);
        ui_init(&g->ui, &g->rules.ui, &g->stage, &g->vm, &g->exe, off + len <= g->exe.len ? g->exe.exe + off : NULL, len);
    } else ui_init(&g->ui, &g->rules.ui, &g->stage, &g->vm, NULL, NULL, 0);
    wave_init(&g->wave, rd_i16(g->ui.ds + 0x5be), rd_i16(g->ui.ds + 0x5c0), 0);   /* DS:05be / 05c0 */
    sprintf(g->iniPath, "%s/izzy.ini", dir);
    g->ui.iniPath = g->iniPath;
    ui_scores_load(&g->ui);
    g->rules.ui.ctx = g;
    g->rules.ui.setPanel = hk_set_panel;
    g->rules.ui.setControls = hk_set_controls;
    g->rules.ui.setDefaultControls = hk_set_default_controls;
    g->scene.hooks.ctx = g; g->scene.hooks.spot = hk_spot; g->scene.hooks.sfx = hk_sfx; g->scene.hooks.done = hk_done; g->scene.hooks.audio = hk_audio; g->scene.hooks.avi = hk_avi; g->scene.hooks.sync = hk_sync;
    g->vm.hookCtx = g;
    g->vm.onInterrupt = cb_interrupt;
    g->vm.onJumpTake = cb_jump_take;
    g->vm.onNarrate = cb_narrate;
    g->vm.onMusic = cb_music;
    g->musicOn = 1;                                             /* the izzy.ini defaults (app_apply_settings sets them) */
    g->noSkipAvi = 1;
    g->playMode = 1;                                            /* take_Player_ctor: +0x5c = 1, +0x5d = 0 */
    g->takeMode = 0;
    g->more = 1;
    vet_new_game(&g->vm, seed);
    return 0;
}

void game_close(Game *g)
{
    if (g->haveScene) scene_close(&g->scene);
    { int i; for (i = 0; i < g->cache.nfiles; i++) free(g->cache.files[i].data); g->cache.nfiles = 0;
      for (i = 0; i < 17; i++) free(g->sfx[i].data); }
    wave_shutdown(&g->wave);
    res_close(&g->exe);
    vet_free(&g->vm);
    if (g->dat) fclose(g->dat);
    dat_dir_free(&g->dir);
}

void game_idle(Game *g, int32_t now)
{   /* orig: 1008:5bfc, 1018:0f3c, 1018:0f88, 1018:0fd4, 1018:1020 - game_Idle (and the VM callback setters used in game_open);
     * the asset-loading and MIDI pumps it also calls are not part of this port; the wave mix pass runs in game_audio_mix */
    int32_t host = now;
    if (host / 10 != g->uiTickAt) {                            /* the UI tick (+0x50) runs every 167 ms, paused or not */
        g->uiTickAt = host / 10;
        ui_tick(&g->ui);
        game_midi(g, NULL, MIDI_CMD_POLL);                     /* 1008:9614 */
    }
    if (g->rebase) { g->clockBase = host - g->now; g->rebase = 0; }
    if (g->paused) { g->idleParity = 0; return; }             /* +0x48: only the stage redraw (1008:54fc) */
    now = host - g->clockBase;                                 /* timer_Update */
    g->now = now;
    game_process_step(g);
    if (g->idleParity == 0) { vet_step(&g->vm, (uint32_t)now, 6); g->idleParity = 1; }
    else { stage_FrameUpdate(&g->stage, now); g->idleParity = 0; }
}
```

`src/game.h`:

```h
/*
 * game.h - the game object: VET script VM + take player + narration + stage, driven by one idle step.
 *
 * orig: the Game object (Game+4 stage, +8 take player, +0x14 UI/hotspots, +0x1c wave player, +0x20 VM,
 * +0x24 60 Hz clock) and the TakePlayer (1010:2d1a take_Player_ctor). The idle step 1008:5bfc game_Idle runs
 * game_ProcessStep every call and alternates vet_Step (TIME unit 6 ticks) and engine_FrameUpdate.
 * The clock is in 60 Hz ticks (1008:5e66 timer_Update: accumulated timeGetTime * 60 / 1000).
 */
#ifndef IZZY_GAME_H
#define IZZY_GAME_H

#include "scene.h"
#include "builtins.h"
#include "ui.h"
#include "audio.h"

/* MIDI goes to the host (orig: MCI "sequencer" through the UI object: +0x20 1008:9358 ui_MidiOpen names the
 * file, +0x24 1008:8e34 ui_MidiCommand runs a command). The host gets these operations: */
enum {
    GAME_MIDI_PLAY = 1,     /* play data from the start ("open sequencer!%s alias music" + "play music from 0") */
    GAME_MIDI_STOP,         /* "stop music" + "close music" */
    GAME_MIDI_PAUSE,        /* remember "status music position", "stop music" */
    GAME_MIDI_RESUME,       /* "play music from <position>" */
    GAME_MIDI_LOOP_ON,      /* restart when it ends (the original polls "status music mode" each tick) */
    GAME_MIDI_LOOP_OFF
};
/* ui_MidiCommand numbers (game_midi): */
enum { MIDI_CMD_POLL = 1, MIDI_CMD_PLAY, MIDI_CMD_STOP, MIDI_CMD_PAUSE, MIDI_CMD_RESUME, MIDI_CMD_LOOP_ON, MIDI_CMD_LOOP_OFF };

typedef struct GameAudio {
    void *ctx;
    void (*midi)(void *ctx, int op, const char *name, const uint8_t *data, size_t len);   /* data only for PLAY */
    /* AVI (TAKE B7; orig: UI +0xac 1008:7bae through MCI): start playing path in the rectangle; return 1 if it
     * plays - the host then calls game_avi_finished() when it ends. */
    int  (*playAvi)(void *ctx, const char *path, int x, int y, int w, int h);
    /* stop the AVI early (orig: UI +0xb0, a click or the pointer on the top 20 rows skips it); the host then
     * calls game_avi_finished() as usual */
    void (*stopAvi)(void *ctx);
} GameAudio;

typedef struct Game {
    Screen    screen;
    Stage     stage;
    VetVM     vm;
    IzzyRules rules;
    UiState   ui;
    NeResources exe;          /* IZZY.EXE: control bitmaps, fonts, the initial data segment */
    struct { uint8_t *data; size_t len; int volume; } sfx[17];   /* Sfx (A2) registry by id 1..16 (TakePlayer +0x72) */
    Scene     scene;
    SceneCache cache;         /* files shared by successive takes, purged when no slot uses them */
    DatDir    dir;
    FILE     *dat;
    GameAudio audio;
    WaveMixer wave;           /* Game+0x1c */
    int32_t   now;            /* Game+0x24: 60 Hz clock */
    int       idleParity;     /* DS:0278 */
    /* take player (TakePlayer +0x36 / +0x5c / +0x5d / asset +0x25c) */
    int       more;           /* 1 = no statement left (moreStatements) */
    int       playMode;       /* +0x5c: 1 = take statements advance only while the VM is busy */
    int       takeMode;       /* +0x5d: 1 = a take, 0 = narration commands (ExecuteNextVepCmnd) */
    int       loadState;      /* 5 = loaded, waiting to start; 2 = running */
    int       haveScene;
    char      takeName[16];   /* +0x5e */
    int32_t   takeClock, takeLast;
    int       musicPending;   /* TakePlayer +0x18c (set by take_SetMusicMode(1)) */   /* ref154: elapsed take ticks and the clock value they were measured at */
    /* narration channels (+0x170 picture, +0x15e voice, +0x17e queued bits; DS:059c / 059d / 2448) */
    char      picture[16], voice[16];
    unsigned  queued;
    int       picState, voiceState;
    int32_t   picStart;
    long      takesStarted, takesMissing;
    int       aviPlaying;     /* UI +0x26 */
    int       noSkipAvi;      /* UI +0xc: izzy.ini [OPTIONS] INTRO (default 1) - clicks do not skip AVIs */
    int       aviSkipped;     /* UI +0x1e: the pointer is in the top 20 rows (skip once) */
    int       musicOn;        /* Game +0x32: [OPTIONS] MUSIC, the "Music Playing" menu item */
    int       override8Bit;   /* Game +0x34: [Options] Override8Bit = "true" */
    char      midiNext[16];   /* UI +0x1b2: the file named by ui_MidiOpen */
    char      midiFile[16];   /* DS:0294: the file the sequencer holds */
    int       midiOpen, midiLoop, midiPaused;   /* DS:0364 / 0366 / 0368 */
    int       creditsRunning; /* UI +0x52: a take started while the VM's S2 (VM +0x6c) is "CREDITS" */
    int       quitRequested;  /* WM 0x401 posted: the credits ended or were clicked away - the host closes */
    int       scriptFixes;    /* how many game_fix_script edits applied (not in the original) */
    int       paused;         /* Game +0x48 (game_Pause): the clock, the mixer and the steps stop */
    int       rebase;         /* timer_Resume re-bases the clock on the next idle */
    int32_t   clockBase;      /* host time - game clock */
    int32_t   uiTickAt;       /* host time / 10 of the last UI tick (DS:027a, every 167 ms) */
    char      gameDir[512];
    char      iniPath[600];   /* izzy.ini: high scores (and options) */
} Game;

/* Load IZZY.DIR / IZZYCD1.DAT / IZZY.VET from `dir`, start a new game at @START. 0 on success. */
int  game_open(Game *g, const char *dir, unsigned seed);
void game_close(Game *g);
/* orig: 1008:5bfc game_Idle - one idle pass at clock `now` (60 Hz ticks). */
void game_idle(Game *g, int32_t now);
/* orig: 1010:1c8c take_JumpToTake (+ the synchronous part of 1010:3afe take_LoadTake). 1 if a take started. */
int  game_jump_to_take(Game *g, const char *name);
/* A mouse click at (x, y) (orig: WM_LBUTTONUP -> UI vtable +0x3c). Returns the ui_click result. */
int  game_click(Game *g, int x, int y);
void game_mouse_move(Game *g, int x, int y);
/* A typed character (orig: WM_CHAR -> UI vtable +0xdc). */
void game_char(Game *g, int ch);
/* Pull rames frames of 22050 Hz 16-bit stereo sound. */
void game_audio_mix(Game *g, int16_t *out, int frames);
/* Save / load (orig: MAINWNDPROC Save / Open: vet_SaveGame + UI +0xcc 1008:7290; vet_LoadState + UI +0xd4 1008:6e14 +
 * UI +0xd0 1008:7334). The file is the VM block (savegame.h) followed by the UI block. 1 on success. */
int  game_save(Game *g, FILE *f);
int  game_load(Game *g, FILE *f);
/* The host's AVI ended (orig: MM_MCINOTIFY -> 1008:7ea6). */
void game_avi_finished(Game *g);

/* orig: 1008:9358 ui_MidiOpen (name, MIDI_CMD_PLAY) / 1008:8e34 ui_MidiCommand (name NULL) */
void game_midi(Game *g, const char *name, int cmd);
/* orig: 1008:5e14 game_Pause / 1008:5dac timer_Resume - the clock stops, MIDI pauses (4) and resumes (5) */
void game_pause(Game *g);
void game_resume(Game *g);
/* orig: MAINWNDPROC "New Game" after the prompts - vet_Restart, UI +0xd4, and the controls cleared */
void game_new_game(Game *g);
/* Not in the original: fixes to IZZY.VET's logic (applied by game_open; see game.c). Returns the number applied. */
int  game_fix_script(Game *g);

#endif
```

`src/indeo3.c`:

```c
/*
 * indeo3.c - the Indeo Video 3.2 decoder (see indeo3.h).
 */
#include "indeo3.h"
#include "third_party/indeo3data.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

enum { H_SPLIT = 0, V_SPLIT = 1, INTRA_NULL = 2, INTER_DATA = 3 };
enum { BS_8BIT_PEL = 1 << 1, BS_KEYFRAME = 1 << 2, BS_MV_Y_HALF = 1 << 4, BS_MV_X_HALF = 1 << 5, BS_NONREF = 1 << 8, BS_BUFFER = 9 };
enum { ERR_NONE = 0, ERR_BAD_DATA = -1, ERR_OUT_OF_DATA = -2, ERR_UNSUPPORTED = -3 };
#define CELL_STACK_MAX 20

typedef struct Cell { int xpos, ypos, width, height, tree; const int8_t *mv; } Cell;   /* positions/sizes in 4-pixel units */

static uint8_t requant_tab[8][128];

static void build_requant_tab(void)
{   /* the prediction is re-quantised when a cell uses a coarser codebook than the one that made its reference */
    static const int8_t offsets[8] = { 1, 1, 2, -3, -3, 3, 4, 4 };
    static const int8_t deltas[8]  = { 0, 1, 0,  4,  4, 1, 0, 1 };
    int i, j;
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        int step = i + 2;
        for (j = 0; j < 128; j++) requant_tab[i][j] = (uint8_t)((j + offsets[i]) / step * step + deltas[i]);
    }
    requant_tab[0][127] = 126;                        /* keep the results 7-bit */
    requant_tab[1][119] = 118; requant_tab[1][120] = 118;
    requant_tab[2][126] = 124; requant_tab[2][127] = 124;
    requant_tab[6][124] = 120; requant_tab[6][125] = 120; requant_tab[6][126] = 120; requant_tab[6][127] = 120;
    requant_tab[1][7] = 10;                           /* as Intel's binary decoders do */
    requant_tab[4][8] = 10;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers */

static uint16_t rd16(const uint8_t *p) { return (uint16_t)(p[0] | (p[1] << 8)); }
static uint32_t rd32(const uint8_t *p) { return (uint32_t)p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24); }
static uint64_t rd64(const uint8_t *p) { return (uint64_t)rd32(p) | ((uint64_t)rd32(p + 4) << 32); }
static void wr16(uint8_t *p, uint32_t v) { p[0] = (uint8_t)v; p[1] = (uint8_t)(v >> 8); }
static void wr32(uint8_t *p, uint32_t v) { p[0] = (uint8_t)v; p[1] = (uint8_t)(v >> 8); p[2] = (uint8_t)(v >> 16); p[3] = (uint8_t)(v >> 24); }
static void wr64(uint8_t *p, uint64_t v) { wr32(p, (uint32_t)v); wr32(p + 4, (uint32_t)(v >> 32)); }

/* bytes are 7-bit, so packed adds never carry between them */
static void avg32(uint8_t *dst, const uint8_t *a, const uint8_t *b) { wr32(dst, ((rd32(a) + rd32(b)) >> 1) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu); }
static void avg64(uint8_t *dst, const uint8_t *a, const uint8_t *b) { wr64(dst, ((rd64(a) + rd64(b)) >> 1) & 0x7F7F7F7F7F7F7F7Full); }
static uint64_t replicate64(uint64_t a) { a &= 0x00FF00FF00FF00FFull; return a | (a << 8); }   /* b0 b0 b2 b2 ... */
static uint32_t replicate32(uint32_t a) { a &= 0x00FF00FFu; return a | (a << 8); }
static void fill64(uint8_t *dst, uint64_t pix, int n, ptrdiff_t stride) { for (; n > 0; n--, dst += stride) wr64(dst, pix); }
static void copy_block4(uint8_t *dst, const uint8_t *src, ptrdiff_t stride, int h) { for (; h > 0; h--, dst += stride, src += stride) memcpy(dst, src, 4); }

static int get_bits(Indeo3 *d, int n)
{   /* MSB first; past the end of the plane's data it reads zeros (a resync can jump beyond it in a damaged file) */
    int v = 0;
    while (n--) {
        int bit = d->bitPos < d->bitEnd ? (d->bits[d->bitPos >> 3] >> (7 - (d->bitPos & 7))) & 1 : 0;
        v = (v << 1) | bit;
        d->bitPos++;
    }
    return v;
}

/* cell data bytes sit in the stream right after the byte holding the current code; the code reader jumps
 * over them once it reaches the next byte boundary */
#define UPDATE_BITPOS(n) do { d->skipBits += (n); d->needResync = 1; } while (0)
#define RESYNC_BITSTREAM do { if (d->needResync && !(d->bitPos & 7)) { d->bitPos += d->skipBits; d->skipBits = 0; d->needResync = 0; } } while (0)

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ cells */

static int mv_ok(const Iv3Plane *p, const Cell *c, int mvx, int mvy)
{   /* -1: the extra line above the picture may be used */
    return !((c->ypos << 2) + mvy < -1 || (c->xpos << 2) + mvx < 0 ||
             ((c->ypos + c->height) << 2) + mvy > p->height || ((c->xpos + c->width) << 2) + mvx > p->width);
}

static int copy_cell(Indeo3 *d, Iv3Plane *p, const Cell *c)
{   /* the cell from the other buffer, moved by its motion vector */
    int mvy = c->mv ? c->mv[0] : 0, mvx = c->mv ? c->mv[1] : 0, y, h = c->height << 2, w = c->width << 2;
    ptrdiff_t off = (ptrdiff_t)(c->ypos << 2) * p->pitch + (c->xpos << 2);
    uint8_t *dst = p->pixels[d->bufSel] + off;
    const uint8_t *src;
    if (!mv_ok(p, c, mvx, mvy)) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
    src = p->pixels[d->bufSel ^ 1] + off + (ptrdiff_t)mvy * p->pitch + mvx;
    for (y = 0; y < h; y++, dst += p->pitch, src += p->pitch) memmove(dst, src, (size_t)w);
    return 0;
}

static int decode_cell_data(Indeo3 *d, const Cell *cell, uint8_t *block, uint8_t *ref_block, ptrdiff_t row_offset,
                            int h_zoom, int v_zoom, int mode, const vqEntry *delta[2], const int swap_quads[2],
                            const uint8_t **data_ptr, const uint8_t *last_ptr)
{
    int x, y, line, num_lines, rle_blocks = 0, skip_flag = 0, is_top_of_cell, is_first_row = 1;
    unsigned code, dyad1, dyad2;
    uint8_t *dst, *ref;
    const vqEntry *delta_tab;
    uint64_t pix64;
    ptrdiff_t blk_row_offset = (row_offset << (2 + v_zoom)) - (cell->width << 2), line_offset = v_zoom ? row_offset : 0;
    (void)d;
    if ((cell->height & v_zoom) || (cell->width & h_zoom)) return ERR_BAD_DATA;

    for (y = 0; y < cell->height; is_first_row = 0, y += 1 + v_zoom) {
        for (x = 0; x < cell->width; x += 1 + h_zoom) {
            ref = ref_block;
            dst = block;
            if (rle_blocks > 0) {                         /* a run of blocks copied from the reference */
                if (mode <= 4) {
                    if (cell->mv || !skip_flag) copy_block4(dst, ref, row_offset, 4 << v_zoom);
                } else if (mode == 10 && !cell->mv) {
                    pix64 = rd64(ref);
                    if (is_first_row) {                   /* the top line of a cell: replicate and interpolate */
                        pix64 = replicate64(pix64);
                        fill64(dst + row_offset, pix64, 7, row_offset);
                        avg64(dst, ref, dst + row_offset);
                    } else fill64(dst, pix64, 8, row_offset);
                }
                rle_blocks--;
            } else {
                for (line = 0; line < 4;) {
                    num_lines = 1;
                    is_top_of_cell = is_first_row && !line;
                    delta_tab = mode <= 4 ? delta[line & 1] : delta[1];   /* modes 1/4 alternate the two codebooks */
                    if (*data_ptr >= last_ptr) return ERR_OUT_OF_DATA;
                    code = *(*data_ptr)++;
                    if (code < 248) {
                        if (code < delta_tab->num_dyads) {     /* two dyads */
                            if (*data_ptr >= last_ptr) return ERR_OUT_OF_DATA;
                            dyad1 = *(*data_ptr)++;
                            dyad2 = code;
                            if (dyad1 >= delta_tab->num_dyads || dyad1 >= 248) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                        } else {                                /* a quad: two dyads in one byte */
                            code -= delta_tab->num_dyads;
                            dyad1 = code / delta_tab->quad_exp;
                            dyad2 = code % delta_tab->quad_exp;
                            if (swap_quads[line & 1]) { unsigned t = dyad1; dyad1 = dyad2; dyad2 = t; }
                        }
                        if (mode <= 4) {                        /* 4x4 (and 4x8: the odd lines interpolated) */
                            wr16(dst + line_offset,     (rd16(ref)     + (uint16_t)delta_tab->deltas[dyad1]) & 0x7F7F);
                            wr16(dst + line_offset + 2, (rd16(ref + 2) + (uint16_t)delta_tab->deltas[dyad2]) & 0x7F7F);
                            if (mode >= 3) {
                                if (is_top_of_cell && !cell->ypos) memcpy(dst, dst + row_offset, 4);
                                else avg32(dst, ref, dst + row_offset);
                            }
                        } else if (mode == 10 && !cell->mv) {   /* 8x8 intra: even lines coded, odd ones interpolated */
                            if (is_top_of_cell) {
                                wr32(dst + row_offset,     (replicate32(rd32(ref))     + (uint32_t)delta_tab->deltas_m10[dyad1]) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu);
                                wr32(dst + row_offset + 4, (replicate32(rd32(ref + 4)) + (uint32_t)delta_tab->deltas_m10[dyad2]) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu);
                            } else {
                                wr32(dst + row_offset,     (rd32(ref)     + (uint32_t)delta_tab->deltas_m10[dyad1]) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu);
                                wr32(dst + row_offset + 4, (rd32(ref + 4) + (uint32_t)delta_tab->deltas_m10[dyad2]) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu);
                            }
                            if (is_top_of_cell && !cell->ypos) memcpy(dst, dst + row_offset, 8);
                            else avg64(dst, ref, dst + row_offset);
                        } else if (mode == 10) {                /* 8x8 inter: two lines per delta, pixels doubled */
                            wr32(dst,                  (rd32(dst)                  + (uint32_t)delta_tab->deltas_m10[dyad1]) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu);
                            wr32(dst + 4,              (rd32(dst + 4)              + (uint32_t)delta_tab->deltas_m10[dyad2]) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu);
                            wr32(dst + row_offset,     (rd32(dst + row_offset)     + (uint32_t)delta_tab->deltas_m10[dyad1]) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu);
                            wr32(dst + row_offset + 4, (rd32(dst + row_offset + 4) + (uint32_t)delta_tab->deltas_m10[dyad2]) & 0x7F7F7F7Fu);
                        } else {                                /* mode 11: 4x8 inter */
                            wr16(dst,                  (rd16(dst)                  + (uint16_t)delta_tab->deltas[dyad1]) & 0x7F7F);
                            wr16(dst + 2,              (rd16(dst + 2)              + (uint16_t)delta_tab->deltas[dyad2]) & 0x7F7F);
                            wr16(dst + row_offset,     (rd16(dst + row_offset)     + (uint16_t)delta_tab->deltas[dyad1]) & 0x7F7F);
                            wr16(dst + row_offset + 2, (rd16(dst + row_offset + 2) + (uint16_t)delta_tab->deltas[dyad2]) & 0x7F7F);
                        }
                    } else {                                    /* RLE escapes */
                        switch (code) {
                        case 0xFC:                              /* copy the rest of this block and the next one */
                            skip_flag = 0; rle_blocks = 1; code = 253;
                            /* fall through */
                        case 0xFF: case 0xFE: case 0xFD:        /* copy 2 / 3 / 4 - line lines */
                            num_lines = 257 - (int)code - line;
                            if (num_lines <= 0) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                            if (mode <= 4) copy_block4(dst, ref, row_offset, num_lines << v_zoom);
                            else if (mode == 10 && !cell->mv) {
                                pix64 = rd64(ref);
                                if (is_top_of_cell) {
                                    pix64 = replicate64(pix64);
                                    fill64(dst + row_offset, pix64, (num_lines << 1) - 1, row_offset);
                                    avg64(dst, ref, dst + row_offset);
                                } else fill64(dst, pix64, num_lines << 1, row_offset);
                            }
                            break;
                        case 0xFB:                              /* a counted run of copied or skipped blocks */
                            if (*data_ptr >= last_ptr) return ERR_OUT_OF_DATA;
                            code = *(*data_ptr)++;
                            rle_blocks = (int)(code & 0x1F) - 1;
                            if (code >= 64 || rle_blocks < 0) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                            skip_flag = code & 0x20;
                            num_lines = 4 - line;
                            if (mode >= 10 || (cell->mv || !skip_flag)) {
                                if (mode <= 4) copy_block4(dst, ref, row_offset, num_lines << v_zoom);
                                else if (mode == 10 && !cell->mv) {
                                    pix64 = rd64(ref);
                                    if (is_top_of_cell) {
                                        pix64 = replicate64(pix64);
                                        fill64(dst + row_offset, pix64, (num_lines << 1) - 1, row_offset);
                                        avg64(dst, ref, dst + row_offset);
                                    } else fill64(dst, pix64, num_lines << 1, row_offset);
                                }
                            }
                            break;
                        case 0xF9:                              /* skip this block and the next */
                            skip_flag = 1; rle_blocks = 1;
                            /* fall through */
                        case 0xFA:                              /* skip this block */
                            if (line) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                            num_lines = 4;
                            if (cell->mv && mode <= 4) copy_block4(dst, ref, row_offset, num_lines << v_zoom);
                            break;
                        default:
                            return ERR_UNSUPPORTED;
                        }
                    }
                    line += num_lines;
                    ref += row_offset * (num_lines << v_zoom);
                    dst += row_offset * (num_lines << v_zoom);
                }
            }
            block += 4 << h_zoom;
            ref_block += 4 << h_zoom;
        }
        ref_block += blk_row_offset;
        block += blk_row_offset;
    }
    return ERR_NONE;
}

static int decode_cell(Indeo3 *d, Iv3Plane *p, Cell *cell, const uint8_t *data_ptr, const uint8_t *last_ptr)
{   /* one VQ cell: descriptor byte (mode, codebook), then the coded blocks. Returns the bytes used. */
    const uint8_t *start = data_ptr;
    unsigned code;
    int mode, vq_index, prim, second, x, err, swap_quads[2];
    ptrdiff_t off;
    uint8_t *block, *ref_block = NULL;
    const vqEntry *delta[2];
    if (data_ptr >= last_ptr) return ERR_OUT_OF_DATA;
    code = *data_ptr++;
    mode = (int)(code >> 4);
    vq_index = (int)(code & 0xF);
    off = (ptrdiff_t)(cell->ypos << 2) * p->pitch + (cell->xpos << 2);
    block = p->pixels[d->bufSel] + off;

    if (!cell->mv) ref_block = block - p->pitch;                /* intra: predict from the line above */
    else if (mode >= 10) {                                      /* inter 10/11: start from the moved cell */
        if (copy_cell(d, p, cell) < 0) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
    } else {                                                    /* inter 0-4: the moved reference */
        int mvy = cell->mv[0], mvx = cell->mv[1];
        if (!mv_ok(p, cell, mvx, mvy)) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
        ref_block = p->pixels[d->bufSel ^ 1] + off + (ptrdiff_t)mvy * p->pitch + mvx;
    }

    if (mode == 1 || mode == 4) {                               /* two codebooks from the alternative table */
        code = d->altQuant[vq_index];
        prim = (int)(code >> 4) + d->cbOffset;
        second = (int)(code & 0xF) + d->cbOffset;
    } else {
        vq_index += d->cbOffset;
        prim = second = vq_index;
    }
    if (prim >= 24 || second >= 24) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
    delta[0] = &vq_tab[second];
    delta[1] = &vq_tab[prim];
    swap_quads[0] = second >= 16;
    swap_quads[1] = prim >= 16;

    if (vq_index >= 8 && ref_block)                             /* requantise the prediction line */
        for (x = 0; x < cell->width << 2; x++) ref_block[x] = requant_tab[vq_index & 7][ref_block[x] & 127];

    switch (mode) {
    case 0: case 1: case 3: case 4:
        if (mode >= 3 && cell->mv) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
        err = decode_cell_data(d, cell, block, ref_block, p->pitch, 0, mode >= 3, mode, delta, swap_quads, &data_ptr, last_ptr);
        break;
    case 10: case 11:
        if (mode == 10 && !cell->mv)
            err = decode_cell_data(d, cell, block, ref_block, p->pitch, 1, 1, mode, delta, swap_quads, &data_ptr, last_ptr);
        else {
            if (mode == 11 && !cell->mv) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
            err = decode_cell_data(d, cell, block, block, p->pitch, mode == 10, 1, mode, delta, swap_quads, &data_ptr, last_ptr);
        }
        break;
    default:
        return ERR_UNSUPPORTED;
    }
    if (err) return err;
    return (int)(data_ptr - start);
}

static int cell_fits(const Iv3Plane *p, const Cell *c)
{
    return c->xpos + c->width <= (p->width >> 2) && c->ypos + c->height <= (p->height >> 2);
}

#define SPLIT_CELL(size) ((size) > 2 ? ((((size) + 2) >> 2) << 1) : 1)

static int parse_bintree(Indeo3 *d, Iv3Plane *p, int code, Cell *ref_cell, int depth, int strip_width)
{   /* the motion-compensation tree, then the VQ tree: split, intra/null, inter/data */
    Cell cur;
    int used;
    if (depth <= 0) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
    cur = *ref_cell;
    if (code == H_SPLIT) {
        cur.height = SPLIT_CELL(ref_cell->height);
        ref_cell->ypos += cur.height;
        ref_cell->height -= cur.height;
        if (ref_cell->height <= 0 || cur.height <= 0) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
    } else if (code == V_SPLIT) {
        if (cur.width > strip_width) cur.width = (cur.width <= (strip_width << 1) ? 1 : 2) * strip_width;   /* strips first */
        else cur.width = SPLIT_CELL(ref_cell->width);
        ref_cell->xpos += cur.width;
        ref_cell->width -= cur.width;
        if (ref_cell->width <= 0 || cur.width <= 0) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
    }

    while (d->bitEnd - d->bitPos >= 2) {
        RESYNC_BITSTREAM;
        if (d->bitEnd - d->bitPos < 2) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
        switch (code = get_bits(d, 2)) {
        case H_SPLIT:
        case V_SPLIT:
            if (parse_bintree(d, p, code, &cur, depth - 1, strip_width)) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
            break;
        case INTRA_NULL:
            if (!cur.tree) {                                    /* MC tree: intra, enter the VQ tree */
                cur.mv = NULL;
                cur.tree = 1;
            } else {                                            /* VQ tree: null cell = copy (code 1 "skip" alike) */
                RESYNC_BITSTREAM;
                code = get_bits(d, 2);
                if (code >= 2) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                if (!cell_fits(p, &cur)) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                return copy_cell(d, p, &cur);
            }
            break;
        case INTER_DATA:
            if (!cur.tree) {                                    /* MC tree: a motion vector index byte */
                unsigned idx;
                if (!d->needResync) d->nextCellData = d->bits + ((d->bitPos + 7) >> 3);
                if (d->nextCellData >= d->lastByte) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                idx = *d->nextCellData++;
                if ((int)idx >= d->numVectors) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                cur.mv = d->mcVectors + idx * 2;
                cur.tree = 1;
                UPDATE_BITPOS(8);
            } else {                                            /* VQ tree: the cell's data */
                if (!d->needResync) d->nextCellData = d->bits + ((d->bitPos + 7) >> 3);
                if (!cell_fits(p, &cur)) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                used = decode_cell(d, p, &cur, d->nextCellData, d->lastByte);
                if (used < 0) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
                UPDATE_BITPOS(used << 3);
                d->nextCellData += used;
                return 0;
            }
            break;
        }
    }
    return ERR_BAD_DATA;
}

static int decode_plane(Indeo3 *d, Iv3Plane *p, const uint8_t *data, int32_t size, int strip_width)
{   /* u32 motion vector count, the vectors (int8 y, x), then the tree bitstream */
    Cell cell;
    uint32_t n;
    if (size < 4) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
    n = rd32(data); data += 4; size -= 4;
    if (n > 256 || (int32_t)(n * 2) > size) return ERR_BAD_DATA;
    d->numVectors = (int)n;
    d->mcVectors = n ? (const int8_t *)data : NULL;
    d->bits = data + n * 2;
    d->bitPos = 0;
    d->bitEnd = (size - (int32_t)n * 2) * 8;
    d->skipBits = 0;
    d->needResync = 0;
    d->lastByte = data + size;
    cell.xpos = cell.ypos = 0;
    cell.width = p->width >> 2;
    cell.height = p->height >> 2;
    cell.tree = 0;
    cell.mv = NULL;
    return parse_bintree(d, p, INTRA_NULL, &cell, CELL_STACK_MAX, strip_width);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ frames */

int iv3_init(Indeo3 *d, int width, int height)
{
    static int tabs;
    /* luma: the picture size (even); chroma: a quarter of it, rounded up to 4; rows padded to 16 bytes */
    int lw = (width + 1) & ~1, lh = (height + 1) & ~1, cw = ((lw >> 2) + 3) & ~3, ch = ((lh >> 2) + 3) & ~3, k, b;
    if (!tabs) { build_requant_tab(); tabs = 1; }
    memset(d, 0, sizeof *d);
    if (width <= 0 || height <= 0 || width > 4096 || height > 4096) return -1;
    d->width = width; d->height = height;
    for (k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        Iv3Plane *p = &d->planes[k];
        p->width = k ? cw : lw;
        p->height = k ? ch : lh;
        p->pitch = (p->width + 15) & ~15;
        for (b = 0; b < 2; b++) {
            p->buffers[b] = (uint8_t *)calloc((size_t)p->pitch * (size_t)(p->height + 1), 1);
            if (!p->buffers[b]) { iv3_free(d); return -1; }
            memset(p->buffers[b], 0x40, (size_t)p->pitch);    /* the intra prediction line: mid grey */
            p->pixels[b] = p->buffers[b] + p->pitch;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

void iv3_free(Indeo3 *d)
{
    int k;
    for (k = 0; k < 3; k++) { free(d->planes[k].buffers[0]); free(d->planes[k].buffers[1]); }
    memset(d, 0, sizeof *d);
}

int iv3_decode(Indeo3 *d, const uint8_t *buf, size_t len)
{
    uint32_t frame_num, word2, check, osize, dsize, starts[3], ends[3];
    const uint8_t *bs;
    int i, j, w, h;
    if (len < 16 + 48) { if (len >= 16) return 0; d->errors++; return -1; }
    frame_num = rd32(buf); word2 = rd32(buf + 4); check = rd32(buf + 8); osize = rd32(buf + 12);
    if ((frame_num ^ word2 ^ osize ^ 0x46524D48u /* "FRMH" */) != check) { d->errors++; return -1; }
    bs = buf + 16;
    if (rd16(bs) != 32) { d->errors++; return -1; }            /* codec version */
    d->frameFlags = rd16(bs + 2);
    dsize = (rd32(bs + 4) + 7) >> 3;
    d->cbOffset = bs[8];
    if (dsize == 16) return 0;                                 /* a sync (null) frame */
    if (dsize > len - 16) dsize = (uint32_t)(len - 16);
    h = rd16(bs + 12); w = rd16(bs + 14);
    if (w != d->width || h != d->height) { d->errors++; return -1; }
    starts[0] = rd32(bs + 16); starts[1] = rd32(bs + 20); starts[2] = rd32(bs + 24);   /* Y, V, U */
    for (j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
        ends[j] = dsize;
        for (i = 2; i >= 0; i--) if (starts[i] < ends[j] && starts[i] > starts[j]) ends[j] = starts[i];
    }
    for (j = 0; j < 3; j++)
        if (starts[j] < 48 || starts[j] >= dsize || ends[j] <= starts[j]) { d->errors++; return -1; }
    d->altQuant = bs + 32;
    if (d->frameFlags & (BS_8BIT_PEL | BS_MV_X_HALF | BS_MV_Y_HALF)) { d->errors++; return -1; }
    d->bufSel = (d->frameFlags >> BS_BUFFER) & 1;
    if (decode_plane(d, &d->planes[0], bs + starts[0], (int32_t)(ends[0] - starts[0]), 40) ||
        decode_plane(d, &d->planes[1], bs + starts[2], (int32_t)(ends[2] - starts[2]), 10) ||   /* U */
        decode_plane(d, &d->planes[2], bs + starts[1], (int32_t)(ends[1] - starts[1]), 10)) {   /* V */
        d->errors++;
        return -1;
    }
    d->frames++;
    return 1;
}

static uint8_t clip8(int v) { return (uint8_t)(v < 0 ? 0 : v > 255 ? 255 : v); }

void iv3_to_bgr(const Indeo3 *d, uint8_t *bgr, int stride)
{   /* samples are 7-bit (x2 for 8-bit); BT.601 studio range */
    const Iv3Plane *py = &d->planes[0], *pu = &d->planes[1], *pv = &d->planes[2];
    int x, y;
    for (y = 0; y < d->height; y++) {
        const uint8_t *ys = py->pixels[d->bufSel] + (ptrdiff_t)y * py->pitch;
        const uint8_t *us = pu->pixels[d->bufSel] + (ptrdiff_t)(y >> 2) * pu->pitch;
        const uint8_t *vs = pv->pixels[d->bufSel] + (ptrdiff_t)(y >> 2) * pv->pitch;
        uint8_t *o = bgr + (ptrdiff_t)y * stride;
        for (x = 0; x < d->width; x++) {
            int Y = ((ys[x] & 0x7F) << 1) - 16, U = ((us[x >> 2] & 0x7F) << 1) - 128, V = ((vs[x >> 2] & 0x7F) << 1) - 128;
            int c = 298 * Y + 128;
            o[3 * x + 2] = clip8((c + 409 * V) >> 8);
            o[3 * x + 1] = clip8((c - 100 * U - 208 * V) >> 8);
            o[3 * x + 0] = clip8((c + 516 * U) >> 8);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/indeo3.h`:

```h
/*
 * indeo3.h - an Indeo Video 3.2 ('IV32') decoder for the game's 40 AVI files.
 *
 * The original game played them through MCI and Intel's codec; modern Windows no longer has a working one, so the
 * port decodes them itself. The bitstream (frame header, binary-tree cells, motion vectors, VQ deltas with RLE
 * escapes, modes 0/1/3/4/10/11) is decoded as Intel's codec does; the 24 VQ codebooks come from FFmpeg
 * (third_party/indeo3data.h, LGPL 2.1).
 *
 * Pictures are YUV 4:1:0 with 7-bit samples (two frame buffers, one predicting the other); iv3_to_bgr converts
 * the last decoded picture to 24-bit BGR.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_INDEO3_H
#define IZZY_INDEO3_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

typedef struct Iv3Plane {
    uint8_t *buffers[2];      /* each with one extra line on top (the INTRA prediction line, 0x40) */
    uint8_t *pixels[2];
    int      width, height, pitch;
} Iv3Plane;

typedef struct Indeo3 {
    int      width, height;   /* the picture */
    Iv3Plane planes[3];       /* Y, U, V */
    int      bufSel;          /* the buffer the last frame went to */
    int      frames, errors;
    /* per frame */
    uint16_t frameFlags;
    int      cbOffset;
    const uint8_t *altQuant;
    /* per plane bit reader */
    const uint8_t *bits; int bitPos, bitEnd;
    int      skipBits, needResync;
    const uint8_t *nextCellData, *lastByte;
    const int8_t  *mcVectors; int numVectors;
} Indeo3;

int  iv3_init(Indeo3 *d, int width, int height);   /* 0 on success */
void iv3_free(Indeo3 *d);
/* one compressed frame: 1 = a new picture, 0 = nothing to show (a null/sync frame), -1 = bad data */
int  iv3_decode(Indeo3 *d, const uint8_t *buf, size_t len);
/* the current picture as 24-bit BGR rows (BT.601), top-down */
void iv3_to_bgr(const Indeo3 *d, uint8_t *bgr, int stride);

#endif

```

`src/izzy_common.c`:

```c
#include "izzy_common.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

int izzy_stricmp(const char *a, const char *b)
{
    /* orig: 1000:1252 _fstricmp folds 'A'..'Z' to lower case before comparing */
    for (;;) {
        int ca = (unsigned char)*a++, cb = (unsigned char)*b++;
        if (ca >= 'A' && ca <= 'Z') ca += 0x20;
        if (cb >= 'A' && cb <= 'Z') cb += 0x20;
        if (ca != cb) return ca < cb ? -1 : 1;
        if (ca == 0) return 0;
    }
}

uint8_t *izzy_read_file(const char *path, size_t *len)
{
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "rb");
    uint8_t *buf;
    long n;
    if (!f) return NULL;
    if (fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END) != 0 || (n = ftell(f)) < 0 || fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET) != 0) { fclose(f); return NULL; }
    buf = (uint8_t *)malloc(n > 0 ? (size_t)n : 1);
    if (buf && fread(buf, 1, (size_t)n, f) != (size_t)n) { free(buf); buf = NULL; }
    fclose(f);
    if (buf && len) *len = (size_t)n;
    return buf;
}
```

`src/izzy_common.h`:

```h
/*
 * izzy_common.h - shared helpers for the Izzy's Quest for Olympic Gold (1995) reconstruction.
 *
 * Portable C99. The original is 16-bit Win16 code (MSVC 1.5x, far data), so:
 *   - every on-disk integer is little-endian and read through the rd_* helpers below;
 *   - the original "int" is 16 bits: int16_t is used wherever the original stored an int.
 * Comments of the form "orig: 1018:82aa anim_LoadCelPack" name the function in IZZY.EXE this code
 * reconstructs (see tools/decomp/names.csv and tools/decomp/out/IZZY/*.c).
 */
#ifndef IZZY_COMMON_H
#define IZZY_COMMON_H

#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

static inline uint16_t rd_u16(const uint8_t *p) { return (uint16_t)(p[0] | (p[1] << 8)); }
static inline int16_t  rd_i16(const uint8_t *p) { return (int16_t)rd_u16(p); }
static inline uint32_t rd_u32(const uint8_t *p) { return (uint32_t)p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24); }
static inline int32_t  rd_i32(const uint8_t *p) { return (int32_t)rd_u32(p); }

/* Case-insensitive ASCII compare, like MSVC _fstricmp (orig: 1000:1252): returns <0, 0, >0. */
int izzy_stricmp(const char *a, const char *b);

/* Read a whole file into a malloc'd buffer. Returns NULL on failure; *len receives the size. */
uint8_t *izzy_read_file(const char *path, size_t *len);

#endif

```

`src/render.c`:

```c
#include "render.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

int render_load_bmp(Screen *s, const uint8_t *b, size_t len)
{
    uint32_t off, hdr;
    int32_t w, h, used, x, y, stride;
    if (len < 54 || b[0] != 'B' || b[1] != 'M') return -1;
    off = rd_u32(b + 10); hdr = rd_u32(b + 14);
    w = rd_i32(b + 18); h = rd_i32(b + 22);
    if (rd_u16(b + 28) != 8 || w <= 0 || h == 0) return -2;
    used = (int32_t)rd_u32(b + 46); if (used == 0) used = 256;
    for (x = 0; x < 256; x++) {
        size_t p = 14 + hdr + 4 * (size_t)x;
        int ok = x < used && p + 2 < len;
        s->pal.b[x] = ok ? b[p] : 0; s->pal.g[x] = ok ? b[p + 1] : 0; s->pal.r[x] = ok ? b[p + 2] : 0;
    }
    stride = (w + 3) & ~3;
    memset(s->px, 0, sizeof s->px);
    for (y = 0; y < (h < 0 ? -h : h) && y < SCREEN_H; y++) {
        int32_t srcRow = h > 0 ? h - 1 - y : y;           /* BMPs are bottom-up unless the height is negative */
        const uint8_t *row = b + off + (size_t)srcRow * (size_t)stride;
        if (off + (size_t)(srcRow + 1) * (size_t)stride > len) break;
        for (x = 0; x < w && x < SCREEN_W; x++) s->px[y * SCREEN_W + x] = row[x];
    }
    return 0;
}

void render_build_shadow_tables(Screen *s) { render_build_shadow_tables_from(s, &s->pal); }

void render_build_shadow_tables_from(Screen *s, const RgbPal *pal)
{
    int c, k, j;
    const RgbPal *src = pal;
    RgbPal copy = *src;
    const RgbPal *p = &copy;
    for (k = 0; k < 10; k++) for (c = 0; c < 256; c++) s->shadow[k][c] = (uint8_t)c;
    for (c = 1; c < 0xF6; c++) {
        for (k = 0; k < 10; k++) {
            long r = (long)(11 - k) * p->r[c] / 12, g = (long)(11 - k) * p->g[c] / 12, b = (long)(11 - k) * p->b[c] / 12;
            long dr = r - p->r[c], dg = g - p->g[c], db = b - p->b[c];
            long best = 2 * dr * dr + 4 * dg * dg + db * db;   /* start with the colour itself */
            int bestIdx = c;
            for (j = 1; j < 0xF6; j++) {
                long er = r - p->r[j], eg = g - p->g[j], eb = b - p->b[j];
                long d = 2 * er * er + 4 * eg * eg + eb * eb;
                if (d < best) { best = d; bestIdx = j; }
            }
            s->shadow[k][c] = (uint8_t)bestIdx;
        }
    }
}

void render_draw_mmp1(Screen *s, const Cel *c, int32_t leftFx, int32_t topFx, int32_t scale, const ClipRect *clip)
{
    /* orig: 1018:cfa8 sprite_DrawMMP1. Positions are 22.10 fixed point; scaleTable[n] = (n+1)*scale. */
    const uint8_t *p = c->bits, *end = c->bits + c->bitsLen;
    int32_t yFx = topFx, rowsLeft = c->height;
    int y, x0;
    if (scale < 1) return;
    if (scale > 0xFFF) scale = 0xFFF;
    y = (int)(topFx >> 10);
    x0 = (int)(leftFx >> 10);
    if (x0 < clip->left) x0 = clip->left;
    if (y < clip->top) y = clip->top;
    if (clip->right <= x0 || clip->bottom <= y) return;

    while (rowsLeft-- > 0 && y < clip->bottom && p < end) {
        int nextY;
        yFx += scale;
        nextY = (int)(yFx >> 10);
        if (nextY <= y) {                                   /* row collapsed by downscaling: skip its tokens */
            while (p < end) { unsigned b = *p++; if (b == 0xFF) break; if (b < 0x80) p += b + 1; else p++; }
            continue;
        }
        {
            /* one source row covers dest rows [y0, nextY): one row when scale <= 1024; several when enlarging
             * (orig: 1018:c8ec, the > 1024 path, and 1018:d430, the 1:1 path, decode the same way) */
            int y0 = y, yy;
            int32_t xFx = leftFx;
            int xPrev = x0, literalLeft = 0;
            if (nextY > clip->bottom) nextY = clip->bottom;
            y = nextY;
            for (;;) {
                unsigned b, v, n = 0;
                int xEnd;
                if (p >= end) break;
                if (literalLeft == 0) {
                    b = *p++;
                    if (b == 0xFF) break;
                    if (b < 0x80) literalLeft = (int)b; else n = b - 0x7F;
                    if (p >= end) break;
                    v = *p++;
                } else { literalLeft--; v = *p++; }
                xFx += (int32_t)(n + 1) * scale;                 /* scaleTable[n] */
                xEnd = (int)(xFx >> 10);
                if (xEnd <= xPrev) continue;                     /* still left of the clip/previous pixel */
                if (xEnd > clip->right) xEnd = clip->right;
                if (v != 0) for (yy = y0; yy < nextY; yy++) {
                    uint8_t *row = s->px + (size_t)yy * SCREEN_W;
                    int x;
                    if (v < 0xF6) for (x = xPrev; x < xEnd; x++) row[x] = (uint8_t)v;
                    else for (x = xPrev; x < xEnd; x++) row[x] = s->shadow[v - 0xF6][row[x]];
                }
                xPrev = xEnd;
                if (xPrev >= clip->right) {                      /* skip the rest of the row */
                    if (literalLeft) { p += literalLeft; literalLeft = 0; }
                    while (p < end) { unsigned bb = *p++; if (bb == 0xFF) break; if (bb < 0x80) p += bb + 1; else p++; }
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

int render_save_bmp(const Screen *s, const char *path)
{
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "wb");
    uint8_t h[54];
    int i, y;
    uint32_t data = 54 + 1024, size = data + SCREEN_W * SCREEN_H;
    if (!f) return -1;
    memset(h, 0, sizeof h);
    h[0] = 'B'; h[1] = 'M';
    h[2] = (uint8_t)size; h[3] = (uint8_t)(size >> 8); h[4] = (uint8_t)(size >> 16); h[5] = (uint8_t)(size >> 24);
    h[10] = (uint8_t)data; h[11] = (uint8_t)(data >> 8);
    h[14] = 40; h[18] = SCREEN_W & 0xFF; h[19] = SCREEN_W >> 8; h[22] = SCREEN_H & 0xFF; h[23] = SCREEN_H >> 8;
    h[26] = 1; h[28] = 8;
    fwrite(h, 1, 54, f);
    for (i = 0; i < 256; i++) { uint8_t q[4] = { s->pal.b[i], s->pal.g[i], s->pal.r[i], 0 }; fwrite(q, 1, 4, f); }
    for (y = SCREEN_H - 1; y >= 0; y--) fwrite(s->px + (size_t)y * SCREEN_W, 1, SCREEN_W, f);
    fclose(f);
    return 0;
}

```

`src/render.h`:

```h
/*
 * render.h - the 8-bit software renderer (orig: the Stage object: 640x480 8-bit DIB, palette, shadow tables).
 */
#ifndef IZZY_RENDER_H
#define IZZY_RENDER_H

#include "cels.h"

#define SCREEN_W 640
#define SCREEN_H 480

typedef struct RgbPal { uint8_t r[256], g[256], b[256]; } RgbPal;

typedef struct Screen {
    uint8_t px[SCREEN_W * SCREEN_H];      /* top-down here; the original's DIB is bottom-up */
    RgbPal  pal;
    uint8_t shadow[10][256];              /* orig: Stage+0x1f1e effectRemap; pixel 0xF6+k uses shadow[k] */
} Screen;

typedef struct ClipRect { int left, top, right, bottom; } ClipRect;

/* Load an 8-bit BMP (background) into the screen and its palette. Returns 0 on success. */
int  render_load_bmp(Screen *s, const uint8_t *bmp, size_t len);

/* orig: 1018:9f3a stage_BuildShadowTables - for palette entries 1..245 and levels k = 0..9, the nearest entry
 * (1..245) to the colour scaled by (11-k)/12, distance 2*dR^2 + 4*dG^2 + dB^2. */
void render_build_shadow_tables(Screen *s);
/* Same, from another palette (the Stage builds them from the scene palette, not the faded one on screen). */
void render_build_shadow_tables_from(Screen *s, const RgbPal *pal);

/* orig: 1018:cfa8 sprite_DrawMMP1 - draw an MMP1 cel with its top-left at (leftFx, topFx) in 22.10 fixed point,
 * scaled by `scale` (1024 = 1:1, clamped to 0..0xFFF), clipped to `clip`. Pixel 0 is transparent;
 * 0xF6..0xFE darken the background through the shadow tables. */
void render_draw_mmp1(Screen *s, const Cel *c, int32_t leftFx, int32_t topFx, int32_t scale, const ClipRect *clip);

/* Write the screen as an 8-bit BMP file. Returns 0 on success. */
int  render_save_bmp(const Screen *s, const char *path);

#endif

```

`src/resource.c`:

```c
/*
 * resource.c - NE resources (see resource.h).
 */
#include "resource.h"
#include <stdlib.h>

int res_open(NeResources *r, const char *exePath)
{
    size_t ne;
    r->exe = izzy_read_file(exePath, &r->len);
    if (!r->exe || r->len < 0x40) return -1;
    ne = rd_u32(r->exe + 0x3c);
    if (ne + 0x40 > r->len || r->exe[ne] != 'N' || r->exe[ne + 1] != 'E') return -1;
    r->table = ne + (uint16_t)rd_i16(r->exe + ne + 0x24);
    r->shift = (uint16_t)rd_i16(r->exe + r->table);
    return 0;
}

void res_close(NeResources *r) { free(r->exe); r->exe = NULL; }

const uint8_t *res_find(const NeResources *r, int type, int id, size_t *len)
{
    size_t p;
    if (!r->exe) return NULL;
    p = r->table + 2;
    while (p + 8 <= r->len) {
        uint16_t t = (uint16_t)rd_i16(r->exe + p), n = (uint16_t)rd_i16(r->exe + p + 2), i;
        if (t == 0) break;
        p += 8;
        for (i = 0; i < n && p + 12 <= r->len; i++, p += 12) {
            uint16_t off = (uint16_t)rd_i16(r->exe + p), ln = (uint16_t)rd_i16(r->exe + p + 2);
            uint16_t rid = (uint16_t)rd_i16(r->exe + p + 6);
            if (t == (0x8000 | type) && rid == (0x8000 | id)) {
                size_t o = (size_t)off << r->shift, l = (size_t)ln << r->shift;
                if (o + l > r->len) return NULL;
                if (len) *len = l;
                return r->exe + o;
            }
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}
```
