`src/resource.h`:

```h
/*
 * resource.h - resources of the NE executable (the control-panel bitmaps, the fonts, the palette).
 * orig: 1008:b86c res_Lock (FindResource RT_BITMAP / LoadResource / LockResource), 1008:b8f8 res_Unlock.
 * NE layout: "NE" header at [0x3c]; resource table at NE+0x24: u16 alignShift, then types
 * { u16 typeId (0x8000|n), u16 count, u32 0, count x { u16 off, u16 len, u16 flags, u16 id, u32 0 } }, 0.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_RESOURCE_H
#define IZZY_RESOURCE_H

#include "izzy_common.h"

typedef struct NeResources { uint8_t *exe; size_t len; size_t table; int shift; } NeResources;

#define RT_BITMAP_ 2

int  res_open(NeResources *r, const char *exePath);     /* 0 on success */
void res_close(NeResources *r);
/* Integer-id resource of `type`; returns a pointer into the file (a packed DIB for bitmaps) or NULL. */
const uint8_t *res_find(const NeResources *r, int type, int id, size_t *len);

#endif
```

`src/savegame.c`:

```c
/*
 * savegame.c - saved games (see savegame.h for the format).
 */
#include "savegame.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static void w16(FILE *f, int v) { fputc(v & 0xff, f); fputc((v >> 8) & 0xff, f); }
static void wstr(FILE *f, const char *s) { if (s) fputs(s, f); fputc(0, f); }   /* orig: 1010:b6c2 (str_AppendChar = str_WriteToStream) */
static int  r16(FILE *f, int16_t *v) { int a = fgetc(f), b = fgetc(f); if (a == EOF || b == EOF) return 0; *v = (int16_t)(a | (b << 8)); return 1; }
static int  rstr(FILE *f, char *buf, size_t cap)
{   /* orig: 1010:b71e str_ReadFromStream - up to the NUL (or end of file) */
    size_t n = 0; int c;
    while ((c = fgetc(f)) != EOF && c != 0) if (n + 1 < cap) buf[n++] = (char)c;
    buf[n] = 0;
    return c != EOF || n > 0;
}
static void wcode(FILE *f, const VetCode *c)
{   /* orig: 1010:d908 code_WriteToStream - u16 length + bytes */
    w16(f, c->data ? c->len : 0);
    if (c->data && c->len > 0) fwrite(c->data, 1, (size_t)c->len, f);
}
static int skipcode(FILE *f) { int16_t n; if (!r16(f, &n)) return 0; if (n > 0) fseek(f, n, SEEK_CUR); return 1; }

static int find_exact(const VetVM *vm, const char *name)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++) if (vm->nodes[i].name && strcmp(vm->nodes[i].name, name) == 0) return i;
    return -1;
}

static void save_variables(const VetVM *vm, FILE *f)
{   /* orig: 1018:6c80 vet_SaveVariables */
    int i, n = 0;
    w16(f, vm->nodeCount);
    w16(f, vm->startNode);
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++) if (vm->nodes[i].value || vm->nodes[i].jitter || vm->nodes[i].arrCount) n++;
    w16(f, n);
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++) {
        const VetNode *p = &vm->nodes[i];
        int k;
        if (!(p->value || p->jitter || p->arrCount)) continue;
        wstr(f, p->name);
        w16(f, p->value); w16(f, p->jitter); w16(f, p->arrCount); w16(f, p->type);
        for (k = 0; k < p->arrCount; k++) w16(f, p->arr[k]);
    }
}

int vet_save_game(const VetVM *vm, const VetSaveExtras *x, const char names[SAVE_PLAYERS][32], FILE *f)
{   /* orig: 1018:69be vet_SaveGame */
    const VetNode *cur = (vm->currentNode >= 0 && vm->currentNode < vm->nodeCount) ? &vm->nodes[vm->currentNode] : NULL;
    static const VetCode none = { NULL, 0, 0 };
    int i;
    if (!f) return 0;
    fwrite(SAVE_MAGIC, 1, strlen(SAVE_MAGIC), f);
    w16(f, vm->version);
    for (i = 0; i < SAVE_PLAYERS; i++) {
        size_t n = strlen(names[i]);
        if (n > 31) n = 31;
        fputc((int)n, f);
        fwrite(names[i], 1, n, f);
    }
    w16(f, x->f1d2); w16(f, x->f1d4); w16(f, x->f1d6); w16(f, x->f1d8); w16(f, vm->pc);
    wstr(f, x->lastJump);
    w16(f, x->state1ea); w16(f, vm->setDone); w16(f, vm->depth);
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) { w16(f, vm->stackNode[i]); w16(f, vm->stackPos[i]); }
    wstr(f, cur ? cur->name : "");
    wstr(f, cur ? cur->desc : "");
    wcode(f, cur ? &cur->code1 : &none);
    wcode(f, cur ? &cur->code2 : &none);
    wstr(f, cur ? cur->s1 : "");
    wstr(f, x->str54);
    wstr(f, x->str60);
    wstr(f, cur ? cur->s2 : "");
    w16(f, x->nodeFlag2); w16(f, x->nodeFlag4); w16(f, vm->currentNode);
    save_variables(vm, f);
    return ferror(f) ? 0 : 1;
}

static int load_variables(VetVM *vm, FILE *f)
{   /* orig: 1018:7492 vet_LoadGame - variables matched by name (fast path: same index) */
    int16_t count, start, n, i;
    if (!r16(f, &count) || count == 0) return 0;     /* "no variables" */
    if (!r16(f, &start) || !r16(f, &n)) return 0;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        char name[256];
        int16_t v, j, ac, ty, k;
        int node;
        if (!rstr(f, name, sizeof name)) return 0;
        node = (i < vm->nodeCount && vm->nodes[i].name && strcmp(vm->nodes[i].name, name) == 0) ? i : find_exact(vm, name);
        if (!r16(f, &v) || !r16(f, &j) || !r16(f, &ac) || !r16(f, &ty)) return 0;
        if (node >= 0) {
            VetNode *p = &vm->nodes[node];
            p->value = v; p->jitter = j; p->arrCount = ac; p->type = ty;
            free(p->arr); p->arr = NULL;
            if (ac > 0) {
                p->arr = (int16_t *)calloc((size_t)ac, sizeof(int16_t));
                if (!p->arr) return 0;
                for (k = 0; k < ac; k++) if (!r16(f, &p->arr[k])) return 0;
            }
        } else {                                      /* unknown name: skip its array */
            for (k = 0; k < ac; k++) { int16_t dummy; if (!r16(f, &dummy)) return 0; }
        }
    }
    return 1;
}

int vet_load_game(VetVM *vm, VetSaveExtras *x, char names[SAVE_PLAYERS][32], FILE *f, int *showDescription)
{   /* orig: 1018:6e44 vet_LoadState */
    char magic[16], s[512];
    int16_t ver, depth, i, cur;
    int node, ok;
    *showDescription = 0;
    if (!f || fread(magic, 1, strlen(SAVE_MAGIC), f) != strlen(SAVE_MAGIC) || memcmp(magic, SAVE_MAGIC, strlen(SAVE_MAGIC)) != 0)
        return 0;                                     /* "Not a valid Save Game file." */
    if (!r16(f, &ver) || ver < 4) return 0;
    vm->version = (uint16_t)ver;
    for (i = 0; i < SAVE_PLAYERS; i++) {
        int len = fgetc(f);
        if (len == EOF || (len != 0x1f && len > 0x1e)) return 0;
        if (fread(names[i], 1, (size_t)len, f) != (size_t)len) return 0;
        names[i][len] = 0;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++) {             /* clear every variable first */
        vm->nodes[i].value = 0; vm->nodes[i].jitter = 0; vm->nodes[i].arrCount = 0;
        free(vm->nodes[i].arr); vm->nodes[i].arr = NULL;
    }
    r16(f, &x->f1d2); r16(f, &x->f1d4); r16(f, &x->f1d6); r16(f, &x->f1d8); r16(f, &vm->pc);
    rstr(f, x->lastJump, sizeof x->lastJump);
    r16(f, &x->state1ea); r16(f, &vm->setDone); r16(f, &depth);
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) { r16(f, &vm->stackNode[i]); r16(f, &vm->stackPos[i]); }
    rstr(f, s, sizeof s);                             /* curName */
    rstr(f, x->curDesc, sizeof x->curDesc);           /* the description shown after loading */
    skipcode(f); skipcode(f);                         /* code1 / code2 copies */
    rstr(f, s, sizeof s);                             /* curS1 */
    rstr(f, x->str54, sizeof x->str54);
    rstr(f, x->str60, sizeof x->str60);
    rstr(f, x->curS2, sizeof x->curS2);
    r16(f, &x->nodeFlag2); r16(f, &x->nodeFlag4); r16(f, &cur);
    ok = load_variables(vm, f);
    node = find_exact(vm, "MAIN_MENU");               /* resume at the main menu */
    if (node >= 0) {
        strncpy(x->lastJump, "MAIN_MENU", sizeof x->lastJump - 1);
        vet_enter_node(vm, node);
        vm->setDone = 0; vm->pc = 0; vm->running = 1;
    }
    vm->depth = 0;
    /* the original then plays curS2 / shows curDesc through UI callbacks (VetVM +0x9a / +0xa2 / +0xaa) and
     * marks a description pending (busyFlags |= 1) unless the sound played and the node has no flag 2.
     * Without the callbacks the sound is "not played". */
    if ((x->nodeFlag4 || x->nodeFlag2) && (x->curDesc[0] || x->nodeFlag2)) { *showDescription = 1; vm->busyFlags |= 1; }
    return ok;
}
```

`src/savegame.h`:

```h
/*
 * savegame.h - saved games (orig: 1018:69be vet_SaveGame, 1018:6c80 vet_SaveVariables, 1018:6e44 vet_LoadState,
 * 1018:7492 vet_LoadGame). All integers little-endian 16-bit; strings NUL-terminated.
 *
 *   "Izzy_Adv_24:"                        12 bytes, no terminator
 *   u16 version                           the VET version (load rejects < 4)
 *   5 x { u8 len (< 32), len chars }      player names (DS:1ff6, 32 bytes each; lengths at DS:2096)
 *   u16 +0x1d2 +0x1d4 +0x1d6 +0x1d8 pc    VM registers
 *   str +0x1de                            last node jumped to
 *   u16 +0x1ea setDone gosubDepth
 *   16 x { u16 gosubNode[i], u16 gosubPos[i] }
 *   str curName, curDesc                  copies of the current node (vet_EnterNode)
 *   code code1, code2                     u16 length + bytes
 *   str curS1, +0x54, +0x60, curS2
 *   u16 nodeFlag2 nodeFlag4 currentNode
 *   u16 nodeCount, startNode, n           then n x { str name, u16 value, jitter, arrCount, type, arrCount x i16 }
 *                                         for every node whose value, jitter or array count is non-zero
 * Loading restores the variables, then enters MAIN_MENU (the saved position itself is not resumed).
 */
#ifndef IZZY_SAVEGAME_H
#define IZZY_SAVEGAME_H

#include <stdio.h>
#include "vet.h"

#define SAVE_MAGIC "Izzy_Adv_24:"
#define SAVE_PLAYERS 5

/* VM state the port does not otherwise model; kept so a save round-trips byte for byte. */
typedef struct VetSaveExtras {
    int16_t f1d2, f1d4, f1d6, f1d8;   /* VetVM +0x1d2..+0x1d8 */
    char    lastJump[64];             /* +0x1de */
    int16_t state1ea;                 /* +0x1ea */
    char    str54[256], str60[256];   /* +0x54, +0x60 */
    int16_t nodeFlag2, nodeFlag4;     /* +0x78 / +0x7a: the current node's flags & 2 / & 4 */
    char    curDesc[512], curS2[256]; /* loaded: the saved node's description and sound name, shown on load */
} VetSaveExtras;

/* Write a save. names: the 5 player names. Returns 1 on success. */
int  vet_save_game(const VetVM *vm, const VetSaveExtras *x, const char names[SAVE_PLAYERS][32], FILE *f);
/* Read a save into a loaded VM. Returns 1 on success, 0 if the file is not a save (or too old).
 * *showDescription is set when the original would open the description box (busyFlags |= 1). */
int  vet_load_game(VetVM *vm, VetSaveExtras *x, char names[SAVE_PLAYERS][32], FILE *f, int *showDescription);

#endif

```

`src/scene.c`:

```c
/*
 * scene.c - TAKE statements -> Stage (see scene.h). scene_exec follows take_ExecuteStatement (1010:12e4)
 * case by case on the argument block the Load*Cmnd readers fill (take_decode).
 */
#include "scene.h"
#include "vep.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static const SceneFile *scene_file(Scene *sc, const char *name)
{   /* orig: 1010:6ef0 audio_LoadTrack (asset manager) - load once per scene, keep for its lifetime */
    int i, g;
    SceneFile *files = sc->cache ? sc->cache->files : sc->files;
    int *nfiles = sc->cache ? &sc->cache->nfiles : &sc->nfiles;
    int cap = sc->cache ? (int)(sizeof sc->cache->files / sizeof sc->cache->files[0]) : SCENE_MAX_FILES;
    if (!name || !*name) return NULL;
    for (i = 0; i < *nfiles; i++) if (izzy_stricmp(files[i].name, name) == 0) return &files[i];
    if (*nfiles >= cap) return NULL;
    for (g = 0; g < sc->ngroups; g++) {
        const DatFileEntry *e = dat_group_find(&sc->groups[g], name);
        if (e) {
            SceneFile *f = &files[*nfiles];
            f->data = dat_read(sc->dat, &sc->groups[g], e);
            if (!f->data) return NULL;
            f->len = e->size;
            strncpy(f->name, name, sizeof f->name - 1); f->name[sizeof f->name - 1] = 0;
            (*nfiles)++;
            return f;
        }
    }
    sc->missing++;
    return NULL;
}

static int scene_eval(void *c, const char *expr, int32_t *out)
{   /* {expr} arguments are evaluated when the statement is parsed (1008:d25a take_ParseVepExpr) */
    Scene *sc = c;
    if (!sc->vm) return 0;
    return vep_eval_take_expr(sc->vm, expr, out);
}

/* orig: 1010:1204 take_IsSlotUnusedLater - 1 when no later Path shows the slot (seq != 0) before a Clear,
 * a Cel for the same slot, or the end of the take */
static int slot_unused_later(const Scene *sc, int slot)
{
    TakeReader r = sc->run.r;
    TakeStmt s;
    TakeFields f;
    r.eval = NULL;                       /* the lookahead must not evaluate (it only compares constants) */
    for (;;) {
        if (take_next(&r, &s) != TAKE_OK) return 1;
        if (s.op == TK_CLEAR) return 1;
        take_decode(&s, &f);
        if (s.op == TK_CEL && take_field(&f, TF_S6) == slot) return 1;
        if (s.op == TK_PATH && take_field(&f, TF_ARG1) == slot && take_field(&f, TF_ARG2) != 0) return 0;
    }
}

static void scene_exec(void *c, const TakeStmt *s, const TakeFields *f)
{   /* orig: 1010:12e4 take_ExecuteStatement */
    Scene *sc = c;
    Stage *st = sc->st;
    const SceneHooks *h = &sc->hooks;
    switch (s->op) {
    case TK_VEP: case TK_CUE: case TK_STAR: case TK_PAL: break;            /* no-ops in the original */
    case TK_AUDIO:                                                         /* A1 */
        if (take_field(f, TF_ARG1) == 1 && h->sync) h->sync(h->ctx, s->time);   /* musicPending */
        if (h->audio) h->audio(h->ctx, take_field(f, TF_ARG1), take_field_str(f, TF_ARG2),
                               take_field(f, TF_4E), (uint16_t)take_field(f, TF_50));
        break;
    case TK_SFX:                                                           /* A2 */
        if (h->sfx) h->sfx(h->ctx, take_field(f, TF_ARG1), take_field_str(f, TF_ARG2), take_field(f, TF_4E));
        break;
    case TK_DONE: if (h->done) h->done(h->ctx); break;                     /* A3: vet_SetBusyFlags(vm, 0, 0xd) */
    case TK_CLEAR:                                                         /* A4 */
        take_ClearScene(st);
        if (h->spot) h->spot(h->ctx, 0, f);                                /* hotspots->remove(0): all */
        break;
    case TK_DROP: case TK_FADE: take_Fade(st, take_field(f, TF_ARG1), take_field32(f, TF_ARG2)); break;
    case TK_BACK: {                                                        /* B1 */
        const SceneFile *bf = scene_file(sc, take_field_str(f, TF_ARG1));
        if (bf) take_SetBackground(st, bf->data);
        break;
    }
    case TK_SHOW: take_Wait(st, take_field32(f, TF_ARG1)); take_SetAnimRunning(st, 1);            /* B2 */
        if (h->sync) h->sync(h->ctx, s->time);
        break;
    case TK_SPOT: if (h->spot) h->spot(h->ctx, take_field(f, TF_ARG2) != 0, f); break;       /* B3 */
    case TK_HIDE: take_SetAnimRunning(st, 0); break;                                         /* B4 */
    case TK_AVI: if (h->avi) h->avi(h->ctx, f); break;                                       /* B7 */
    case TK_GRID:                                                          /* C1: corners in pixels -> 22.10 */
        take_SetView(st, take_field(f, TF_ARG1), take_field(f, TF_ARG2),
                     (int32_t)take_field(f, TF_ARG2HI) << 10, (int32_t)take_field(f, TF_44) << 10,
                     (int32_t)take_field(f, TF_S0) << 10, (int32_t)take_field(f, TF_S2) << 10,
                     (int32_t)take_field(f, TF_S4) << 10, (int32_t)take_field(f, TF_S6) << 10,
                     take_field(f, TF_4E));
        break;
    case TK_CEL: {                                                         /* C2 */
        const SceneFile *af = scene_file(sc, take_field_str(f, TF_ARG1));
        if (af) {
            int slot = take_field(f, TF_S6);
            take_SetSlotPack(st, slot, af->data, af->len);
            stage_SetSlotScale(st, slot, (uint16_t)take_field(f, TF_4E));
        }
        break;
    }
    case TK_PATH: {                                                        /* C3 */
        int slot = take_field(f, TF_ARG1), seq = take_field(f, TF_ARG2);
        unsigned fl = (uint16_t)take_field(f, TF_4E), pf = 0;
        if (fl & 1) pf |= SF_PERSPECTIVE;
        if (fl & 2) pf |= SF_PATHMODE;
        take_SetSpritePath(st, slot, take_field(f, TF_44), take_field(f, TF_S0), take_field(f, TF_S2),
                           take_field32(f, TF_S4), pf);
        take_SetSlotSeqDir(st, slot, seq, take_field(f, TF_ARG2HI));
        if (seq == 0 && slot_unused_later(sc, slot)) take_SetSlotPack(st, slot, NULL, 0);
        break;
    }
    default: break;
    }
}

int scene_add_group(Scene *sc, const DatDir *dir, const char *groupName)
{
    const DatDirEntry *e;
    if (sc->ngroups >= SCENE_MAX_GROUPS) return -1;
    e = dat_dir_lookup(dir, groupName);
    if (!e || !e->disc) return -1;
    if (dat_group_open(&sc->groups[sc->ngroups], sc->dat, e->offset) != 0) return -1;
    sc->ngroups++;
    return 0;
}

int scene_open(Scene *sc, Stage *st, VetVM *vm, const DatDir *dir, FILE *dat, const char *name)
{
    char grp[32], def[32];
    const DatFileEntry *e;
    TakeHost h;
    SceneHooks keep = sc->hooks;
    SceneCache *cache = sc->cache;
    memset(sc, 0, sizeof *sc);
    sc->hooks = keep; sc->cache = cache;
    sc->st = st; sc->vm = vm; sc->dat = dat;
    sprintf(grp, "%.12s.tke", name);
    sprintf(def, "%.12s.DEF", name);
    if (scene_add_group(sc, dir, grp) != 0) return -1;
    e = dat_group_find(&sc->groups[0], def);
    if (!e) return -1;
    sc->takeData = dat_read(dat, &sc->groups[0], e);
    sc->takeLen = e->size;
    if (!sc->takeData) return -1;
    memset(&h, 0, sizeof h);
    h.ctx = sc;
    h.exec = scene_exec;
    h.eval = scene_eval;
    return take_runner_start(&sc->run, sc->takeData, sc->takeLen, &h) == 0 ? 0 : -1;
}

int scene_tick(Scene *sc, int32_t ticks) { return take_runner_tick(&sc->run, ticks); }

void scene_close(Scene *sc)
{
    int i;
    SceneHooks keep = sc->hooks;
    SceneCache *cache = sc->cache;
    for (i = 0; i < sc->nfiles; i++) free(sc->files[i].data);
    for (i = 0; i < sc->ngroups; i++) dat_group_free(&sc->groups[i]);
    free(sc->takeData);
    memset(sc, 0, sizeof *sc);
    sc->hooks = keep; sc->cache = cache;
}

```

`src/scene.h`:

```h
/*
 * scene.h - plays a take (.DEF) on the Stage: the stage half of take_ExecuteStatement (1010:12e4).
 * Files named by the take are loaded from the take's DAT group (orig: audio_LoadTrack through the asset
 * manager, which also looks in the shared groups; here the caller may give extra groups).
 */
#ifndef IZZY_SCENE_H
#define IZZY_SCENE_H

#include <stdio.h>
#include "dat.h"
#include "take.h"
#include "stage.h"
#include "vet.h"

#define SCENE_MAX_FILES 160
#define SCENE_MAX_GROUPS 4

typedef struct SceneFile { char name[16]; uint8_t *data; size_t len; } SceneFile;

/* The non-stage parts of take_ExecuteStatement, supplied by the game layer (all optional). */
typedef struct SceneHooks {
    void *ctx;
    void (*audio)(void *ctx, int channel, const char *file, int volume, unsigned flags);   /* A1 */
    void (*sfx)(void *ctx, int id, const char *file, int volume);                         /* A2 */
    void (*done)(void *ctx);                                                              /* A3 */
    void (*spot)(void *ctx, int add, const TakeFields *f);                                /* B3; Clear removes all */
    void (*avi)(void *ctx, const TakeFields *f);                                          /* B7 */
    void (*sync)(void *ctx, int32_t time);           /* A1 channel 1 / B2: the take clock may re-sync to 	ime */
} SceneHooks;

/* Optional file cache shared by successive scenes (orig: the asset manager keeps files while referenced). */
typedef struct SceneCache { SceneFile files[1024]; int nfiles; } SceneCache;

typedef struct Scene {
    SceneCache *cache;              /* if set (before scene_open), files live here and outlive the scene */
    SceneHooks hooks;               /* set before scene_open; kept across open/close */
    Stage *st;
    VetVM *vm;                      /* optional: evaluates {VAR + k} arguments */
    FILE *dat;
    DatGroup groups[SCENE_MAX_GROUPS]; int ngroups;
    SceneFile files[SCENE_MAX_FILES]; int nfiles;
    uint8_t *takeData; size_t takeLen;
    TakeRunner run;
    long missing;                   /* files a statement named that were not found */
} Scene;

/* Open take `name` (e.g. "SBC00010") from the group "<name>.tke" in `dir`. Returns 0 on success. */
int  scene_open(Scene *sc, Stage *st, VetVM *vm, const DatDir *dir, FILE *dat, const char *name);
/* Add another group (by directory name) searched for files. */
int  scene_add_group(Scene *sc, const DatDir *dir, const char *groupName);
/* Run the statements due at take time `ticks`; 1 while more remain, 0 at the end, -1 on error. */
int  scene_tick(Scene *sc, int32_t ticks);
void scene_close(Scene *sc);

#endif

```

`src/stage.c`:

```c
/*
 * stage.c - slots, animation stepping, the view and the frame composer (see stage.h).
 * Every function names the original it reconstructs; Stage offsets are in stage.h.
 */
#include "stage.h"
#include <math.h>
#include <string.h>

/* DS:00ee / DS:01b0 - glyph x offsets in the font strips for characters 0x20..0x7f; DS:00ec / DS:01ae line heights */
static const int16_t g_font1[96] = {
    0,5,9,13,25,33,46,57,60,67,73,80,90,93,101,104,113,123,130,139,147,157,166,176,185,194,204,208,212,222,232,241,
    248,261,273,282,292,302,311,318,329,341,344,352,361,369,383,394,407,416,429,438,447,455,466,477,493,504,515,525,
    529,538,543,549,560,565,573,583,592,602,612,619,627,636,639,643,651,654,667,676,686,696,707,713,720,726,734,743,
    758,766,776,785,790,793,799,804 };
static const int16_t g_font2[96] = {
    0,6,12,20,34,45,62,75,80,89,98,107,120,125,135,141,153,166,174,186,197,209,221,233,245,256,269,274,281,294,306,319,
    329,345,360,371,385,397,408,417,432,446,452,462,473,483,501,514,531,541,558,569,580,591,604,619,637,651,664,677,
    684,695,702,712,724,731,742,755,766,779,792,801,812,824,829,837,847,852,868,879,892,905,919,927,937,945,956,967,
    984,996,1008,1020,1028,1033,1040,1050 };

#define SLOT_OK(s) ((s) >= 0 && (s) < STAGE_SLOTS)

int take_SetView(Stage *st, int32_t w, int32_t h, int32_t tlx, int32_t trx, int32_t ty, int32_t blx, int32_t brx, int32_t by, int32_t scale);

static int32_t ftol_(double d) { return (int32_t)d; }      /* orig: 1000:13e0 _ftol (truncates) */
static void rect_set(StageRect *r, int l, int t, int rr, int b) { r->left = l; r->top = t; r->right = rr; r->bottom = b; }
static void rect_empty(StageRect *r) { rect_set(r, 0, 0, 0, 0); }
static int rect_intersect(StageRect *o, const StageRect *a, const StageRect *b)
{   /* Win16 IntersectRect: empty result -> all zero, returns 0 */
    o->left = a->left > b->left ? a->left : b->left;   o->top = a->top > b->top ? a->top : b->top;
    o->right = a->right < b->right ? a->right : b->right; o->bottom = a->bottom < b->bottom ? a->bottom : b->bottom;
    if (o->left >= o->right || o->top >= o->bottom) { rect_empty(o); return 0; }
    return 1;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ init */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ setters */

int take_SetSlotPack(Stage *st, int slot, const uint8_t *ani, size_t len)
{   /* orig: 1008:4dae - asset reference counting (asset_Release +1/-1 for slots < 0x20) is the caller's */
    StageSlot *p;
    if (!st->initialized || slot < 1 || slot > 0x67) return 0;
    p = &st->slot[slot];
    if (p->pendPackFlag && p->pendPack == ani) return 1;
    p->pendPack = ani; p->pendPackLen = len; p->pendPackFlag = 1;
    return 1;
}

int take_SetSlotSeqDir(Stage *st, int slot, int seq, int dir)
{   /* orig: 1008:4e76 (named take_SetSpritePosition before) - dir is 1-based */
    if (!st->initialized || slot < 1 || slot > 0x67) return 0;
    st->slot[slot].pendSeq = seq; st->slot[slot].pendDir = dir; st->slot[slot].pendSeqFlag = 1;
    return 1;
}

int take_SetSlotDib(Stage *st, int slot, const uint8_t *dib)
{   /* orig: 1008:4b9c take_SetBackgroundAsset */
    StageSlot *p;
    if (!st->initialized || slot < 1 || slot > 0x67) return 0;
    p = &st->slot[slot];
    if (p->pendDibFlag && p->pendDib == dib) return 1;
    p->pendDib = dib; p->pendDibFlag = 1;
    return 1;
}

int take_SetBackground(Stage *st, const uint8_t *bmp)
{   /* orig: 1008:49da (Back B1) */
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    if (st->bgDirty && st->bgPending == bmp) return 1;
    st->bgPending = bmp; st->bgDirty = 1;
    if (st->bgPending && st->bgAnimSlot) { take_SetSlotDib(st, st->bgAnimSlot, NULL); st->bgAnimSlot = 0; }
    return 1;
}

int stage_SetSlotPosition(Stage *st, int slot, int32_t x, int32_t y)
{   /* orig: 1008:5064 (stage_SetSlotParamA) */
    if (!st->initialized || !SLOT_OK(slot)) return 0;
    st->slot[slot].pendX = x; st->slot[slot].pendY = y; st->slot[slot].pendPosFlag = 1;
    return 1;
}

int stage_SetSlotDepth(Stage *st, int slot, int depth)
{   /* orig: 1008:50bc (stage_SetSlotParamB) */
    if (!st->initialized || !SLOT_OK(slot)) return 0;
    st->slot[slot].pendDepth = depth; st->slot[slot].pendDepthFlag = 1;
    return 1;
}

int stage_SetSlotScale(Stage *st, int slot, int32_t scale)
{   /* orig: 1008:50fe (stage_SetSlotSequence) - 1024 = 1:1; Cel (C2) passes its scale argument */
    if (!st->initialized || !SLOT_OK(slot)) return 0;
    st->slot[slot].pendScale = scale; st->slot[slot].pendScaleFlag = 1;
    return 1;
}

int stage_SetSlotFlags(Stage *st, int slot, int set, unsigned mask)
{   /* orig: 1008:514c (stage_SetSlotParamC) */
    StageSlot *p;
    if (!st->initialized || !SLOT_OK(slot)) return 0;
    p = &st->slot[slot];
    if (!p->pendFlagsFlag) { p->flagsAnd = 0xffff; p->flagsOr = 0; }
    if (set) p->flagsOr |= mask; else p->flagsAnd &= ~mask & 0xffff;
    p->pendFlagsFlag = 1;
    return 1;
}

int stage_SetSlotText(Stage *st, int slot, const char *text, int font, int dx, int dy, unsigned align)
{   /* orig: 1008:51e2 (stage_SetSlotParamD) */
    StageSlot *p;
    if (!st->initialized || !SLOT_OK(slot)) return 0;
    p = &st->slot[slot];
    p->pendText = text; p->pendFont = font; p->pendTextDx = dx; p->pendTextDy = dy; p->pendTextAlign = (int)align;
    p->pendTextFlag = 1;
    return 1;
}

int take_SetSpritePath(Stage *st, int slot, int32_t x, int32_t y, int z, int32_t ticks, unsigned pathFlags)
{   /* orig: 1008:4ec2 (Path C3). ticks < 1: place at once (position, depth, flags & 0x0fff | pathFlags). */
    StageSlot *p;
    if (!st->initialized || slot < 0 || slot > 0x67) return 0;
    p = &st->slot[slot];
    if (ticks < 1) {
        stage_SetSlotPosition(st, slot, x, y);
        stage_SetSlotDepth(st, slot, z);
        if (!p->pendFlagsFlag) { p->flagsAnd = 0xffff; p->flagsOr = 0; }
        p->flagsAnd &= 0x0fff;          /* (the original runs the two stage_SetSlotFlags bodies inline) */
        p->flagsOr |= pathFlags; p->pendFlagsFlag = 1;
    } else {
        p->pathToX = x; p->pathToY = y; p->pathToZ = z; p->pathTime = ticks; p->pathFlags = pathFlags;
        p->pendPathFlag = 1;
    }
    return 1;
}

int take_SetView(Stage *st, int32_t w, int32_t h, int32_t tlx, int32_t trx, int32_t ty,
                 int32_t blx, int32_t brx, int32_t by, int32_t scale)
{   /* orig: 1008:4c64 - validates and stores; stage_ApplyView converts on the next tick. Grid (C1) passes
     * corners in pixels << 10. Returns -1 when an argument had to be corrected. */
    int r = 1;
    if (w < 1) { w = 1; r = -1; }
    if (h < 1) { h = 1; r = -1; }
    if (trx <= tlx) { trx = tlx + 1; r = -1; }
    if (brx <= blx) { brx = blx + 1; r = -1; }
    if (by <= ty) { by = ty + 1; r = -1; }
    if (scale < 0) { scale = 0; r = -1; }
    if (scale > 0xfff) { scale = 0xfff; r = -1; }
    st->view.raw[0] = w; st->view.raw[1] = h; st->view.raw[2] = tlx; st->view.raw[3] = trx; st->view.raw[4] = ty;
    st->view.raw[5] = blx; st->view.raw[6] = brx; st->view.raw[7] = by; st->view.raw[8] = scale;
    st->view.dirty = 1;
    return r;
}

void take_SetDefaultView(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1008:4d70 - 640x480 units onto the 640x480 screen, scale 1:1 */
    take_SetView(st, 0xa0000, 0x78000, 0, 0xa0000, 0, 0, 0xa0000, 0x78000, 0x400);
}

int take_ClearScene(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1008:4586 (Clear A4) */
    int s;
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    st->bgPending = NULL; st->bgDirty = 1;
    for (s = 1; s < 0x20; s++) take_SetSlotPack(st, s, NULL, 0);
    take_SetDefaultView(st);
    return 1;
}

int take_SetAnimRunning(Stage *st, int on)
{   /* orig: 1008:54c4 (Show B2 / Hide B4) */
    if (!st->initialized) { st->animRunning = 0; return 0; }
    st->animRunning = on ? 1 : 0;
    return 1;
}

int take_Wait(Stage *st, int32_t ticks)
{   /* orig: 1008:4932 */
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    st->waitStart = st->now; st->waitDuration = ticks; st->waiting = 1; st->fading = 0;
    return 1;
}

int take_Fade(Stage *st, int level, int32_t ticks)
{   /* orig: 1008:496e (Drop B0 / Fade B6) */
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    if (level < 0) level = 0;
    if (level > 0x7f) level = 0x7f;
    st->fadeLevel = level;
    st->waitStart = st->now; st->waitDuration = ticks;
    st->fading = 1; st->waiting = 0; st->fadeActive = 1;
    st->fillColor = level;
    return 1;
}

int stage_SetRunning(Stage *st, int on)
{   /* orig: 1008:54a8 - running = 1, or -1 (paused: the clock stops) */
    st->running = on ? 1 : -1;
    return 1;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ palette */

static void show_entry(Stage *st, int i, const uint8_t *rgb)
{   /* the displayed palette: orig keeps three copies (0x151e logical palette, the DIB colour table, a work table) */
    st->screen->pal.r[i] = rgb[0]; st->screen->pal.g[i] = rgb[1]; st->screen->pal.b[i] = rgb[2];
}

static int pal_range(int count, int *start, int *end, int *skip)
{
    *end = *start + count; *skip = 0;
    if (*start < 0 || *end > 0x100 || *end <= *start) return -1;
    if (*end > 0xf6) *end = 0xf6;
    if (*start < 1) { *skip = 1 - *start; *start = 1; }
    return 0;
}

int stage_PaletteDiffers(const Stage *st, int count, int start, const uint8_t (*rgb)[3])
{   /* orig: 1008:4668 (stage_SetPaletteRangeTarget) */
    int end, k, i;
    if (!st->initialized || !rgb) return 0;
    if (pal_range(count, &start, &end, &k) != 0) return -1;
    for (i = start; i < end; i++, k++)
        if (rgb[k][0] != st->pal[i][0] || rgb[k][1] != st->pal[i][1] || rgb[k][2] != st->pal[i][2]) return 1;
    return 0;
}

int stage_SetPaletteRange(Stage *st, int count, int start, const uint8_t (*rgb)[3])
{   /* orig: 1008:4780 (stage_SetPaletteRangeNow) */
    int end, k, i;
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    if (!rgb || pal_range(count, &start, &end, &k) != 0) return -1;
    for (i = start; i < end; i++, k++) {
        memcpy(st->pal[i], rgb[k], 3);
        if (st->paletteVisible) { memcpy(st->palShown[i], rgb[k], 3); show_entry(st, i, rgb[k]); }
    }
    return 1;
}

static void stage_PaletteChanged(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1018:9f3a - rebuild the shadow tables from the scene palette */
    RgbPal p; int i;
    for (i = 0; i < 256; i++) { p.r[i] = st->pal[i][0]; p.g[i] = st->pal[i][1]; p.b[i] = st->pal[i][2]; }
    render_build_shadow_tables_from(st->screen, &p);
}

static void stage_ShowPalette(Stage *st, int flag)
{   /* orig: 1018:a72c (stage_ResetPaletteWork) - palShown = displayed = scene palette */
    int i;
    for (i = 1; i < 0xf6; i++) { memcpy(st->palShown[i], st->pal[i], 3); show_entry(st, i, st->pal[i]); }
    if (flag) st->paletteVisible = 1;
}

static void stage_ShowFadeColour(Stage *st, int flag)
{   /* orig: 1018:a7da (stage_ApplyFadeToPalette) - palFaded = displayed = the solid colour pal[fadeLevel] */
    int i;
    for (i = 1; i < 0xf6; i++) { memcpy(st->palFaded[i], st->pal[st->fadeLevel], 3); show_entry(st, i, st->pal[st->fadeLevel]); }
    if (flag) st->paletteVisible = 0;
}

static int stage_UpdateWait(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1018:a322 - Show (B2): cross-fade from palFaded to the scene palette over waitDuration ticks */
    uint32_t el = (uint32_t)(st->now - st->waitStart), dur = (uint32_t)st->waitDuration;
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    if (!st->waiting) return -1;
    if (el < dur) {
        uint32_t rest = dur - el; int i, c;
        for (i = 1; i < 0xf6; i++) {
            for (c = 0; c < 3; c++)
                st->palShown[i][c] = (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)st->palFaded[i][c] * rest + (uint32_t)st->pal[i][c] * el) / dur);
            show_entry(st, i, st->palShown[i]);
        }
    } else {
        stage_ShowPalette(st, 1);
        st->waiting = 0;
        st->paletteVisible = 1;
    }
    st->needsFullRedraw = 0;
    return 1;
}

static int stage_UpdateFade(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1018:a55c - Drop (B0) / Fade (B6): fade from palShown to the solid colour pal[fadeLevel] */
    uint32_t el = (uint32_t)(st->now - st->waitStart), dur = (uint32_t)st->waitDuration;
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    if (!st->fading) return -1;
    if (el < dur) {
        uint32_t rest = dur - el; int i, c;
        const uint8_t *to = st->pal[st->fadeLevel];
        for (i = 1; i < 0xf6; i++) {
            for (c = 0; c < 3; c++)
                st->palFaded[i][c] = (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)st->palShown[i][c] * rest + (uint32_t)to[c] * el) / dur);
            show_entry(st, i, st->palFaded[i]);
        }
    } else {
        stage_ShowFadeColour(st, 1);
        st->fading = 0;
        st->needsFullRedraw = 1;
    }
    st->paletteVisible = 0;
    return 1;
}

static int stage_UpdateWaitOrFade(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1018:a2aa - once per clock change */
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    if ((st->waiting || st->fading) && st->now != st->lastEffectTick) {
        st->lastEffectTick = st->now;
        if (st->waiting) return stage_UpdateWait(st);
        return stage_UpdateFade(st);
    }
    return -1;
}

static int stage_UpdateEffects(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:a92a - take palette entries 1..n-1 (at most 245) from a slot's BMP; the game lets this
     * happen once (DS:0a3a starts at 1), so the first background shown sets the palette for good */
    const uint8_t *f = st->slot[s].dib, *h;
    uint8_t rgb[256][3];
    int n = 0, i, ok = 0;
    if (!f || !st->slot[s].celDib) return -1;
    if (st->paletteLoadsLeft == 0) return -1;
    st->paletteLoadsLeft--;
    h = f + 14;
    if (rd_u32(h) == 12) {                                      /* BITMAPCOREHEADER */
        if (rd_i16(h + 4) == st->slot[s].width && rd_i16(h + 6) == st->slot[s].height && rd_i16(h + 10) < 24) {
            n = 1 << rd_i16(h + 10);
            for (i = 1; i < n; i++) { const uint8_t *q = h + 12 + 3 * i; rgb[i - 1][0] = q[2]; rgb[i - 1][1] = q[1]; rgb[i - 1][2] = q[0]; }
            ok = 1;
        }
    } else if (rd_i16(h + 4) == st->slot[s].width && rd_i16(h + 8) == st->slot[s].height) {
        n = rd_i16(h + 0x20);
        if (n == 0 && rd_i16(h + 14) < 24) n = 1 << rd_i16(h + 14);
        if (n <= 0x100) {
            for (i = 1; i < n; i++) { const uint8_t *q = h + rd_u32(h) + 4 * i; rgb[i - 1][0] = q[2]; rgb[i - 1][1] = q[1]; rgb[i - 1][2] = q[0]; }
            ok = 1;
        }
    }
    if (ok && n - 1 > 0) {
        int cnt = n - 1 < 0xf6 ? n - 1 : 0xf5;
        if (stage_PaletteDiffers(st, cnt, 1, (const uint8_t (*)[3])rgb)) {
            stage_SetPaletteRange(st, cnt, 1, (const uint8_t (*)[3])rgb);
            stage_PaletteChanged(st);
        }
        return 1;
    }
    return -1;
}

void stage_init(Stage *st, Screen *screen)
{
    /* orig: 1008:3ce0 stage_Init - per-slot defaults; the window, WinG DC, palette and regions are port-layer. */
    int i;
    memset(st, 0, sizeof *st);
    st->screen = screen;
    for (i = 0; i < STAGE_SLOTS; i++) {
        StageSlot *p = &st->slot[i];
        p->slotScale = 0x400; p->scale = 0x400; p->dScale = 1.0;   /* DS:10fc = 1.0 */
        p->sortKey = i; p->orderKey = i;
        st->drawOrder[i] = i;
    }
    st->slot[0].depth = -0x7fff; st->slot[0].sortKey = -0x7fff; st->slot[0].orderKey = -0x7fff;
    take_SetDefaultView(st);
    st->slot[0].width = 640; st->slot[0].height = 480;
    rect_set(&st->slot[0].bounds, 0, 0, 640, 480);
    st->slot[0].flags = SF_VISIBLE | SF_DIRTY | SF_DRAWABLE | SF_OPAQUE;    /* 0x8b */
    st->needsFullRedraw = 1;
    st->fontOffsets[0] = g_font1; st->fontHeight[0] = 16;
    st->fontOffsets[1] = g_font2; st->fontHeight[1] = 20;
    st->initialized = 1;
    st->running = 1;
    /* palette: the original starts from the system palette (1018:a1f6 stage_BuildRemapTables); entries 1..245
     * are black until the first background brings the game palette */
    st->paletteLoadsLeft = 1;
    st->paletteVisible = 1;
    stage_ShowFadeColour(st, 0);
    stage_ShowPalette(st, 0);
    stage_PaletteChanged(st);
}

int stage_HitTest(const Stage *st, int x, int y)
{   /* orig: 1008:5250 (stage_SetSlotParamE) - front to back through drawOrder */
    int i;
    for (i = 0x67; i >= 0; i--) {
        int s = st->drawOrder[i];
        if (s > 0 && s < 0x65) {
            const StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
            if ((p->flags & SF_VISIBLE) && (p->flags & SF_DRAWABLE) &&
                p->bounds.left <= x && x < p->bounds.right && p->bounds.top <= y && y < p->bounds.bottom)
                return s;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ view */

static void stage_ApplyView(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1018:960c (named stage_UpdatePaletteFade before). 0x13d0 = log. */
    StageView *v = &st->view;
    v->w = v->raw[0]; v->h = v->raw[1]; v->tlx = v->raw[2]; v->trx = v->raw[3]; v->ty = v->raw[4];
    v->blx = v->raw[5]; v->brx = v->raw[6]; v->by = v->raw[7]; v->nearScale = v->raw[8];
    if (v->nearScale < 1.0) v->nearScale = 1.0;                                   /* DS:110e */
    v->farScale = (v->trx - v->tlx) * v->nearScale / (v->brx - v->blx);
    if (v->nearScale < v->farScale) v->farScale = v->nearScale;
    if (v->farScale < 1.0) v->farScale = 1.0;
    v->curved = v->farScale != v->nearScale;
    if (v->curved) {
        v->logNear = log(v->nearScale);
        v->logFar = log(v->farScale);
        v->flat = 0;
    } else {
        v->logFar = log(v->farScale);
        v->logNear = v->logFar;
        v->flat = v->tlx == 0.0 && v->w == v->trx && v->ty == 0.0 && v->blx == 0.0 && v->w == v->brx && v->h == v->by;
    }
}

static void anim_ApplyScale(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:eb64 - view position (x, y) -> screen position (fxX, fxY) and scale */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    const StageView *v = &st->view;
    if (!st->initialized) return;
    if (!(p->flags & SF_PERSPECTIVE)) {
        p->fxX = p->x << 10; p->fxY = p->y << 10; p->scale = p->slotScale;
    } else if (v->flat) {
        p->fxX = p->x << 10; p->fxY = p->y << 10; p->scale = ftol_(p->dScale * v->nearScale);
    } else {
        double sx = p->dX / v->w, sy = p->dY / v->h;
        double u = 1.0 - sy, t = sy, left, right, wx, wy, sc;
        if (v->curved) {
            double l = log(v->farScale * sy + v->nearScale * (1.0 - sy));
            u = (l - v->logFar) / (v->logNear - v->logFar);
            t = 1.0 - u;
        }
        left = v->tlx * u + v->blx * t;
        right = v->trx * u + v->brx * t;
        wx = (1.0 - sx) * left + sx * right;
        wy = v->ty * u + v->by * t;
        sc = (v->farScale * u + v->nearScale * t) * p->dScale;
        if (sc < 0.0) sc = 0.0;                                                   /* DS:1126 */
        if (sc > 4095.0) sc = 4095.0;                                             /* DS:112e */
        p->fxX = ftol_(wx); p->fxY = ftol_(wy); p->scale = ftol_(sc);
    }
}

static void anim_UpdateMotion(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1020:0000 - screen position (after dx/dy motion) -> view position; 0x13db = exp */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    const StageView *v = &st->view;
    if (!st->initialized) return;
    if (!(p->flags & SF_PERSPECTIVE)) {
        p->x = p->fxX >> 10; p->y = p->fxY >> 10; p->scale = p->slotScale;
    } else if (v->flat) {
        p->x = p->fxX >> 10; p->y = p->fxY >> 10; p->scale = ftol_(p->dScale * v->nearScale);
    } else {
        double fx = (double)p->fxX, fy = (double)p->fxY;
        double t = (fy - v->ty) / (v->by - v->ty), u = 1.0 - t;
        double left = v->tlx * u + v->blx * t, right = v->trx * u + v->brx * t;
        double sc = v->farScale * u + v->nearScale * t, w = right - left, sx;
        sx = w == 0.0 ? 0.5 : (fx - left) / w;                                     /* DS:1136 = 0.5 */
        if (v->curved) {
            double e = exp((v->logNear - v->logFar) * u + v->logFar);
            u = (e - v->farScale) / (v->nearScale - v->farScale);
            t = 1.0 - u;
        }
        sc *= p->dScale;
        if (sc < 0.0) sc = 0.0;
        if (sc > 4095.0) sc = 4095.0;
        p->dX = v->w * sx; p->dY = v->h * t;
        p->x = ftol_(p->dX); p->y = ftol_(p->dY); p->scale = ftol_(sc);
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ slot contents */

static int anim_LoadCelPack(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:82aa - swap in the pending pack, reset the slot, validate (cels_open) */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    const uint8_t *data;
    size_t len;
    if (s < 1 || s > 0x67) return 0;
    data = p->pendPack; len = p->pendPackLen;
    p->pendPack = NULL; p->pendPackLen = 0;
    p->havePack = 0;
    p->curCel = NULL; p->curCelLen = 0; p->celDib = NULL; p->celBits = NULL;
    p->depth = 0; p->offX = p->offY = 0;
    p->cel = p->frame = p->dir = p->seq = 0; p->lastSeq = 0;
    p->flags = (p->flags & 0xfff2) | SF_DIRTY;
    if (!data) return 1;
    if (cels_open(&p->pack, data, len) != CELS_OK) return -1;   /* the original shows a message box in debug mode */
    p->nCels = p->pack.nCels; p->nSeq = p->pack.nSeq; p->nDir = p->pack.nDir; p->nFrames = p->pack.nFrames;
    p->havePack = 1;
    p->frame = 0;
    p->nextFrameTime = st->now;
    if (p->seq < 0 || p->nSeq < p->seq) p->seq = 0;
    if (p->dir < 0 || p->nDir < p->dir) p->dir = 0;
    p->flags |= SF_PACK;
    return 1;
}

static int anim_LoadPendingPack(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:896e - apply the pending sequence / direction */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    unsigned old;
    if (s < 1 || s > 0x67) return 0;
    if (!(p->flags & SF_PACK)) return -1;
    p->seq = p->pendSeq;
    p->dir = p->pendDir - 1;
    if (p->seq < 0 || p->nSeq <= p->seq) p->seq = 0;
    if (p->dir < 0 || p->nDir <= p->dir) p->dir = 0;
    old = p->flags;
    p->flags = p->seq < 1 ? (p->flags & ~SF_DRAWABLE) : (p->flags | SF_DRAWABLE);
    if (p->flags != old) p->flags |= SF_DIRTY;
    return 1;
}

static void dib_header(const uint8_t *dib, const uint8_t **hdr, const uint8_t **bits, int *ok)
{   /* BMP file ("BM" + BITMAPFILEHEADER) or a packed DIB (BITMAPINFOHEADER + colour table) */
    *ok = 0;
    if (!dib) return;
    if (dib[0] == 'B' && dib[1] == 'M') { *hdr = dib + 14; *bits = dib + rd_u32(dib + 10); *ok = 1; }
    else if (rd_u32(dib) == 0x28) {
        uint32_t clr = rd_u32(dib + 32);
        if (clr == 0 || clr > 0x100) clr = 0x100;
        *hdr = dib; *bits = dib + (clr + 10) * 4; *ok = 1;
    }
}

static int stage_RemapDib(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:ab2a (stage_DecodeBackground) - map a picture with its own colours onto the stage palette:
     * every colour-table entry -> nearest of entries 1..245 (2dR^2 + 4dG^2 + dB^2), the table is rewritten with
     * the stage palette and every pixel is translated in place. */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    uint8_t map[256];
    uint8_t *f = (uint8_t *)(uintptr_t)p->dib, *h, *tbl, *px;
    int n, i, j, step, stride, y, x;
    if (!p->celDib || !f) return -1;
    for (i = 0; i < 256; i++) map[i] = (uint8_t)i;
    h = (f[0] == 'B' && f[1] == 'M') ? f + 14 : f;
    if (rd_u32(h) == 12) { if (rd_i16(h + 10) > 23) goto remap; n = 1 << rd_i16(h + 10); tbl = h + 12; step = 3; }
    else {
        n = rd_i16(h + 0x20);
        if (n == 0 && rd_i16(h + 14) < 24) n = 1 << rd_i16(h + 14);
        if (n > 0x100) goto remap;
        tbl = h + rd_u32(h); step = 4;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        uint8_t *q = tbl + (size_t)step * (size_t)i;    /* B G R */
        long best = 0x7fffffffL; int bi = i;
        for (j = 1; j < 0xf6; j++) {
            long dr = (long)q[2] - st->pal[j][0], dg = (long)q[1] - st->pal[j][1], db = (long)q[0] - st->pal[j][2];
            long d = (dg * dg * 2 + dr * dr) * 2 + db * db;
            if (d < best) { best = d; bi = j; }
        }
        map[i] = (uint8_t)bi;
        q[2] = st->pal[i][0]; q[1] = st->pal[i][1]; q[0] = st->pal[i][2];
    }
remap:
    px = (uint8_t *)(uintptr_t)p->celBits;
    stride = (p->width + 3) & ~3;
    for (y = 0; y < p->height; y++) for (x = 0; x < p->width; x++) px[(size_t)y * stride + x] = map[px[(size_t)y * stride + x]];
    return 1;
}

int take_ShowPicture(Stage *st, const uint8_t *dib)
{   /* orig: 1008:4a8a (take_ResetScene) - NULL removes the picture; otherwise clear the scene and show `dib`
     * fitted to the screen in slot 0x1f, in front of everything (depth 31999), palette-mapped */
    int s;
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    if (!dib) {
        if (st->bgAnimSlot) { take_SetSlotDib(st, st->bgAnimSlot, NULL); st->bgAnimSlot = 0; }
        return 1;
    }
    st->bgPending = NULL; st->bgDirty = 1;
    for (s = 1; s < 0x20; s++) take_SetSlotPack(st, s, NULL, 0);
    take_SetDefaultView(st);
    st->bgAnimSlot = 0x1f;
    take_SetSlotDib(st, 0x1f, dib);
    st->animRunning = 1;
    return 1;
}

static int stage_LoadSlotDib(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:8c3a stage_LoadBackground - a DIB / BMP shown in a sprite slot (UI bitmaps, fonts, bgAnimSlot) */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    const uint8_t *dib, *hdr = NULL, *bits = NULL;
    int ok, valid = 0;
    if (s < 1 || s > 0x67) return 0;
    dib = p->pendDib; p->pendDib = NULL;
    p->dib = dib;
    p->celDib = dib; p->celBits = NULL; p->curCel = NULL;
    p->flags |= SF_DIBUP;
    if (!dib) {
        p->flags &= ~SF_VISIBLE;
        rect_empty(&p->bounds);
        p->flags |= SF_DIRTY;
        return -1;
    }
    dib_header(dib, &hdr, &bits, &ok);
    if (ok) {
        p->width = (int16_t)rd_u32(hdr + 4); p->height = (int16_t)rd_u32(hdr + 8);
        p->offX = p->offY = 0;
        valid = p->width >= 1 && p->height >= 1;
    }
    if (valid) {
        p->celDib = hdr;
        p->celBits = bits;
        p->flags |= SF_VISIBLE | SF_DRAWABLE;
        /* the original tests the slot's previous compression value here (it never reads biCompression) */
        if (p->compression == 0) p->flags &= ~SF_COMPRESSED; else p->flags |= SF_COMPRESSED;
        if (st->bgAnimSlot == s) {
            /* fit the picture into 640x480 (scale capped at 2:1), centred horizontally */
            stage_RemapDib(st, s);
            int32_t sc = 0x78000 / p->height, sw = 0xa0000 / p->width;
            if (sw < sc) sc = sw;
            if (sc > 0x800) sc = 0x800;
            p->x = (0xa0000 - sc * p->width) / 2;     /* stored unshifted in both x and fxX, as in the original */
            p->fxX = p->x;
            p->y = 0; p->fxY = 0;
            p->dX = (double)p->x; p->dY = 0.0;
            p->slotScale = sc; p->scale = sc; p->dScale = sc / 1024.0;
            p->depth = 31999;
            p->flags &= 0xcfff;
        }
    } else {
        p->celDib = NULL; p->celBits = NULL;
        p->flags &= ~(SF_VISIBLE | SF_DRAWABLE);
    }
    p->flags |= SF_DIRTY;
    return 1;
}

static void stage_UploadBackground(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1018:8a96 stage_UploadBackground - Back (B1): the BMP becomes slot 0 */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[0];
    const uint8_t *bmp = st->bgPending;
    st->bgPending = NULL;
    p->dib = bmp;
    p->celDib = NULL; p->celBits = NULL;
    p->width = 640; p->height = 480;
    if (bmp && bmp[0] == 'B' && bmp[1] == 'M') {
        int32_t w = (int32_t)rd_u32(bmp + 0x12), h = (int32_t)rd_u32(bmp + 0x16);
        if (w > 0 && h > 0) {
            p->celDib = bmp + 14;
            p->celBits = bmp + rd_u32(bmp + 10);
            p->width = (int16_t)w; p->height = (int16_t)h;
            p->flags |= SF_DIBUP | SF_OPAQUE;
            if (!st->needsFullRedraw) p->flags |= SF_DIRTY;
            st->bgPaletteDirty = 1;          /* f_04f8: stage_UpdateEffects reads the palette at the next redraw */
            goto done;
        }
    }
    p->celDib = NULL;
    st->needsFullRedraw = 1;
done:
    rect_set(&p->bounds, 0, 0, p->width, p->height);
}

static int anim_SetCel(Stage *st, int s, const uint8_t *cel, size_t celLen)
{   /* orig: 1018:9172 - make a pack cel current (sprite cels: MMP1; others are DIB cels) */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    if (s < 1 || s > 0x67) return 0;
    p->curCel = cel; p->curCelLen = celLen;
    p->celDib = NULL; p->celBits = NULL;
    p->flags |= SF_DIBUP;
    if (!cel) {
        p->flags &= ~SF_VISIBLE;
        rect_empty(&p->bounds);
        p->flags |= SF_DIRTY;
        return -1;
    }
    p->width = (int16_t)rd_u32(cel + 4); p->height = (int16_t)rd_u32(cel + 8);
    p->flags |= SF_VISIBLE;
    p->flags &= ~(SF_DIBUP | SF_COMPRESSED);
    if (!(p->celFlags & 4)) {
        p->celDib = cel; p->celBits = cel + 0x28;
        p->compression = (int32_t)rd_u32(cel + 0x10);
        if (p->compression != 0) p->flags |= SF_COMPRESSED;
    } else {
        p->compression = 0;
        if (cel[0x10] == 'M' && cel[0x11] == 'M' && cel[0x12] == 'P') {    /* DS(1018):b064 "MMP" */
            p->compression = (int8_t)cel[0x13];
            p->flags |= SF_COMPRESSED;
        }
    }
    p->flags |= SF_DIRTY;
    return 1;
}

static uint16_t text_sum(const char *t)
{   /* checksum used to notice in-place text changes: Fibonacci weights 3, 5, 8, 13, ... (16-bit) */
    uint16_t sum = 0, a = 1, b = 2;
    if (!t) return 0;
    while (*t) { uint16_t w = (uint16_t)(a + b); sum = (uint16_t)(sum + w * (uint8_t)*t++); a = b; b = w; }
    return sum;
}

static int anim_ApplyText(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:988a (anim_SavePrevBounds) */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    p->text = p->pendText;
    if (!p->text) p->textAlign = 0;
    else {
        p->font = p->pendFont; p->textDx = p->pendTextDx; p->textDy = p->pendTextDy;
        p->textAlign = (unsigned)p->pendTextAlign | 1;
        p->flags |= SF_VISIBLE | SF_DRAWABLE;
    }
    p->flags |= SF_DIRTY;
    p->textSum = text_sum(p->text);
    return 1;
}

static int anim_StartPath(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:94a0 (anim_UpdateBounds) */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    p->pathFromX = p->x; p->pathFromY = p->y; p->pathFromZ = p->depth;
    p->pathEndX = p->pathToX; p->pathEndY = p->pathToY; p->pathEndZ = p->pathToZ;
    p->nextFrameTime = st->now;
    p->pathStart = p->nextFrameTime;
    p->pathDur = p->pathTime;
    p->flags &= 0x0fff;
    p->flags |= p->pathFlags | SF_MOVING;
    if (p->pathEndX != p->pathFromX || p->pathEndY != p->pathFromY || p->pathEndZ != p->pathFromZ) p->flags |= SF_TIMED;
    return 1;
}

static int anim_UpdatePath(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1020:0346 - linear interpolation of x, y, z over pathDur ticks */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    int32_t el = st->now - p->pathStart, nx, ny;
    int nz;
    double X, Y;
    if (el >= 0 && el < p->pathDur) {
        double f = (double)el / (double)p->pathDur, g = 1.0 - f;
        X = g * (double)p->pathFromX + f * (double)p->pathEndX; nx = ftol_(X);
        Y = g * (double)p->pathFromY + f * (double)p->pathEndY; ny = ftol_(Y);
        nz = (int16_t)ftol_((double)(int16_t)ftol_(g * p->pathFromZ + f * p->pathEndZ));
    } else {
        nx = p->pathEndX; ny = p->pathEndY; nz = p->pathEndZ;
        X = (double)nx; Y = (double)ny;
        p->flags &= ~SF_MOVING;
    }
    if (p->x != nx || p->y != ny || p->depth != nz) {
        p->dX = X; p->dY = Y; p->x = nx; p->y = ny; p->depth = nz;
        return 1;
    }
    return -1;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ bounds and order */

static int stage_SetSlotDrawOrder(Stage *st, int32_t key, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:c7a6 - keep drawOrder sorted by (key, slot); moves the slot up or down */
    int i = 0, moved = 0;
    if (!st->initialized || !SLOT_OK(s)) return 0;
    while (st->drawOrder[i] != s) i++;
    while (i < 0x67) {
        int nx = st->drawOrder[i + 1];
        int32_t k = st->slot[nx].orderKey;
        if (key <= k && (k != key || s <= nx)) break;
        st->drawOrder[i] = nx; st->drawOrder[i + 1] = s; i++; moved = 1;
    }
    while (i > 0) {
        int pv = st->drawOrder[i - 1];
        int32_t k = st->slot[pv].orderKey;
        if (k <= key && (k != key || pv <= s)) break;
        st->drawOrder[i] = pv; st->drawOrder[i - 1] = s; i--; moved = 1;
    }
    st->slot[s].orderKey = key;
    if (moved) st->slot[s].flags |= SF_DIRTY;
    return 1;
}

static int text_width_line(const Stage *st, int font, const char *t, int stopAtNewline)
{
    const int16_t *tbl = st->fontOffsets[font == 1 ? 0 : 1];
    int w = 0, best = 0;
    for (; *t; t++) {
        unsigned c = (uint8_t)*t;
        if (c >= 0x20 && c < 0x7f) w += tbl[c - 0x1f] - tbl[c - 0x20];
        else if (c == 10) { if (stopAtNewline) break; if (best < w) best = w; w = 0; }
    }
    if (stopAtNewline) return w;
    return best < w ? w : best;
}

static int stage_InvalidateSlot(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:c350 - screen bounds, draw-order key and text rectangle */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    int32_t offY = p->offY;
    int left, top;
    if ((p->flags & SF_PERSPECTIVE) && (p->flags & SF_PATHMODE)) offY += p->depth;
    p->drawX = p->fxX - (int32_t)p->offX * p->scale;
    p->drawY = p->fxY - p->scale * offY;
    left = (int)(p->drawX >> 10); top = (int)(p->drawY >> 10);
    rect_set(&p->bounds, left, top, (int)(((int32_t)p->width * p->scale + p->drawX + 0x3ff) >> 10),
             (int)(((int32_t)p->height * p->scale + p->drawY + 0x3ff) >> 10));
    if (s > 0) {
        p->sortKey = (p->flags & SF_PERSPECTIVE) ? p->y : p->depth;
        stage_SetSlotDrawOrder(st, p->sortKey, s);
    }
    if (!(p->textAlign & 1) || !p->text) rect_empty(&p->textRect);
    else {
        int x = p->textDx + left, y = p->textDy + top, w = text_width_line(st, p->font, p->text, 0);
        int lines = 1, last = 0, h;
        const char *t;
        for (t = p->text; *t; t++) {
            unsigned c = (uint8_t)*t;
            if (c >= 0x20 && c < 0x7f) last = lines; else if (c == 10) lines++;
        }
        h = last * st->fontHeight[p->font == 1 ? 0 : 1];
        if (p->textAlign & 8) x -= w; else if (p->textAlign & 2) x -= w / 2;
        if (p->textAlign & 4) y -= h / 2;
        rect_set(&p->textRect, x, y, x + w, y + h);
    }
    return 1;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ animation */

static int frame_cel(const StageSlot *p, int frame)
{
    int c;
    if (p->seq == 0) return 0;
    c = rd_i16(p->pack.tblFrame + 2 * ((size_t)(p->dir * p->nSeq + p->seq) * (size_t)p->nFrames + (size_t)frame));
    return (c < 0 || p->nCels <= c) ? 0 : c;
}

static int frame_delay(const StageSlot *p, int frame)
{
    if (p->seq == 0) return 0;
    return rd_i16(p->pack.tblDelay + 2 * ((size_t)p->seq * (size_t)p->nFrames + (size_t)frame));
}

static int anim_NextFrame(Stage *st, int frame, int s)
{   /* orig: 1020:0596 - next frame with a non-empty cel, wrapping to 0; a new sequence starts at frame 0 */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    int f = 0, c = 0;
    if (p->seq == 0) return 0;
    if (p->lastSeq != p->seq) { p->lastSeq = p->seq; return 0; }
    f = frame;
    do {
        f++;
        if (f < 0 || p->nFrames <= f) f = 0;
        else c = frame_cel(p, f);
    } while (c == 0 && f != 0);
    return f;
}

int anim_Step(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1020:069c */
    StageSlot *p;
    int f, c, ret = -1, pathMoved = 0, motion = 0, changed = 0, timing;
    int32_t delay;
    if (!st->initialized || !SLOT_OK(s)) return 0;
    p = &st->slot[s];
    if (!(p->flags & SF_PACK)) return -1;
    f = p->frame;
    c = frame_cel(p, f);
    if (c == 0 && p->seq != 0) { f = anim_NextFrame(st, f, s); c = frame_cel(p, f); }
    delay = frame_delay(p, f);
    timing = (p->flags & SF_TIMED) ? (p->flags & SF_MOVING) != 0 : delay > 0;
    if (timing) {
        do {
            if (st->now - p->nextFrameTime < 0) { timing = 0; break; }
            if (delay > 0) {
                f = anim_NextFrame(st, f, s);
                c = frame_cel(p, f);
                delay = frame_delay(p, f);
            }
            if (p->flags & SF_MOVING) {
                if (!(p->flags & SF_PATHMODE)) {
                    int32_t mx = (int32_t)rd_i16(p->pack.tblDx + 2 * (size_t)c) * p->scale;
                    int32_t my = (int32_t)rd_i16(p->pack.tblDy + 2 * (size_t)c) * p->scale;
                    if (mx != 0 || my != 0) { p->fxX += mx; p->fxY += my; motion = 1; }
                } else if (anim_UpdatePath(st, s) == 1) pathMoved = 1;
            }
            timing = delay > 0;
            if (timing) p->nextFrameTime += delay;
            else if (!(p->flags & SF_TIMED)) { p->nextFrameTime = st->now; break; }
            else p->nextFrameTime += 5;
        } while (timing);
    } else p->nextFrameTime = st->now;
    p->frame = f;
    if (p->cel != c) {
        const uint8_t *cp = NULL; size_t cl = 0;
        p->cel = c;
        p->celFlags = rd_i16(p->pack.tblFlags + 2 * (size_t)c);
        p->offX = -rd_i16(p->pack.tblHotX + 2 * (size_t)c);
        p->offY = -rd_i16(p->pack.tblHotY + 2 * (size_t)c);
        if (c != 0 && p->havePack) {
            int32_t off = (int32_t)rd_u32(p->pack.tblOffset + 4 * (size_t)c);
            if (off > 0) { cp = p->pack.data + off; cl = p->pack.len - (size_t)off; }
        }
        if (p->curCel != cp) anim_SetCel(st, s, cp, cl);
        ret = 1; p->flags |= SF_DIRTY; changed = 1;
    }
    if (pathMoved || motion) {
        if (pathMoved) anim_ApplyScale(st, s); else anim_UpdateMotion(st, s);
        ret = 1; p->flags |= SF_DIRTY; changed = 1;
    }
    if (changed) stage_InvalidateSlot(st, s);
    return ret;
}

int anim_TickSlot(Stage *st, int s)
{   /* orig: 1018:99ec - apply the pending state */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    int inval = 0, rescale = 0;
    if (p->pendFlagsFlag) {
        unsigned old = p->flags;
        p->flags = (p->flags & p->flagsAnd) | p->flagsOr;
        p->flagsAnd = 0; p->flagsOr = 0;
        inval = p->flags != old;
        p->pendFlagsFlag = 0;
    }
    if (p->pendPackFlag) { anim_LoadCelPack(st, s); p->pendPackFlag = 0; }
    if (p->pendSeqFlag) { anim_LoadPendingPack(st, s); p->pendSeqFlag = 0; }
    if (p->pendDibFlag) { if (stage_LoadSlotDib(st, s) == 1) inval = 1; p->pendDibFlag = 0; }
    if (p->pendPosFlag) {
        if (p->pendX != p->x || p->pendY != p->y) {
            p->x = p->pendX; p->y = p->pendY;
            p->dX = (double)p->pendX; p->dY = (double)p->pendY;
            p->flags |= SF_DIRTY; rescale = 1;
        } else rescale = 0;
        p->pendPosFlag = 0;
    }
    if (p->pendOffFlag) {
        if (p->pendOffX != p->offX || p->pendOffY != p->offY) {
            p->offX = p->pendOffX; p->offY = p->pendOffY; p->flags |= SF_DIRTY; inval = 1;
        }
        p->pendOffFlag = 0;
    }
    if (p->pendDepthFlag) {
        if (p->pendDepth != p->depth) { p->depth = p->pendDepth; p->flags |= SF_DIRTY; inval = 1; }
        p->pendDepthFlag = 0;
    }
    if (p->pendScaleFlag) {
        if (p->pendScale != p->slotScale) {
            p->slotScale = p->pendScale; p->dScale = (double)p->slotScale / 1024.0;   /* DS:1106 */
            p->flags |= SF_DIRTY; rescale = 1;
        }
        p->pendScaleFlag = 0;
    }
    if (p->pendPathFlag) { anim_StartPath(st, s); p->pendPathFlag = 0; }
    if (p->pendTextFlag) { if (anim_ApplyText(st, s) == 1) inval = 1; p->pendTextFlag = 0; }
    else if (p->text) {
        uint16_t sum = text_sum(p->text);
        if (p->textSum != sum) { p->textSum = sum; p->flags |= SF_DIRTY; inval = 1; }
    }
    if (rescale) { anim_ApplyScale(st, s); inval = 1; }
    if (inval) stage_InvalidateSlot(st, s);
    return 1;
}

int anim_TickAllSlots(Stage *st)
{   /* orig: 1018:9ec2 */
    int s;
    if (!st->initialized) return 0;
    if (st->bgDirty) { stage_UploadBackground(st); stage_InvalidateSlot(st, 0); st->bgDirty = 0; }
    if (st->view.dirty) { stage_ApplyView(st); st->view.dirty = 0; }
    for (s = 0; s < STAGE_SLOTS; s++) if (s != SLOT_CURSOR) anim_TickSlot(st, s);
    return 1;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ drawing */

static void put_px(Screen *scr, int x, int y, uint8_t v, int opaque)
{
    uint8_t *d = scr->px + (size_t)y * SCREEN_W + x;
    if (opaque) *d = v;
    else if (v == 0) return;
    else if (v >= 0xf6) *d = scr->shadow[v - 0xf6][*d];
    else *d = v;
}

static const uint8_t *dib_row(const StageSlot *p, int row)
{   /* top-down row index -> pixel row */
    int stride = (p->width + 3) & ~3;
    return (p->flags & SF_DIBUP) ? p->celBits + (size_t)(p->height - 1 - row) * (size_t)stride
                                   : p->celBits + (size_t)row * (size_t)stride;
}

static int stage_BlitDib(Stage *st, int s, const StageRect *clip)
{   /* orig: 1018:bf6a, 1018:bc60, 1018:b850, 1018:b276 - stage_BlitCel (1:1 transparent, inline), blit_DibOpaque,
     * blit_DibScaled, blit_Rle8 */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    StageRect r;
    int x, y;
    if (!p->celBits || p->scale <= 0) return -1;
    if (p->flags & SF_COMPRESSED) {
        if (p->compression != 1) return -1;
        {   /* BI_RLE8: 00 00 end of line, 00 01 end, 00 02 dx dy (the original only skips dy lines), 00 n literal
             * (word aligned), n v run. Rows are bottom-up; 0 is transparent; drawn 1:1 at bounds.left/bottom. */
            const uint8_t *q = p->celBits;
            int row = 0, col = 0, guard = 0;
            if (!rect_intersect(&r, &p->bounds, clip)) return -1;
            while (row < p->height && guard++ < 4 * 1024 * 1024) {
                uint8_t n = *q++, v = *q++;
                int yy = p->bounds.bottom - 1 - row;
                if (n) { while (n--) { int xx = p->bounds.left + col++;
                            if (xx >= r.left && xx < r.right && yy >= r.top && yy < r.bottom) put_px(st->screen, xx, yy, v, 0); } }
                else if (v == 0) { row++; col = 0; }
                else if (v == 1) break;
                else if (v == 2) { q++; row += *q++; }
                else { int k = v; while (k--) { uint8_t pv = *q++; int xx = p->bounds.left + col++;
                            if (xx >= r.left && xx < r.right && yy >= r.top && yy < r.bottom) put_px(st->screen, xx, yy, pv, 0); }
                       if (v & 1) q++; }
            }
        }
        return 1;
    }
    if (p->scale != 0x400) {
        int32_t yFx = p->drawY, sc = p->scale;
        int cur = (int)(p->drawY >> 10), x0 = (int)(p->drawX >> 10), row;
        if (x0 < clip->left) x0 = clip->left;
        if (cur < clip->top) cur = clip->top;
        if (x0 >= clip->right || cur >= clip->bottom) return -1;
        for (row = 0; row < p->height && cur < clip->bottom; row++) {
            int ny;
            yFx += sc; ny = (int)(yFx >> 10);
            if (ny <= cur) continue;
            if (ny > clip->bottom) ny = clip->bottom;
            {
                const uint8_t *src = dib_row(p, row);
                int32_t xFx = p->drawX; int curX = x0, i;
                for (i = 0; i < p->width && curX < clip->right; i++) {
                    uint8_t v = src[i]; int nx;
                    xFx += sc; nx = (int)(xFx >> 10);
                    if (nx <= curX) continue;
                    if (nx > clip->right) nx = clip->right;
                    if (v) for (y = cur; y < ny; y++) for (x = curX; x < nx; x++) put_px(st->screen, x, y, v, 0);
                    curX = nx;
                }
            }
            cur = ny;
        }
        return 1;
    }
    if (!rect_intersect(&r, &p->bounds, clip)) return -1;
    for (y = r.top; y < r.bottom; y++) {
        const uint8_t *src = dib_row(p, y - (p->bounds.bottom - p->height));   /* anchored at the bottom edge */
        for (x = r.left; x < r.right; x++) put_px(st->screen, x, y, src[x - p->bounds.left], (p->flags & SF_OPAQUE) != 0);
    }
    return 1;
}

static int stage_DrawSprite(Stage *st, int s, const StageRect *clip)
{   /* orig: 1018:e1d8, 1018:ddba, 1018:d7ea, 1018:d430, 1018:cfa8, 1018:c8ec - the sprite draw dispatchers.
     * Every cel in the shipped packs is MMP1 (cel flag 4, 'MMP1'); the older 7-bit RLE branches of the
     * dispatchers are unreachable with the game data and are not reproduced. */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    Cel cel; ClipRect c;
    if (!p->curCel || p->scale <= 0) return -1;
    if (!(p->flags & SF_COMPRESSED) || p->compression != '1') return -1;
    if (cels_get(&p->pack, p->cel, &cel) != 0) return -1;
    c.left = clip->left; c.top = clip->top; c.right = clip->right; c.bottom = clip->bottom;
    render_draw_mmp1(st->screen, &cel, p->drawX, p->drawY, p->scale, &c);
    return 1;
}

static int stage_DrawText(Stage *st, int s, const StageRect *clip)
{   /* orig: 1018:e5dc (stage_DrawSlotClipped) - glyphs are cut from the font slot's strip with stage_BlitCel */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s], *fs;
    int fi = p->font == 1 ? 0 : 1, fslot = p->font == 1 ? SLOT_FONT1 : SLOT_FONT2;
    const int16_t *tbl = st->fontOffsets[fi];
    int lh = st->fontHeight[fi], y = p->textRect.top, rw = p->textRect.right - p->textRect.left;
    const char *t = p->text;
    StageRect saved;
    fs = &st->slot[fslot];
    if (!t || !fs->celDib) return -1;
    saved = fs->bounds;
    for (;;) {
        int x = p->textRect.left;
        unsigned c;
        if (p->textAlign & (8 | 2)) {
            int lw = text_width_line(st, p->font, t, 1);
            x = p->textRect.left + ((p->textAlign & 8) ? rw - lw : (rw - lw) / 2);
        }
        do {
            c = (uint8_t)*t++;
            if (c >= 0x20 && c < 0x7f) {
                int sx = tbl[c - 0x20], gw = tbl[c - 0x1f] - sx;
                StageRect g, cl;
                rect_set(&g, x, y, x + gw, y + lh);
                if (rect_intersect(&cl, clip, &g)) {
                    rect_set(&fs->bounds, x - sx, y, x + gw, y + lh);
                    stage_BlitDib(st, fslot, &cl);
                }
                x += gw;
            }
        } while (c != 0 && c != 10);
        y += lh;
        if (c == 0) break;
    }
    fs->bounds = saved;
    return 1;
}

static int stage_DrawSlot(Stage *st, int s, const StageRect *clip)
{   /* orig: 1018:e7f6 */
    StageSlot *p = &st->slot[s];
    StageRect r;
    int ret = -1;
    if ((p->flags & SF_VISIBLE) && (p->flags & SF_DRAWABLE)) {
        if (rect_intersect(&r, &p->bounds, clip)) {
            if (p->celDib) ret = stage_BlitDib(st, s, &r);
            else if (p->curCel) ret = stage_DrawSprite(st, s, &r);
        }
        if ((p->textAlign & 1) && p->text && rect_intersect(&r, &p->textRect, clip)) ret = stage_DrawText(st, s, &r);
    }
    p->flags &= ~SF_REDRAW;
    return ret;
}

void stage_Compose(Stage *st)
{   /* the redraw half of engine_FrameUpdate. orig: 1018:b0da, 1018:b06a - stage_FillRect / stage_CopyPalette fill what no
     * opaque slot covers with the fill colour, the slots are drawn in drawOrder, then the cursor slot */
    StageRect full;
    int i;
    rect_set(&full, 0, 0, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H);
    memset(st->screen->px, (uint8_t)st->fillColor, sizeof st->screen->px);
    for (i = 0; i < STAGE_SLOTS; i++) {
        int s = st->drawOrder[i];
        if (s < 0x65) stage_DrawSlot(st, s, &full);
    }
    stage_DrawSlot(st, SLOT_CURSOR, &full);
}

void stage_FrameUpdate(Stage *st, int32_t tick)
{   /* orig: 1008:5602 engine_FrameUpdate; the animation half of 1020:0dd6 stage_ComputeDirtyRects */
    int s;
    if (!st->initialized) return;
    if (st->running == 1) { st->now += tick - st->lastTick; st->lastTick = tick; }
    if (st->running != 1) return;
    if (st->animRunning == 1) anim_TickAllSlots(st);
    stage_UpdateWaitOrFade(st);
    if (st->bgPaletteDirty) { stage_UpdateEffects(st, 0); st->bgPaletteDirty = 0; }
    for (s = 0; s < 0x65; s++) {
        if (!(st->slot[s].flags & SF_PACK)) st->slot[s].nextFrameTime = st->now;
        else anim_Step(st, s);
    }
    anim_TickSlot(st, SLOT_CURSOR);           /* orig: 1008:54fc stage_UpdateCursor (its own redraw with save-under) */
    stage_Compose(st);
    for (s = 0; s < STAGE_SLOTS; s++) st->slot[s].flags &= ~SF_DIRTY;   /* 1020:1408 stage_BuildClipRegion */
}

```

`src/stage.h`:

```h
/*
 * stage.h - the Stage: 104 sprite slots, their animation, the view (perspective) and the frame composer.
 *
 * The original Stage object (allocated by stage_Init 1008:3ce0) keeps every per-slot attribute in parallel
 * arrays of 104 entries. Most attributes have a *pending* copy written by the TAKE executors and UI code
 * (take_SetSlotPack, stage_SetSlotPosition, ...); anim_TickSlot (1018:99ec) applies the pending copies once
 * per frame, then the frame composer steps the animations (anim_Step 1020:069c) and draws the slots in depth
 * order. This module keeps that model; field comments give the original Stage offsets.
 *
 * Slots: 0 = background (Back B1), 1..0x64 = sprites/UI, 0x65 = mouse cursor (skipped by anim_TickAllSlots,
 * drawn last), 0x66 / 0x67 = the two bitmap fonts used for slot text.
 *
 * Coordinates: a slot's position (x, y) is in *view* units. With the default view (flat) one unit is one
 * screen pixel. The screen position fxX/fxY is 22.10 fixed point (1024 = one pixel); scale 1024 = 1:1.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_STAGE_H
#define IZZY_STAGE_H

#include "render.h"

#define STAGE_SLOTS   0x68
#define SLOT_CURSOR   0x65
#define SLOT_FONT1    0x66
#define SLOT_FONT2    0x67

/* slotFlags (Stage+0x4ea8) */
enum {
    SF_VISIBLE   = 0x0001,   /* has something to draw */
    SF_DIRTY     = 0x0002,   /* changed since the last frame */
    SF_PACK      = 0x0004,   /* a CELS pack is loaded (anim_Step runs) */
    SF_DRAWABLE  = 0x0008,   /* has bits */
    SF_REDRAW    = 0x0010,   /* marked by stage_ComputeDirtyRects */
    SF_DIBUP     = 0x0020,   /* DIB rows are stored bottom-up (set for DIBs, cleared for pack cels) */
    SF_COMPRESSED= 0x0040,   /* MMP1 (sprite) or RLE8 (DIB) */
    SF_OPAQUE    = 0x0080,   /* drawn with a plain copy; hides what is below */
    SF_PERSPECTIVE = 0x1000, /* position is mapped through the view (Path flag bit 0) */
    SF_PATHMODE  = 0x2000,   /* motion follows the path, not the cels' dx/dy (Path flag bit 1); z lifts the sprite */
    SF_MOVING    = 0x4000,   /* path or dx/dy motion active */
    SF_TIMED     = 0x8000    /* keep ticking every 5 ticks even when the frame delay is 0 */
};

typedef struct StageRect { int left, top, right, bottom; } StageRect;

typedef struct StageSlot {
    /* ---- pending (written by setters, applied by anim_TickSlot) ---- */
    int pendPackFlag;  const uint8_t *pendPack; size_t pendPackLen;      /* 0x2948 / 0x2a18 */
    int pendSeqFlag;   int pendSeq, pendDir;                             /* 0x2ae8 / 0x2bb8 / 0x2c88 (dir 1-based) */
    int pendDibFlag;   const uint8_t *pendDib;                           /* 0x2d58 / 0x2e28 (BMP file or DIB) */
    int pendPosFlag;   int32_t pendX, pendY;                             /* 0x2ef8 / 0x2fc8 / 0x3168 */
    int pendScaleFlag; int32_t pendScale;                                /* 0x3308 / 0x33d8 */
    int pendOffFlag;   int pendOffX, pendOffY;                           /* 0x3578 / 0x3648 / 0x3718 */
    int pendDepthFlag; int pendDepth;                                    /* 0x37e8 / 0x38b8 */
    int pendFlagsFlag; unsigned flagsAnd, flagsOr;                       /* 0x3988 / 0x3a58 / 0x3b28 */
    int pendPathFlag;  int32_t pathToX, pathToY; int pathToZ; int32_t pathTime; unsigned pathFlags;
                                                     /* 0x3bf8 / 0x3cc8 / 0x3e68 / 0x4008 / 0x40d8 / 0x4278 */
    int pendTextFlag;  const char *pendText; int pendFont, pendTextDx, pendTextDy, pendTextAlign;
                                                     /* 0x4348 / 0x4418 / 0x45b8 / 0x4688 / 0x4758 / 0x4828 */
    /* ---- live ---- */
    const uint8_t *dib;          /* 0x48f8 handle -> the DIB / BMP data now shown */
    const uint8_t *celDib;       /* 0x49c8 BITMAPINFOHEADER of a DIB cel (NULL for sprite cels) */
    const uint8_t *celBits;      /* 0x4b68 DIB pixel bits */
    const uint8_t *curCel;       /* 0x4d08 current pack cel (BITMAPINFOHEADER + MMP1 data) */
    size_t curCelLen;
    unsigned flags;              /* 0x4ea8 */
    int32_t compression;         /* 0x4f78 DIB biCompression, or '1' for MMP1 sprites */
    int width, height;           /* 0x5118 / 0x51e8 */
    int offX, offY;              /* 0x52b8 / 0x5388 = -hotX / -hotY */
    int depth;                   /* 0x5458 z: draw order key (or lift in path mode) */
    int32_t x, y;                /* 0x5528 / 0x56c8 view position */
    int32_t slotScale;           /* 0x5868 */
    int32_t sortKey;             /* 0x5a08 */
    double dX, dY, dScale;       /* 0x5ba8 / 0x5ee8 / 0x6228 */
    int32_t fxX, fxY;            /* 0x6568 / 0x6708 screen position (22.10) */
    int32_t drawX, drawY;        /* 0x68a8 / 0x6a48 top-left (22.10) */
    int32_t scale;               /* 0x6be8 */
    int32_t orderKey;            /* 0x6d88 key the slot is sorted by in drawOrder */
    const char *text;            /* 0x6f28 */
    int font, textDx, textDy;    /* 0x70c8 / 0x7198 / 0x7268 */
    unsigned textAlign;          /* 0x7338 bit0 on, bit1 centre, bit2 v-centre, bit3 right */
    uint16_t textSum;            /* 0x7408 */
    StageRect bounds;            /* 0x75a8 */
    StageRect textRect;          /* 0x7c28 */
    int32_t pathFromX, pathFromY; int pathFromZ;  /* 0x85e8 / 0x8788 / 0x8928 */
    int32_t pathEndX, pathEndY;   int pathEndZ;   /* 0x89f8 / 0x8b98 / 0x8d38 */
    int32_t pathStart, pathDur;                   /* 0x8e08 / 0x8fa8 */
    /* pack state */
    int nSeq, nDir, nCels, nFrames;               /* 0x9148 / 0x9218 / 0x92e8 / 0x93b8 */
    int seq, dir, frame, cel, celFlags;           /* 0x9488 / 0x9558 / 0x9628 / 0x96f8 / 0x97c8 */
    int32_t nextFrameTime;                        /* 0x9898 */
    int lastSeq;                                  /* 0x9a38 */
    CelPack pack; int havePack;                   /* 0x9b08 / 0x9bd8 and the table pointers 0x9d78..0xaa78 */
} StageSlot;

typedef struct StageView {   /* 0x2924..0x2944 (longs) and 0x46c..0x4ce (doubles) */
    int32_t raw[9]; int dirty;                     /* 0x2924 W H TLx TRx Ty BLx BRx By scale, 0x2922 */
    double w, h, tlx, trx, ty, blx, brx, by;       /* 0x46c..0x4a4 */
    double farScale, logFar, nearScale, logNear;   /* 0x4ac 0x4b4 0x4bc 0x4c4 */
    int flat, curved;                              /* 0x4cc 0x4ce */
} StageView;

typedef struct Stage {
    int initialized, running, animRunning;        /* 0x504 0x500 0x502 */
    int32_t now, lastTick;                        /* 0x4d0 0x4d4 */
    int fillColor;                                /* 0x4d8 (also the fade level argument) */
    int fadeLevel;                                /* 0x4da */
    int32_t waitStart, waitDuration;              /* 0x4dc 0x4e4 */
    int waiting, fading, fadeActive;              /* 0x4e8 0x4ea 0x4ec */
    int needsFullRedraw;                          /* 0x4ee */
    int bgDirty; const uint8_t *bgPending;        /* 0x291e / 0x2920 */
    int bgAnimSlot;                               /* 0x512 */
    StageView view;
    int drawOrder[STAGE_SLOTS];                   /* 0x74d8 */
    StageSlot slot[STAGE_SLOTS];
    const int16_t *fontOffsets[2]; int fontHeight[2];   /* DS:00ee / DS:00ec and DS:01b0 / DS:01ae */
    /* palette: entries 1..245 are the game's; 0 and 246..255 are fixed */
    uint8_t pal[256][3];          /* 0x71e / 0x91e / 0xb1e - scene palette (the shadow tables are built from it) */
    uint8_t palShown[256][3];     /* 0xd1e - colours while not faded (Show / Wait cross-fades into it) */
    uint8_t palFaded[256][3];     /* 0x111e - colours while fading to / faded to the fade colour */
    int paletteVisible;           /* 0x4f2: 1 = palShown is on screen, 0 = faded */
    int bgPaletteDirty;           /* 0x4f8: take the palette from the new background (once, see below) */
    int32_t lastEffectTick;       /* 0x4e0 */
    int paletteLoadsLeft;         /* DS:0a3a = 1: only the first background ever sets the palette */
    Screen *screen;               /* screen->pal is the displayed palette (0x151e / the DIB colour table) */
} Stage;

/* orig: 1008:3ce0 stage_Init (the GDI / WinG parts are the port layer's job). */
void stage_init(Stage *st, Screen *screen);

/* ---- setters (pending state) ---- */
int  take_SetSlotPack(Stage *st, int slot, const uint8_t *ani, size_t len);          /* orig: 1008:4dae */
int  take_SetSlotSeqDir(Stage *st, int slot, int seq, int dir);                      /* orig: 1008:4e76 */
int  take_SetSlotDib(Stage *st, int slot, const uint8_t *dib);                       /* orig: 1008:4b9c */
int  take_SetBackground(Stage *st, const uint8_t *bmp);                              /* orig: 1008:49da */
int  stage_SetSlotPosition(Stage *st, int slot, int32_t x, int32_t y);               /* orig: 1008:5064 */
int  stage_SetSlotDepth(Stage *st, int slot, int depth);                             /* orig: 1008:50bc */
int  stage_SetSlotScale(Stage *st, int slot, int32_t scale);                         /* orig: 1008:50fe */
int  stage_SetSlotFlags(Stage *st, int slot, int set, unsigned mask);                /* orig: 1008:514c */
int  stage_SetSlotText(Stage *st, int slot, const char *text, int font, int dx, int dy, unsigned align); /* orig: 1008:51e2 */
int  take_SetSpritePath(Stage *st, int slot, int32_t x, int32_t y, int z, int32_t ticks, unsigned pathFlags); /* orig: 1008:4ec2 */
int  take_SetView(Stage *st, int32_t w, int32_t h, int32_t tlx, int32_t trx, int32_t ty,
                  int32_t blx, int32_t brx, int32_t by, int32_t scale);               /* orig: 1008:4c64 */
void take_SetDefaultView(Stage *st);                                                 /* orig: 1008:4d70 */
int  take_ClearScene(Stage *st);                                                     /* orig: 1008:4586 */
int  take_SetAnimRunning(Stage *st, int on);                                         /* orig: 1008:54c4 */
int  take_Wait(Stage *st, int32_t ticks);                                            /* orig: 1008:4932 */
int  take_Fade(Stage *st, int level, int32_t ticks);                                 /* orig: 1008:496e */
int  stage_SetRunning(Stage *st, int on);                                            /* orig: 1008:54a8 */
/* Show a picture fitted to the screen in slot 0x1f (NULL removes it). orig: 1008:4a8a take_ResetScene */
int  take_ShowPicture(Stage *st, const uint8_t *dib);
/* Palette entries [start, start+count) (capped at 245) from RGB triples: 1 if any differs. orig: 1008:4668 */
int  stage_PaletteDiffers(const Stage *st, int count, int start, const uint8_t (*rgb)[3]);
/* Set palette entries [start, start+count) (capped at 245); shown at once unless faded. orig: 1008:4780 */
int  stage_SetPaletteRange(Stage *st, int count, int start, const uint8_t (*rgb)[3]);

/* Topmost visible slot 1..0x64 whose bounds contain (x, y), or 0. orig: 1008:5250 */
int  stage_HitTest(const Stage *st, int x, int y);

/* ---- per-frame ---- */
int  anim_TickAllSlots(Stage *st);              /* orig: 1018:9ec2 */
int  anim_TickSlot(Stage *st, int slot);        /* orig: 1018:99ec */
int  anim_Step(Stage *st, int slot);            /* orig: 1020:069c */
/* orig: 1008:5602 engine_FrameUpdate. Advances the clock by (tick - lastTick) while running, applies pending
```
