```h
 * state, steps every animation and redraws the whole screen (the original redraws only dirty rectangles; the
 * resulting picture is the same). */
void stage_FrameUpdate(Stage *st, int32_t tick);
void stage_Compose(Stage *st);                  /* draw every visible slot in order (no stepping) */

#endif

```

`src/take.c`:

```c
#include "take.h"

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

int take_open(TakeReader *r, const uint8_t *data, size_t len)
{
    memset(r, 0, sizeof *r);
    if (len < 8 || memcmp(data, "TAKE", 4) != 0) return -1;
    r->data = data;
    r->len = len;
    r->pos = 8;   /* orig: game_ProcessStep sets readPtr = data + 8 */
    return 0;
}

static int rd(TakeReader *r, uint8_t *b) { if (r->pos >= r->len) return 0; *b = r->data[r->pos++]; return 1; }

static int read_delimited(TakeReader *r, uint8_t closer, char *out, size_t cap)
{   /* orig: 1008:d200 take_ReadString (9A..9B) and the 9C..9D expression text */
    size_t n = 0;
    uint8_t b;
    for (;;) {
        if (!rd(r, &b)) return 0;
        if (b == closer) break;
        if (n + 1 < cap) out[n++] = (char)b;
    }
    out[n] = 0;
    return 1;
}

int take_next(TakeReader *r, TakeStmt *s)
{
    /* orig: 1010:0944 take_ParseStatement */
    uint8_t b;
    memset(s, 0, sizeof *s);
    if (r->ended) return TAKE_EOF;
    if (!rd(r, &b)) return TAKE_ERR;
    if (b == 0x90) {                                   /* start time */
        uint8_t lo, hi;
        if (!rd(r, &lo) || !rd(r, &hi)) return TAKE_ERR;
        s->time = (int16_t)(lo | (hi << 8));
        if (!rd(r, &b)) return TAKE_ERR;
    }
    s->op = b;
    if (b == TK_END) { r->ended = 1; return TAKE_EOF; }
    if (!((b >= 0xA0 && b <= 0xA5) || (b >= 0xB0 && b <= 0xB7) || (b >= 0xC0 && b <= 0xC3)))
        return TAKE_ERR;                               /* "Invalid Take Command" */
    for (;;) {                                         /* arguments until FE (orig: take_TokenClass classes) */
        TakeArg *a;
        if (!rd(r, &b)) return TAKE_ERR;               /* "File ends unexpected-Load...Cmnd" */
        if (b == 0xFE) return TAKE_OK;
        if (s->nargs >= TAKE_MAX_ARGS) return TAKE_ERR;
        a = &s->args[s->nargs++];
        if (b >= 0x81 && b <= 0x89) { a->type = TA_INT; a->v = b - 0x80; continue; }   /* small constants 1..9 */
        switch (b) {
        case 0x8A: { uint8_t v; if (!rd(r, &v)) return TAKE_ERR; a->type = TA_INT; a->v = v; break; }
        case 0x8B: {
            if (r->pos + 2 > r->len) return TAKE_ERR;
            a->type = TA_INT; a->v = rd_i16(r->data + r->pos); r->pos += 2; break;
        }
        case 0x8D: {
            if (r->pos + 4 > r->len) return TAKE_ERR;
            a->type = TA_INT; a->v = rd_i32(r->data + r->pos); r->pos += 4; break;
        }
        case 0x90: {
            if (r->pos + 2 > r->len) return TAKE_ERR;
            a->type = TA_TIME; a->v = rd_i16(r->data + r->pos); r->pos += 2; break;
        }
        case 0x91: { uint8_t v; if (!rd(r, &v)) return TAKE_ERR; a->type = TA_FLAG; a->v = v; break; }
        case 0x92: {                                   /* 16-bit flag word (orig: LoadPathCmnd reads two bytes) */
            if (r->pos + 2 > r->len) return TAKE_ERR;
            a->type = TA_FLAG; a->v = (uint16_t)rd_i16(r->data + r->pos); r->pos += 2; break;
        }
        case 0x9A: a->type = TA_STR;  if (!read_delimited(r, 0x9B, a->s, sizeof a->s)) return TAKE_ERR; break;
        case 0x9C: a->type = TA_EXPR; if (!read_delimited(r, 0x9D, a->s, sizeof a->s)) return TAKE_ERR;
                   if (r->eval) a->evaluated = r->eval(r->evalCtx, a->s, &a->v);   /* evaluated now, as in the original */
                   break;
        default: return TAKE_ERR;                      /* "Invalid data type-Load...Cmnd" */
        }
    }
}

const char *take_op_name(uint8_t op)
{
    switch (op) {
    case TK_VEP: return "Vep";   case TK_AUDIO: return "Audio"; case TK_SFX: return "Sfx";
    case TK_DONE: return "Done"; case TK_CLEAR: return "Clear"; case TK_CUE: return "Cue";
    case TK_DROP: return "Drop"; case TK_BACK: return "Back";   case TK_SHOW: return "Show";
    case TK_SPOT: return "Spot"; case TK_HIDE: return "Hide";   case TK_STAR: return "Star";
    case TK_FADE: return "Fade"; case TK_AVI: return "Avi";
    case TK_PAL: return "Pal";   case TK_GRID: return "Grid";   case TK_CEL: return "Cel";
    case TK_PATH: return "Path"; case TK_END: return "End";
    default: return "?";
    }
}

int32_t take_arg_int(const TakeStmt *s, int i)
{
    const char *e, *p;
    if (i < 0 || i >= s->nargs) return 0;
    if (s->args[i].type != TA_EXPR || s->args[i].evaluated) return s->args[i].v;
    e = s->args[i].s;                       /* "NAME + k": the constant part; the variable needs the VM */
    p = strrchr(e, '+');
    if (!p) p = strrchr(e, '-');
    if (!p) return 0;
    return (*p == '-' ? -1 : 1) * (int32_t)strtol(p + 1, NULL, 10);
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- the Load*Cmnd readers */

typedef struct ReaderRule {
    uint8_t op;
    int8_t ints[12]; int8_t nints;   /* field offset of the n-th integer; nints < 0: every integer -> ints[0] */
    int8_t str;                      /* where the string goes (-1 none) */
    int8_t flag;                     /* where a 91/92 flag goes */
    int8_t time;                     /* where a 90 time goes (u16; the following word stays 0) */
    uint16_t def4e;
} ReaderRule;

static const ReaderRule g_rules[] = {
    /* A1 Audio: channel, volume, "file", flag */          { 0xA1, { TF_ARG1, TF_4E }, 2, TF_ARG2, TF_50, -1, 0 },
    /* A2 Sfx:   id, volume, "file" */                     { 0xA2, { TF_ARG1, TF_4E }, 2, TF_ARG2, -1, -1, 0 },
    /* B0 Drop:  level, t= */                              { 0xB0, { TF_ARG1 }, -1, -1, -1, TF_ARG2, 0 },
    /* B1 Back:  "file" */                                 { 0xB1, { 0 }, 0, TF_ARG1, -1, -1, 0 },
    /* B2 Show:  t= */                                     { 0xB2, { 0 }, 0, -1, -1, TF_ARG1, 0 },
    /* B3 Spot:  id, mode, n, 4 corners (x, y), flag */    { 0xB3, { TF_ARG1, TF_ARG2, TF_ARG2HI, TF_44, TF_S0, TF_S2, TF_S4, TF_S6,
                                                                     TF_4E, TF_50, TF_52, TF_54 }, 12, -1, TF_ARG2, -1, 0 },
    /* B5 Star:  n */                                      { 0xB5, { TF_ARG1 }, -1, -1, -1, -1, 0 },
    /* B6 Fade:  level, t= */                              { 0xB6, { TF_ARG1 }, -1, -1, -1, TF_ARG2, 0 },
    /* B7 Avi:   x, y, w, h, "file" */                     { 0xB7, { TF_ARG1, TF_ARG2, TF_ARG2HI, TF_44 }, 4, TF_S0, -1, -1, 0 },
    /* C0 Pal:   "file" */                                 { 0xC0, { 0 }, 0, TF_ARG1, -1, -1, 0 },
    /* C1 Grid:  W H tlx trx ty blx brx by scale */        { 0xC1, { TF_ARG1, TF_ARG2, TF_ARG2HI, TF_44, TF_S0, TF_S2, TF_S4, TF_S6, TF_4E }, 9, -1, -1, -1, 0 },
    /* C2 Cel:   slot, scale (0x400), "file" */            { 0xC2, { TF_S6, TF_4E }, 2, TF_ARG1, -1, -1, 0x400 },
    /* C3 Path:  slot seq dir x y z, t=, flag */           { 0xC3, { TF_ARG1, TF_ARG2, TF_ARG2HI, TF_44, TF_S0, TF_S2 }, 6, -1, TF_4E, TF_S4, 0 },
};

static void put16(TakeFields *f, int off, int32_t v) { f->b[off] = (uint8_t)v; f->b[off + 1] = (uint8_t)(v >> 8); }

void take_decode(const TakeStmt *s, TakeFields *f)
{
    size_t r;
    int i, n = 0;
    memset(f, 0, sizeof *f);                     /* the readers clear 6 dwords from arg1 */
    for (r = 0; r < sizeof g_rules / sizeof g_rules[0]; r++) if (g_rules[r].op == s->op) break;
    if (r == sizeof g_rules / sizeof g_rules[0]) return;
    if (g_rules[r].def4e) put16(f, TF_4E, g_rules[r].def4e);
    for (i = 0; i < s->nargs; i++) {
        const TakeArg *a = &s->args[i];
        const ReaderRule *q = &g_rules[r];
        switch (a->type) {
        case TA_INT: case TA_EXPR:
            if (q->nints < 0) put16(f, q->ints[0], take_arg_int(s, i));
            else if (n < q->nints) put16(f, q->ints[n], take_arg_int(s, i));
            n++;
            break;
        case TA_FLAG: if (q->flag >= 0) put16(f, q->flag, a->v); break;
        case TA_TIME: if (q->time >= 0) { put16(f, q->time, (uint16_t)a->v); put16(f, q->time + 2, 0); } break;
        case TA_STR:
            if (q->str >= 0) {
                size_t k, lim = sizeof f->b - (size_t)q->str - 1;
                if (lim > 13) lim = 13;
                for (k = 0; k < lim && a->s[k]; k++) f->b[q->str + k] = (uint8_t)a->s[k];
                f->b[q->str + k] = 0;
            }
            break;
        default: break;
        }
    }
}

int16_t take_field(const TakeFields *f, int off) { return (int16_t)(f->b[off] | (f->b[off + 1] << 8)); }
int32_t take_field32(const TakeFields *f, int off) { return (int32_t)((uint32_t)(uint16_t)take_field(f, off) | ((uint32_t)(uint16_t)take_field(f, off + 2) << 16)); }
const char *take_field_str(const TakeFields *f, int off) { return (const char *)f->b + off; }

int take_nth_int(const TakeStmt *s, int n, int32_t def)
{
    /* the Load*Cmnd readers number integer and expression arguments in order, apart from strings/flags/times */
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < s->nargs; i++)
        if (s->args[i].type == TA_INT || s->args[i].type == TA_EXPR) { if (n-- == 0) return i; }
    (void)def;
    return -1;
}

int32_t take_typed_arg(const TakeStmt *s, int type, int32_t def)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < s->nargs; i++) if (s->args[i].type == type) return s->args[i].v;
    return def;
}

const char *take_arg_str(const TakeStmt *s, int i)
{
    for (; i < s->nargs; i++) if (s->args[i].type == TA_STR) return s->args[i].s;
    return "";
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- runner */

int take_runner_start(TakeRunner *t, const uint8_t *data, size_t len, const TakeHost *host)
{
    memset(t, 0, sizeof *t);
    if (host) t->host = *host;
    if (take_open(&t->r, data, len) != 0) return -1;
    t->r.eval = t->host.eval; t->r.evalCtx = t->host.ctx;
    t->status = take_next(&t->r, &t->cur);
    t->have = (t->status == TAKE_OK);
    return t->status == TAKE_ERR ? -1 : 0;
}

static void execute(TakeRunner *t, const TakeStmt *s)
{
    /* orig: 1010:12e4 take_ExecuteStatement - opcode dispatch (Cue A5, Star B5 and Pal C0 are no-ops there) */
    TakeHost *h = &t->host;
    if (h->exec) {                       /* the host reproduces take_ExecuteStatement from the reader fields */
        TakeFields f;
        take_decode(s, &f);
        h->exec(h->ctx, s, &f);
        t->executed++;
        return;
    }
    switch (s->op) {
    case TK_AUDIO: if (h->audio) h->audio(h->ctx, take_arg_int(s, 0), take_arg_str(s, 1), take_arg_int(s, 2), take_arg_int(s, 3)); break;
    case TK_SFX:   if (h->sfx) h->sfx(h->ctx, take_arg_int(s, 0), take_arg_str(s, 1), take_arg_int(s, 2)); break;
    case TK_DONE:  if (h->done) h->done(h->ctx); break;
    case TK_CLEAR: if (h->clear) h->clear(h->ctx); break;
    case TK_BACK:  if (h->background) h->background(h->ctx, take_arg_str(s, 0)); break;
    case TK_SHOW:  if (h->show) h->show(h->ctx, s->nargs ? take_arg_int(s, 0) : 0); break;
    case TK_HIDE:  if (h->hide) h->hide(h->ctx); break;
    case TK_SPOT:  if (h->spot) h->spot(h->ctx, s); break;
    case TK_DROP: case TK_FADE: if (h->fade) h->fade(h->ctx, take_arg_int(s, 0), take_arg_int(s, 1)); break;
    case TK_AVI:   if (h->avi) h->avi(h->ctx, take_arg_int(s, 0), take_arg_int(s, 1), take_arg_int(s, 2), take_arg_int(s, 3), take_arg_str(s, 4)); break;
    case TK_GRID:  if (h->grid) h->grid(h->ctx, s); break;
    case TK_CEL:   if (h->cel) {   /* LoadCelCmnd: 1st integer = slot, 2nd = scale (default 0x400), string = file */
                       int i0 = take_nth_int(s, 0, 0), i1 = take_nth_int(s, 1, 0);
                       h->cel(h->ctx, i0 >= 0 ? take_arg_int(s, i0) : 0, take_arg_str(s, 0), i1 >= 0 ? take_arg_int(s, i1) : 0x400);
                   } break;
    case TK_PATH:  if (h->path) h->path(h->ctx, s); break;
    case TK_VEP:   if (h->vep) h->vep(h->ctx, s); break;
    default: break;   /* Cue, Star, Pal */
    }
    t->executed++;
}

int take_runner_tick(TakeRunner *t, int32_t now)
{
    /* orig: take_ExecuteStatement returns early while now < statement time; game_ProcessStep calls it per tick */
    while (t->have && t->cur.time <= now) {
        execute(t, &t->cur);
        t->status = take_next(&t->r, &t->cur);
        t->have = (t->status == TAKE_OK);
    }
    if (t->status == TAKE_ERR) return -1;
    return t->have ? 1 : 0;
}

```

`src/take.h`:

```h
/*
 * take.h - TAKE scene scripts (.DEF): parser and time-driven runner.
 *
 * File: "TAKE" u32 length, then statements:
 *   [90 lo hi]  start time in 60 Hz ticks since the take began (sign-extended; orig TakePlayer+0x38)
 *   op          A0..A5, B0..B7, C0..C3;  FF = end of take
 *   args...     8A u8 | 8B i16 | 8D i32 | 9A str 9B | 9C expr-text 9D | 91/92 flag-byte | 90 u16 (time arg)
 *   FE          end of statement
 * orig: 1010:0944 take_ParseStatement + the Load*Cmnd argument readers; 1010:12e4 take_ExecuteStatement;
 *       1010:2486 game_ProcessStep (per-tick driver).
 * Command names are the developers' own, from the readers' error strings ("...-Load<Name>Cmnd").
 */
#ifndef IZZY_TAKE_H
#define IZZY_TAKE_H

#include "izzy_common.h"

enum TakeOp {
    TK_VEP = 0xA0, TK_AUDIO = 0xA1, TK_SFX = 0xA2, TK_DONE = 0xA3, TK_CLEAR = 0xA4, TK_CUE = 0xA5,
    TK_DROP = 0xB0, TK_BACK = 0xB1, TK_SHOW = 0xB2, TK_SPOT = 0xB3, TK_HIDE = 0xB4, TK_STAR = 0xB5,
    TK_FADE = 0xB6, TK_AVI = 0xB7,
    TK_PAL = 0xC0, TK_GRID = 0xC1, TK_CEL = 0xC2, TK_PATH = 0xC3,
    TK_END = 0xFF
};

enum TakeArgType { TA_INT, TA_STR, TA_EXPR, TA_FLAG, TA_TIME };

typedef struct TakeArg {
    uint8_t type;
    int32_t v;          /* TA_INT / TA_FLAG / TA_TIME */
    char    s[64];      /* TA_STR (file name) / TA_EXPR (VEP expression source text, e.g. "STATE1 + 0") */
    int     evaluated;  /* TA_EXPR: v holds the value computed when the statement was parsed */
} TakeArg;

#define TAKE_MAX_ARGS 16

typedef struct TakeStmt {
    uint8_t op;
    int32_t time;       /* start time in ticks (0 when the statement has no 90 prefix) */
    int     nargs;
    TakeArg args[TAKE_MAX_ARGS];
} TakeStmt;

typedef struct TakeReader {
    const uint8_t *data; size_t len, pos;
    int ended;          /* saw FF (orig: TakePlayer->moreStatements) */
    /* optional: evaluates {expr} arguments while parsing, as the original does (1008:d25a take_ParseVepExpr) */
    int (*eval)(void *ctx, const char *expr, int32_t *out);
    void *evalCtx;
} TakeReader;

enum { TAKE_OK = 1, TAKE_EOF = 0, TAKE_ERR = -1 };

/* Open a take in memory (checks the "TAKE" header). Returns 0 on success. */
int  take_open(TakeReader *r, const uint8_t *data, size_t len);
/* Parse the next statement. TAKE_OK, TAKE_EOF (FF reached), or TAKE_ERR (bad token / truncated). */
int  take_next(TakeReader *r, TakeStmt *s);
const char *take_op_name(uint8_t op);

/* Integer value of argument i (expressions: the trailing "+ k" constant; variables are host-evaluated). */
int32_t take_arg_int(const TakeStmt *s, int i);
const char *take_arg_str(const TakeStmt *s, int i);   /* first string argument from index i on, or "" */
/* Index (into args) of the n-th integer/expression argument, or -1 (the readers' "iVar == n" numbering). */
int  take_nth_int(const TakeStmt *s, int n, int32_t def);
/* The TakePlayer argument block (+0x3e..+0x55) as the Load*Cmnd readers fill it. Offsets in b[]:
 *   0 arg1, 2 arg2 (lo), 4 arg2 hi, 6 +0x44, 8/10/12/14 argString[0/2/4/6], 16 +0x4e, 18 +0x50, 20 +0x52, 22 +0x54.
 * Strings are copied into the block at a per-command offset (up to 13 characters), overlapping later fields. */
typedef struct TakeFields { uint8_t b[32]; } TakeFields;
enum { TF_ARG1 = 0, TF_ARG2 = 2, TF_ARG2HI = 4, TF_44 = 6, TF_S0 = 8, TF_S2 = 10, TF_S4 = 12, TF_S6 = 14,
       TF_4E = 16, TF_50 = 18, TF_52 = 20, TF_54 = 22 };
/* orig: 1008:d3aa, 1008:d80c, 1008:dbd8, 1008:de80, 1008:e064, 1008:e220, 1008:e588, 1008:e7c8, 1008:ea6e,
 *       1008:ed98, 1010:0000, 1010:02c2, 1010:05be, 1008:d0e8, 1008:d152, 1008:d200 -
 * LoadAudioCmnd, LoadsfxCmnd, LoadDropCmnd, LoadBackCmnd, LoadShowCmnd, LoadSpotCmnd, LoadStarCmnd, LoadFadeCmnd,
 * LoadAviCmnd, LoadPalCmnd, LoadGridCmnd, LoadCelCmnd, LoadPathCmnd and the token helpers take_TokenClass,
 * take_ReadByteArgs, take_ReadString (the token grammar itself is in take_next) */
void take_decode(const TakeStmt *s, TakeFields *f);
int16_t take_field(const TakeFields *f, int off);
int32_t take_field32(const TakeFields *f, int off);
const char *take_field_str(const TakeFields *f, int off);
/* Value of the first TA_TIME / TA_FLAG argument, or def. */
int32_t take_typed_arg(const TakeStmt *s, int type, int32_t def);

/* ---- runner: executes statements when the take clock reaches their start time ---- */
typedef struct TakeHost {
    void *ctx;
    void (*audio)(void *ctx, int channel, const char *file, int volume, int flags);   /* file "" = stop */
    void (*sfx)(void *ctx, int id, const char *file, int volume);
    void (*done)(void *ctx);                                  /* clear busy flags (VetVM +0x1da & ~0xD) */
    void (*clear)(void *ctx);                                 /* free background, unload slots 1..31, reset view */
    void (*background)(void *ctx, const char *file);
    void (*show)(void *ctx, int32_t ticks);                   /* run animations and wait */
    void (*hide)(void *ctx);                                  /* stop animations */
    void (*spot)(void *ctx, const TakeStmt *s);               /* hotspot quad add/remove */
    void (*fade)(void *ctx, int level, int32_t ticks);        /* Drop (B0) / Fade (B6) */
    void (*avi)(void *ctx, int x, int y, int w, int h, const char *file);
    void (*grid)(void *ctx, const TakeStmt *s);               /* view extents + scale */
    void (*cel)(void *ctx, int slot, const char *file, int scale);
    void (*path)(void *ctx, const TakeStmt *s);               /* sprite sequence / position / z */
    void (*vep)(void *ctx, const TakeStmt *s);                /* inline VEP expression */
    int  (*eval)(void *ctx, const char *expr, int32_t *out);  /* {expr} arguments, evaluated at parse time */
    void (*exec)(void *ctx, const TakeStmt *s, const struct TakeFields *f);  /* if set, replaces the callbacks above */
} TakeHost;

typedef struct TakeRunner {
    TakeReader r;
    TakeStmt   cur;
    int        have;     /* cur holds a parsed statement waiting for its time */
    int        status;   /* last take_next result */
    TakeHost   host;
    long       executed;
} TakeRunner;

int  take_runner_start(TakeRunner *t, const uint8_t *data, size_t len, const TakeHost *host);
/* Execute every statement whose start time is <= now (ticks since the take began).
 * Returns 1 while the take has more statements, 0 at its end, -1 on a parse error. */
int  take_runner_tick(TakeRunner *t, int32_t now);

#endif

```

`src/tests/avi_dump.c`:

```c
/*
 * avi_dump.c - decode a game AVI with the built-in Indeo 3.2 decoder.
 *   avi_dump <file.avi> [out_prefix [frame ...]]   prints the stream info and decode results; writes
 *                                                  out_prefix_<frame>.bmp for each frame number given
 */
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "../avi.h"
#include "../indeo3.h"

static void save_bmp(const char *path, const uint8_t *bgr, int w, int h)
{   /* 24-bit bottom-up */
    int stride = (w * 3 + 3) & ~3, y;
    uint32_t size = 54 + (uint32_t)stride * (uint32_t)h;
    uint8_t hd[54] = { 'B', 'M' };
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "wb");
    uint8_t pad[3] = { 0, 0, 0 };
    if (!f) return;
#define W32(o, v) do { hd[o] = (uint8_t)(v); hd[o + 1] = (uint8_t)((v) >> 8); hd[o + 2] = (uint8_t)((v) >> 16); hd[o + 3] = (uint8_t)((v) >> 24); } while (0)
    W32(2, size); W32(10, 54); W32(14, 40); W32(18, (uint32_t)w); W32(22, (uint32_t)h);
    hd[26] = 1; hd[28] = 24;
    fwrite(hd, 1, 54, f);
    for (y = h - 1; y >= 0; y--) { fwrite(bgr + (size_t)y * (size_t)w * 3, 1, (size_t)w * 3, f); fwrite(pad, 1, (size_t)(stride - w * 3), f); }
    fclose(f);
}

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    Avi a;
    Indeo3 d;
    int i, k, shown = 0, nulls = 0;
    uint8_t *bgr;
    if (argc < 2 || avi_open(&a, argv[1]) != 0) { fprintf(stderr, "usage: avi_dump <file.avi> [out_prefix [frame ...]]\n"); return 2; }
    if (iv3_init(&d, (int)a.width, (int)a.height) != 0) return 1;
    bgr = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)a.width * a.height * 3);
    for (i = 0; i < a.nframes; i++) {
        int r = a.frames[i].size ? iv3_decode(&d, a.data + a.frames[i].offset, a.frames[i].size) : 0;
        if (r == 0) nulls++;
        if (r > 0) shown++;
        for (k = 3; k < argc; k++)
            if (atoi(argv[k]) == i) {
                char p[1024];
                iv3_to_bgr(&d, bgr, (int)a.width * 3);
                snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s_%03d.bmp", argv[2], i);
                save_bmp(p, bgr, (int)a.width, (int)a.height);
            }
    }
    printf("%s: %ux%u %.4s %.2f fps, %d frames (%d decoded, %d null, %d errors), audio %d Hz %d-bit x%d, %u ms\n",
           argv[1], a.width, a.height, (const char *)&a.handler, (double)a.rate / a.scale, a.nframes, shown, nulls, d.errors,
           a.sampleRate, a.bits, a.channels, avi_duration_ms(&a));
    free(bgr);
    iv3_free(&d);
    avi_close(&a);
    return d.errors ? 1 : 0;
}
```

`src/tests/game_run.c`:

```c
/*
 * game_run - run the whole game (VET + takes + stage) from @START with no input.
 *   game_run <gamedir> <ticks> [out.bmp] [every]
 * Prints every node change and take start; saves the last frame (or one every `every` ticks).
 */
#include "../game.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static Game g;

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    int32_t ticks, t, every = 0;
    int lastNode = -1; long clicks = 0;
    char lastTake[16] = "";
    if (argc < 3) { fprintf(stderr, "usage: game_run <gamedir> <ticks> [out.bmp] [every]\n"); return 2; }
    setvbuf(stdout, NULL, _IONBF, 0);
    ticks = atol(argv[2]);
    if (argc > 4) every = atol(argv[4]);
    if (game_open(&g, argv[1], 1) != 0) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot open the game in %s\n", argv[1]); return 1; }
    FILE *wav = getenv("GAME_WAV") ? fopen(getenv("GAME_WAV"), "wb") : NULL;
    long wavFrames = 0; static int16_t abuf[2 * 800];
    if (wav) { uint8_t hdr[44] = {0}; fwrite(hdr, 1, 44, wav); }
    for (t = 0; t <= ticks; t++) {
        game_idle(&g, t);
        game_idle(&g, t);
        if (wav) { long want = (long)(t + 1) * 22050 / 60 - wavFrames; game_audio_mix(&g, abuf, (int)want); fwrite(abuf, 4, (size_t)want, wav); wavFrames += want; }
        if (getenv("GAME_TYPE") && t == atol(getenv("GAME_TYPE_AT") ? getenv("GAME_TYPE_AT") : "0")) {
            const char *k = getenv("GAME_TYPE");   /* scripted keys; '|' = Enter */
            for (; *k; k++) game_char(&g, *k == '|' ? 13 : *k);
        }
        if (getenv("GAME_AUTOPLAY") && t > 12100 && t % 120 == 0) {   /* random click on something clickable */
            int cand[64][2], n = 0, i, k;
            for (i = 0; i < UI_HOTSPOTS; i++)
                if ((g.ui.bi->hotspotsEnabled & (1u << i)) && g.ui.spot[i].active && n < 64) {
                    cand[n][0] = (g.ui.spot[i].x[0] + g.ui.spot[i].x[3]) / 2; cand[n][1] = (g.ui.spot[i].y[0] + g.ui.spot[i].y[3]) / 2; n++;
                }
            for (k = 0x21; k < 0x65; k++) {
                const StageSlot *q = &g.stage.slot[k];
                if (g.ui.cur[k - 0x20] > 1 && (q->flags & 1) && q->bounds.right > q->bounds.left && n < 64) {
                    cand[n][0] = (q->bounds.left + q->bounds.right) / 2; cand[n][1] = (q->bounds.top + q->bounds.bottom) / 2; n++;
                }
            }
            if (n) {
                static unsigned seed = 0; int c;
                if (!seed) seed = (unsigned)atol(getenv("GAME_AUTOPLAY")) * 2654435761u + 1;
                seed = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u; c = (int)((seed >> 16) % (unsigned)n);
                if (game_click(&g, cand[c][0], cand[c][1]) != 0) clicks++;
            }
        }
        if (getenv("GAME_CLICKS")) {           /* "tick:x,y;tick:x,y;..." */
            const char *q = getenv("GAME_CLICKS");
            while (q && *q) {
                long at = 0; int cx = 0, cy = 0;
                if (sscanf(q, "%ld:%d,%d", &at, &cx, &cy) == 3 && at == t)
                    printf("t=%6ld click %d,%d -> %d\n", (long)t, cx, cy, game_click(&g, cx, cy));
                q = strchr(q, ';'); if (q) q++;
            }
        }
        if (getenv("GAME_CLICK") && t == atol(getenv("GAME_CLICK_AT") ? getenv("GAME_CLICK_AT") : "0")) {
            int cx = 0, cy = 0; sscanf(getenv("GAME_CLICK"), "%d,%d", &cx, &cy);
            printf("t=%6ld click %d,%d -> %d\n", (long)t, cx, cy, game_click(&g, cx, cy));
        }
        if (g.vm.currentNode != lastNode) {
            lastNode = g.vm.currentNode;
            printf("t=%6ld node %s\n", (long)t, g.vm.nodes[lastNode].name);
        }
        if (strcmp(lastTake, g.takeName) != 0 || (g.loadState == 2 && g.takeClock == 0)) {
            if (strcmp(lastTake, g.takeName) != 0) printf("t=%6ld take %s\n", (long)t, g.takeName);
            strcpy(lastTake, g.takeName);
        }
        if (every > 0 && argc > 3 && t % every == 0) {
            char out[1024], base[1000];
            strncpy(base, argv[3], sizeof base - 1); base[sizeof base - 1] = 0;
            if (strlen(base) > 4) base[strlen(base) - 4] = 0;
            sprintf(out, "%s_%06ld.bmp", base, (long)t);
            render_save_bmp(&g.screen, out);
        }
    }
    if (argc > 3 && !every) render_save_bmp(&g.screen, argv[3]);
    if (wav) {   /* RIFF header */
        uint8_t h[44] = { 'R','I','F','F',0,0,0,0,'W','A','V','E','f','m','t',' ',16,0,0,0,1,0,2,0, 0x22,0x56,0,0, 0x88,0x58,1,0, 4,0,16,0, 'd','a','t','a' };
        uint32_t n = (uint32_t)wavFrames * 4, r = n + 36; int i;
        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) { h[4 + i] = (uint8_t)(r >> (8 * i)); }
        fseek(wav, 0, SEEK_SET); fwrite(h, 1, 40, wav); fputc(n & 255, wav); fputc((n >> 8) & 255, wav); fputc((n >> 16) & 255, wav); fputc(n >> 24, wav); fclose(wav);
    }
    printf("end: node %s, busy %x, takes started %ld (missing %ld), %ld clicks\n", g.vm.nodes[g.vm.currentNode].name,
           vet_get_busy(&g.vm), g.takesStarted, g.takesMissing, clicks);
    game_close(&g);
    return 0;
}
```
