`src/tests/scene_render.c`:

```c
/*
 * scene_render - play a take on the Stage and save frames.
 *   scene_render <gamedir> <TAKE> <ticks> <out.bmp> [every]
 * Runs the take's statements and the 60 Hz frame update for `ticks` ticks; writes the last frame to out.bmp,
 * or every `every` ticks to out_NNNN.bmp when `every` is given.
 */
#include "../scene.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    static Screen scr;
    static Stage st;
    static Scene sc;
    DatDir d; FILE *dat; char p[1024];
    int32_t ticks, t, every = 0;
    if (argc < 5) { fprintf(stderr, "usage: scene_render <gamedir> <TAKE> <ticks> <out.bmp> [every]\n"); return 2; }
    ticks = atol(argv[3]);
    if (argc > 5) every = atol(argv[5]);
    sprintf(p, "%s/IZZY.DIR", argv[1]);
    if (dat_dir_load(&d, p) != 0) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot read %s\n", p); return 1; }
    sprintf(p, "%s/IZZYCD1.DAT", argv[1]);
    dat = fopen(p, "rb");
    if (!dat) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot open %s\n", p); return 1; }
    stage_init(&st, &scr);
    if (scene_open(&sc, &st, NULL, &d, dat, argv[2]) != 0) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot open take %s\n", argv[2]); return 1; }
    for (t = 0; t <= ticks; t++) {
        scene_tick(&sc, t);
        stage_FrameUpdate(&st, t);
        if (every > 0 && t % every == 0) {
            char out[1024], base[1000];
            strncpy(base, argv[4], sizeof base - 1); base[sizeof base - 1] = 0;
            if (strlen(base) > 4) base[strlen(base) - 4] = 0;
            sprintf(out, "%s_%04ld.bmp", base, (long)t);
            render_save_bmp(&scr, out);
        }
    }
    if (!every) render_save_bmp(&scr, argv[4]);
    printf("%s: %ld statements, %d files loaded, %ld missing\n", argv[2], sc.run.executed, sc.nfiles, sc.missing);
    if (getenv("SCENE_DUMP")) {
        int s;
        printf("now=%ld anim=%d wait=%d/%ld\n", (long)st.now, st.animRunning, st.waiting, (long)st.waitDuration);
        for (s = 0; s < STAGE_SLOTS; s++) {
            const StageSlot *q = &st.slot[s];
            if (!q->flags && !q->havePack) continue;
            printf("slot %3d flags %04x seq %d dir %d frame %d cel %d nF %d nS %d x %ld y %ld z %d scale %ld next %ld bounds %d,%d-%d,%d\n",
                   s, q->flags, q->seq, q->dir, q->frame, q->cel, q->nFrames, q->nSeq, (long)q->x, (long)q->y, q->depth,
                   (long)q->scale, (long)q->nextFrameTime, q->bounds.left, q->bounds.top, q->bounds.right, q->bounds.bottom);
        }
    }
    scene_close(&sc);
    fclose(dat);
    dat_dir_free(&d);
    return 0;
}

```

`src/tests/selftest.c`:

```c
/*
 * selftest.c - checks the reconstructed modules against the real game files.
 * Usage: selftest <game folder: IZZY.DIR, IZZYCD1.DAT, IZZY.VET, IZZY.EXE, the AVIs...> [output folder]
 * The output folder (default: the current one) receives the pictures, saves and the decompiled script it writes;
 * nothing is written into the game folder. Every expected value below was established independently
 * (see tools/decomp/TECHNICAL_NOTES.md).
 */
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "../dat.h"
#include "../cels.h"
#include "../vet.h"
#include "../take.h"
#include "../builtins.h"
#include "../render.h"
#include "../scene.h"
#include "../vep.h"
#include "../savegame.h"
#include "../game.h"
#include "../audio.h"
#include "../app.h"
#include "../config.h"
#include "../vepc.h"
#include "../avi.h"
#include "../indeo3.h"

/* recording host for the app test */
typedef struct AppTestHost { int plays, stops, quit, answer; int checked[0x80]; char last[16]; } AppTestHost;
static int test_message(void *c, int id, const char *text, int style)
{
    AppTestHost *h = c; (void)id; (void)text;
    if (style == APP_MB_RETRYCANCEL) return APP_ID_CANCEL;
    return h->answer ? h->answer : APP_ID_NO;
}
static void test_quit(void *c) { ((AppTestHost *)c)->quit = 1; }
static void test_check(void *c, int cmd, int on) { AppTestHost *h = c; if (cmd >= 0 && cmd < 0x80) h->checked[cmd] = on; }
static void test_midi(void *c, int op, const char *name, const uint8_t *data, size_t len)
{
    AppTestHost *h = c; (void)data; (void)len;
    if (op == GAME_MIDI_PLAY) { h->plays++; strncpy(h->last, name, sizeof h->last - 1); }
    if (op == GAME_MIDI_STOP) h->stops++;
}

static int16_t call_builtin(VetVM *vm, const char *name, int16_t arg)
{
    int n = vet_find(vm, name);
    if (n < 0 || !vm->nodes[n].builtinFn) return -9999;
    return vm->nodes[n].builtinFn(vm->nodes[n].builtinCtx, vm, n, arg);
}
static int16_t var_get(VetVM *vm, const char *name, int index)
{
    int16_t v = 0, j = 0;
    vet_get_variable(vm, vet_find(vm, name), index, &v, &j);
    return v;
}
static void var_set(VetVM *vm, const char *name, int index, int16_t v)
{
    vet_store_variable(vm, vet_find(vm, name), index, v, 0);
}

/* recording host for the TAKE runner test */
static char g_log[8192];
static void logf_(const char *s) { if (strlen(g_log) + strlen(s) + 2 < sizeof g_log) { strcat(g_log, s); strcat(g_log, ";"); } }
static void h_audio(void *c, int ch, const char *f, int v, int fl) { char b[96]; (void)c; (void)v; (void)fl; sprintf(b, "Audio %d %s", ch, f); logf_(b); }
static void h_sfx(void *c, int id, const char *f, int v) { char b[96]; (void)c; (void)v; sprintf(b, "Sfx %d %s", id, f); logf_(b); }
static void h_done(void *c) { (void)c; logf_("Done"); }
static void h_clear(void *c) { (void)c; logf_("Clear"); }
static void h_back(void *c, const char *f) { char b[96]; (void)c; sprintf(b, "Back %s", f); logf_(b); }
static void h_show(void *c, int32_t t) { char b[32]; (void)c; sprintf(b, "Show %ld", (long)t); logf_(b); }
static void h_hide(void *c) { (void)c; logf_("Hide"); }
static void h_spot(void *c, const TakeStmt *s) { char b[32]; (void)c; sprintf(b, "Spot %ld", (long)take_arg_int(s, 0)); logf_(b); }
static void h_cel(void *c, int slot, const char *f, int sc) { char b[96]; (void)c; sprintf(b, "Cel %d %s %d", slot, f, sc); logf_(b); }
static void h_path(void *c, const TakeStmt *s) { char b[32]; (void)c; sprintf(b, "Path %ld", (long)take_arg_int(s, 0)); logf_(b); }

static int failures = 0;
#define CHECK(cond, ...) do { if (cond) { printf("ok   "); } else { printf("FAIL "); failures++; } printf(__VA_ARGS__); printf("\n"); } while (0)

static void path(char *out, const char *dir, const char *name) { sprintf(out, "%s/%s", dir, name); }

/* Run the plain statements (kind 1) of a code block: 81 k, 82|85, <statement>, 88. */
static void run_plain_statements(VetVM *vm, const VetCode *block)
{
    VetCode c = *block;
    while (c.pos < c.len) {
        int16_t kind, end;
        uint8_t t;
        if (c.data[c.pos] != 0x81) break;
        kind = rd_i16(c.data + c.pos + 1);
        c.pos += 3;
        t = c.data[c.pos];
        if (t == 0x82 || t == 0x85) c.pos++;
        /* find this statement's 0x88 terminator, skipping operands */
        end = c.pos;
        while (end < block->len && block->data[end] != 0x88) {
            uint8_t k = block->data[end];
            end += (k == 0x81 || k == 0x83 || k == 0x84 || k == 0x86 || k == 0x87 || k == 0x89) ? 3 : 1;
        }
        if (kind == 1) {
            VetCode s = c;
            s.len = end;
            vet_exec_statement(vm, &s);
        }
        c.pos = (int16_t)(end + 1);
    }
}

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    const char *dir = argc > 1 ? argv[1] : ".";
    const char *odir = argc > 2 ? argv[2] : ".";            /* test output, never the game folder */
    char p[1024];

    /* ---- DAT */
    {
        DatDir d;
        const DatDirEntry *e;
        FILE *dat;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.DIR");
        CHECK(dat_dir_load(&d, p) == 0, "IZZY.DIR loads");
        CHECK(d.count == 677, "IZZY.DIR has 677 records (got %u)", (unsigned)d.count);
        e = dat_dir_lookup(&d, "CREDITTK.TKE");
        CHECK(e && e->offset == 0x163000 && e->disc == 1, "lookup credittk.tke -> disc 1, offset 0x163000");
        path(p, dir, "IZZYCD1.DAT");
        dat = fopen(p, "rb");
        CHECK(dat != NULL, "IZZYCD1.DAT opens");
        if (dat && e) {
            DatGroup g;
            const DatFileEntry *f;
            CHECK(dat_group_open(&g, dat, e->offset) == 0, "group credittk opens");
            f = dat_group_find(&g, "credittk.def");
            CHECK(f && f->size == 0xC47, "credittk.def is 0xC47 bytes");
            if (f) {
                uint8_t *b = dat_read(dat, &g, f);
                CHECK(b && memcmp(b, "TAKE", 4) == 0, "credittk.def starts with TAKE");
                free(b);
            }
            /* CELS test from the sac00010 group */
            {
                const DatDirEntry *s = dat_dir_lookup(&d, "sac00010.tke");
                DatGroup g2;
                if (s && dat_group_open(&g2, dat, s->offset) == 0) {
                    const DatFileEntry *a = dat_group_find(&g2, "CACCRY51.ANI");
                    uint8_t *ab = a ? dat_read(dat, &g2, a) : NULL;
                    CelPack pk;
                    CHECK(ab && cels_open(&pk, ab, a->size) == CELS_OK, "CACCRY51.ANI validates");
                    if (ab) {
                        Cel c;
                        CHECK(pk.nCels == 3 && pk.nSeq == 3 && pk.nDir == 1 && pk.nFrames == 1, "CACCRY51 header 3/3/1/1");
                        CHECK(cels_get(&pk, 1, &c) == 0 && c.width == 187 && c.height == 34, "cel 1 is 187x34");
                        if (c.width > 0) {
                            uint8_t *px = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)c.width * c.height);
                            int x, n = 0;
                            mmp1_decode(&c, px);
                            for (x = 0; x < c.width; x++) if (px[x]) n++;
                            /* row 0: runs FE 00 (128) + 98 00 (26) transparent, 1 literal pixel (0xF6), then transparent */
                            CHECK(px[154] == 0xF6 && n == 1, "row 0 has one pixel 0xF6 at x=154");
                            free(px);
                        }
                        free(ab);
                    }
                    dat_group_free(&g2);
                }
            }
            dat_group_free(&g);
        }
        /* full sweep: every record findable by binary search (=> sorted), every group opens,
         * every .ANI validates, every cel decodes. Totals must match the C# converter's (tools\Convert-IzzyAssets.ps1) output:
         * 2582 .ANI files (anim.json) and 29341 decoded cels (cel_*.png). */
        if (dat) {
            size_t i, k, found = 0, groups = 0, anis = 0, anisOk = 0, cels = 0;
            for (i = 0; i < d.count; i++) {
                DatGroup gg;
                if (dat_dir_lookup(&d, d.entries[i].name) == &d.entries[i]) found++;
                if (d.entries[i].disc != 1) continue;
                if (dat_group_open(&gg, dat, d.entries[i].offset) != 0) continue;
                groups++;
                for (k = 0; k < gg.count; k++) {
                    size_t nl = strlen(gg.files[k].name);
                    uint8_t *b;
                    CelPack pk;
                    char ext[5] = {0};
                    /* 14 archive names carry a trailing space ("CFECRM52.ANI "); no take references them */
                    while (nl > 0 && gg.files[k].name[nl - 1] == ' ') nl--;
                    if (nl < 4) continue;
                    memcpy(ext, gg.files[k].name + nl - 4, 4);
                    if (izzy_stricmp(ext, ".ANI") != 0) continue;
                    anis++;
                    b = dat_read(dat, &gg, &gg.files[k]);
                    if (b && cels_open(&pk, b, gg.files[k].size) == CELS_OK) {
                        int c;
                        anisOk++;
                        for (c = 1; c < pk.nCels; c++) {
                            Cel cel;
                            if (cels_get(&pk, c, &cel) == 0 && cel.width > 0 && cel.height > 0 && cel.width < 4096 && cel.height < 4096) {
                                uint8_t *px = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)cel.width * cel.height);
                                if (px) { mmp1_decode(&cel, px); cels++; free(px); }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    free(b);
                }
                dat_group_free(&gg);
            }
            CHECK(found == d.count, "every IZZY.DIR record is found by binary search (%u/%u)", (unsigned)found, (unsigned)d.count);
            CHECK(groups == 677, "all groups open (%u)", (unsigned)groups);
            CHECK(anis == 2582 && anisOk == anis, ".ANI packs: %u, valid %u (expected 2582)", (unsigned)anis, (unsigned)anisOk);
            CHECK(cels == 29341, "decoded cels: %u (C# converter produced 29341)", (unsigned)cels);
        }
        if (dat) fclose(dat);
        dat_dir_free(&d);
    }

    /* ---- VET */
    {
        VetVM vm;
        int ring, loc, sac, start;
        int16_t v, j;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.VET");
        CHECK(vet_load(&vm, p) == 0, "IZZY.VET loads with no trailing bytes");
        CHECK(vm.nodeCount == 1315 && vm.version == 5, "1315 nodes, version 5");
        start = vm.startNode;
        CHECK(start == 1205 && vm.nodes[start].name && strcmp(vm.nodes[start].name, "START") == 0, "start node is @START");
        CHECK(vm.exprMode == 1, "version 5 uses precedence (exprMode 1)");
        sac = vet_find(&vm, "SAC00010");
        ring = vet_find(&vm, "RING");
        loc = vet_find(&vm, "LOCATION");
        if (sac >= 0 && ring >= 0 && loc >= 0) {
            run_plain_statements(&vm, &vm.nodes[sac].code1);
            vet_get_variable(&vm, ring, 0, &v, &j);
            CHECK(v == 4, "SAC00010 setup sets RING = 4 (got %d)", v);
            vet_get_variable(&vm, loc, 0, &v, &j);
            CHECK(v == 17, "SAC00010 setup sets LOCATION = 17 (got %d)", v);
        } else {
            CHECK(0, "nodes SAC00010 / RING / LOCATION exist");
        }
        /* robustness: run every script node's setup block (plain statements only) */
        {
            int i, nodes = 0;
            for (i = 0; i < vm.nodeCount; i++) {
                if (vm.nodes[i].type != 1 || vm.nodes[i].code1.len == 0) continue;
                run_plain_statements(&vm, &vm.nodes[i].code1);
                nodes++;
            }
            CHECK(nodes > 0, "executed the setup code of %d script nodes without crashing", nodes);
        }
        vet_free(&vm);
    }

    /* ---- TAKE scene scripts: parse all 677, compare opcode totals with the earlier C# listings */
    {
        DatDir d;
        FILE *dat;
        long count[256];
        size_t i, k, parsed = 0, ended = 0, errors = 0;
        static const struct { int op; long n; } expect[] = {
            {0xA1, 2435}, {0xA2, 161}, {0xA3, 676}, {0xA4, 562}, {0xB1, 670}, {0xB2, 867}, {0xB3, 418},
            {0xB4, 869}, {0xB7, 40}, {0xC1, 13}, {0xC2, 2582}, {0xC3, 4591},
            {0xA0, 0}, {0xA5, 0}, {0xB0, 0}, {0xB5, 0}, {0xB6, 0}, {0xC0, 0} };
        int mismatches = 0;
        memset(count, 0, sizeof count);
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.DIR"); dat_dir_load(&d, p);
        path(p, dir, "IZZYCD1.DAT"); dat = fopen(p, "rb");
        for (i = 0; dat && i < d.count; i++) {
            DatGroup g;
            if (d.entries[i].disc != 1 || dat_group_open(&g, dat, d.entries[i].offset) != 0) continue;
            for (k = 0; k < g.count; k++) {
                size_t nl = strlen(g.files[k].name);
                uint8_t *b;
                TakeReader r;
                TakeStmt s;
                int st;
                if (nl < 4 || izzy_stricmp(g.files[k].name + nl - 4, ".DEF") != 0) continue;
                b = dat_read(dat, &g, &g.files[k]);
                if (!b || take_open(&r, b, g.files[k].size) != 0) { errors++; free(b); continue; }
                parsed++;
                while ((st = take_next(&r, &s)) == TAKE_OK) count[s.op]++;
                if (st == TAKE_EOF) ended++; else errors++;
                free(b);
            }
            dat_group_free(&g);
        }
        CHECK(parsed == 677 && ended == 677 && errors == 0, "all %u takes parse and end at FF (errors %u)", (unsigned)parsed, (unsigned)errors);
        for (i = 0; i < sizeof expect / sizeof expect[0]; i++)
            if (count[expect[i].op] != expect[i].n) {
                printf("     %s (%02X): %ld, expected %ld\n", take_op_name((uint8_t)expect[i].op), expect[i].op, count[expect[i].op], expect[i].n);
                mismatches++;
            }
        CHECK(mismatches == 0, "opcode totals match the C# listings (C2 Cel %ld, C3 Path %ld)", count[0xC2], count[0xC3]);

        /* runner: sac00010 with a recording host */
        {
            const DatDirEntry *e = dat ? dat_dir_lookup(&d, "sac00010.tke") : NULL;
            DatGroup g;
            if (e && dat_group_open(&g, dat, e->offset) == 0) {
                const DatFileEntry *f = dat_group_find(&g, "SAC00010.DEF");
                uint8_t *b = f ? dat_read(dat, &g, f) : NULL;
                TakeRunner tr;
                TakeHost h;
                int32_t now;
                memset(&h, 0, sizeof h);
                h.audio = h_audio; h.sfx = h_sfx; h.done = h_done; h.clear = h_clear; h.background = h_back;
                h.show = h_show; h.hide = h_hide; h.spot = h_spot; h.cel = h_cel; h.path = h_path;
                g_log[0] = 0;
                if (b && take_runner_start(&tr, b, f->size, &h) == 0) {
                    for (now = 0; now < 100000 && take_runner_tick(&tr, now) == 1; now += 6) { }
                    CHECK(strncmp(g_log, "Hide;Clear;Back BAC00010.BMP;Cel 1 CACOBO41.ANI 1024;", 52) == 0,
                          "sac00010 starts Hide, Clear, Back BAC00010.BMP, Cel 1 CACOBO41.ANI 1024");
                    CHECK(strstr(g_log, "Audio 9 MUCOR002.MID") && strstr(g_log, "Sfx 1 FAC00041.WAV") && strstr(g_log, "Spot 1"),
                          "sac00010 issues Audio 9 MUCOR002.MID, Sfx 1 FAC00041.WAV, Spot 1 (%ld statements)", tr.executed);
                }
                free(b);
                dat_group_free(&g);
            }
        }
        if (dat) fclose(dat);
        dat_dir_free(&d);
    }

    /* ---- VET control flow (vet_step) */
    {
        VetVM vm;
        int gold, player, help, siz, accept, show, steps, sawShow = 0, i, visited = 0, errors = 0;
        int16_t g0, g1, h, j;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.VET");
        vet_load(&vm, p);
        vm.onError = NULL;
        reset_counters:
        vet_new_game(&vm, 1);
        gold = vet_find(&vm, "GOLD"); player = vet_find(&vm, "PLAYER"); help = vet_find(&vm, "HELP_MEDAL");
        siz = vet_find(&vm, "SIZ00011"); accept = vet_find(&vm, "ACCEPT_GOLD"); show = vet_find(&vm, "ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD");
        CHECK(gold >= 0 && player >= 0 && help >= 0 && siz >= 0 && accept >= 0 && show >= 0, "control-flow test nodes exist");
        if (gold >= 0 && player >= 0 && help >= 0 && siz >= 0 && accept >= 0 && show >= 0) {
            vet_store_variable(&vm, player, 0, 1, 0);
            vet_get_variable(&vm, gold, 1, &g0, &j);
            vet_enter_node(&vm, accept);
            vm.setDone = 0; vm.pc = 0;
            for (steps = 0; steps < 400 && vm.currentNode != siz; steps++) {
                vet_step(&vm, 1000u + 50u * (uint32_t)steps, 6);
                if (vm.currentNode == show) sawShow = 1;
            }
            vet_get_variable(&vm, gold, 1, &g1, &j);
            vet_get_variable(&vm, help, 1, &h, &j);
            CHECK(vm.currentNode == siz, "ACCEPT_GOLD reaches GOTO @SIZ00011 (%d steps)", steps);
            CHECK(sawShow, "GOSUB @ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD was taken");
            CHECK(g1 == g0 + 1, "GOLD[PLAYER] += 1 (%d -> %d)", g0, g1);
            CHECK(h == 100 && vm.depth == 0, "RETURN resumed after the GOSUB: HELP_MEDAL[PLAYER] = TRUE, stack empty");
        }

        /* new game from @START: run with stub built-ins (all return 0) and count distinct nodes */
        {
            static unsigned char seen[4096];
            static int errCount;
            memset(seen, 0, sizeof seen);
            errCount = 0;
            vet_new_game(&vm, 1);
            for (i = 0; i < 2000; i++) {
                vet_step(&vm, 5000u + 50u * (uint32_t)i, 6);
                if (vm.currentNode >= 0 && vm.currentNode < 4096 && !seen[vm.currentNode]) { seen[vm.currentNode] = 1; visited++; }
            }
            errors = errCount;
            CHECK(visited >= 3, "new game from @START visits %d distinct nodes in 2000 ticks", visited);
            printf("     now at node %d (%s), %s\n", vm.currentNode,
                   vm.currentNode >= 0 ? vm.nodes[vm.currentNode].name : "-", vm.looping ? "waiting in a LOOP" : "running");
            (void)errors;
        }
        vet_free(&vm);
        if (0) goto reset_counters;
    }

    /* ---- built-ins (game rules) */
    {
        VetVM vm;
        IzzyRules rules;
        int i, loc, counts[8] = {0}, bonus = 0, okRange = 1, perm = 1, eligible = 1, visited = 0;
        int seenMedal[31] = {0};
        static unsigned char seen[4096];
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.VET");
        vet_load(&vm, p);
        izzy_rules_init(&rules, &vm);
        vet_new_game(&vm, 1);
        CHECK(call_builtin(&vm, "INIT_IZZY_GAME", 0) == 100, "INIT_IZZY_GAME runs");

        CHECK(call_builtin(&vm, "HIDE_EASTER_EGGS", 2) == 100, "HIDE_EASTER_EGGS(2) runs");
        for (loc = 1; loc <= 25; loc++) {
            counts[var_get(&vm, "ITEM_1", loc) & 7]++; counts[var_get(&vm, "ITEM_2", loc) & 7]++;
            counts[var_get(&vm, "ITEM_3", loc) & 7]++; counts[var_get(&vm, "ITEM_4", loc) & 7]++;
            bonus += var_get(&vm, "ITEM_5", loc);
        }
        CHECK(counts[6] == 1 && counts[2] == 10 && counts[3] == 20 && counts[1] == 10 && counts[5] == 10 && counts[4] == 35 && bonus == 10,
              "eggs: type6 %d, 2 %d, 3 %d, 1 %d, 5 %d, 4 %d, bonus %d (expect 1/10/20/10/10/35/10)",
              counts[6], counts[2], counts[3], counts[1], counts[5], counts[4], bonus);

        var_set(&vm, "GOLD", 1, 7);
        CHECK(call_builtin(&vm, "SETS_READY", 1) == 100, "SETS_READY(1) with 7 golds");
        call_builtin(&vm, "FUSE_SETS", 1);
        CHECK(var_get(&vm, "GOLD", 1) == 4 && var_get(&vm, "SUNCATCHER", 0) == 1, "FUSE_SETS: 3 golds -> 1 suncatcher (gold now %d)", var_get(&vm, "GOLD", 1));

        var_set(&vm, "GOLD", 2, 0); var_set(&vm, "SILVER", 2, 1); var_set(&vm, "BRONZE", 2, 2);
        CHECK(call_builtin(&vm, "MEDAL_TO_WIN", 2) == 2 && call_builtin(&vm, "MEDAL_TO_LOSE", 2) == 1, "MEDAL_TO_WIN 2 (silver), MEDAL_TO_LOSE 1 (bronze)");
        CHECK(call_builtin(&vm, "HAS_MEDALS", 2) == 100 && call_builtin(&vm, "HAS_MEDALS", 3) == 0, "HAS_MEDALS");

        for (i = 0; i < 30; i++) { int v = call_builtin(&vm, "NEXT_MEDAL", 1); if (v < 1 || v > 30 || seenMedal[v]++) perm = 0; }
        CHECK(perm, "NEXT_MEDAL(1) deals a permutation of 1..30");
        for (i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
            int c = call_builtin(&vm, "NEXT_COMPETITION", 0);
            if (c < 1 || c > 50 || c == 5 || c == 6 || izzy_competitions[c - 1].winner != var_get(&vm, "COMP_WINNER", 0)) eligible = 0;
        }
        CHECK(eligible, "NEXT_COMPETITION never deals entries 5/6 and sets COMP_WINNER from the table");
        for (i = 0; i < 1000; i++) { int v = call_builtin(&vm, "RND", 7); if (v < 0 || v > 6) okRange = 0; }
        CHECK(okRange && call_builtin(&vm, "RND", 1) == 0, "RND(7) in 0..6, RND(1) = 0");

        /* full new game with the real built-ins */
        memset(seen, 0, sizeof seen);
        vet_new_game(&vm, 1);
        for (i = 0; i < 2000; i++) {
            vet_step(&vm, 5000u + 50u * (uint32_t)i, 6);
            if (vm.currentNode >= 0 && vm.currentNode < 4096 && !seen[vm.currentNode]) { seen[vm.currentNode] = 1; visited++; }
        }
        CHECK(visited >= 3, "new game with built-ins visits %d nodes, now at %s (%s)", visited,
              vm.currentNode >= 0 ? vm.nodes[vm.currentNode].name : "-", vm.looping ? "waiting in a LOOP" : "running");
        vet_free(&vm);
    }

    /* ---- renderer: compose sbc00010 (background + first frame of each placed sprite) */
    {
        DatDir d; FILE *dat; const DatDirEntry *e; DatGroup g;
        static Screen scr;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.DIR"); dat_dir_load(&d, p);
        path(p, dir, "IZZYCD1.DAT"); dat = fopen(p, "rb");
        e = dat ? dat_dir_lookup(&d, "sbc00010.tke") : NULL;
        if (e && dat_group_open(&g, dat, e->offset) == 0) {
            const DatFileEntry *def = dat_group_find(&g, "SBC00010.DEF");
            uint8_t *db = def ? dat_read(dat, &g, def) : NULL;
            TakeReader r; TakeStmt s;
            char anim[104][64]; int placed[104][5]; int have[104]; int i, drawn = 0, shadows = 0;
            memset(anim, 0, sizeof anim); memset(have, 0, sizeof have);
            if (db && take_open(&r, db, def->size) == 0) {
                while (take_next(&r, &s) == TAKE_OK) {
                    if (s.op == TK_BACK && !scr.px[0] && !scr.pal.r[1]) {
                        const DatFileEntry *bf = dat_group_find(&g, take_arg_str(&s, 0));
                        uint8_t *bb = bf ? dat_read(dat, &g, bf) : NULL;
                        if (bb) { render_load_bmp(&scr, bb, bf->size); free(bb); }
                    } else if (s.op == TK_CEL) {
                        int slot = take_arg_int(&s, 0);
                        if (slot > 0 && slot < 104) strncpy(anim[slot], take_arg_str(&s, 1), 63);
                    } else if (s.op == TK_PATH && s.nargs >= 6) {
                        int slot = take_arg_int(&s, 0);
                        if (slot > 0 && slot < 104 && !have[slot]) {
                            for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) placed[slot][i] = take_arg_int(&s, i + 1);
                            have[slot] = 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
                render_build_shadow_tables(&scr);
                for (i = 0; i < 104; i++) {          /* draw in z order (lower z first), z < 0 hidden */
                    int best = -1, bz = 0x7fffffff, k;
                    for (k = 1; k < 104; k++) if (have[k] == 1 && placed[k][4] < bz) { bz = placed[k][4]; best = k; }
                    if (best < 0) break;
                    have[best] = 2;
                    if (placed[best][4] < 0 || !anim[best][0]) continue;
                    {
                        const DatFileEntry *af = dat_group_find(&g, anim[best]);
                        uint8_t *ab = af ? dat_read(dat, &g, af) : NULL;
                        CelPack pk; Cel cel; int f, ci = 0;
                        ClipRect clip = {0, 0, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H};
                        if (ab && cels_open(&pk, ab, af->size) == CELS_OK) {
                            int seq = placed[best][0] >= 0 && placed[best][0] < pk.nSeq ? placed[best][0] : 0;
                            for (f = 0; f < pk.nFrames && ci == 0; f++) ci = rd_i16(pk.tblFrame + 2 * (seq * pk.nFrames + f));
                            if (ci > 0 && cels_get(&pk, ci, &cel) == 0 && rd_u32((const uint8_t *)cel.bits - 24) != 0) {
                                const uint8_t *q;
                                render_draw_mmp1(&scr, &cel, (int32_t)(placed[best][2] + cel.hotX) << 10,
                                                 (int32_t)(placed[best][3] + cel.hotY) << 10, 1024, &clip);
                                drawn++;
                                for (q = cel.bits; q < cel.bits + cel.bitsLen; q++) if (*q >= 0xF6 && *q != 0xFF) { shadows++; break; }
                            }
                        }
                        free(ab);
                    }
                }
                CHECK(drawn == 4, "sbc00010: %d sprites composed (the C# preview drew 4)", drawn);
                {
                    char out[1024];
                    sprintf(out, "%s/_c_render_sbc00010.bmp", odir);
                    CHECK(render_save_bmp(&scr, out) == 0, "wrote %s", out);
                }
            }
            free(db);
            dat_group_free(&g);
        }
        if (dat) fclose(dat);
        dat_dir_free(&d);
    }

    /* ---- vep: TAKE {expressions} compiled to VET byte code and evaluated */
    {
        static VetVM vm;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.VET");
        if (vet_load(&vm, p) == 0) {
            int n1 = vet_find(&vm, "STATE1"), n5 = vet_find(&vm, "STATE5");
            int32_t r1 = 0, r2 = 0, r3 = 0;
            int16_t r4 = 0;
            uint8_t code[64];
            int len;
            CHECK(n1 >= 0 && n5 >= 0, "vep: STATE1 / STATE5 nodes exist");
            vet_store_variable(&vm, n1, 0, 3, 0);
            vet_store_variable(&vm, n5, 0, 4, 0);
            vep_eval_take_expr(&vm, "STATE1 + 0", &r1);
            vep_eval_take_expr(&vm, "STATE5 * 2", &r2);
            vep_eval_take_expr(&vm, "STATE1 + 12", &r3);
            CHECK(r1 == 3 && r2 == 8 && r3 == 15, "vep: {STATE1 + 0} = %ld, {STATE5 * 2} = %ld, {STATE1 + 12} = %ld",
                  (long)r1, (long)r2, (long)r3);
            vep_eval_text(&vm, "(STATE1 + 1) * 2 = 8", &r4);
            CHECK(r4 == 100, "vep: (STATE1 + 1) * 2 = 8 is TRUE (100), got %d", r4);
            len = vep_compile_statement(&vm, 0, "STATE1 <= 2", code, (int)sizeof code);
            CHECK(len == 9 && code[0] == 0x83 && code[3] == 0x87 && code[4] == ('<' | 0x80) && code[5] == 0x81 && code[6] == 0x84,
                  "vep: 'STATE1 <= 2' -> 83 n n 87 BC 81 84 02 00 (%d bytes)", len);
            vet_free(&vm);
        }
    }

    /* ---- wave mixer */
    {
        static WaveMixer m;
        uint8_t mono[8 + 4] = { 'M','O','N','O', 4,0,0,0, 0x80, 0xff, 0x00, 0x90 };
        uint8_t ster[8 + 4] = { 'S','T','E','R', 4,0,0,0, 0xc0, 0x40, 0x80, 0x80 };
        int16_t out[2 * 6];
        wave_init(&m, 0, 0, 0);
        wave_play(&m, 0, mono, sizeof mono, 8, 1);     /* level 8 + 0 - 1 = 7: shift 1 */
        wave_play(&m, 1, ster, sizeof ster, 8, 0);
        wave_mix(&m, out, 6);
        CHECK(out[0] == 0 + (int16_t)0x4000 / 2 && out[1] == 0 + (int16_t)0xc000 / 2 && out[2] == 0x7f00 / 2 &&
              out[4] == -0x4000 + 0 && out[6] == 0x1000 / 2 && out[8] == 0 && !wave_busy(&m, 0) && !wave_busy(&m, 1),
              "audio: mono + stereo mixed at level 7, channels end with their data (%d %d %d %d %d)", out[0], out[1], out[2], out[4], out[6]);
        wave_play(&m, 0, mono, sizeof mono, 8, 1);
        wave_play(&m, 0, mono, sizeof mono, 8, 1 | 8);  /* queued part */
        wave_mix(&m, out, 6);
        CHECK(out[8] == 0 && out[10] == 0x7f00 / 2 && wave_busy(&m, 0), "audio: an appended part follows the first");
        wave_shutdown(&m);
    }

    /* ---- the whole game: VET + take player + stage from @START, no input */
    {
        static Game gm;
        if (game_open(&gm, dir, 1) == 0) {
            int32_t t;
            static char ini[1024];
            sprintf(ini, "%s/_c_izzy.ini", odir); remove(ini);
            gm.ui.iniPath = ini;                         /* keep the test's high scores out of the game folder */
            ui_scores_insert(&gm.ui, "Test", 75000);
            CHECK(strcmp((char *)gm.ui.ds + 0x2278 + 0x20 * 3, "Test") == 0 && strcmp((char *)gm.ui.ds + 0x2278 + 0x20 * 4, "Owen") == 0 &&
                  strcmp((char *)gm.ui.ds + 0x23b8 + 10 * 3, "75,000") == 0 && strcmp((char *)gm.ui.ds + 0x2278 + 0x20 * 9, "Spartin") == 0,
                  "high scores: 75,000 ranks 4th, Owen moves down, Caruthers drops off");
            ui_scores_load(&gm.ui);
            int at = -1, names = vet_find(&gm.vm, "ENTER_NAMES");
            for (t = 0; t <= 13000 && at < 0; t++) {
                game_idle(&gm, t); game_idle(&gm, t);
                if (gm.vm.currentNode == names) at = t;
            }
            CHECK(at > 11000 && gm.takesStarted == 8 && gm.takesMissing == 0,
                  "game: the title and the 7 intro takes play, ENTER_NAMES reached at tick %ld (%ld takes)", (long)at, gm.takesStarted);
            {   /* type a name, click START GAME (control 10), reach the main menu */
                int r = 0, menu = vet_find(&gm.vm, "MAIN_MENU"), seen = 0;
                for (; t <= at + 600; t++) { game_idle(&gm, t); game_idle(&gm, t); }
                game_char(&gm, 'i'); game_char(&gm, 'z'); game_char(&gm, 'z'); game_char(&gm, 'y');
                for (; t <= at + 700; t++) { game_idle(&gm, t); game_idle(&gm, t); }
                r = game_click(&gm, 320, 370);
                for (; t <= at + 900; t++) { game_idle(&gm, t); game_idle(&gm, t); if (gm.vm.currentNode == menu) seen = 1; }
                CHECK(r == 10 && seen && strncmp((char *)gm.ui.ds + 0x2016, "Izzy", 4) == 0,
                      "game: name 'Izzy' typed, START GAME clicked (control %d), MAIN_MENU reached", r);
            }
            {   /* a found medal: the ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD_nn scene must play its take to the end (not return at once) */
                static Game g3;
                if (game_open(&g3, dir, 1) == 0) {
                    int32_t t3, shown = 0, scene = -1;
                    g3.ui.iniPath = ini;
                    for (t3 = 0; t3 <= t; t3++) { game_idle(&g3, t3); game_idle(&g3, t3); }   /* the same intro */
                    vet_jump_to(&g3.vm, "ACCEPT_GOLD");
                    for (; t3 <= t + 1500; t3++) {
                        const char *nm;
                        game_idle(&g3, t3); game_idle(&g3, t3);
                        nm = g3.vm.nodes[g3.vm.currentNode].name;
                        if (strncmp(nm, "ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD_", 17) == 0 && strcmp(nm, "ACCEPT_SHOW_GOLD_R") != 0) { shown++; scene = g3.vm.currentNode; }
                    }
                    CHECK(shown > 600 && strncmp(g3.takeName, "SIZ", 3) == 0,
                          "game: a gold medal plays its scene %s for %ld ticks, then returns to the map (%s)",
                          scene >= 0 ? g3.vm.nodes[scene].name : "-", (long)shown, g3.takeName);
                    {   /* a bronze: the same, then Izzy's announcement SIZ00013 (fixed script: IZZY.VET never plays it) */
                        int sawAnnounce = 0;
                        vet_set_by_name(&g3.vm, "MEDAL", vet_read_named(&g3.vm, "ITEM_BRONZE_MEDAL", 0), 0);
                        vet_jump_to(&g3.vm, "ACCEPT_YES");
                        for (shown = 0; t3 <= t + 3000; t3++) {
                            game_idle(&g3, t3); game_idle(&g3, t3);
                            if (strcmp(g3.takeName, "SIZ00013") == 0) sawAnnounce = 1;
                            if (strncmp(g3.vm.nodes[g3.vm.currentNode].name, "ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_", 19) == 0) shown++;
                        }
                        CHECK(g3.scriptFixes == 2 && shown > 600 && sawAnnounce,
                              "game: a bronze medal plays its scene (%ld ticks) and then Izzy's announcement SIZ00013 (%d script fixes)",
                              (long)shown, g3.scriptFixes);
                    }
                    game_close(&g3);
                }
            }
            {   /* autoplay: a random clickable thing every 2 s for 5 minutes of game time */
                unsigned seed = 12345; long clicks = 0, start = gm.takesStarted; int32_t end = t + 18000;
                for (; t <= end; t++) {
                    game_idle(&gm, t); game_idle(&gm, t);
                    if (t % 120 == 0) {
                        int cand[64][2], n = 0, i, k;
                        for (i = 0; i < UI_HOTSPOTS; i++)
                            if ((gm.ui.bi->hotspotsEnabled & (1u << i)) && gm.ui.spot[i].active && n < 64) {
                                cand[n][0] = (gm.ui.spot[i].x[0] + gm.ui.spot[i].x[3]) / 2; cand[n][1] = (gm.ui.spot[i].y[0] + gm.ui.spot[i].y[3]) / 2; n++;
                            }
                        for (k = 0x21; k < 0x65; k++) {
                            const StageSlot *q = &gm.stage.slot[k];
                            if (gm.ui.cur[k - 0x20] > 1 && (q->flags & 1) && q->bounds.right > q->bounds.left && n < 64) {
                                cand[n][0] = (q->bounds.left + q->bounds.right) / 2; cand[n][1] = (q->bounds.top + q->bounds.bottom) / 2; n++;
                            }
                        }
                        if (n) { seed = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u; if (game_click(&gm, cand[(seed >> 16) % (unsigned)n][0], cand[(seed >> 16) % (unsigned)n][1])) clicks++; }
                    }
                }
                CHECK(gm.takesStarted - start > 30 && gm.takesMissing == 0 && clicks > 50,
                      "game: autoplay %ld clicks, %ld takes, none missing", clicks, gm.takesStarted - start);
            }
            {   /* save mid-game, load into a fresh game, play on */
                static Game g2;
                char sp[1024]; FILE *sf; int ok = 0, okl = 0, menu; int32_t t2;
                int16_t gold = 0, j = 0, gold2 = -1;
                vet_get_variable(&gm.vm, vet_find(&gm.vm, "GOLD"), 1, &gold, &j);
                sprintf(sp, "%s/_c_midgame.sav", odir);
                sf = fopen(sp, "wb"); if (sf) { ok = game_save(&gm, sf); fclose(sf); }
                if (game_open(&g2, dir, 2) == 0) {
                    g2.ui.iniPath = gm.ui.iniPath;
                    g2.ui.iniPath = gm.ui.iniPath;
                    menu = vet_find(&g2.vm, "MAIN_MENU");
                    sf = fopen(sp, "rb"); if (sf) { okl = game_load(&g2, sf); fclose(sf); }
                    vet_get_variable(&g2.vm, vet_find(&g2.vm, "GOLD"), 1, &gold2, &j);
                    CHECK(ok && okl && g2.vm.currentNode == menu && gold2 == gold && strncmp((char *)g2.ui.ds + 0x2016, "Izzy", 4) == 0,
                          "game: a mid-game save loads (MAIN_MENU, GOLD[1] = %d, player 1 'Izzy')", gold2);
                    for (t2 = 0; t2 < 3000; t2++) { game_idle(&g2, t2); game_idle(&g2, t2); }
                    CHECK(g2.takesStarted > 0 && g2.takesMissing == 0, "game: play continues after loading (%ld takes)", g2.takesStarted);
                    game_close(&g2);
                }
            }
            game_close(&gm);
        }
    }

    /* ---- the VEP source compiler: IZZY.VET -> source -> compiled again, byte for byte */
    {
        static VetVM vm;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.VET");
        if (vet_load(&vm, p) == 0) {
            char src[1024]; FILE *f; size_t orig = 0, len = 0, at = 0;
            uint8_t *a = izzy_read_file(p, &orig), *b = NULL;
            char *text = NULL; size_t tl = 0;
            int nodes;
            sprintf(src, "%s/_c_izzy.vp0", odir);
            f = fopen(src, "wb");
            nodes = f ? vep_decompile(&vm, f) : 0;
            if (f) fclose(f);
            text = (char *)izzy_read_file(src, &tl);
            CHECK(nodes == 1134 && text, "vepc: IZZY.VET decompiled to %s (%d script nodes, %ld bytes)", src, nodes, (long)tl);
            if (text) {
                char *z = (char *)realloc(text, tl + 1);
                if (z) { text = z; text[tl] = 0; b = vepc_compile_text(text, 5, &len, NULL, NULL); }
            }
            if (a && b) while (at < len && at < orig && a[at] == b[at]) at++;
            CHECK(a && b && len == orig && at == orig, "vepc: recompiled %ld bytes, identical to IZZY.VET (%ld; first difference at %ld)",
                  (long)len, (long)orig, (long)at);
            free(a); free(b); free(text);
            vet_free(&vm);
        }
    }

    /* ---- the 40 videos: AVI reader + Indeo 3.2 decoder, every frame */
    {
        static const char *names[] = { "VIDARC1","VIDBOX1","VIDFEN1","VIDFEN2","VIDGYM1","VIDGYM3","VIDGYM5","VIDGYM7","VIDHAN1","VIDHAN2",
            "VIDHAN3","VIDHAN4","VIDHIJ1","VIDHOC1","VIDHOC2","VIDHOP1","VIDHOP2","VIDHOP3","VIDHOP4","VIDIVE1","VIDJUD1","VIDJUD2",
            "VIDLJ12","VIDMHT1","VIDMLJ1","VIDMPV1","VIDMPV2","VIDRES1","VIDRES2","VIDSOC1","VIDSOC2","VIDTEN1","VIDTEN2","VIDVOL1",
            "VIDVOL2","VIDWET1","VIDWET2","VIDWJ12","VIDWJ13","VIDWJ23" };
        int k, opened = 0, frames = 0, decoded = 0, errors = 0;
        long sum = 0;
        for (k = 0; k < 40; k++) {
            static Avi a; static Indeo3 d;
            char fn[64]; int i;
            sprintf(fn, "%s.AVI", names[k]);
            path(p, dir, fn);
            if (avi_open(&a, p) != 0) continue;
            opened++;
            if (iv3_init(&d, (int)a.width, (int)a.height) == 0) {
                for (i = 0; i < a.nframes; i++) {
                    int r = a.frames[i].size ? iv3_decode(&d, a.data + a.frames[i].offset, a.frames[i].size) : 0;
                    frames++; if (r > 0) decoded++; if (r < 0) errors++;
                }
                if (k == 10) { int x; for (x = 0; x < 240; x++) sum += d.planes[0].pixels[d.bufSel][90 * d.planes[0].pitch + x]; }
                iv3_free(&d);
            }
            avi_close(&a);
        }
        CHECK(opened == 40 && errors == 0 && decoded > frames * 95 / 100,   /* the rest are empty "no change" frames */
              "video: 40 AVIs, %d frames: %d pictures and %d no-change frames from the built-in Indeo 3.2 decoder, %d errors",
              frames, decoded, frames - decoded - errors, errors);
        CHECK(sum > 240 * 20 && sum < 240 * 110, "video: VIDHAN3's last frame has picture content (row 90 luma sum %ld)", sum);
    }

    /* ---- the application shell: izzy.ini options, the Sound menu, MIDI gating, quitting through the credits */
    {
        static App app;
        static AppTestHost h;
        static char ini[1024];
        AppSettings s;
        FILE *f;
        sprintf(ini, "%s/_c_app.ini", odir);
        f = fopen(ini, "w");
        if (f) { fputs("[OPTIONS]\nVOLUME=-1\nMUSIC=0\nINTRO=0\n[Options]\nOverride8Bit=true\n", f); fclose(f); }
        app_load_settings(&s, ini);
        CHECK(s.volume == -1 && s.music == 0 && s.intro == 0 && s.override8Bit == 1,
              "app: [OPTIONS] VOLUME -1, MUSIC 0, INTRO 0, [Options] Override8Bit true read back");
        remove(ini);
        app_load_settings(&s, ini);
        CHECK(s.volume == 0 && s.music == 1 && s.intro == 1 && s.override8Bit == 0, "app: defaults VOLUME 0, MUSIC 1, INTRO 1, Override8Bit FALSE");
        CHECK(app_string(APP_STR_QUIT) && strcmp(app_string(APP_STR_QUIT), "Do you really want to quit?") == 0 &&
              app_string(APP_STR_TYPE_NAME) && !app_string(0x1b58), "app: string table 0x1b59..0x1b6d");
        app.host.ctx = &h; app.host.message = test_message; app.host.check_menu = test_check; app.host.quit = test_quit;
        if (app_open(&app, dir, 1) == 0) {
            Game *g = &app.game;
            int32_t t; int ok = 1, credits = vet_find(&g->vm, "CREDITS"), quitAt = -1;
            strcpy(g->iniPath, ini); g->ui.iniPath = g->iniPath;        /* keep the options and scores out of the game folder */
            g->musicOn = 1; app.settings.music = 1;
            g->audio.ctx = &h; g->audio.midi = test_midi;
            for (t = 0; t <= 12000; t++) app_idle(&app, t);
            CHECK(h.plays > 0, "app: the intro plays MIDI (%d plays, last %s)", h.plays, h.last);
            app_command(&app, APP_CMD_VOLUME_LOW);
            ok = g->wave.masterVolume == -2 && h.checked[APP_CMD_VOLUME_LOW] == 1 && h.checked[APP_CMD_VOLUME_HIGH] == 0;
            CHECK(ok, "app: Volume Low -> master volume -2, menu checks follow");
            h.stops = 0;
            app_command(&app, APP_CMD_MUSIC);
            for (; t <= 12030; t++) app_idle(&app, t);
            CHECK(!g->musicOn && h.checked[APP_CMD_MUSIC] == 0 && (h.stops > 0 || !g->midiOpen) && !g->midiOpen,
                  "app: Music Playing off -> the next tick stops the MIDI (%d stops)", h.stops);
            {   /* a damaged save is refused and leaves the running game alone; a good one loads */
                char good[1024], bad[1024]; size_t n = 0; uint8_t *b; FILE *bf; int node = g->vm.currentNode, refused, loaded;
                sprintf(good, "%s/_c_app_good.sav", odir); sprintf(bad, "%s/_c_app_bad.sav", odir);
                vet_set_by_name(&g->vm, "STATE1", 77, 0);
                app_save_to(&app, good);
                vet_set_by_name(&g->vm, "STATE1", 55, 0);
                b = izzy_read_file(good, &n);
                if ((bf = fopen(bad, "wb")) != NULL) { if (b) fwrite(b, 1, n / 2, bf); fclose(bf); }
                free(b);
                refused = !app_load_from(&app, bad);
                CHECK(refused && vet_read_named(&g->vm, "STATE1", 0) == 55 && g->vm.currentNode == node,
                      "app: a damaged save (half the file) is refused and the running game is untouched");
                loaded = app_load_from(&app, good);
                CHECK(loaded && vet_read_named(&g->vm, "STATE1", 0) == 77, "app: the good save then loads (STATE1 = %d)", vet_read_named(&g->vm, "STATE1", 0));
                remove(good); remove(bad);
            }
            h.answer = APP_ID_YES;
            app_request_quit(&app);
            CHECK(g->vm.currentNode == credits && app.closed, "app: quitting runs the CREDITS node");
            {
                long tk = g->takesStarted;
                for (; t <= 40000 && quitAt < 0; t++) {
                    if (!app_idle(&app, t)) quitAt = t;
                    if (t % 120 == 0 && !g->creditsRunning) {   /* CREDITS_HIGH_SCORES waits for a click */
                        int k, cx = 320, cy = 240;                  /* the first enabled control (ACCEPT) */
                        for (k = 0x21; k < 0x65; k++) {
                            const StageSlot *q = &g->stage.slot[k];
                            if (g->ui.cur[k - 0x20] > 1 && (q->flags & 1) && q->bounds.right > q->bounds.left) {
                                cx = (q->bounds.left + q->bounds.right) / 2; cy = (q->bounds.top + q->bounds.bottom) / 2; break;
                            }
                        }
                        game_click(g, cx, cy);
                    }
                }
                CHECK(quitAt > 0 && h.quit && g->creditsRunning,
                      "app: ACCEPT on the high scores, END_CREDITS plays take %s (%ld takes), then the app closes at tick %ld",
                      g->takeName, g->takesStarted - tk, (long)quitAt);
            }
            {
                char v[16], m[16];
                ini_get(ini, "OPTIONS", "VOLUME", "", v, sizeof v); ini_get(ini, "OPTIONS", "MUSIC", "", m, sizeof m);
                CHECK(strcmp(v, "-2") == 0 && strcmp(m, "0") == 0, "app: VOLUME=%s MUSIC=%s written on quit", v, m);
            }
            app_close(&app);
        }
    }

    /* ---- narration pages (vet_FormatDescription) */
    {
        static VetVM vm;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.VET");
        if (vet_load(&vm, p) == 0) {
            int i, nodes = 0, pages = 0, n = vet_find(&vm, "ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_01");
            char page[512]; uint8_t m;
            int next = n >= 0 ? vet_format_page(vm.nodes[n].desc, 0, page, sizeof page, &m) : -1;
            CHECK(next == 26 && strcmp(page, "MDBR0001.BMP\nDDBR0001.WAV") == 0 && m == 3,
                  "narration: ACCEPT_SHOW_BRONZE_01 page 1 = picture MDBR0001.BMP + voice DDBR0001.WAV (next %d)", next);
            for (i = 0; i < vm.nodeCount; i++) {
                int off = 0, k = 0;
                if (vm.nodes[i].type != 1 || !vm.nodes[i].desc || !vm.nodes[i].desc[0]) continue;
                nodes++;
                do { off = vet_format_page(vm.nodes[i].desc, off, page, sizeof page, &m); if (page[0]) pages++; } while (off && ++k < 50);
            }
            CHECK(nodes == 713 && pages == 789, "narration: %d nodes describe %d pages (713 / 789)", nodes, pages);
            vet_free(&vm);
        }
    }

    /* ---- savegame: round trip through the "Izzy_Adv_24:" format */
    {
        static VetVM a, b;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.VET");
        if (vet_load(&a, p) == 0 && vet_load(&b, p) == 0) {
            static VetSaveExtras xa, xb;
            char names[SAVE_PLAYERS][32] = { "Izzy", "Bo", "", "Player Four", "" }, back[SAVE_PLAYERS][32];
            char tmp[1024];
            FILE *f;
            int i, diff = 0, desc = 0, mm = vet_find(&b, "MAIN_MENU");
            vet_new_game(&a, 1234);
            vet_set_by_name(&a, "STATE1", 3, 0);
            vet_set_by_name(&a, "GOLD", 7, 2);
            vet_store_variable(&a, vet_find(&a, "GOLD"), 4, 9, 1);       /* creates GOLD[1..4] */
            sprintf(tmp, "%s/_c_selftest.sav", odir);
            f = fopen(tmp, "wb");
            CHECK(f && vet_save_game(&a, &xa, (const char (*)[32])names, f) == 1, "savegame: wrote %s", tmp);
            if (f) fclose(f);
            f = fopen(tmp, "rb");
            CHECK(f && vet_load_game(&b, &xb, back, f, &desc) == 1, "savegame: read it back");
            if (f) fclose(f);
            for (i = 0; i < a.nodeCount; i++) {
                int k;
                if (a.nodes[i].value != b.nodes[i].value || a.nodes[i].jitter != b.nodes[i].jitter ||
                    a.nodes[i].arrCount != b.nodes[i].arrCount) { diff++; continue; }
                for (k = 0; k < a.nodes[i].arrCount; k++) if (a.nodes[i].arr[k] != b.nodes[i].arr[k]) { diff++; break; }
            }
            CHECK(diff == 0 && strcmp(back[0], "Izzy") == 0 && strcmp(back[3], "Player Four") == 0,
                  "savegame: all %d variables and the player names survive (%d differ)", a.nodeCount, diff);
            CHECK(b.currentNode == mm, "savegame: loading resumes at MAIN_MENU");
            vet_free(&a); vet_free(&b);
        }
    }

    /* ---- stage: play takes through the Stage (setters -> anim_TickSlot -> anim_Step -> compose) */
    {
        static Screen s1, s2;
        static Stage st;
        static Scene sc;
        DatDir d; FILE *dat; int32_t t;
        path(p, dir, "IZZY.DIR"); dat_dir_load(&d, p);
        path(p, dir, "IZZYCD1.DAT"); dat = fopen(p, "rb");
        if (dat) {
            /* SBC00010 at tick 0 must equal the static composition above */
            {
                char ref[1024]; size_t rl; uint8_t *rb;
                stage_init(&st, &s1);
                CHECK(scene_open(&sc, &st, NULL, &d, dat, "SBC00010") == 0, "stage: opened take SBC00010");
                scene_tick(&sc, 0); stage_FrameUpdate(&st, 0);
                sprintf(ref, "%s/_c_render_sbc00010.bmp", odir);
                rb = izzy_read_file(ref, &rl);
                if (rb && rl >= 54 + 1024 + SCREEN_W * SCREEN_H) {
                    long diff = 0; int y, x;
                    for (y = 0; y < SCREEN_H; y++) for (x = 0; x < SCREEN_W; x++)
                        if (rb[54 + 1024 + (size_t)(SCREEN_H - 1 - y) * SCREEN_W + x] != s1.px[(size_t)y * SCREEN_W + x]) diff++;
                    CHECK(diff == 0, "stage: SBC00010 frame 0 equals the static composition (%ld pixels differ)", diff);
                }
                free(rb);
                scene_close(&sc);
            }
            /* SIN00010 (intro): slot 2 walks from x=-57 to x=264 over 312 ticks starting at tick 1830 */
            {
                int ok1 = 0, ok2 = 0, moved = 0; int32_t lastX = 0;
                stage_init(&st, &s2);
                CHECK(scene_open(&sc, &st, NULL, &d, dat, "SIN00010") == 0, "stage: opened take SIN00010");
                for (t = 0; t <= 2150; t++) {
                    scene_tick(&sc, t); stage_FrameUpdate(&st, t);
                    if (t == 1829 + 1) ok1 = st.slot[2].x == -57;
                    if (t > 1835 && t < 2140 && st.slot[2].x != lastX) moved++;
                    if (t > 1830) lastX = st.slot[2].x;
                    if (t == 2141) ok2 = st.slot[2].x >= 250 && st.slot[2].x <= 264;   /* ends at 2142, when the next Path moves it */
                }
                CHECK(ok1 && ok2 && moved > 10, "stage: intro path -57 -> 264 (%d position updates, x=%ld at 2141)", moved, (long)lastX);
                {
                    char out[1024];
                    sprintf(out, "%s/_c_stage_sin00010_t2150.bmp", odir);
                    CHECK(render_save_bmp(&s2, out) == 0, "wrote %s", out);
                }
                scene_close(&sc);
            }
            fclose(dat);
        }
        dat_dir_free(&d);
    }

    printf("%s (%d failure%s)\n", failures ? "FAILED" : "ALL PASSED", failures, failures == 1 ? "" : "s");
    return failures ? 1 : 0;
}

```

`src/tests/stress.c`:

```c
/*
 * stress.c - play the game unattended to find bugs.
 *   stress <gamedir> <seeds> <minutes per game>   (scratch .ini / .sav files go to the current folder;
 *   STRESS_FIRST=n starts at seed n, for running several in parallel)
 * Each game: the intro, a name, START GAME, then every 1-2 s a click on something clickable (sometimes anywhere),
 * now and then a typed key, a save + reload into a second Game (the variables must survive), and a New Game.
 * Reported: VET runtime errors, missing takes / pictures, stalls (nothing clickable, no take or narration running
 * and no node change for 60 s), nodes visited, and the AVIs / medal scenes / announcements seen.
 */
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "../game.h"

static long g_errors;
static char g_lastError[200];
static void on_error(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, const char *msg)
{
    (void)ctx; (void)vm;
    g_errors++;
    strncpy(g_lastError, msg, sizeof g_lastError - 1);
}

static int g_aviRequests; static char g_aviLast[64];
static int fake_avi(void *ctx, const char *path, int x, int y, int w, int h)
{   /* a pretend player: every request "plays" for 2 s (the harness ends it) */
    (void)ctx; (void)x; (void)y; (void)w; (void)h;
    g_aviRequests++; strncpy(g_aviLast, path, sizeof g_aviLast - 1);
    return 1;
}

static unsigned rnd(unsigned *s) { *s = *s * 1103515245u + 12345u; return (*s >> 16) & 0x7fff; }

static int clickables(Game *g, int cand[64][2])
{
    int n = 0, i, k;
    for (i = 0; i < UI_HOTSPOTS; i++)
        if ((g->ui.bi->hotspotsEnabled & (1u << i)) && g->ui.spot[i].active && n < 64) {
            cand[n][0] = (g->ui.spot[i].x[0] + g->ui.spot[i].x[3]) / 2; cand[n][1] = (g->ui.spot[i].y[0] + g->ui.spot[i].y[3]) / 2; n++;
        }
    for (k = 0x21; k < 0x65; k++) {
        const StageSlot *q = &g->stage.slot[k];
        if (g->ui.cur[k - 0x20] > 1 && (q->flags & 1) && q->bounds.right > q->bounds.left && n < 64) {
            cand[n][0] = (q->bounds.left + q->bounds.right) / 2; cand[n][1] = (q->bounds.top + q->bounds.bottom) / 2; n++;
        }
    }
    return n;
}

static Game g, g2;
static unsigned char visited[4096];

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    int seeds = argc > 2 ? atoi(argv[2]) : 3, s, problems = 0;
    long minutes = argc > 3 ? atol(argv[3]) : 20;
    if (argc < 2) { fprintf(stderr, "usage: stress <gamedir> [seeds] [minutes]\n"); return 2; }
    int first = getenv("STRESS_FIRST") ? atoi(getenv("STRESS_FIRST")) : 1;
    for (s = first; s < first + seeds; s++) {
        unsigned seed = (unsigned)s * 2654435761u + 7;
        int32_t t, lastChange = 0, end;
        int lastNode = -1, names = 0, saves = 0, loads = 0, newGames = 0, avis = 0, stalls = 0, clicks = 0, i, n;
        long takesAt = 0, missingAt = 0;
        char lastTake[16] = "", ini[600];
        int avisSeen = 0, comps = 0, medalScenes = 0, announce[4] = { 0, 0, 0, 0 };
        g_errors = 0; g_lastError[0] = 0;
        if (game_open(&g, argv[1], (unsigned)s) != 0) { printf("seed %d: cannot open\n", s); return 1; }
        snprintf(ini, sizeof ini, "_c_stress%d.ini", s);   /* in the current folder, not the game's */ remove(ini);
        g.ui.iniPath = ini;
        g.vm.onError = on_error;
        g.audio.playAvi = fake_avi; g_aviRequests = 0;
        end = (int32_t)(12000 + minutes * 3600);
        for (t = 0; t <= end; t++) {
            const char *node;
            game_idle(&g, t); game_idle(&g, t);
            if (g.vm.currentNode != lastNode) {
                lastNode = g.vm.currentNode; lastChange = t;
                if (lastNode >= 0 && lastNode < 4096) visited[lastNode] = 1;
                node = lastNode >= 0 ? g.vm.nodes[lastNode].name : "";
                if (!strncmp(node, "COMP_VIDEO_", 11)) comps++;
                if (!strncmp(node, "ACCEPT_SHOW_", 12) && strstr(node, "_R") == NULL && strlen(node) > 18) medalScenes++;
            }
            if (strcmp(lastTake, g.takeName) != 0) {
                strcpy(lastTake, g.takeName); lastChange = t;
                if (!strcmp(lastTake, "SIZ00011")) announce[1]++;
                if (!strcmp(lastTake, "SIZ00012")) announce[2]++;
                if (!strcmp(lastTake, "SIZ00013")) announce[3]++;
            }
            if (g.aviPlaying) { if (++avisSeen % 120 == 0) game_avi_finished(&g); }   /* end the pretend video after 2 s */
            node = g.vm.currentNode >= 0 ? g.vm.nodes[g.vm.currentNode].name : "";
            if (!strcmp(node, "ENTER_NAMES") && t % 300 == 0 && names < 40) {   /* type a name, press START */
                const char *nm = (names & 1) ? "zed" : "Abigail";
                for (i = 0; nm[i]; i++) game_char(&g, nm[i]);
                names++;
            }
            if (t > 12000 && t % (60 + (int)(rnd(&seed) % 60)) == 0) {
                int cand[64][2];
                n = clickables(&g, cand);
                if (n && rnd(&seed) % 10) { int k = (int)(rnd(&seed) % (unsigned)n); game_click(&g, cand[k][0], cand[k][1]); clicks++; }
                else { game_click(&g, (int)(rnd(&seed) % 640), (int)(rnd(&seed) % 480)); clicks++; }
                game_mouse_move(&g, (int)(rnd(&seed) % 640), (int)(rnd(&seed) % 480));
                if (rnd(&seed) % 40 == 0) game_char(&g, 'a' + (int)(rnd(&seed) % 26));
                if (rnd(&seed) % 25 == 0) game_char(&g, 8);
            }
            if (t > 15000 && t % 9000 == 4500) {                       /* save, load into a second game, compare */
                char sp[700]; FILE *f; int ok = 0, okl = 0, diff = 0, k;
                snprintf(sp, sizeof sp, "_c_stress%d.sav", s);
                if ((f = fopen(sp, "wb")) != NULL) { ok = game_save(&g, f); fclose(f); }
                saves++;
                if (ok && game_open(&g2, argv[1], 99) == 0) {
                    g2.ui.iniPath = ini;
                    if ((f = fopen(sp, "rb")) != NULL) { okl = game_load(&g2, f); fclose(f); }
                    for (k = 0; k < g.vm.nodeCount; k++)
                        if (g.vm.nodes[k].type == 2 && g.vm.nodes[k].value != g2.vm.nodes[k].value && strcmp(g.vm.nodes[k].name, "TIME")) diff++;
                    if (!okl || diff) { printf("seed %d t=%ld: save/load %s, %d variables differ\n", s, (long)t, okl ? "ok" : "FAILED", diff); problems++; }
                    else loads++;
                    for (k = 0; k < 600; k++) { game_idle(&g2, k); game_idle(&g2, k); }
                    game_close(&g2);
                }
            }
            if (t > 20000 && t % 54000 == 27000) { game_new_game(&g); newGames++; lastChange = t; }
            {   /* a stall: nothing to click, nothing playing, no change for 60 s */
                int cand[64][2];
                if (t - lastChange > 3600) {
                    n = clickables(&g, cand);
                    if (n == 0 && g.more && vet_get_busy(&g.vm) == 0 && !g.vm.running) {
                        printf("seed %d t=%ld: STALL at node %s take %s (busy %x, more %d)\n", s, (long)t, node, g.takeName, vet_get_busy(&g.vm), g.more);
                        stalls++; problems++;
                    }
                    lastChange = t;
                }
            }
        }
        (void)takesAt; (void)missingAt; (void)avis;
        n = 0; for (i = 0; i < 4096; i++) n += visited[i];
        printf("seed %d: %ld min, %d clicks, %ld takes (%ld missing), %d competition videos (%d requested), %d medal scenes, announcements gold %d silver %d bronze %d, "
               "%d saves / %d reloads ok, %d new games, VET errors %ld%s%s, stalls %d, nodes visited so far %d\n",
               s, minutes, clicks, g.takesStarted, g.takesMissing, comps, g_aviRequests, medalScenes, announce[1], announce[2], announce[3],
               saves, loads, newGames, g_errors, g_errors ? ": " : "", g_lastError, stalls, n);
        if (g_errors) problems++;
        game_close(&g);
    }
    printf("%d problems\n", problems);
    return problems ? 1 : 0;
}

```

`src/tests/vep_tool.c`:

```c
/*
 * vep_tool.c - the script toolchain from the command line.
 *   vep_tool decompile IZZY.VET out.VP0     write the compiled script as programmer source
 *   vep_tool compile <base>                 vet_LoadOrCompile: base.VEP or base.VP0-9 + base.VW0-9 -> base.VET
 *   vep_tool build in.VP0 out.VET           compile one programmer script (version 5)
 */
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "../vet.h"
#include "../vepc.h"

static void say(void *ctx, int code, const char *msg) { (void)ctx; (void)code; fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", msg); }

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    static VetVM vm;
    if (argc == 4 && strcmp(argv[1], "decompile") == 0) {
        FILE *f;
        int n;
        if (vet_load(&vm, argv[2]) != 0) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot read %s\n", argv[2]); return 1; }
        f = fopen(argv[3], "wb");
        if (!f) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot write %s\n", argv[3]); return 1; }
        n = vep_decompile(&vm, f);
        fclose(f);
        printf("%d script nodes of %d written to %s\n", n, vm.nodeCount, argv[3]);
        return 0;
    }
    if (argc == 3 && strcmp(argv[1], "compile") == 0) {
        int r = vepc_load_or_compile(&vm, argv[2], say, NULL);
        if (r == 0) printf("%s.VET: %d nodes, start %s\n", argv[2], vm.nodeCount, vm.nodes[vm.startNode].name);
        return r == 0 ? 0 : 1;
    }
    if (argc == 4 && strcmp(argv[1], "build") == 0) {
        size_t tl = 0, len = 0;
        char *t = (char *)izzy_read_file(argv[2], &tl), *z;
        uint8_t *img;
        FILE *f;
        if (!t) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot read %s\n", argv[2]); return 1; }
        z = (char *)realloc(t, tl + 1); if (!z) return 1; t = z; t[tl] = 0;
        img = vepc_compile_text(t, 5, &len, say, NULL);
        if (!img || !(f = fopen(argv[3], "wb"))) return 1;
        fwrite(img, 1, len, f); fclose(f);
        printf("%s: %ld bytes\n", argv[3], (long)len);
        return 0;
    }
    fprintf(stderr, "usage: vep_tool decompile <in.VET> <out.VP0> | compile <base> | build <in.VP0> <out.VET>\n");
    return 2;
}
```

```md
# Third-party code

## `indeo3data.h` — Indeo Video 3 codebook tables (from FFmpeg)

- **Source:** `libavcodec/indeo3data.h` from FFmpeg (https://github.com/FFmpeg/FFmpeg), downloaded 2026-11-24.
- **Copyright:** (c) 2009–2011 Maxim Poliakovski.
- **Licence:** GNU Lesser General Public License, version 2.1 or later. The licence header is kept in the file. The full text is at https://www.gnu.org/licenses/old-licenses/lgpl-2.1.html.
- **Changes:** FFmpeg's `#include "config.h"` is replaced by a `HAVE_BIGENDIAN 0` default, and the header comment notes where the file came from.
- **Used by:** `src/indeo3.c`, the game's Indeo 3.2 video decoder. Only the data tables come from FFmpeg; the decoder code in `indeo3.c` was written for this project.

If you distribute builds that include this file, the LGPL applies to it. Keep this notice and the licence text with them, and make the source of this file available.
```

`src/third_party/indeo3data.h`:

```h
/*
 * Indeo Video v3 compatible decoder - VQ tables
 * (izzy: libavcodec/indeo3data.h from FFmpeg, unmodified except for the config.h include)
 * Copyright (c) 2009 - 2011 Maxim Poliakovski
 *
 * This file is part of FFmpeg.
 *
 * FFmpeg is free software; you can redistribute it and/or
 * modify it under the terms of the GNU Lesser General Public
 * License as published by the Free Software Foundation; either
 * version 2.1 of the License, or (at your option) any later version.
 *
 * FFmpeg is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,
 * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of
 * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the GNU
 * Lesser General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU Lesser General Public
 * License along with FFmpeg; if not, write to the Free Software
 * Foundation, Inc., 51 Franklin Street, Fifth Floor, Boston, MA 02110-1301 USA
 */

#ifndef AVCODEC_INDEO3DATA_H
#define AVCODEC_INDEO3DATA_H

#include <stdint.h>

/* izzy: adapted to build outside FFmpeg - FFmpeg's config.h is not used; the port targets little-endian hosts */
#ifndef HAVE_BIGENDIAN
#define HAVE_BIGENDIAN 0
#endif

/*
 * Define compressed VQ tables.
 */

#define TAB_1_1 \
    PD(   0,   0), E2(   2,   2), E4(  -1,   3), E2(   4,   4), E4(   1,   5),\
    E2(  -4,   4), E4(  -2,   6), E4(   4,   9), E2(   9,   9), E4(   1,  10),\
    E4(  -5,   8), E4(   9,  15), E4(  -3,  12), E4(   4,  16), E2(  16,  16),\
    E4(   0,  18), E2( -12,  12), E4(  -9,  16), E4(  11,  27), E4(  19,  28),\
    E4(  -6,  22), E4(   4,  29), E2(  30,  30), E4(  -2,  33), E4( -18,  23),\
    E4( -15,  30), E4(  22,  46), E4(  13,  47), E4(  35,  49), E4( -11,  41),\
    E4(   4,  51), E2(  54,  54), E2( -34,  34), E4( -29,  42), E4(  -6,  60),\
    E4(  27,  76), E4(  43,  77), E4( -24,  55), E4(  14,  79), E4(  63,  83),\
    E4( -20,  74), E4(   2,  88), E2(  93,  93), E4( -52,  61), E4(  52, 120),\
    E4( -45,  75), E4(  75, 125), E4(  33, 122), E4( -13, 103), E4( -40,  96),\
    E4( -34, 127), E2( -89,  89), E4( -78, 105), E2(  12,  12), E2(  23,  23),\
    E2(  42,  42), E2(  73,  73)

#define TAB_1_2 \
    PD(   0,   0), E2(   3,   3), E4(  -1,   4), E2(   7,   7), E4(   2,   8),\
    E4(  -2,   9), E2(  -6,   6), E4(   6,  13), E2(  13,  13), E4(   1,  14),\
    E4(  -8,  12), E4(  14,  23), E4(  -5,  18), E4(   6,  24), E2(  24,  24),\
    E4(  -1,  27), E2( -17,  17), E4( -13,  23), E4(  16,  40), E4(  28,  41),\
    E4(  -9,  33), E4(   6,  43), E2(  46,  46), E4(  -4,  50), E4( -27,  34),\
    E4( -22,  45), E4(  34,  69), E4(  19,  70), E4(  53,  73), E4( -17,  62),\
    E4(   5,  77), E2(  82,  82), E2( -51,  51), E4( -43,  64), E4( -10,  90),\
    E4(  41, 114), E4(  64, 116), E4( -37,  82), E4(  22, 119), E4(  95, 124),\
    E4( -30, 111), E4( -78,  92), E4( -68, 113), E2(  18,  18), E2(  34,  34),\
    E2(  63,  63), E2( 109, 109)

#define TAB_1_3 \
    PD(   0,   0), E2(   4,   4), E4(  -1,   5), E4(   3,  10), E2(   9,   9),\
    E2(  -7,   7), E4(  -3,  12), E4(   8,  17), E2(  17,  17), E4(   1,  19),\
    E4( -11,  16), E4(  -6,  23), E4(  18,  31), E4(   8,  32), E2(  33,  33),\
    E4(  -1,  36), E2( -23,  23), E4( -17,  31), E4(  21,  54), E4(  37,  55),\
    E4( -12,  44), E4(   8,  57), E2(  61,  61), E4(  -5,  66), E4( -36,  45),\
    E4( -29,  60), E4(  45,  92), E4(  25,  93), E4(  71,  97), E4( -22,  83),\
    E4(   7, 102), E2( 109, 109), E2( -68,  68), E4( -57,  85), E4( -13, 120),\
    E4( -49, 110), E4(-104, 123), E2(  24,  24), E2(  46,  46), E2(  84,  84)

#define TAB_1_4 \
    PD(   0,   0), E2(   5,   5), E4(  -2,   7), E2(  11,  11), E4(   3,  13),\
    E2(  -9,   9), E4(  -4,  15), E4(  11,  22), E2(  21,  21), E4(   2,  24),\
    E4( -14,  20), E4(  23,  38), E4(  -8,  29), E4(  11,  39), E2(  41,  41),\
    E4(  -1,  45), E2( -29,  29), E4( -22,  39), E4(  27,  67), E4(  47,  69),\
    E4( -15,  56), E4(  11,  71), E2(  76,  76), E4(  -6,  83), E4( -45,  57),\
    E4( -36,  75), E4(  56, 115), E4(  31, 117), E4(  88, 122), E4( -28, 104),\

/**
 * Pack two delta values (a,b) into one 16-bit word
 * according with endianness of the host machine.
 */
#if HAVE_BIGENDIAN
#define PD(a,b) (((a) * (1 << 8)) + (b))
#else
#define PD(a,b) (((b) * (1 << 8)) + (a))
#endif

/**
 * Expand a pair of delta values (a,b)
 * into two/four delta entries.
 */
#define E2(a, b) PD(a, b), PD(-(a), -(b))
#define E4(a, b) PD(a, b), PD(-(a), -(b)), PD(b, a), PD(-(b), -(a))

/*
 * VQ tables for 4x4 block modes.
 * Let the compiler decompress and build the tables for us.
 */
static const int16_t delta_tab_1_1[195] = { TAB_1_1 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_1_2[159] = { TAB_1_2 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_1_3[133] = { TAB_1_3 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_1_4[115] = { TAB_1_4 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_1_5[101] = { TAB_1_5 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_1_6[93]  = { TAB_1_6 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_1_7[87]  = { TAB_1_7 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_1_8[77]  = { TAB_1_8 };

static const int16_t delta_tab_2_1[195] = { TAB_2_1 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_2_2[159] = { TAB_2_2 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_2_3[133] = { TAB_2_3 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_2_4[115] = { TAB_2_4 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_2_5[101] = { TAB_2_5 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_2_6[93]  = { TAB_2_6 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_2_7[87]  = { TAB_2_7 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_2_8[77]  = { TAB_2_8 };

static const int16_t delta_tab_3_1[128] = { TAB_3_1 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_3_2[79]  = { TAB_3_2 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_3_3[79]  = { TAB_3_3 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_3_4[79]  = { TAB_3_4 };
static const int16_t delta_tab_3_5[79]  = { TAB_3_5 };

#undef PD

/**
 * Pack four delta values (a,a,b,b) into one 32-bit word
 * according with endianness of the host machine.
 */
#if HAVE_BIGENDIAN
#define PD(a,b) (((a) * (1 << 24)) + ((a) * (1 << 16)) + ((b) * (1 << 8)) + (b))
#else
#define PD(a,b) (((b) * (1 << 24)) + ((b) * (1 << 16)) + ((a) * (1 << 8)) + (a))
#endif

/*
 * VQ tables for 8x8 block modes.
 * Those are based on the same delta tables by using
 * each value twice: ABCD --> AABBCCDD.
 */
static const int32_t delta_tab_1_1_m10[195] = { TAB_1_1 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_1_2_m10[159] = { TAB_1_2 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_1_3_m10[133] = { TAB_1_3 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_1_4_m10[115] = { TAB_1_4 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_1_5_m10[101] = { TAB_1_5 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_1_6_m10[93]  = { TAB_1_6 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_1_7_m10[87]  = { TAB_1_7 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_1_8_m10[77]  = { TAB_1_8 };

static const int32_t delta_tab_2_1_m10[195] = { TAB_2_1 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_2_2_m10[159] = { TAB_2_2 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_2_3_m10[133] = { TAB_2_3 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_2_4_m10[115] = { TAB_2_4 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_2_5_m10[101] = { TAB_2_5 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_2_6_m10[93]  = { TAB_2_6 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_2_7_m10[87]  = { TAB_2_7 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_2_8_m10[77]  = { TAB_2_8 };

static const int32_t delta_tab_3_1_m10[128] = { TAB_3_1 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_3_2_m10[79]  = { TAB_3_2 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_3_3_m10[79]  = { TAB_3_3 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_3_4_m10[79]  = { TAB_3_4 };
static const int32_t delta_tab_3_5_m10[79]  = { TAB_3_5 };


typedef struct vqEntry {
    const int16_t  *deltas;     ///< delta tables for 4x4 block modes
    const int32_t  *deltas_m10; ///< delta tables for 8x8 block modes
    uint8_t        num_dyads;   ///< number of two-pixel deltas
    uint8_t        quad_exp;    ///< log2 of four-pixel deltas
} vqEntry;

static const vqEntry vq_tab[24] = {
    /* set 1 */
    { delta_tab_1_1, delta_tab_1_1_m10, 195,  7 },
    { delta_tab_1_2, delta_tab_1_2_m10, 159,  9 },

    { delta_tab_1_8, delta_tab_1_8_m10,  77, 13 },

    /* set 2 */
    { delta_tab_2_1, delta_tab_2_1_m10, 195,  7 },
    { delta_tab_2_2, delta_tab_2_2_m10, 159,  9 },
    { delta_tab_2_3, delta_tab_2_3_m10, 133, 10 },
    { delta_tab_2_4, delta_tab_2_4_m10, 115, 11 },
```
