```h
    { delta_tab_3_5, delta_tab_3_5_m10,  79, 13 },
    { delta_tab_3_5, delta_tab_3_5_m10,  79, 13 },
    { delta_tab_3_5, delta_tab_3_5_m10,  79, 13 }
};

#endif /* AVCODEC_INDEO3DATA_H */

```

`src/ui.c`:

```c
/*
 * ui.c - hotspots and clicks (see ui.h).
 */
#include "ui.h"
#include "config.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define DSTEXT(o) (0x10000L + (o))
#include "ui_controls.inc"

static void fmt_number(char *s, long v)
{   /* ltoa + orig: 1008:6efa - thousands commas */
    int len, groups, out;
    sprintf(s, "%ld", v);
    len = (int)strlen(s);
    if (len <= 0) return;
    groups = (len - 1) / 3; out = groups + len;
    if (groups && out < 10) {
        s[out] = 0;
        for (; groups; groups--) {
            s[out - 1] = s[len - 1]; s[out - 2] = s[len - 2]; len -= 3; s[out - 3] = s[len];
            out -= 4; s[out] = ',';
        }
    }
}

static int16_t read_elem(UiState *u, int nodeSlot, int index)
{   /* orig: 1018:5d4e / 1018:5db2 (vet_RandomPickA / B): jittered read of node[index] */
    int16_t v = 0, j = 0;
    int node = u->node[nodeSlot];
    if (!u->vm || node <= 0) return 0;
    if (vet_get_variable(u->vm, node, index, &v, &j) && j > 0) {
        int (*rnd)(void) = u->vm->rand ? u->vm->rand : vet_msvc_rand;
        int a = rnd() % (j + 1), b = rnd() % (j + 1);
        v = (int16_t)(v + a - b);
    }
    return v;
}

static char *ds_str(UiState *u, int off) { return (char *)u->ds + off; }
static char *name_of(UiState *u, int p) { return ds_str(u, 0x1ff6 + 0x20 * p); }

enum { N_PLAYERS, N_TURNS, N_PLAYER, N_GOLD, N_SILVER, N_BRONZE, N_BONUS, N_SUNCATCHER, N_COMPETE, N_MORPH,
       N_CORIBA, N_LEFT, N_RIGHT, N_EGG };

void ui_init(UiState *u, IzzyUi *bi, Stage *st, VetVM *vm, const NeResources *res, const uint8_t *dgroup, size_t dgroupLen)
{   /* orig: 1008:5fce ui_ctor, 1008:98ae ui_BuildControlTable, 1008:8bb0 (variable cache); the definitions made by
     * 1008:970c, 1008:9780, 1008:97b8, 1008:97ea, 1008:984c are the generated table in ui_controls.inc */
    static const char *names[14] = { "N_PLAYERS", "N_TURNS", "PLAYER", "GOLD", "SILVER", "BRONZE", "BONUS",
        "SUNCATCHER", "COMPETE_CARD", "MORPH_CARD", "CORIBA_CARD", "PLAYER_LEFT", "PLAYER_RIGHT", "EASTER_EGG" };
    size_t i;
    int k;
    memset(u, 0, sizeof *u);
    u->bi = bi; u->stage = st; u->vm = vm; u->res = res;
    ui_spot_remove(u, 0);
    if (dgroup) memcpy(u->ds, dgroup, dgroupLen < UI_DS_SIZE ? dgroupLen : UI_DS_SIZE);
    for (k = 0; k < UI_CONTROLS; k++) u->cur[k] = -1;
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof g_controls / sizeof g_controls[0]; i++) {
        const UiControlDef *d = &g_controls[i];
        if (d->ctrl >= 0 && d->ctrl < UI_CONTROLS && d->state >= 0 && d->state < UI_STATES) u->def[d->ctrl][d->state] = d;
    }
    {   /* 1008:97ea calls from 98ae: the font strips, bitmaps 402 / 403 in slots 0x66 / 0x67 */
        static const UiControlDef fonts[2] = { { 68, 2, 402, 0, 0, 0x7ffd, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0 }, { 69, 2, 403, 0, 0, 0x7ffd, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0 } };
        u->def[68][2] = &fonts[0]; u->def[69][2] = &fonts[1];
    }
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof g_controlIds / sizeof g_controlIds[0]; i++) {
        int c = (g_controlIds[i][0] - 0x2be) / 2;
        if (c >= 0 && c < UI_CONTROLS) u->ctrlId[c] = g_controlIds[i][1];
    }
    for (k = 0; k < 14; k++) if (vm) { int n = vet_find(vm, names[k]); u->node[k] = (int16_t)(n > 0 ? n : 0); }
    u->savedPanel = (int16_t)rd_i16(u->ds + 0x592);
    u->lastPlayer = u->last0458 = u->last045a = u->last045c = -1;
    if (st) { ui_set_control_state(u, 2, 68); ui_set_control_state(u, 2, 69); }  /* the two font strips */
}

/* the control slot of (ctrl, state): 970c gives ctrl + 0x20; 97ea gives an explicit slot (the fonts) */
static int def_slot(const UiControlDef *d) { return d->ctrl >= 68 ? (d->ctrl == 68 ? 0x66 : 0x67) : d->ctrl + 0x20; }

int ui_set_control_state(UiState *u, int state, int ctrl)
{   /* orig: 1008:b674 game_SetControlState */
    const UiControlDef *d = NULL;
    const uint8_t *bmp = NULL;
    int s, slot = 0;
    if (ctrl < 0 || ctrl >= UI_CONTROLS) return 0;
    if (u->cur[ctrl] == state) return 1;
    u->cur[ctrl] = (int16_t)state;
    for (s = state < UI_STATES ? state : UI_STATES - 1; s >= 0; s--) if (u->def[ctrl][s]) { d = u->def[ctrl][s]; break; }
    if (!d || !u->stage) return 0;
    slot = def_slot(d);
    if (d->res > 0 && d->res < 0x10000 && u->res) bmp = res_find(u->res, RT_BITMAP_, (int)d->res, NULL);   /* 1008:b86c res_Lock */
    if (!bmp) take_SetSlotDib(u->stage, slot, NULL);
    else {
        int y = d->y;
        if (ctrl == 11 && u->cur[0] == 0 && u->def[0][2]) y += 0x1e0 - u->def[0][2]->y;   /* panel hidden: drop down */
        take_SetSlotDib(u->stage, slot, bmp);
        stage_SetSlotPosition(u->stage, slot, d->x, y);
        stage_SetSlotDepth(u->stage, slot, d->depth);
        stage_SetSlotFlags(u->stage, slot, 1, d->flags);
    }
    stage_SetSlotText(u->stage, slot, d->text >= 0x10000 ? ds_str(u, (int)(d->text - 0x10000)) : NULL,
                      d->font, d->dx, d->dy, (unsigned)d->align);
    return bmp != NULL;
}

void ui_set_default_controls(UiState *u, unsigned mask)
{   /* orig: 1008:955a game_SetControlMask */
    int i; unsigned bit = 1;
    u->mask188 = (uint16_t)mask;
    for (i = 0; i < 0x33; i++, bit = (bit << 1) & 0xffff) {
        if (mask & bit) { u->enabled[i + 1] = 1; u->defEnabled[i + 1] = 1; }
        else u->defEnabled[i + 1] = 0;
    }
}

void ui_set_controls(UiState *u, unsigned mask)
{   /* orig: 1008:95ac */
    int i; unsigned bit = 1;
    u->mask186 = (uint16_t)mask;
    for (i = 0; i < 0x33; i++, bit = (bit << 1) & 0xffff) u->enabled[i + 1] = (mask & bit) ? 1 : u->defEnabled[i + 1];
}

static void reset_states(UiState *u)
{   /* orig: 1008:85a0 game_ResetControlStates */
    int k;
    for (k = 0; k < 0x44; k++)
        if (u->want[k] < 4) u->want[k] = (int16_t)((u->enabled[k] == 0 || k == 3 || k == 6 || k == 9) ? 0 : 2);
    if (u->bi->message != 0 && 0x30 + u->bi->message < UI_CONTROLS) u->want[0x30 + u->bi->message] = 1;
}

static void refresh_hud(UiState *u)
{   /* orig: 1008:8448 game_RefreshHud */
    int player = read_elem(u, N_PLAYER, 0), k;
    u->want[0] = 2;
    for (k = 1; k < 12; k++) {
        if (u->want[k] >= 4) continue;
        if (k == 3) u->want[k] = read_elem(u, N_COMPETE, player) > 0 ? 2 : 0;
        else if (k == 6) u->want[k] = read_elem(u, N_MORPH, player) > 0 ? 2 : 0;
        else if (k == 9) u->want[k] = read_elem(u, N_CORIBA, player) > 0 ? 2 : 0;
        else u->want[k] = (int16_t)(u->enabled[k] ? 2 : 1);
    }
    for (k = 12; k < 49; k++) u->want[k] = 0;
    for (k = 0x31; k < 0x33; k++) u->want[k] = (int16_t)(k - u->bi->message == 0x30 ? 1 : 0);
    for (k = 0x34; k < 0x44; k++)
        u->want[k] = (int16_t)((rd_i16(u->ds + 0x36a) != 0 && (u->bi->hotspotsEnabled & (1u << (k - 0x34)))) ? 2 : 0);
}

static void last_turn(UiState *u) { if (u->nPlayers == 1) { u->want[0x2e] = 1; u->want[0x2f] = 1; } }

static void pick_name(UiState *u, int nodeSlot, int dst)
{   /* panel 9: PLAYER_LEFT / PLAYER_RIGHT names (5 = LANSE) */
    int p = read_elem(u, nodeSlot, 0);
    if (p >= 1 && p <= 4) strcpy(ds_str(u, dst), name_of(u, p));
    else if (p == 5) strcpy(ds_str(u, dst), "LANSE");
    else *ds_str(u, dst) = 0;
}

void ui_set_panel(UiState *u, int panel)
{   /* orig: 1008:7fe2 ui_SetPanel */
    int k;
    for (k = 0; k < UI_CONTROLS; k++) u->want[k] = u->cur[k];
    if (panel == 0) u->panel = 0;
    else if (panel == 1) u->panel = u->savedPanel;
    else if (panel == 6) { for (k = 0; k < 0x43; k++) if (u->want[k] == 4) u->want[k] = 2; }
    else if (panel != 5) { u->savedPanel = (int16_t)panel; u->panel = (int16_t)panel; }
    u->editWant = 0;                                              /* +0x18c */
    if (u->active) {
        switch (u->panel) {
        case 2: refresh_hud(u); last_turn(u); break;
        case 4: refresh_hud(u); for (k = 22; k < 42; k++) u->want[k] = 1; last_turn(u); break;
        case 7:                                                   /* the name entry */
            reset_states(u);
            for (k = 12; k < 16; k++) {
                if (u->enabled[k] == 0) u->want[k] = (int16_t)(u->bi->nameLen[k - 11] == 0 ? 2 : 3);
                else { u->want[k] = 4; u->editWant = (int16_t)(k - 11); }
            }
            break;
        case 8:
            reset_states(u);
            for (k = 16; k < 20; k++) u->want[k] = (int16_t)((u->bi->hotspotsEnabled & (1u << (k - 16))) ? 3 : 1);
            last_turn(u);
            break;
        case 9:
            refresh_hud(u);
            u->want[20] = 3; u->want[21] = 3;
            pick_name(u, N_LEFT, 0x412); pick_name(u, N_RIGHT, 0x432);
            last_turn(u);
            break;
        case 10: case 11: {
            int a = u->panel == 10 ? 0x2a : 0x2c;
            for (k = 0; k < 0x44; k++) {
                if (u->want[k] >= 4) continue;
                u->want[k] = 0;
                if ((k == a && u->enabled[2]) || (k == a + 1 && u->enabled[11])) u->want[k] = 2;
            }
            if (u->panel == 10) {
                for (k = 0x34; k < 0x44; k++)
                    u->want[k] = (int16_t)((rd_i16(u->ds + 0x36a) != 0 && (u->bi->hotspotsEnabled & (1u << (k - 0x34)))) ? 2 : 0);
                u->want[5] = 1;
            }
            break;
        }
        case 12: ui_scores_save(u); refresh_hud(u); for (k = 22; k < 42; k++) u->want[k] = 2; last_turn(u); break;
        case 13: reset_states(u); last_turn(u); break;
        default: reset_states(u); break;
        }
        ui_update_hud(u, 0);
    }
    for (k = 0; k < UI_CONTROLS - 2; k++) if (u->cur[k] != u->want[k]) ui_set_control_state(u, u->want[k], k);
}

void ui_update_hud(UiState *u, int force)
{   /* orig: 1008:8604 ui_UpdateHudText */
    int player, v, p;
    if (u->node[N_PLAYER] == 0) return;
    player = read_elem(u, N_PLAYER, 0);
    if (force || u->lastPlayer != player) {
        u->lastPlayer = (int16_t)player;
        if (player >= 1 && player <= 4) strcpy(name_of(u, 0), name_of(u, player)); else *name_of(u, 0) = 0;
        u->last0458 = u->last045a = u->last045c = -1;
    }
    v = read_elem(u, N_COMPETE, player);
    if (force || u->last0458 != v) { u->last0458 = (int16_t)v; if (v < 2) *ds_str(u, 0x2108) = 0; else sprintf(ds_str(u, 0x2108), "%d", v); }
    v = read_elem(u, N_CORIBA, player);
    if (force || u->last045a != v) { u->last045a = (int16_t)v; if (v < 2) *ds_str(u, 0x2118) = 0; else sprintf(ds_str(u, 0x2118), "%d", v); }
    v = read_elem(u, N_MORPH, player);
    if (force || u->last045c != v) { u->last045c = (int16_t)v; if (v < 2) *ds_str(u, 0x2128) = 0; else sprintf(ds_str(u, 0x2128), "%d", v); }
    u->nPlayers = read_elem(u, N_PLAYERS, 0);
    if (u->nPlayers == 1) {
        v = read_elem(u, N_TURNS, 0);
        if (v != u->lastTurns) { u->lastTurns = (int16_t)v; if (v == 1) strcpy(ds_str(u, 0x560), "LAST TURN"); else sprintf(ds_str(u, 0x560), "%d", v); }
    }
    for (p = 1; p <= 4; p++) {
        long gold = read_elem(u, N_GOLD, p) * 1000L, silver = read_elem(u, N_SILVER, p) * 300L;
        long bronze = read_elem(u, N_BRONZE, p) * 100L, bonus = read_elem(u, N_BONUS, p) * 100L;
        long total = read_elem(u, N_SUNCATCHER, p) * 10000L + bonus + bronze + silver + gold;
        long val[4]; int col, base[4] = { 0x2138, 0x2188, 0x21d8, 0x2228 };
        val[0] = gold; val[1] = silver; val[2] = bronze; val[3] = total;
        for (col = 0; col < 4; col++)
            if (force || u->lastScore[p - 1][col] != val[col]) {
                u->lastScore[p - 1][col] = val[col];
                fmt_number(ds_str(u, base[col] + 16 * p), val[col]);
            }
    }
    if (force || u->bi->message != u->lastMessage) {
        if (u->bi->message == 1 && u->lastNamePlayer != u->lastPlayer && u->lastPlayer >= 0 && u->lastPlayer < 5) {
            const char *nm = name_of(u, u->lastPlayer);
            size_t n = strlen(nm);
            u->lastNamePlayer = u->lastPlayer;
            sprintf(ds_str(u, 0x460), "It's %s%s turn.", nm, (n && nm[n - 1] == 's') ? "'" : "'s");
        }
        u->lastMessage = u->bi->message;
    }
    if (u->editWant != u->editCur) {                       /* leave the old name: drop the cursor and trailing blanks */
        int e = u->editCur;
        if (e >= 1 && e <= 4) {
            int len = u->bi->nameLen[e];
            char *nm = name_of(u, e);
            if (u->blinkOn) { nm[len] = 0; u->blinkOn = 0; u->blinkWait = 0; }
            while (len > 0 && (nm[len - 1] == '_' || nm[len - 1] == ' ')) { len--; nm[len] = 0; }
            u->bi->nameLen[e] = (int16_t)len;
        }
        u->editCur = u->editWant;
    }
    if (u->editCur >= 1 && u->editCur <= 4 && u->bi->nameLen[u->editCur] < 0x1f) {   /* the blinking cursor */
        int len = u->bi->nameLen[u->editCur];
        char *nm = name_of(u, u->editCur);
        if (u->blinkWait) { u->blinkWait--; return; }
        u->blinkOn = (int16_t)!u->blinkOn;
        if (u->blinkOn) { nm[len] = '_'; nm[len + 1] = 0; } else nm[len] = 0;
        u->blinkWait = 3;
    }
}

static int32_t sv_get(const UiState *u, int i) { int32_t v; memcpy(&v, u->ds + 0x241c + 4 * i, 4); return v; }
static void sv_set(UiState *u, int i, int32_t v) { memcpy(u->ds + 0x241c + 4 * i, &v, 4); }
static char *score_name(UiState *u, int i) { return (char *)u->ds + 0x2278 + 0x20 * i; }
static char *score_str(UiState *u, int i) { return (char *)u->ds + 0x23b8 + 10 * i; }

static void strip_commas(char *s)
{   /* orig: 1008:6f7e */
    char *d = s;
    do { if (*s != ',') *d++ = *s; } while (*s++);
}

void ui_scores_load(UiState *u)
{   /* orig: the [SCORE] section reads in 1008:5fce ui_ctor (defaults are the game's characters) */
    static const char *defName[10] = { "Coriba", "Rocko", "Flo", "Owen", "Trudy", "Lockerbee", "Scatman", "Martin", "Spartin", "Caruthers" };
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        char key[16], def[16], buf[32];
        sprintf(key, "NAME%d", i + 1);
        if (u->iniPath) ini_get(u->iniPath, "SCORE", key, defName[i], score_name(u, i), 0x1f);
        else strcpy(score_name(u, i), defName[i]);
        sprintf(key, "SCORE%d", i + 1); sprintf(def, "%d", (10 - i) * 10000);
        if (u->iniPath) ini_get(u->iniPath, "SCORE", key, def, buf, 10); else strcpy(buf, def);
        strip_commas(buf);
        sv_set(u, i, atol(buf));
        fmt_number(score_str(u, i), sv_get(u, i));
    }
}

void ui_scores_insert(UiState *u, const char *name, int32_t score)
{   /* orig: 1008:6fa8 game_ParseResultsTable */
    int i, e = 10, same = 10;
    for (i = 9; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (strcmp(name, score_name(u, i)) == 0) same = i;
        if (sv_get(u, i) <= score) e = i;
    }
    if (e >= 10) return;
    if (e != same) {
        int k;
        if (same <= e) return;                       /* already listed higher */
        k = same - 1; if (k > 8) k = 8;
        for (i = k; i >= e; i--) {
            strcpy(score_name(u, i + 1), score_name(u, i));
            strcpy(score_str(u, i + 1), score_str(u, i));
            sv_set(u, i + 1, sv_get(u, i));
        }
        strncpy(score_name(u, e), name, 0x1f); score_name(u, e)[0x1f] = 0;
    }
    fmt_number(score_str(u, e), score);
    sv_set(u, e, score);
}

void ui_scores_save(UiState *u)
{   /* orig: 1008:718e - every named player's total goes into the table, which is written to izzy.ini */
    int p, i;
    for (p = 1; p <= 4; p++)
        if (u->bi->nameLen[p] != 0) {
            char nm[32]; int n = u->bi->nameLen[p];
            if (n > 31) n = 31;
            memcpy(nm, name_of(u, p), (size_t)n); nm[n] = 0;
            ui_scores_insert(u, nm, u->lastScore[p - 1][3]);
        }
    if (!u->iniPath) return;
    for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        char key[16];
        sprintf(key, "name%d", i + 1);  ini_set(u->iniPath, "SCORE", key, score_name(u, i));
        sprintf(key, "score%d", i + 1); ini_set(u->iniPath, "SCORE", key, score_str(u, i));
    }
}

static int font1_width(const UiState *u, const char *s)
{   /* orig: 1018:e554 - text width in font 1 */
    const int16_t *t = u->stage ? u->stage->fontOffsets[0] : NULL;
    int w = 0;
    if (!t) return 0;
    for (; *s; s++) { unsigned c = (uint8_t)*s; if (c >= 0x20 && c < 0x7f) w += t[c - 0x1f] - t[c - 0x20]; }
    return w;
}

void ui_char(UiState *u, int ch)
{   /* orig: 1008:7408 - typing a player name */
    int e = u->editCur, len, nl;
    char *nm;
    if (e < 1 || e > 4) return;                    /* (the original's space-bar debug cheat is not reproduced) */
    len = u->bi->nameLen[e]; nl = len;
    nm = name_of(u, e);
    if (ch == 8) { if (len > 0) { nl = len - 1; u->bi->nameLen[e] = (int16_t)nl; } }
    else if (ch == 9 || ch == 13) u->bi->hotspotClicked = (uint16_t)(1u << ((e < 4 ? e + 1 : 1) - 1));   /* vtable +0x40 */
    else if (ch > 0x1f && ch < 0x7f && len < 0x1f && (len > 0 || ch > ' ')) {
        char c = (char)ch;
        if (len < 1 || nm[len - 1] == ' ') c = (char)((c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') ? c - 0x20 : c);
        nm[len] = c; nl = len + 1; nm[nl] = 0;
        u->bi->nameLen[e] = (int16_t)nl;
        if (font1_width(u, nm) > 0x8c) { u->bi->nameLen[e] = (int16_t)len; nl = len; }
    }
    if (nl < 0x1f) { nm[nl] = '_'; nm[nl + 1] = 0; u->blinkOn = 1; u->blinkWait = 6; }
}
int ui_spot_add(UiState *u, int id, int kind, const int16_t xy[8])
{   /* orig: 1008:bc0a */
    int i, k;
    for (i = 0; i < UI_HOTSPOTS; i++) {
        if (u->spot[i].id == 0) {
            UiHotspot *h = &u->spot[i];
            h->active = 1; h->id = (int16_t)id; h->kind = (int16_t)kind;
            for (k = 0; k < 4; k++) { h->x[k] = xy[2 * k]; h->y[k] = xy[2 * k + 1]; }
            return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

int ui_spot_remove(UiState *u, int id)
{   /* orig: 1008:bcbc - cleared entries get -1 corners */
    int i, k, r = id == 0;
    for (i = 0; i < UI_HOTSPOTS; i++) {
        UiHotspot *h = &u->spot[i];
        if (id == 0 || h->id == id) {
            for (k = 0; k < 4; k++) { h->x[k] = -1; h->y[k] = -1; }
            h->id = 0; h->active = 0; h->kind = 0;
            r = 1;
        }
    }
    return r;
}

int ui_spot_contains(const UiState *u, int i, int x, int y)
{   /* orig: 1008:bd7e - CreatePolygonRgn(TL, TR, BR, BL, ALTERNATE) + PtInRegion, or an exclusive rectangle */
    const UiHotspot *h = &u->spot[i];
    int px[4], py[4], k, j, in = 0;
    if (h->y[1] == h->y[0] && h->y[3] == h->y[2] && h->x[2] == h->x[0] && h->x[1] == h->x[3])
        return h->x[0] < x && x < h->x[3] && h->y[0] < y && y < h->y[3];
    px[0] = h->x[0]; py[0] = h->y[0]; px[1] = h->x[1]; py[1] = h->y[1];
    px[2] = h->x[3]; py[2] = h->y[3]; px[3] = h->x[2]; py[3] = h->y[2];
    for (k = 0, j = 3; k < 4; j = k++) {                         /* even-odd rule at the pixel centre */
        double cy = y + 0.5, cx = x + 0.5;
        if ((py[k] > cy) != (py[j] > cy) &&
            cx < (double)(px[j] - px[k]) * (cy - py[k]) / (double)(py[j] - py[k]) + px[k]) in = !in;
    }
    return in;
}

int ui_hit_test(const UiState *u, const Stage *st, int x, int y)
{   /* orig: 1008:93a4 game_HitTestControl */
    int s, i;
    if (u->refreshTimer != 0 || u->bi->controlPressed != 0 || u->bi->hotspotClicked != 0) return 0;
    s = stage_HitTest(st, x, y);
    if (s > 0x20 && s < 0x65 && u->cur[s - 0x20] > 1) {
        int c = u->ctrlId[s - 0x20], on;
        if (c >= 1) on = c < 0x48 && u->enabled[c] != 0;
        else if (c < 0) on = (u->bi->hotspotsEnabled & (1u << (-c - 1))) != 0;
        else on = 0;
        if (c != 0 && on) return c;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < UI_HOTSPOTS; i++)
        if ((u->bi->hotspotsEnabled & (1u << i)) && u->spot[i].active && ui_spot_contains(u, i, x, y)) return -1 - i;
    return 0;
}

int ui_click(UiState *u, const Stage *st, int x, int y)
{   /* orig: 1008:94a0 game_HandleInput */
    int r = ui_hit_test(u, st, x, y);
    if (r > 0) {
        u->bi->controlPressed = (uint16_t)(1u << (r - 1));
        { int k; for (k = 1; k < 0x34; k++) u->enabled[k] = 0; }     /* vtable +0x4c (1008:95f6) */
        ui_set_control_state(u, u->controlState, 4);                  /* vtable +0x58 */
        u->refreshTimer = 5;
    } else if (r < 0) {
        int ch = -r;                            /* orig: 1008:9520 game_SelectChannel */
        if (ch > 0 && ch < 0x12) u->bi->hotspotClicked = (uint16_t)(1u << (ch - 1));
    }
    return r;
}

void ui_mouse_move(UiState *u, const Stage *st, int x, int y)
{   /* orig: 1008:7a60 - shape 1 (hand) over controls and hotspots; a hotspot of kind 1 or 2 uses that shape */
    int r = ui_hit_test(u, st, x, y);
    u->cursorKind = 0;
    if (r > 0) u->cursorKind = 1;
    else if (r < 0) { int k = u->spot[-1 - r].kind; u->cursorKind = (int16_t)((k >= 1 && k <= 2) ? k : 1); }
}

void ui_tick(UiState *u)
{   /* orig: 1008:9614, 1008:75a2, 1008:95f6, 1008:3498 - ui_Tick - (the MCI MIDI pump +0x24 is platform); then the panel is re-evaluated */
    if (u->refreshTimer > 0 && --u->refreshTimer == 0) ui_set_panel(u, 5);
    ui_set_panel(u, 5);
}

```

`src/ui.h`:

```h
/*
 * ui.h - the game's UI object (orig: Game+0x14, vtable 1008:c89c, built by 1008:5fce): hotspots placed by the
 * TAKE Spot (B3) command, mouse hit testing and clicks. The control panel itself is reconstructed separately.
 *
 * Hotspot table (UI +0x3266, 17 entries x 0x16 bytes): 4 corners (x, y) in the order the take gives them
 * (top-left, top-right, bottom-left, bottom-right), +0x10 id, +0x12 active, +0x14 kind (cursor shape 1/2).
 */
#ifndef IZZY_UI_H
#define IZZY_UI_H

#include "stage.h"
#include "builtins.h"
#include "resource.h"

#define UI_HOTSPOTS 17

typedef struct UiHotspot { int16_t x[4], y[4]; int16_t id, active, kind; } UiHotspot;

#define UI_CONTROLS 70
#define UI_STATES   5
#define UI_DS_SIZE  0x2960          /* the data segment image the control texts point into */

/* one control state (UI +0x4ae + (ctrl*5+state)*0x20), as defined by 1008:970c */
typedef struct UiControlDef {
    int16_t ctrl, state; int32_t res; int16_t x, y, depth; uint16_t flags;
    int32_t text; int16_t font, dx, dy, align;
} UiControlDef;

typedef struct UiState {
    IzzyUi   *bi;                  /* the built-ins' view: controlPressed (+0x17e), hotspotClicked (+0x180),
                                      hotspotsEnabled (+0x184), message (+0x18a), name lengths (DS:2096) */
    UiHotspot spot[UI_HOTSPOTS];
    Stage    *stage;
    VetVM    *vm;
    const NeResources *res;
    const UiControlDef *def[UI_CONTROLS][UI_STATES];
    int16_t  cur[UI_CONTROLS];     /* +0x232 shown state (-1 = never set) */
    int16_t  want[UI_CONTROLS];    /* +0x34a wanted state */
    int16_t  ctrlId[UI_CONTROLS];  /* +0x2be control number (or -n: hotspot n) */
    int16_t  enabled[0x48];        /* +0xf2 + 2*k */
    int16_t  defEnabled[0x48];     /* +0x66 + 2*k */
    uint16_t mask186, mask188;     /* +0x186 / +0x188 */
    int16_t  editWant, editCur;    /* +0x18c / +0x18e: player whose name is being typed (0 = none) */
    int16_t  active;               /* +0x190: the panel follows SET_CONTROL_PANEL once a take or narration ran */
    int16_t  panel;                /* +0x3d6 */
    int16_t  savedPanel;           /* DS:0592 */
    int16_t  lastMessage;          /* DS:0594 */
    int16_t  lastPlayer, lastNamePlayer, last0458, last045a, last045c, lastTurns;   /* DS:0452 0596 0458 045a 045c 0456 */
    int16_t  nPlayers;             /* DS:0454 */
    int32_t  lastScore[4][4];      /* DS:20b8 / 20cc / 20e0 / 20f4 */
    int16_t  blinkOn, blinkWait;   /* DS:20b4 / 20b6 */
    int16_t  node[14];             /* DS:0570..058a cached node indexes (N_PLAYERS .. EASTER_EGG) */
    uint8_t  ds[UI_DS_SIZE];       /* DS image: names at 0x1ff6 + 0x20*n (n = 1..4, 0 = current player), ... */
    int16_t  controlState;         /* +0x182 */
    int16_t  u46a, u4e;
    const char *iniPath;           /* izzy.ini (high scores); NULL = keep them in memory only */            /* +0x46a / +0x4e: saved with the game (meaning unknown) */
    int16_t  refreshTimer;         /* +0x3de: clicks are ignored while it runs (5 UI ticks) */
    int16_t  cursorKind;           /* +0x47a: 0 arrow, 1 hand */
} UiState;

/* orig: 1008:5fce ui_ctor + 1008:98ae ui_BuildControlTable. dgroup = the EXE's initial data segment (or NULL). */
void ui_init(UiState *u, IzzyUi *bi, Stage *st, VetVM *vm, const NeResources *res, const uint8_t *dgroup, size_t dgroupLen);
/* orig: 1008:7fe2 - SET_CONTROL_PANEL (panel ids 0, 2, 3, 4, 7..13; 1 = restore, 5 = keep, 6 = un-select) */
void ui_set_panel(UiState *u, int panel);
/* orig: 1008:955a / 1008:95ac - SET_DEFAULT_CONTROLS / SET_CONTROLS masks */
void ui_set_default_controls(UiState *u, unsigned mask);
void ui_set_controls(UiState *u, unsigned mask);
/* orig: 1008:b674 - show control ctrl in state; 1 if a bitmap is shown */
int  ui_set_control_state(UiState *u, int state, int ctrl);
/* orig: 1008:8604 - HUD texts; force = 1 rewrites all */
void ui_update_hud(UiState *u, int force);
/* High scores (10 entries: names DS:2278, score strings DS:23b8, values DS:241c).
 * orig: the [SCORE] reads in 1008:5fce, 1008:6fa8 insert (UI +0xc4), 1008:718e save (UI +0xc8). */
void ui_scores_load(UiState *u);
void ui_scores_insert(UiState *u, const char *name, int32_t score);
void ui_scores_save(UiState *u);
/* orig: 1008:7408 - a typed character (WM_CHAR) */
void ui_char(UiState *u, int ch);
/* orig: 1008:bc0a - add a hotspot in the first free entry; 1 on success */
int  ui_spot_add(UiState *u, int id, int kind, const int16_t xy[8]);
/* orig: 1008:bcbc - remove hotspot `id`, or all when id == 0 */
int  ui_spot_remove(UiState *u, int id);
/* orig: 1008:bd7e - point in hotspot i (rectangle fast path, else the polygon) */
int  ui_spot_contains(const UiState *u, int i, int x, int y);
/* orig: 1008:93a4 - > 0 control number, < 0 -(hotspot index + 1), 0 nothing */
int  ui_hit_test(const UiState *u, const Stage *st, int x, int y);
/* orig: 1008:94a0 game_HandleInput (+ 1008:9520 SelectChannel). Returns the hit (see ui_hit_test);
 * for a hotspot the caller plays the Sfx registered for channel (index + 1). */
int  ui_click(UiState *u, const Stage *st, int x, int y);
/* orig: 1008:7a60 ui_SetCursor (cursor shape part) */
void ui_mouse_move(UiState *u, const Stage *st, int x, int y);
/* orig: 1008:9614 - the UI tick (every 167 ms): counts the click lock-out down */
void ui_tick(UiState *u);

#endif
```

`src/vep.c`:

```c
/*
 * vep.c - VEP source compiler: expressions/statement bodies (see vep.h).
 */
#include "vep.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* DS:0a87 MSVC ctype table: 1 upper, 2 lower, 4 digit, 8 space */
static int c_space(char c) { return c == ' ' || (c >= 9 && c <= 13); }
static int c_alnum(char c) { return (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9'); }
static int c_bracket(char c) { return c == '(' || c == ')' || c == '[' || c == ']'; }

static int read_operator_token(const char *s, int *pos, char *ch, int *assign, int *jitter, int *negate)
{   /* orig: 1010:e32e vep_ReadOperatorToken - a run of operator characters (spaces inside are skipped):
     * the first char is the operator, '=' sets assign, '%' jitter, a later '-' negates the operand, a later
     * '+' is accepted and anything else after the first char is ignored */
    int r = 0;
    char c;
    *ch = 0; *assign = 0; *jitter = 0; *negate = 0;
    while ((c = s[*pos]) != 0 && c_space(c)) (*pos)++;
    for (;;) {
        c = s[*pos];
        if (c == 0 || c_alnum(c) || c_bracket(c)) return r;
        if (!c_space(c)) {
            if (c == '%') { *jitter = 1; r = 1; }
            else if (c == '=') { *assign = 1; r = 1; }
            else if (*ch == 0 && !*assign) { *ch = c; r = 1; }
            else if (c == '-') { *negate = 1; r = 1; }
            else if (c == '+') r = 1;
        }
        (*pos)++;
    }
}

static int read_identifier(const char *s, int *pos, char *buf)
{   /* orig: 1010:e268 vep_ReadIdentifier - letters/digits/'.'/'_' (31 kept); 1 = identifier, 0 = number/empty */
    int n = 0, digits = 1;
    char c;
    while ((c = s[*pos]) != 0 && c_space(c)) (*pos)++;
    while ((c = s[*pos]) != 0 && (c_alnum(c) || c == '.' || c == '_')) {
        if (!(c >= '0' && c <= '9')) digits = 0;
        if (n < 31) buf[n++] = c;
        (*pos)++;
    }
    buf[n] = 0;
    return digits ? 0 : (n > 0);
}

static int find_node(const VetVM *vm, const char *name)
{   /* orig: binary search of the sorted node names (VetVM +0x2de / +0x2e2) */
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++) if (vm->nodes[i].name && strcmp(vm->nodes[i].name, name) == 0) return i;
    return -1;
}

static int find_vm(void *ctx, const char *name) { return find_node((const VetVM *)ctx, name); }

int vep_compile_statement(const VetVM *vm, uint8_t kind, const char *text, uint8_t *out, int cap)
{
    return vep_compile_statement_with(find_vm, (void *)vm, kind, text, out, cap);
}

int vep_compile_statement_with(int (*find)(void *ctx, const char *name), void *ctx, uint8_t kind, const char *text, uint8_t *out, int cap)
{   /* orig: 1018:28c8 vep_CompileStatement */
    int n = 0, pos = 0, len = (int)strlen(text);
    /* orig: 1010:d432, 1010:d36e, 1010:d6de - code_AppendByte / code_AppendWord / code_Reset */
#define EMIT(b) do { if (n >= cap) return -1; out[n++] = (uint8_t)(b); } while (0)
    if (len == 0) return 0;
    if (kind) EMIT(kind);
    do {
        char ch, buf[32];
        int assign, jitter, negate;
        while (text[pos] && c_space(text[pos])) pos++;
        if (c_bracket(text[pos])) {
            ch = text[pos++];
            EMIT(0x89); EMIT(ch | 0x80); EMIT(ch | 0x80);
        } else if (read_operator_token(text, &pos, &ch, &assign, &jitter, &negate)) {
            EMIT(0x87); EMIT((uint8_t)ch | 0x80);
            EMIT(0x80 | (assign ? 1 : 0) | (negate ? 2 : 0) | (jitter ? 4 : 0));
        } else if (read_identifier(text, &pos, buf)) {
            int node = find(ctx, buf);
            if (node >= 0) { EMIT(0x83); EMIT(node & 0xff); EMIT((node >> 8) & 0xff); }
        } else if (buf[0]) {
            int v = atoi(buf);
            EMIT(0x84); EMIT(v & 0xff); EMIT((v >> 8) & 0xff);
        }
    } while (pos < len);
#undef EMIT
    return n;
}

int vep_eval_text(VetVM *vm, const char *text, int16_t *out)
{   /* orig: 1018:6866 take_CompileVepExpr */
    uint8_t code[512];
    VetCode c;
    int n = vep_compile_statement(vm, 0, text, code, (int)sizeof code);
    *out = 0;
    if (n < 0) return 0;
    c.data = code; c.len = (int16_t)n; c.pos = 0;
    if (n == 0) return 1;
    return vet_eval(vm, &c, out);
}

int vep_eval_take_expr(VetVM *vm, const char *text, int32_t *out)
{   /* orig: 1008:d25a take_ParseVepExpr */
    char expr[41], num[6];
    const char *plus = strchr(text, '+');
    int16_t v = 0;
    size_t el = plus ? (size_t)(plus - text) : strlen(text);
    if (el > 40) el = 40;
    memcpy(expr, text, el); expr[el] = 0;
    num[0] = 0;
    if (plus) { strncpy(num, plus + 1, 5); num[5] = 0; }
    vep_eval_text(vm, expr, &v);
    *out = (int32_t)atoi(num) + v;
    return 1;
}
```

`src/vep.h`:

```h
/*
 * vep.h - the VEP source compiler (text -> the VET byte code that vet.c runs).
 *
 * The shipped game runs the precompiled IZZY.VET, but it still compiles VEP source at run time for the
 * {expression} arguments of TAKE statements (orig: 1008:d25a take_ParseVepExpr -> 1018:6866
 * take_CompileVepExpr -> 1018:28c8 vep_CompileStatement -> 1018:3e62 vet_EvalExpression).
 *
 * Expression byte code (as in .VET files):
 *   83 lo hi   variable = node index          84 lo hi  literal
 *   87 c f     operator c|0x80; f = 0x80 | 1 '=' seen | 2 '-' after the operator | 4 '%' (jitter)
 *   89 c c     ( ) [ ]  (the character |0x80, twice)
 */
#ifndef IZZY_VEP_H
#define IZZY_VEP_H

#include "vet.h"

/* Compile one expression/statement body. `kind` != 0 is emitted first (statement kind byte).
 * Identifiers that name no node are dropped, as in the original. Returns the byte count, or -1 if `cap`
 * is too small. orig: 1018:28c8 vep_CompileStatement, 1010:e32e vep_ReadOperatorToken, 1010:e268 vep_ReadIdentifier */
int vep_compile_statement(const VetVM *vm, uint8_t kind, const char *text, uint8_t *out, int cap);
/* the same with a caller-supplied name lookup (node index or -1) - the source compiler's sorted name table */
int vep_compile_statement_with(int (*find)(void *ctx, const char *name), void *ctx, uint8_t kind, const char *text, uint8_t *out, int cap);

/* Compile and evaluate an expression. orig: 1018:6866 take_CompileVepExpr. Returns 1 on success. */
int vep_eval_text(VetVM *vm, const char *text, int16_t *out);

/* A TAKE {expression}: "<expr> + <k>" -> eval(expr) + atoi(k) (k: up to 5 characters).
 * orig: 1008:d25a take_ParseVepExpr. Returns 1 on success. */
int vep_eval_take_expr(VetVM *vm, const char *text, int32_t *out);

#endif

```

`src/vepc.c`:

```c
/*
 * vepc.c - the VEP source-file compiler (see vepc.h).
 */
#include "vepc.h"
#include "vep.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ strings */

static void vs_set(VepStr *s, const char *t)
{   /* orig: 1010:b294 (String = const char *) */
    size_t n = t ? strlen(t) : 0;
    if (n + 1 > s->cap) { s->cap = (n + 16) & ~(size_t)15; s->s = (char *)realloc(s->s, s->cap); }
    if (s->s) { if (n) memcpy(s->s, t, n); s->s[n] = 0; s->n = n; }
}
static void vs_add(VepStr *s, const char *t)
{   /* orig: 1010:b492 (String += const char *) */
    size_t n = t ? strlen(t) : 0;
    if (s->n + n + 1 > s->cap) { s->cap = (s->n + n + 16) & ~(size_t)15; s->s = (char *)realloc(s->s, s->cap); }
    if (s->s) { if (n) memcpy(s->s + s->n, t, n); s->n += n; s->s[s->n] = 0; }
}
static const char *vs(const VepStr *s) { return s->s ? s->s : ""; }
static void vs_free(VepStr *s) { free(s->s); s->s = NULL; s->n = s->cap = 0; }

/* DS:0a87 - the MSVC ctype table: 1 upper, 2 lower, 4 digit, 8 space, 0x10 punct, 0x20 control */
static int c_upper(int c) { return c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z'; }
static int c_lower(int c) { return c >= 'a' && c <= 'z'; }
static int c_digit(int c) { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; }
static int c_alnum(int c) { return c_upper(c) || c_lower(c) || c_digit(c); }
static int c_space(int c) { return c == ' ' || (c >= 9 && c <= 13); }
static int c_cntrl(int c) { return (c >= 0 && c < 32) || c == 127; }
static int c_bracket(int c) { return c == '(' || c == ')' || c == '[' || c == ']'; }
static void upcase(char *s) { for (; *s; s++) if (c_lower((unsigned char)*s)) *s = (char)(*s - 0x20); }

static void warn(VepCompiler *c, int code, const char *fmt, const char *arg)
{   /* VetVM +0xb2 / +0xb6 callback (callback_Thunk0e) */
    char msg[400];
    snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, fmt, arg);
    c->warnings++;
    if (c->warn) c->warn(c->warnCtx, code, msg);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ input */

typedef struct Src { FILE *f; const char *mem; size_t pos, len; } Src;

static int src_get(Src *s)
{   /* istream::get in text mode: CR LF -> LF */
    int ch;
    if (s->f) { ch = fgetc(s->f); if (ch == '\r') { int d = fgetc(s->f); if (d == '\n') return '\n'; if (d != EOF) ungetc(d, s->f); } return ch; }
    if (s->pos >= s->len) return EOF;
    ch = (unsigned char)s->mem[s->pos++];
    if (ch == '\r' && s->pos < s->len && s->mem[s->pos] == '\n') ch = (unsigned char)s->mem[s->pos++];
    return ch;
}

static int src_getline(Src *s, char *buf, int cap)
{   /* istream::getline(buf, 256, '\n'): 0 = fail (nothing extracted, or the line did not fit) */
    int n = 0, ch;
    for (;;) {
        if (n == cap - 1) { buf[n] = 0; return 0; }
        ch = src_get(s);
        if (ch == EOF) { buf[n] = 0; return n > 0; }
        if (ch == '\n') { buf[n] = 0; return 1; }
        buf[n++] = (char)ch;
    }
}

static int trim_header(VepCompiler *c)
{   /* orig: 1010:e8f6 vep_TrimLine - "@NAME" alone on the line: the line becomes NAME (upper case) and 1 is returned */
    char *b = c->line;
    int i = 0, s, e, k;
    while (b[i] && b[i] < ' ') i++;
    if (!b[i] || b[i] != '@') return 0;
    i++;
    while (b[i] && b[i] < ' ') i++;
    s = i;
    if (!b[i]) return 0;
    for (e = s; b[e] && b[e] >= '!'; e++)
        if (!(c_alnum((unsigned char)b[e]) || b[e] == '_' || b[e] == '.')) return 0;
    for (k = e; b[k]; k++) if (b[k] >= '!') return 0;
    for (k = 0; s < e; k++, s++) { char ch = b[s]; if (c_lower((unsigned char)ch)) ch = (char)(ch - 0x20); b[k] = ch; }
    b[k] = 0;
    return 1;
}

static void parse_header(VepCompiler *c)
{   /* orig: 1010:e9d6 vep_ParseHeader - a leading keyword sets the section and is removed */
    static const struct { const char *kw; int sec; } k[] = {
        {"DESC:", 0x80}, {"SHOW:", 2}, {"SET:", 4}, {"LOOP:", 8}, {"LOC:", 0x20}, {"AUTHOR:", 0x40}, {"TAKE:", 0x81}, {"START:", 0x82} };
    char w[256];
    int n = 0, i, sec = 0;
    while (c->line[n] && c->line[n] >= '!' && n < 255) { w[n] = c->line[n]; n++; }
    w[n] = 0;
    upcase(w);
    for (i = 0; i < (int)(sizeof k / sizeof k[0]); i++) if (strcmp(w, k[i].kw) == 0) { sec = k[i].sec; break; }
    if (sec) {
        int j = 0;
        c->section = sec;
        while (c->line[n] && c->line[n] <= ' ') n++;
        while (c->line[n]) c->line[j++] = c->line[n++];
        c->line[j] = 0;
    }
}

static int read_line(VepCompiler *c, Src *s)
{   /* orig: 1010:ec4a vep_ReadSourceLine. Returns 0 at the end of the file. */
    int n;
    c->lineNo++;
    c->eof = !src_getline(s, c->line, 256);
    if (c->eof) return 0;
    n = (int)strlen(c->line);
    while (n > 0 && c->line[n - 1] <= 0x1f && c->line[n - 1] >= 0) n--;
    c->line[n] = 0;
    if (trim_header(c)) { c->section = 1; return 1; }
    if (c->section == 1) c->section = 0x10;
    {
        int i = 0, j = 0;
        while (i < n && c->line[i] <= ' ' && c->line[i] > 0) i++;
        while (c->line[i]) c->line[j++] = c->line[i++];
        c->line[j] = 0;
    }
    if (!c->line[0]) c->section = 0x10;                 /* a blank line ends the section */
    else parse_header(c);
    return 1;
}

static void read_block(VepCompiler *c, Src *s, VepStr *dst)
{   /* orig: 1018:0e2e vep_ReadChar - "{ ... }" text (may start on the keyword line), CR LF kept */
    char chunk[260];
    int open = 0, done = 0, i, ch;
    for (i = 0; c->line[i] && c->line[i] != '{'; i++) ;
    if (c->line[i] == '{') { vs_add(dst, c->line + i + 1); open = 1; }
    while (!done) {
        int n = 0;
        while (n < 0xfe) {
            ch = src_get(s);
            if (ch == EOF) { done = 1; break; }
            if (ch == '{') { open = 1; continue; }
            if (ch == '}') { done = 1; break; }
            if (open) { if (ch == '\n') chunk[n++] = '\r'; chunk[n++] = (char)ch; }
        }
        if (n) { chunk[n] = 0; vs_add(dst, chunk); }
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ nodes */

static VepSrcNode *find_node(VepCompiler *c, int create, const char *name)
{   /* orig: 1018:0338 vep_ParseNodeHeader */
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < c->count; i++)
        if (izzy_stricmp(vs(&c->nodes[i].name), name) == 0) {
            if (!c->dupIsError) return &c->nodes[i];
            warn(c, 0, "duplicate node @%s", name);          /* 1018:7c9a */
            c->errors++;
            return NULL;
        }
    if (!create) return NULL;
    if (c->count == c->cap) {
        c->cap = c->cap ? c->cap * 2 : 256;
        c->nodes = (VepSrcNode *)realloc(c->nodes, sizeof *c->nodes * (size_t)c->cap);
        if (!c->nodes) return NULL;
    }
    memset(&c->nodes[c->count], 0, sizeof c->nodes[0]);
    vs_set(&c->nodes[c->count].name, name);
    return &c->nodes[c->count++];
}

static void add_text(VepStr *dst, char *line, int upper)
{   /* "\r\n" between lines, the line upper-cased (SHOW/SET/LOOP) and right-trimmed */
    int n = (int)strlen(line);
    if (dst->n) vs_add(dst, "\r\n");
    if (upper) upcase(line);
    while (n > 0 && line[n - 1] < '!') n--;
    line[n] = 0;
    vs_add(dst, line);
}

static void add_prose(VepStr *dst, const char *line, int blank)
{   /* the description text: a blank line -> a paragraph break, else one space (two after . : ! ?), none after ' ' '-' */
    if (dst->n) {
        if (blank) vs_add(dst, "\r\n\r\n");
        else {
            char last = dst->s[dst->n - 1];
            if (last != ' ' && last != '-') vs_add(dst, (last == '.' || last == ':' || last == '!' || last == '?') ? "  " : " ");
        }
    }
    vs_add(dst, line);
}

static int read_programmer(VepCompiler *c, Src *s)
{   /* orig: 1018:07fc vep_CompileFile */
    VepSrcNode *n = NULL;
    int blank = 0;
    c->section = 0x10;
    while (read_line(c, s)) {
        if (!c->line[0]) { blank = 1; continue; }
        switch (c->section) {
        case 0x10: if (n) add_prose(&n->desc, c->line, blank); break;
        case 1:    n = find_node(c, 1, c->line); break;
        case 2:    if (n) add_text(&n->show, c->line, 1); break;
        case 4:    if (n) add_text(&n->set, c->line, 1); break;
        case 8:    if (n) add_text(&n->loop, c->line, 1); break;
        case 0x20: if (n) add_text(&n->loc, c->line, 0); break;
        case 0x40: if (n) read_block(c, s, &n->author); break;
        case 0x80: if (n) read_block(c, s, &n->desc); break;
        case 0x81: case 0x82:
            if (n) {
                if (!n->take.n) { int k = (int)strlen(c->line); upcase(c->line); while (k > 0 && c->line[k - 1] < '!') k--; c->line[k] = 0; vs_set(&n->take, c->line); }
                if (c->section == 0x81) n->takeFlag = 1; else n->startFlag = 1;
            }
            break;
        }
        blank = 0;
    }
    return 1;
}

static int read_writer(VepCompiler *c, Src *s)
{   /* orig: 1018:0574 vep_ReadSourceFile - only text, LOC, AUTHOR and DESC; nodes must exist */
    VepSrcNode *n = NULL;
    int blank = 0;
    c->section = 0x10;
    while (read_line(c, s)) {
        if (!c->line[0]) { blank = 1; continue; }
        switch (c->section) {
        case 1:    n = find_node(c, 0, c->line); break;
        case 0x10: if (n) add_prose(&n->desc, c->line, blank); break;
        case 0x20: if (n) add_text(&n->loc, c->line, 0); break;
        case 0x40: if (n) read_block(c, s, &n->author); break;
        case 0x80: if (n) read_block(c, s, &n->desc); break;
        }
        blank = 0;
    }
    return 1;
}

int vepc_read_programmer(VepCompiler *c, FILE *f) { Src s = { f, NULL, 0, 0 }; return f ? read_programmer(c, &s) : 0; }
int vepc_read_writer(VepCompiler *c, FILE *f) { Src s = { f, NULL, 0, 0 }; return f ? read_writer(c, &s) : 0; }

void vepc_init(VepCompiler *c) { /* orig: 1018:1204 vet_VM_ctor (the compiler half) */ memset(c, 0, sizeof *c); c->section = 0x10; }

void vepc_free(VepCompiler *c)
{   /* orig: 1018:2454 vep_FreeCompilerState, 1018:2292 vet_FreeNodes, 1010:dd42 vet_FlushPending, 1018:1564 vet_Reset */
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < c->count; i++) {
        VepSrcNode *n = &c->nodes[i];
        vs_free(&n->name); vs_free(&n->show); vs_free(&n->set); vs_free(&n->loop);
        vs_free(&n->desc); vs_free(&n->loc); vs_free(&n->author); vs_free(&n->take);
    }
    for (i = 0; i < c->nnames; i++) free(c->names[i]);
    free(c->nodes); free(c->names);
    memset(c, 0, sizeof *c);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ statements */

static int next_token(const char *s, int *pos, VepStr *tok)
{   /* orig: 1010:e44a vep_NextToken - a run of word characters (alnum . _ @) or of other characters (a bracket
     * ends a run); ' ' is appended unless a control character (end of the line) follows. Returns that ' ' or 0. */
    char buf[260];
    int n = 0, ch, trail;
    while ((ch = (unsigned char)s[*pos]) != 0 && (c_space(ch) || c_cntrl(ch))) (*pos)++;
    if (ch) {
        int cls = c_alnum(ch) || ch == '.' || ch == '_' || ch == '@', cls2;
        for (;;) {
            if (n < 0xfe) buf[n++] = (char)ch;
            (*pos)++;
            ch = (unsigned char)s[*pos];
            cls2 = c_alnum(ch) || ch == '.' || ch == '_' || ch == '@';
            if (ch == 0 || cls2 != cls || c_space(ch) || c_cntrl(ch) || c_bracket(ch)) break;
        }
    }
    trail = (!c_cntrl(ch) || ch == 9) ? ' ' : 0;
    buf[n] = (char)trail;
    if (trail) buf[n + 1] = 0;
    vs_set(tok, buf);
    return trail;
}

enum { K_EXPR = 1, K_TARGET = 2, K_COND = 4, K_NOT = 8, K_GOTO = 0x10, K_GOSUB = 0x20, K_RETURN = 0x40, K_LOOP = 0x80, K_INIT = 0x100 };

static unsigned classify(VepCompiler *c, const char *s, int *pos, VepStr *target, VepStr *action, VepStr *cond)
{   /* orig: 1010:e5c2 vep_ClassifyStatement - one line: the keyword bits, the condition, the expression, the target */
    static VepStr tok;
    unsigned k = 0;
    int inTarget = 0, inCond = 0;
    vs_set(target, ""); vs_set(action, ""); vs_set(cond, "");
    for (;;) {
        int tc = next_token(s, pos, &tok), add = 1;
        if (tok.n) {
            const char *t = tok.s;
            if (tc == 0) {
                if (izzy_stricmp(t, "NOT") == 0) vs_set(&tok, " < 100");
                else if (izzy_stricmp(t, "RETURN") == 0) { k |= K_RETURN; add = 0; }
                else if (izzy_stricmp(t, "LOOP") == 0) { k |= K_LOOP; add = 0; }
                else if (izzy_stricmp(t, "INITIALIZE") == 0) { k |= K_INIT; add = 0; }
                else if (strcmp(t, "@") == 0) vs_set(&tok, c->curName);
            } else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "IF ") || !izzy_stricmp(t, "ON ") || !izzy_stricmp(t, "WHEN ") || !izzy_stricmp(t, "WHILE ")) {
                k |= K_COND; inCond = 1; add = 0; inTarget = 0;
            } else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "UNTIL ")) {
                k = (k | K_COND) ^ K_NOT; inCond = 1; add = 0; inTarget = 0;
            } else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "NOT ")) vs_set(&tok, "100 > ");
            else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "GOTO ")) { k |= K_GOTO; inTarget = 1; add = 0; }
            else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "GOSUB ")) { k |= K_GOSUB; inTarget = 1; add = 0; }
            else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "RETURN ")) { k |= K_RETURN; add = 0; }
            else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "LOOP ")) { k |= K_LOOP; add = 0; }
            else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "INITIALIZE ")) { k |= K_INIT; add = 0; }
            else if (!strcmp(t, "@ ")) { vs_set(&tok, c->curName); vs_add(&tok, " "); }
            else if (!izzy_stricmp(t, "THEN ")) { add = 0; inCond = 0; }
            if (add) {
                if (inTarget) { vs_add(target, tok.s); k |= K_TARGET; }
                else { vs_add(inCond ? cond : action, tok.s); k |= K_EXPR; }
            }
        }
        if (tc == 0) return k;
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ names */

static char *vepc_strdup(const char *s) { size_t n = strlen(s) + 1; char *d = (char *)malloc(n); if (d) memcpy(d, s, n); return d; }

static int name_cmp(const char *a, const char *b)
{   /* orig: 1010:b118 str_Compare - REPE CMPSB (unsigned bytes) */
    return strcmp(a, b);
}

static int name_find(VepCompiler *c, const char *name)
{   /* orig: 1018:2b26 vet_FindNode - binary search of the sorted table (VetVM +0x2de / +0x2e2) */
    int lo = 0, hi = c->nnames - 1;
    while (lo <= hi) {
        int mid = (lo + hi) >> 1, r = name_cmp(c->names[mid], name);
        if (r == 0) return mid;
        if (r < 0) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid - 1;
    }
    return -1;
}
static int name_find_cb(void *ctx, const char *name) { return name_find((VepCompiler *)ctx, name); }

static void name_add(VepCompiler *c, const char *name)
{   /* orig: 1010:ddbe vet_BuildRuntime(1, name) - sorted insert, duplicates kept once */
    int lo = 0, hi = c->nnames;
    if (!name || !*name) return;
    while (lo < hi) {
        int mid = (lo + hi) >> 1, r = name_cmp(c->names[mid], name);
        if (r == 0) return;
        if (r < 0) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid;
    }
    if (c->nnames == c->ncap) {
        c->ncap = c->ncap ? c->ncap * 2 : 512;
        c->names = (char **)realloc(c->names, sizeof *c->names * (size_t)c->ncap);
        if (!c->names) return;
    }
    memmove(c->names + lo + 1, c->names + lo, sizeof *c->names * (size_t)(c->nnames - lo));
    c->names[lo] = vepc_strdup(name);
    c->nnames++;
}

static void add_identifiers(VepCompiler *c, const char *s)
{   /* orig: 1018:19e4 vet_InitRuntimeNames - every run of alnum . _ that is not all digits (31 characters kept) */
    int i = 0;
    for (;;) {
        char w[32];
        int n = 0, digits = 1, ch;
        while ((ch = (unsigned char)s[i]) != 0 && !c_alnum(ch)) i++;
        while ((ch = (unsigned char)s[i]) != 0 && (c_alnum(ch) || ch == '.' || ch == '_')) {
            if (!c_digit(ch)) digits = 0;
            if (n < 31) w[n++] = (char)ch;
            i++;
        }
        if (n && !digits) { w[n] = 0; name_add(c, w); }
        if (!s[i]) return;
    }
}

static void build_names(VepCompiler *c)
{   /* orig: 1018:1764 vet_Start - the node names and every identifier of the SET and LOOP statements */
    VepStr target = {0}, action = {0}, cond = {0};
    int i, k;
    for (i = 0; i < c->count; i++) {
        VepSrcNode *n = &c->nodes[i];
        c->curName = vs(&n->name);                       /* orig: 1010:dbe4 vet_SetCurrentNode */
        name_add(c, c->curName);
        for (k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
            const char *t = vs(k ? &n->loop : &n->set);
            int pos = 0, len = (int)strlen(t);
            while (pos < len) {
                classify(c, t, &pos, &target, &action, &cond);
                add_identifiers(c, vs(&cond));
                add_identifiers(c, vs(&action));
            }
        }
    }
    vs_free(&target); vs_free(&action); vs_free(&cond);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ code */

typedef struct Buf { uint8_t *b; size_t n, cap; } Buf;
static void bput(Buf *b, const void *p, size_t n)
{
    if (b->n + n > b->cap) { b->cap = (b->n + n) * 2 + 1024; b->b = (uint8_t *)realloc(b->b, b->cap); }
    if (b->b) { memcpy(b->b + b->n, p, n); b->n += n; }
}
static void bbyte(Buf *b, int v) { uint8_t x = (uint8_t)v; bput(b, &x, 1); }
static void bword(Buf *b, int v) { uint8_t x[2]; x[0] = (uint8_t)(v & 0xff); x[1] = (uint8_t)((v >> 8) & 0xff); bput(b, x, 2); }
static void bstr(Buf *b, const char *s) { bput(b, s, strlen(s) + 1); }

static void compile_block(VepCompiler *c, const char *text, Buf *code)
{   /* orig: 1018:2620 vep_CompileNode - per line: 81 kind; 82 condition; 85 expression; 86 target; 88 */
    VepStr target = {0}, action = {0}, cond = {0};
    int pos = 0, len = (int)strlen(text);
    uint8_t tmp[4096];
    while (pos < len) {
        unsigned k = classify(c, text, &pos, &target, &action, &cond);
        if (k) {
            int m;
            bbyte(code, 0x81); bword(code, (int)k);
            m = vep_compile_statement_with(name_find_cb, c, 0x82, vs(&cond), tmp, (int)sizeof tmp);
            if (m > 0) bput(code, tmp, (size_t)m);
            m = vep_compile_statement_with(name_find_cb, c, 0x85, vs(&action), tmp, (int)sizeof tmp);
            if (m > 0) bput(code, tmp, (size_t)m);
            if (target.n) {
                int idx = name_find(c, target.s);
                bbyte(code, 0x86);
                if (idx < 0) {
                    idx = c->nnames + 1;
                    warn(c, 4, "Compile warning -- Script references Node %s that dosen't exist.", target.s);   /* 1018:7e14 */
                }
                bword(code, idx);
            }
        }
        bbyte(code, 0x88);
    }
    vs_free(&target); vs_free(&action); vs_free(&cond);
}

uint8_t *vepc_compile(VepCompiler *c, int version, int withDesc, size_t *len)
{   /* orig: 1018:1ad2 vep_Compile, written as 1018:2bdc vet_WriteCompiled does */
    typedef struct { int script, src, prev, next; } Slot;
    Slot *slot;
    Buf out = {0};
    int i, start = 0, prev = -1;
    build_names(c);
    slot = (Slot *)calloc((size_t)(c->nnames > 0 ? c->nnames : 1), sizeof *slot);
    if (!slot) return NULL;
    for (i = 0; i < c->count; i++) {
        int idx = name_find(c, vs(&c->nodes[i].name));
        if (idx < 0) { warn(c, 0xb, "Compile warning -- A node %s, was found in the linked list that was not fould in the table. \nCall the Programmer!.", vs(&c->nodes[i].name)); continue; }
        if (slot[idx].script)
            warn(c, 0xd, "Compile warning -- Found more than one node named %s in your script. It is unlikly that this will work the way you expect", vs(&c->nodes[i].name));
        slot[idx].script = 1; slot[idx].src = i;
        if (prev < 0) start = idx;
        else { slot[idx].prev = prev; slot[prev].next = idx; }
        prev = idx;
    }
    bword(&out, version); bword(&out, c->nnames); bword(&out, start);
    for (i = 0; i < c->nnames; i++) {
        bstr(&out, c->names[i]);
        bword(&out, 0); bword(&out, 0); bword(&out, 0);           /* value, jitter, array length */
        bword(&out, slot[i].script ? 1 : 2);
        if (slot[i].script) {
            VepSrcNode *n = &c->nodes[slot[i].src];
            Buf code = {0};
            c->curName = vs(&n->name);
            bstr(&out, vs(&n->show));
            compile_block(c, vs(&n->set), &code);  bword(&out, (int)code.n); bput(&out, code.b, code.n); code.n = 0;
            compile_block(c, vs(&n->loop), &code); bword(&out, (int)code.n); bput(&out, code.b, code.n);
            free(code.b);
            bstr(&out, withDesc ? vs(&n->desc) : "");
            bstr(&out, vs(&n->take));
            bword(&out, (n->f64 ? 1 : 0) | (n->takeFlag ? 2 : 0) | (n->startFlag ? 4 : 0));
            bword(&out, 0);
            bword(&out, slot[i].prev);
            bword(&out, slot[i].next);
        }
    }
    free(slot);
    *len = out.n;
    return out.b;
}

uint8_t *vepc_compile_text(const char *text, int version, size_t *len, VepWarnFn w, void *ctx)
{
    VepCompiler c;
    Src s;
    uint8_t *r;
    vepc_init(&c);
    c.warn = w; c.warnCtx = ctx;
    c.dupIsError = 1;
    s.f = NULL; s.mem = text; s.pos = 0; s.len = strlen(text);
    read_programmer(&c, &s);
    r = vepc_compile(&c, version, 0, len);
    vepc_free(&c);
    return r;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ load or compile */

static int file_time(const char *p, long long *t)
{
    struct stat st;
    if (stat(p, &st) != 0) return 0;
    *t = (long long)st.st_mtime;
    return 1;
}

static int needs_recompile(const char *base)
{   /* orig: 1018:0000 vet_NeedsRecompile (the original also deletes the stale .VET) */
    char p[1024];
    long long tv, t;
    int i;
    snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VET", base);
    if (!file_time(p, &tv)) return 1;
    snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VEP", base);
    if (file_time(p, &t)) return tv < t;
    for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VP%d", base, i); if (file_time(p, &t) && tv < t) return 1;
        snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VW%d", base, i); if (file_time(p, &t) && tv < t) return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static void say(VepCompiler *c, const char *m) { if (c->warn) c->warn(c->warnCtx, 0, m); }

int vepc_load_or_compile(VetVM *vm, const char *base, VepWarnFn w, void *ctx)
{   /* orig: 1018:6196 vet_LoadOrCompile */
    char p[1024];
    VepCompiler c;
    FILE *f;
    int ok = 0, version = 4, i;
    uint8_t *img;
    size_t len;
    snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VET", base);
    if (!needs_recompile(base) && vet_load(vm, p) == 0) return 0;
    vepc_init(&c);
    c.warn = w; c.warnCtx = ctx;
    snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VEP", base);
    if ((f = fopen(p, "r")) != NULL) {
        say(&c, "Reading and compiling .VEP script...");
        c.dupIsError = 1;
        ok = vepc_read_programmer(&c, f);
        fclose(f);
    } else {
        say(&c, "Reading .VP* Programmer scripts...");
        version = 5; c.dupIsError = 1;
        for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VP%d", base, i);
            if ((f = fopen(p, "r")) != NULL) { if (vepc_read_programmer(&c, f)) ok = 1; fclose(f); }
        }
        if (c.errors) { char m[64]; snprintf(m, sizeof m, "%d duplicate nodes in programmer script", c.errors); say(&c, m); }
        say(&c, "Reading .VW* Writer scripts...");
        c.dupIsError = 0;
        for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VW%d", base, i);
            if ((f = fopen(p, "r")) != NULL) { vepc_read_writer(&c, f); fclose(f); }
        }
    }
    if (!ok) { say(&c, "Error reading VEP script."); vepc_free(&c); return -1; }
    say(&c, "Compiling new .VET script...");
    img = vepc_compile(&c, version, 0, &len);
    vepc_free(&c);
    if (!img) return -1;
    snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s.VET", base);
    if ((f = fopen(p, "wb")) != NULL) { fwrite(img, 1, len, f); fclose(f); }
    return vet_load_buffer(vm, img, len) == 0 ? 0 : -1;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ decompiler */

static void put_expr(const VetVM *vm, const uint8_t *b, int n, FILE *o)
{   /* tokens back to text, separated by spaces; re-reading them gives the same bytes */
    int i = 0;
    while (i < n) {
        int t = b[i];
        if (t == 0x83 && i + 2 < n) {
            int idx = b[i + 1] | (b[i + 2] << 8);
            fprintf(o, " %s", idx < vm->nodeCount ? vm->nodes[idx].name : "?");
            i += 3;
        } else if (t == 0x84 && i + 2 < n) {
            fprintf(o, " %u", (unsigned)(b[i + 1] | (b[i + 2] << 8)));
            i += 3;
        } else if (t == 0x87 && i + 2 < n) {
            int ch = b[i + 1] & 0x7f, f = b[i + 2];
            fputc(' ', o);
            if (ch) fputc(ch, o);
            if (f & 1) fputc('=', o);
            if (f & 4) fputc('%', o);
            if (f & 2) fputc('-', o);
            i += 3;
        } else if (t == 0x89 && i + 2 < n) {
            fprintf(o, " %c", b[i + 1] & 0x7f);
            i += 3;
        } else i++;
    }
}

static void put_block(const VetVM *vm, const VetCode *code, FILE *o)
{
    const uint8_t *b = code->data;
    int i = 0, n = code->len;
    while (i < n) {
        unsigned k = 0;
        int cs = -1, ce = -1, as = -1, ae = -1, target = -1;
        if (b[i] == 0x88) { i++; continue; }                 /* an empty statement cannot be written */
        if (b[i] == 0x81 && i + 2 < n) { k = b[i + 1] | (b[i + 2] << 8); i += 3; }
        while (i < n && b[i] != 0x88) {
            int t = b[i];
            if (t == 0x82) { cs = i + 1; if (as >= 0 && ae < 0) ae = i; i++; continue; }
            if (t == 0x85) { as = i + 1; if (cs >= 0 && ce < 0) ce = i; i++; continue; }
            if (t == 0x86 && i + 2 < n) {
                if (cs >= 0 && ce < 0) ce = i;
                if (as >= 0 && ae < 0) ae = i;
                target = b[i + 1] | (b[i + 2] << 8); i += 3; continue;
            }
            i += (t == 0x83 || t == 0x84 || t == 0x87 || t == 0x89) ? 3 : 1;
        }
        if (cs >= 0 && ce < 0) ce = i;
        if (as >= 0 && ae < 0) ae = i;
        i++;                                                  /* 0x88 */
        if (k & K_LOOP) fputs(" LOOP", o);
        if (k & K_INIT) fputs(" INITIALIZE", o);
        if (k & K_RETURN) fputs(" RETURN", o);
        if (k & K_COND) fputs((k & K_NOT) ? " UNTIL" : ((k & K_LOOP) ? " WHILE" : " IF"), o);
        if (cs >= 0) put_expr(vm, b + cs, ce - cs, o);
        if (as >= 0) { if (k & K_COND) fputs(" THEN", o); put_expr(vm, b + as, ae - as, o); }
        if (target >= 0) {
            fputs((k & K_GOSUB) ? " GOSUB" : " GOTO", o);
            fprintf(o, " %s", target < vm->nodeCount ? vm->nodes[target].name : "?");
        }
        fputc('\n', o);
    }
}

int vep_decompile(const VetVM *vm, FILE *o)
{
    int k = vm->startNode, count = 0;
    fprintf(o, "; decompiled from a version %d .VET (%d nodes)\n\n", vm->version, vm->nodeCount);
    while (k >= 0 && k < vm->nodeCount && vm->nodes[k].type == 1 && count <= vm->nodeCount) {
        const VetNode *n = &vm->nodes[k];
        const char *d = n->desc ? n->desc : "";
        fprintf(o, "@%s\n", n->name);
        while (*d) {
            size_t len = strcspn(d, "\r\n");
            if (len) fprintf(o, "SHOW: %.*s\n", (int)len, d);
            d += len;
            while (*d == '\r' || *d == '\n') d++;
        }
        if (n->code1.len) { fputs("SET:\n", o); put_block(vm, &n->code1, o); }
        if (n->code2.len) { fputs("LOOP:\n", o); put_block(vm, &n->code2, o); }
        if (n->s2 && *n->s2) fprintf(o, "%s %s\n", (n->flags & 4) && !(n->flags & 2) ? "START:" : "TAKE:", n->s2);
        fputc('\n', o);
        count++;
        if (!n->next) break;
        k = n->next;
    }
    return count;
}

```

`src/vepc.h`:

```h
/*
 * vepc.h - the VEP source-file compiler: .VEP / .VP0-9 / .VW0-9 script text -> a compiled .VET.
 *
 * The shipped game only loads IZZY.VET (version 5, built from programmer .VP* and writer .VW* scripts that do
 * not ship). This is the development path of vet_LoadOrCompile, reconstructed so the script toolchain is
 * complete: vep_decompile turns a .VET back into source that this compiler turns into the same bytes.
 *
 * Source format (orig: 1010:ec4a vep_ReadSourceLine, 1010:e8f6 vep_TrimLine, 1010:e9d6 vep_ParseHeader):
 *   @NAME                      a node (alone on its line; the name is upper-cased)
 *   SHOW: text                 a narration line (the node's description; one file name per line)
 *   SET:  / LOOP:              statements, one per line, run on entry / each step
 *   TAKE: NAME  START: NAME    the take (S2); flags 2 / 4
 *   LOC: text   AUTHOR: {..}   DESC: {..}   notes for the writers (not compiled; DESC becomes S1 on request)
 *   other text                 description prose (joined into the DESC text)
 * A section runs until a blank line. Keywords are the first word of a line; SHOW/SET/LOOP/TAKE text is
 * upper-cased. Statements (1010:e5c2 vep_ClassifyStatement):
 *   [LOOP] [INITIALIZE] [RETURN] [IF|ON|WHEN|WHILE|UNTIL <condition>] [THEN] [<expression>] [GOTO|GOSUB <node>]
 *   NOT x -> "100 > x" (x NOT -> "x < 100"); @ -> the current node's name.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_VEPC_H
#define IZZY_VEPC_H

#include "vet.h"
#include <stdio.h>

typedef void (*VepWarnFn)(void *ctx, int code, const char *msg);

typedef struct VepStr { char *s; size_t n, cap; } VepStr;

typedef struct VepSrcNode {
    VepStr name, show, set, loop, desc, loc, author, take;   /* +4 +0x10 +0x1c +0x28 +0x34 +0x40 +0x4c +0x58 */
    int    f64, takeFlag, startFlag;                         /* +0x64 +0x66 +0x68 */
} VepSrcNode;

typedef struct VepCompiler {
    VepSrcNode *nodes; int count, cap;       /* the source nodes in file order (VetVM +8 listA) */
    char **names; int nnames, ncap;          /* the sorted name table (VetVM +0x10 'pending', count +0x1d2) */
    int  section;                            /* VetVM +0xba */
    int  dupIsError;                         /* VetVM +0xbc: programmer scripts reject duplicate nodes */
    int  eof;                                /* VetVM +0xc0 */
    int  lineNo;
    int  errors;                             /* DS:0a28 duplicate nodes */
    int  warnings;
    char line[260];                          /* VetVM +0xc4 */
    const char *curName;                     /* VetVM +0x18: '@' */
    VepWarnFn warn; void *warnCtx;
} VepCompiler;

void vepc_init(VepCompiler *c);
void vepc_free(VepCompiler *c);
/* orig: 1018:07fc vep_CompileFile - a .VEP / .VP* script (nodes, SHOW/SET/LOOP/TAKE/START, text). 1 = read */
int  vepc_read_programmer(VepCompiler *c, FILE *f);
/* orig: 1018:0574 vep_ReadSourceFile - a .VW* writer script: text, LOC, AUTHOR, DESC of existing nodes only */
int  vepc_read_writer(VepCompiler *c, FILE *f);
/* orig: 1018:1764 vet_Start (name table) + 1018:1ad2 vep_Compile + 1018:2bdc vet_WriteCompiled.
 * withDesc: keep the DESC text as S1 (the original's argument; 0 for the shipped file). Returns a malloc'ed .VET image. */
uint8_t *vepc_compile(VepCompiler *c, int version, int withDesc, size_t *len);

/* orig: 1018:6196 vet_LoadOrCompile + 1018:0000 vet_NeedsRecompile. base = path without extension ("dir/IZZY").
 * Loads base.VET unless base.VEP / base.VP0-9 / base.VW0-9 are newer (or it is missing); then compiles them and
 * writes base.VET. Returns 0 on success. */
int  vepc_load_or_compile(VetVM *vm, const char *base, VepWarnFn warn, void *warnCtx);

/* Not in the original: write a compiled VET as one .VP0 programmer script that compiles to the same bytes. */
int  vep_decompile(const VetVM *vm, FILE *out);
/* compile in memory from a source text (one programmer script); returns the .VET image */
uint8_t *vepc_compile_text(const char *text, int version, size_t *len, VepWarnFn warn, void *warnCtx);

#endif

```

`src/vet.c`:

```c
#include "vet.h"

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- MSVC rand (orig: 1000:0af0 / 1000:0ad8) */

static uint32_t g_seed = 1;
int  vet_msvc_rand(void) { g_seed = g_seed * 0x343FDu + 0x269EC3u; return (int)((g_seed >> 16) & 0x7FFF); }
void vet_msvc_srand(unsigned seed) { g_seed = seed; }

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- code cursor (orig: 1010:d6xx-d8xx code_*) */

/* The Code class is a byte buffer with a read cursor; VetCode keeps data/len/pos and these helpers read it.
 * orig: 1010:d76c, 1010:d81c, 1010:d874, 1010:d882, 1010:d890, 1010:d8bc, 1010:d8ec, 1010:d6f2 - code_ReadWordB,
 * code_ReadWordA, code_Tell, code_Length, code_Skip, code_Seek, code_SetLength, code_CurrentPtr */
static int code_read_byte(VetCode *c, uint8_t *b)
{   /* orig: 1010:d708 code_ReadByte */
    *b = 0;
    if (c->pos < c->len && c->len != 0 && c->pos >= 0) { *b = c->data[c->pos++]; return 1; }
    return 0;
}

static int code_read_word(VetCode *c, int16_t *w)
{   /* orig: 1010:d7c4 / d76c / d81c code_ReadWord* */
    if (c->len - 2 < c->pos || c->len == 0 || c->pos < 0) return 0;
    *w = rd_i16(c->data + c->pos);
    c->pos += 2;
    return 1;
}

/* "89 c c" punctuation test: if the next token is punctuation `ch`, consume it and return 1. */
static int code_take_punct(VetCode *c, char ch)
{
    int16_t save = c->pos;
    uint8_t t, p, p2;
    if (code_read_byte(c, &t) && t == 0x89 && code_read_byte(c, &p) && (p & 0x7F) == ch) {
        code_read_byte(c, &p2);
        return 1;
    }
    c->pos = save;
    return 0;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- token helpers */

static int16_t skip_balanced(VetCode *c, char close, char open)
{
    /* orig: 1018:3468 vet_SkipBalanced - returns the position of the matching closing punctuation token */
    int depth = 1, ok;
    int16_t at;
    do {
        uint8_t t, p;
        at = c->pos;
        ok = code_read_byte(c, &t);
        if (!ok) break;
        switch (t) {
        case 0x81: case 0x83: case 0x84: case 0x86: case 0x87: c->pos += 2; break;
        case 0x82: case 0x85: break;
        case 0x89:
            code_read_byte(c, &p); c->pos += 1;
            p &= 0x7F;
            if (p == open) depth++; else if (p == close) depth--;
            break;
        default: c->pos = at; ok = 0; break;
        }
    } while (ok && depth > 0);
    return at;
}

typedef struct VetOp { char ch; int prec; int assign; int negate; int jitter; } VetOp;

static int read_operator(const VetVM *vm, VetCode *c, VetOp *op)
{
    /* orig: 1018:3cd0 vet_ReadOperator - "87 c f": c = char|0x80, f bit0 assign/or-equal, bit1 negate rhs,
     * bit2 target jitter. Returns 0 if the next token is not an operator. */
    uint8_t t, ch, f;
    memset(op, 0, sizeof *op);
    if (!code_read_byte(c, &t) || t != 0x87) return 0;
    if (!code_read_byte(c, &ch)) return 0;
    op->ch = (char)(ch & 0x7F);
    if (code_read_byte(c, &f)) {
        op->assign = (f & 1) != 0;
        op->jitter = (f & 4) != 0;
        op->negate = (f & 2) != 0;
    }
    if (op->assign && op->ch == 0) op->ch = '=';
    if (vm->exprMode == 0) {
        if (op->ch == 0) return 1;
        op->prec = (op->ch == '&' || op->ch == '|') ? 1 : 2;
        return 1;
    }
    switch (op->ch) {
    case '|': op->prec = 3; break;
    case '&': op->prec = 4; break;
    case '=': op->prec = 8; break;
    case '<': case '>': op->prec = 9; break;
    case '+': case '-': op->prec = op->assign ? 1 : 0xb; break;
    case '*': case '/': op->prec = op->assign ? 1 : 0xc; break;
    default: op->prec = 0; break;
    }
    return 1;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- variables */

int vet_get_variable(const VetVM *vm, int node, int index, int16_t *value, int16_t *jitter)
{
    /* orig: 1018:39ca vet_GetVariable */
    const VetNode *n;
    if (!vm->nodes || node < 0 || node >= vm->nodeCount) return 0;
    n = &vm->nodes[node];
    if (index < 1) { *value = n->value; *jitter = n->jitter; return 1; }
    if (n->arrCount <= (index - 1) * 2) { *value = 0; *jitter = 0; return 1; }
    *value = n->arr[(index - 1) * 2];
    *jitter = n->arr[(index - 1) * 2 + 1];
    return 1;
}

int vet_store_variable(VetVM *vm, int node, int index, int16_t value, int16_t jitter)
{
    /* orig: 1018:3a8a vet_StoreVariable. Indexes 1..0x1FFF address array elements; the array is created and
     * grown on demand (orig: 1018:38b0 vet_EnsureArray) - IZZY.VET itself ships with no arrays at all.
     * Any other index stores the scalar. A change marks the VM running. */
    VetNode *n;
    if (!vm->nodes || node < 0 || node >= vm->nodeCount) return 0;
    n = &vm->nodes[node];
    if (index < 1 || index > 0x1FFF) {
        if (n->value == value && n->jitter == jitter) return 1;
        n->value = value; n->jitter = jitter;
    } else {
        if (n->arrCount < index * 2) {
            int16_t *grown = (int16_t *)realloc(n->arr, sizeof(int16_t) * 2 * (size_t)index);
            if (!grown) return 0;
            memset(grown + n->arrCount, 0, sizeof(int16_t) * (size_t)(index * 2 - n->arrCount));
            n->arr = grown;
            n->arrCount = (int16_t)(index * 2);
        }
        if (n->arr[(index - 1) * 2] == value && n->arr[(index - 1) * 2 + 1] == jitter) return 1;
        n->arr[(index - 1) * 2] = value;
        n->arr[(index - 1) * 2 + 1] = jitter;
    }
    vm->running = 1;
    return 1;
}

static int16_t apply_jitter(const VetVM *vm, int16_t v, int16_t j)
{   /* value + rand()%(j+1) - rand()%(j+1), as in vet_EvalVariableRef */
    if (j > 0) {
        int r1 = vm->rand(), r2;
        v = (int16_t)(v + r1 % (j + 1));
        r2 = vm->rand();
        v = (int16_t)(v - r2 % (j + 1));
    }
    return v;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- expressions */

static int eval_expr(VetVM *vm, int16_t *out, int minPrec, int16_t end, int16_t start, VetCode *c);

static int eval_operand(VetVM *vm, int16_t *out, VetCode *c)
{
    /* orig: 1018:3524 vet_EvalVariableRef - variable, VAR[index], BUILTIN(arg), or literal */
    uint8_t t;
    int16_t node, idx = 0, arg = 0, j = 0;
    *out = 0;
    if (!code_read_byte(c, &t)) return 0;
    if (t == 0x84) return code_read_word(c, out);              /* literal */
    if (t != 0x83 || !code_read_word(c, &node)) return 0;      /* variable reference */

    if (code_take_punct(c, '[')) {                             /* VAR[expr] */
        int16_t inner = c->pos, close = skip_balanced(c, ']', '['), after = c->pos;
        if (inner < close) eval_expr(vm, &idx, 0, close, inner, c);
        c->pos = after;
        if (!vet_get_variable(vm, node, idx, out, &j)) return 1;
        *out = apply_jitter(vm, *out, j);
        return 1;
    }
    if (code_take_punct(c, '(')) {                             /* BUILTIN(expr) */
        int16_t inner = c->pos, close = skip_balanced(c, ')', '('), after = c->pos;
        if (inner < close) eval_expr(vm, &arg, 0, close, inner, c);
        c->pos = after;
        if (node >= 0 && node < vm->nodeCount && vm->nodes[node].builtinFn)
            *out = vm->nodes[node].builtinFn(vm->nodes[node].builtinCtx, vm, node, arg);
        return 1;
    }
    if (vet_get_variable(vm, node, 0, out, &j)) { *out = apply_jitter(vm, *out, j); return 1; }
    return 0;
}

static int16_t apply_binary(char ch, int orEqual, int16_t a, int16_t b)
{
    /* the operator switch of 1018:3e62 vet_EvalExpression */
    switch (ch) {
    case '|': return (a >= 100 || b >= 100) ? 100 : 0;
    case '&': return (a >= 100 && b >= 100) ? 100 : 0;
    case '-': return (int16_t)(a - b);
    case '+': return (int16_t)(a + b);
    case '*': return (int16_t)(a * b);
    case '/': return b != 0 ? (int16_t)(a / b) : a;
    case '!': return a != b ? 100 : 0;
    case '<': return (a < b || (orEqual && a == b)) ? 100 : 0;
    case '>': return (a > b || (orEqual && a == b)) ? 100 : 0;
    case '=': return a == b ? 100 : 0;
    default:  return orEqual ? (a == b ? 100 : 0) : a;
    }
}

static int eval_expr(VetVM *vm, int16_t *out, int minPrec, int16_t end, int16_t start, VetCode *c)
{
    /* orig: 1018:3e62 vet_EvalExpression - precedence climbing over [start, end) */
    int16_t savedLen = c->len, resume;
    int ok;
    c->len = end;
    c->pos = start;
    if (code_take_punct(c, '(')) {                             /* parenthesised primary */
        int16_t inner = c->pos, close = skip_balanced(c, ')', '('), after = c->pos;
        ok = 0;
        if (inner < close) ok = eval_expr(vm, out, 0, close, inner, c);
        c->pos = after;
        c->len = end;
    } else {
        ok = eval_operand(vm, out, c);
    }
    resume = c->pos;
    while (ok) {
        VetOp op;
        int16_t rhs = 0;
        int more = read_operator(vm, c, &op);
        if (!more || minPrec >= op.prec) break;
        if (!eval_expr(vm, &rhs, op.prec, end, c->pos, c)) break;
        resume = c->pos;
        if (op.negate) rhs = (int16_t)-rhs;
        *out = apply_binary(op.ch, op.assign, *out, rhs);
    }
    c->len = savedLen;
    c->pos = resume;
    return ok;
}

int vet_eval(VetVM *vm, VetCode *code, int16_t *out)
{
    return eval_expr(vm, out, 0, code->len, code->pos, code);
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- statements */

int vet_exec_statement(VetVM *vm, VetCode *c)
{
    /* orig: 1018:40ec vet_ExecStatement - "VAR[idx] op= expr ..." or a bare expression (e.g. a built-in call) */
    int16_t start = c->pos, node = 0, idx = 0, val = 0, jit = 0, oldVal, oldJit;
    uint8_t t;
    int isVar = code_read_byte(c, &t) && t == 0x83 && code_read_word(c, &node);
    VetOp op;
    int targetJitter = 0;

    if (isVar) {
        if (code_take_punct(c, '[')) {
            int16_t inner = c->pos, close = skip_balanced(c, ']', '['), after = c->pos;
            if (inner < close) eval_expr(vm, &idx, 0, close, inner, c);
            c->pos = after;
        }
        isVar = vet_get_variable(vm, node, idx, &val, &jit);
    }
    if (!isVar) return 1;
    oldVal = val; oldJit = jit;

    if (!read_operator(vm, c, &op)) {                          /* expression statement */
        int16_t ignored;
        c->pos = start;
        eval_expr(vm, &ignored, 0, c->len, c->pos, c);
        return 1;
    }
    do {
        int16_t rhs = 0;
        int16_t *tgt;
        targetJitter |= op.jitter;
        tgt = targetJitter ? &jit : &val;
        if (eval_expr(vm, &rhs, 0, c->len, c->pos, c)) {
            if (op.negate) rhs = (int16_t)-rhs;
            switch (op.ch) {
            case '=': case 0: if (op.assign || op.jitter) *tgt = rhs; break;
            case '+': if (rhs != 0) *tgt = (int16_t)(*tgt + rhs); break;
            case '-': if (rhs != 0) *tgt = (int16_t)(*tgt - rhs); break;
            case '*': if (rhs != 1) *tgt = (int16_t)(*tgt * rhs); break;
            case '/': if (rhs != 0 && rhs != 1) *tgt = (int16_t)(*tgt / rhs); break;
            default: break;
            }
        }
    } while (read_operator(vm, c, &op));

    if (val != oldVal || jit != oldJit) {
        vet_store_variable(vm, node, idx, val, jit);
        if (node >= 0 && node < vm->nodeCount && vm->nodes[node].watchFn)
            vm->nodes[node].watchFn(vm->nodes[node].watchCtx, vm, node);
    }
    return 1;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- loader */

static char *read_cstr(const uint8_t *b, size_t len, size_t *p)
{
    size_t q = *p;
    char *s;
    while (q < len && b[q]) q++;
    s = (char *)malloc(q - *p + 1);
    if (s) { memcpy(s, b + *p, q - *p); s[q - *p] = 0; }
    *p = q < len ? q + 1 : len;
    return s;
}

static int read_code(const uint8_t *b, size_t len, size_t *p, VetCode *c)
{   /* orig: 1010:d95a code_ReadFromStream - u16 length + bytes */
    uint16_t n;
    if (*p + 2 > len) return 0;
    n = rd_u16(b + *p); *p += 2;
    if (*p + n > len) return 0;
    c->data = b + *p; c->len = (int16_t)n; c->pos = 0;
    *p += n;
    return 1;
}

int vet_load(VetVM *vm, const char *path)
{
    size_t len;
    uint8_t *b = izzy_read_file(path, &len);
    if (!b) { memset(vm, 0, sizeof *vm); return -1; }
    return vet_load_buffer(vm, b, len);
}

int vet_load_buffer(VetVM *vm, uint8_t *blob, size_t len)
{
    /* orig: 1018:2e58 vet_ReadCompiled */
    size_t p = 6;
    int i;
    memset(vm, 0, sizeof *vm);
    vm->rand = vet_msvc_rand;
    vm->exprMode = 1;
    vm->blob = blob;
    if (!vm->blob || len < 6) return -1;
    vm->version = rd_u16(vm->blob);
    vm->exprMode = vm->version > 4;   /* orig: vet_LoadOrCompile - precedence only for VET version > 4 */
    vm->nodeCount = rd_i16(vm->blob + 2);
    vm->startNode = rd_i16(vm->blob + 4);
    vm->nodes = (VetNode *)calloc(vm->nodeCount > 0 ? (size_t)vm->nodeCount : 1, sizeof(VetNode));
    if (!vm->nodes) return -2;
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++) {
        VetNode *n = &vm->nodes[i];
        int k;
        if (p >= len) return -3;
        n->name = read_cstr(vm->blob, len, &p);
        if (p + 8 > len) return -3;
        n->value = rd_i16(vm->blob + p);
        n->jitter = rd_i16(vm->blob + p + 2);
        n->arrCount = rd_i16(vm->blob + p + 4);
        n->type = rd_i16(vm->blob + p + 6);
        p += 8;
        if (n->arrCount > 0) {
            if (p + 2 * (size_t)n->arrCount > len) return -3;
            n->arr = (int16_t *)malloc(2 * (size_t)n->arrCount);
            for (k = 0; k < n->arrCount; k++) n->arr[k] = rd_i16(vm->blob + p + 2 * k);
            p += 2 * (size_t)n->arrCount;
        }
        if (n->type == 1) {
            n->desc = read_cstr(vm->blob, len, &p);
            if (!read_code(vm->blob, len, &p, &n->code1) || !read_code(vm->blob, len, &p, &n->code2)) return -3;
            n->s1 = read_cstr(vm->blob, len, &p);
            n->s2 = read_cstr(vm->blob, len, &p);
            if (p + 8 > len) return -3;
            n->flags = rd_i16(vm->blob + p);
            n->v16 = rd_i16(vm->blob + p + 2);
            n->v1a = rd_i16(vm->blob + p + 4);
            n->next = rd_i16(vm->blob + p + 6);   /* VetScript +0x18: successor node */
            p += 8;
        }
    }
    vm->timeVar = (int16_t)vet_find(vm, "TIME");
    vm->currentNode = -1;
    return p == len ? 0 : 1;   /* 1 = parsed, but trailing bytes remain */
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- statement splitter */

typedef struct VetStmt {
    int16_t kind, label;
```
