```c
    VetCode cond, expr;   /* condition bytes after 0x82, statement bytes after 0x85 */
} VetStmt;

static int split_statement(VetCode *c, VetStmt *s)
{
    /* orig: 1018:44b2 vet_ExecBlock - read tokens up to 0x88; returns the statement kind (0 at end of code) */
    int16_t condStart = 0, condLen = 0, exprStart = 0, exprLen = 0;
    memset(s, 0, sizeof *s);
    for (;;) {
        uint8_t t;
        if (!code_read_byte(c, &t)) break;
        switch (t) {
        case 0x81: if (!code_read_word(c, &s->kind)) goto done; break;
        case 0x82:
            if (exprStart && !exprLen) exprLen = c->pos - exprStart;
            if (!condStart) condStart = c->pos;
            break;
        case 0x83: case 0x84: case 0x87: case 0x89: c->pos += 2; break;
        case 0x85:
            if (condStart && !condLen) condLen = c->pos - condStart;
            if (!exprStart) exprStart = c->pos;
            break;
        case 0x86: if (!code_read_word(c, &s->label)) goto done; break;
        case 0x88:
            if (condStart && !condLen) condLen = c->pos - condStart - 1;
            if (exprStart && !exprLen) exprLen = c->pos - exprStart - 1;
            goto done;
        default: break;
        }
    }
done:
    s->cond.data = c->data + condStart; s->cond.len = condLen; s->cond.pos = 0;
    s->expr.data = c->data + exprStart; s->expr.len = exprLen; s->expr.pos = 0;
    return s->kind;
}

static int16_t eval_cond(VetVM *vm, VetStmt *s)
{
    int16_t v = 0;
    VetCode c = s->cond;
    eval_expr(vm, &v, 0, c.len, 0, &c);
    return v;
}

static void report(VetVM *vm, const char *msg)
{
    if (vm->onError) vm->onError(vm->hookCtx, vm, msg);
}

static void set_named(VetVM *vm, const char *name, int16_t v)
{   /* orig: 1018:5b90 (misnamed vet_CallBuiltin): store a value into the variable called `name` */
    int n = vet_find(vm, name);
    if (n >= 0) vet_store_variable(vm, n, 0, v, 0);
}

static void reset_variables(VetVM *vm)
{   /* orig: 1018:593c vet_InitGlobals (also the variable part of 1018:5f2e vet_NewGame) */
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++) { vm->nodes[i].value = 0; vm->nodes[i].jitter = 0; }
    vm->depth = 0;
    set_named(vm, "TRUE", 100);
    set_named(vm, "FALSE", 0);
    set_named(vm, "TIME", 0);
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- node control */

void vet_enter_node(VetVM *vm, int node)
{   /* orig: 1018:4644 vet_EnterNode - only script nodes (type 1) can be entered */
    if (node < 0 || node >= vm->nodeCount || vm->nodes[node].type != 1) return;
    vm->currentNode = (int16_t)node;
    vm->nodes[node].code1.pos = 0;
    vm->nodes[node].code2.pos = 0;
}

void vet_new_game(VetVM *vm, unsigned seed)
{   /* orig: 1018:5f2e - enter the start node, clear variables, seed rand with timeGetTime */
    vet_enter_node(vm, vm->startNode);
    reset_variables(vm);
    vm->pc = 0; vm->setDone = 0; vm->returning = 0; vm->looping = 0;
    vm->running = 1;
    vet_msvc_srand(seed);
}

int vet_run_set(VetVM *vm)
{
    /* orig: 1018:4822 vet_RunCode - the SET block allows EXPR, IF and INITIALIZE only */
    VetNode *n;
    VetCode c;
    int ok = 1;
    if (vm->currentNode < 0) return 0;
    n = &vm->nodes[vm->currentNode];
    c = n->code1; c.pos = 0;
    for (;;) {
        VetStmt s;
        int k = split_statement(&c, &s);
        if (k == 0) break;
        if (k == VK_EXPR) ok = vet_exec_statement(vm, &s.expr);
        else if (k == (VK_IF | VK_EXPR)) { if (eval_cond(vm, &s) > 99) ok = vet_exec_statement(vm, &s.expr); }
        else if (k == VK_INIT) reset_variables(vm);
        else report(vm, "Set Error -- GOTO, GOSUB, RETURN and LOOP are allowed only in the LOOP block");
    }
    vm->setDone = 1;
    return ok;
}

static int ct_alnum(char c) { return (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9'); }
static char ct_upper(char c) { return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') ? (char)(c - 0x20) : c; }

int vet_format_page(const char *desc, int start, char *page, size_t cap, uint8_t *mask)
{   /* orig: 1018:49c0, 1010:b598 - vet_FormatDescription (lines joined with str_AppendChar 10) */
    char bmp[128] = "", wav[128] = "", mid[128] = "";
    int pos = start, wordStart = start, more = 1;
    page[0] = 0; *mask = 0;
    if (!desc) return 0;
    do {
        char name[124], ext[4], c;
        int nl = 0, xl = 0;
        do { wordStart = pos; c = desc[pos++]; } while (c != 0 && !ct_alnum(c));   /* skip to a word */
        if (c == 0) break;
        while (c != 0 && c > ' ' && c != '.') {                                    /* NAME (123 kept) */
            if (nl < 123) name[nl++] = ct_upper(c);
            c = desc[pos++];
        }
        name[nl] = 0;
        if (c == '.') {                                                             /* .EXT (3 kept) */
            c = desc[pos++];
            while (c != 0 && c > ' ') {
                if (xl < 3) ext[xl++] = ct_upper(c);
                c = desc[pos++];
            }
            if (c == 0) pos--;
        } else if (c == 0) pos--;
        ext[xl] = 0;
        if (nl && xl) {
            char *slot = NULL; const char *suffix = NULL;
            if (strcmp(ext, "BMP") == 0) { slot = bmp; suffix = ".BMP"; }
            else if (strcmp(ext, "WAV") == 0) { slot = wav; suffix = ".WAV"; }
            else if (strcmp(ext, "MID") == 0) { slot = mid; suffix = ".MID"; }
            if (slot) {
                if (slot[0] == 0) { strncpy(slot, name, 120); slot[120] = 0; strcat(slot, suffix); }
                else more = 0;                                                      /* repeated type: next page */
            }
        }
    } while (more);
    if (bmp[0]) { strncpy(page, bmp, cap - 1); page[cap - 1] = 0; *mask |= 1; }
    if (wav[0]) { if (page[0]) strncat(page, "\n", cap - strlen(page) - 1); strncat(page, wav, cap - strlen(page) - 1); *mask |= 2; }
    if (mid[0]) { if (page[0]) strncat(page, "\n", cap - strlen(page) - 1); strncat(page, mid, cap - strlen(page) - 1); *mask |= 4; }
    return page[0] ? wordStart : 0;
}

int vet_step(VetVM *vm, uint32_t nowMs, unsigned tickMs)
{
    /* orig: 1018:4d7a vet_Step */
    VetNode *n;
    VetCode c;
    int jump = 0, toLabel = 0, loop = 0, result = 1;
    int16_t target = 0;

    /* TIME advances by whole ticks of real time */
    if (nowMs < vm->lastTick) vm->lastTick = nowMs;
    else {
        unsigned unit = tickMs ? tickMs : 6;
        uint32_t elapsed = nowMs - vm->lastTick;
        if (elapsed >= unit) {
            uint32_t k = elapsed / unit;
            vm->lastTick += k * unit;
            if (vm->timeVar >= 0) vm->nodes[vm->timeVar].value = (int16_t)(vm->nodes[vm->timeVar].value + k);
        }
    }
    if (!vm->nodes || vm->currentNode < 0) return 1;
    /* throttle: within one tick interval only a running VM steps (DS:25a0) */
    if (nowMs - vm->lastRun < (tickMs ? tickMs : 6)) { if (!vm->running) return 1; }
    else vm->lastRun = nowMs;

    n = &vm->nodes[vm->currentNode];
    if ((vm->pc != 0 || vm->setDone != 0) && vm->returning == 0) {
        /* inside a node: turn the narration pages once the previous page's picture / voice are done */
        if (vm->narrActive && (vm->busyFlags == 0 ||
            (nowMs - vm->narrLast > 499 && (!(vm->narrMask & 2) || !(vm->busyFlags & 4)) &&
             (!(vm->narrMask & 1) || !(vm->busyFlags & 8))))) {
            if (vm->narrNext == 0) { vm->busyFlags &= ~1u; vm->narrActive = 0; }
            else {
                if (vm->onNarrate) vm->onNarrate(vm->hookCtx, vm, vm->narrPage, 0);
                vm->busyFlags |= 1;
                vm->narrNext = (int16_t)vet_format_page(n->desc, vm->narrNext, vm->narrPage, sizeof vm->narrPage, &vm->narrMask);
                vm->narrLast = nowMs;
            }
        }
    } else {
        /* entering a node (or resuming after RETURN) */
        int fresh = vm->setDone == 0, hasDesc = 0, narrated = 0;
        const char *s2 = n->s2 ? n->s2 : "", *desc = n->desc ? n->desc : "";
        int flag2 = (n->flags & 2) != 0, flag4 = (n->flags & 4) != 0;
        if (!vm->returning) {
            hasDesc = desc[0] != 0;
            if ((hasDesc || flag2) && vm->onInterrupt) vm->onInterrupt(vm->hookCtx, vm, s2);
            if (vm->onEnter) vm->onEnter(vm->hookCtx, vm, vm->currentNode);
            vet_run_set(vm);                           /* SET block: once per entry, not after RETURN */
        }
        vm->started = 0;
        if ((flag4 || flag2) && izzy_stricmp(s2, "MIDI") != 0 && vm->onJumpTake)
            vm->started = (int16_t)vm->onJumpTake(vm->hookCtx, vm, s2);
        if (vm->started == 0 && hasDesc && fresh) {
            vm->narrNext = (int16_t)vet_format_page(desc, 0, vm->narrPage, sizeof vm->narrPage, &vm->narrMask);
            if (vm->narrPage[0] == 0) vm->narrNext = 0;
            else {
                /* orig: 1018:5222 - a page holding only a MID plays it and does NOT start a narration (DS:0a34 stays as
                 * it was); only picture / voice pages do (1018:5302). Otherwise a later node with a take (e.g. the medal
                 * scenes after ACCEPT_SHOW_*_R's "SHOW: STOP.MID") would see a finished narration and lose its busy bit. */
                if (vm->narrMask == 4) { if (vm->onMusic) vm->onMusic(vm->hookCtx, vm, vm->narrPage, 1); }
                else {
                    if (vm->onNarrate && vm->onNarrate(vm->hookCtx, vm, vm->narrPage, 1)) narrated = 1;
                    if (vm->narrNext != 0) {
                        vm->narrNext = (int16_t)vet_format_page(desc, vm->narrNext, vm->narrPage, sizeof vm->narrPage, &vm->narrMask);
                        vm->narrLast = nowMs;
                    }
                    vm->narrActive = 1;
                }
            }
        } else vm->narrNext = 0;
        if ((vm->started == 0 && narrated) || flag2) vm->busyFlags |= 1;
    }
    vm->returning = 0;

    n = &vm->nodes[vm->currentNode];
    c = n->code2;
    c.pos = (vm->pc < 0 || vm->pc > c.len) ? 0 : vm->pc;
    vm->pc = 0;
    for (;;) {
        VetStmt s;
        int k = split_statement(&c, &s);
        int16_t cond;
        target = s.label;
        switch (k) {
        case 0: break;
        case VK_EXPR: result = vet_exec_statement(vm, &s.expr); break;
        case VK_IF | VK_EXPR:          if (eval_cond(vm, &s) > 99) result = vet_exec_statement(vm, &s.expr); break;
        case VK_NOT | VK_IF | VK_EXPR: if (eval_cond(vm, &s) < 100) result = vet_exec_statement(vm, &s.expr); break;

        case VK_GOTO | VK_LABEL:                        jump = toLabel = 1; break;
        case VK_IF | VK_GOTO | VK_LABEL | VK_EXPR:      if (eval_cond(vm, &s) > 99) jump = toLabel = 1; break;
        case VK_NOT | VK_IF | VK_GOTO | VK_LABEL | VK_EXPR: if (eval_cond(vm, &s) < 100) jump = toLabel = 1; break;

        case VK_GOSUB | VK_LABEL:
        case VK_IF | VK_GOSUB | VK_LABEL | VK_EXPR:
        case VK_NOT | VK_IF | VK_GOSUB | VK_LABEL | VK_EXPR:
            if (k != (VK_GOSUB | VK_LABEL)) {
                cond = eval_cond(vm, &s);
                if ((k & VK_NOT) ? cond >= 100 : cond < 100) break;
            }
            if (vm->depth > 15) { report(vm, "GOSUB stack overflow"); vm->depth = 0; }
            vm->stackNode[vm->depth] = vm->currentNode;
            vm->stackPos[vm->depth] = c.pos;          /* resume after this statement */
            vm->depth++;
            jump = toLabel = 1;
            break;

        case VK_RETURN:
        case VK_RETURN | VK_IF | VK_EXPR:
        case VK_RETURN | VK_NOT | VK_IF | VK_EXPR:
            if (k != VK_RETURN) {
                cond = eval_cond(vm, &s);
                if ((k & VK_NOT) ? cond >= 100 : cond < 100) break;
            }
            if (vm->depth < 1) { report(vm, "RETURN without GOSUB"); vm->depth = 16; }
            vm->depth--;
            target = vm->stackNode[vm->depth];
            vm->pc = vm->stackPos[vm->depth];
            vm->stackNode[vm->depth] = vm->stackPos[vm->depth] = 0;
            jump = toLabel = 1;
            vm->returning = 1;
            break;

        case VK_LOOP:
        case VK_LOOP | VK_GOTO:
            jump = 1; loop = 1; break;
        case VK_LOOP | VK_IF | VK_EXPR:
        case VK_LOOP | VK_GOTO | VK_IF | VK_EXPR:
            if (eval_cond(vm, &s) > 99) { jump = 1; loop = 1; } break;
        case VK_LOOP | VK_NOT | VK_IF | VK_EXPR:
        case VK_LOOP | VK_GOTO | VK_NOT | VK_IF | VK_EXPR:
            if (eval_cond(vm, &s) < 100) { jump = 1; loop = 1; } break;

        default:
            report(vm, k == VK_INIT ? "INITIALIZE is only allowed in the SET block" : "unknown statement kind");
            break;
        }
        if (k != 0 && !jump) continue;

        if (toLabel) {                       /* GOTO / GOSUB / RETURN */
            vm->looping = 0;
            {
                int keepPc = vm->returning;
                int16_t pc = vm->pc;
                vet_enter_node(vm, target);
                if (keepPc) vm->pc = pc;
            }
            if (!vm->returning) vm->setDone = 0;
            vm->running = 1;
        } else if (loop) {                   /* LOOP: stop here, rerun the body from the top next tick */
            vm->looping = 1;
            vm->running = 0;
        } else {                             /* end of body: continue with the successor node */
            vm->looping = 0;
            vet_enter_node(vm, n->next);
            vm->setDone = 0;
            vm->running = 1;
        }
        return result;
    }
}

void vet_free(VetVM *vm)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; vm->nodes && i < vm->nodeCount; i++) {
        free(vm->nodes[i].name); free(vm->nodes[i].arr);
        free(vm->nodes[i].desc); free(vm->nodes[i].s1); free(vm->nodes[i].s2);
    }
    free(vm->nodes); free(vm->blob);
    for (i = 0; i < vm->npatched; i++) free(vm->patched[i]);
    memset(vm, 0, sizeof *vm);
}

int vet_statements(const VetCode *c, int *starts, int max)
{   /* 81 k k, then tokens (83/84/86/87/89 carry two bytes, 82/85 none) up to 88 */
    int i = 0, n = 0;
    while (i < c->len) {
        int b = c->data[i];
        if (b == 0x81) { if (n < max) starts[n] = i; n++; i += 3; continue; }
        if (b == 0x83 || b == 0x84 || b == 0x86 || b == 0x87 || b == 0x89) i += 3;
        else i++;
    }
    return n;
}

int vet_replace_code(VetVM *vm, int node, int block, int at, int len, const uint8_t *ins, int insLen)
{
    VetCode *c;
    uint8_t *d;
    if (node < 0 || node >= vm->nodeCount || vm->nodes[node].type != 1 || vm->npatched >= 8) return 0;
    c = block == 1 ? &vm->nodes[node].code1 : &vm->nodes[node].code2;
    if (at < 0 || len < 0 || at + len > c->len) return 0;
    d = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)(c->len - len + insLen) + 1);
    if (!d) return 0;
    memcpy(d, c->data, (size_t)at);
    memcpy(d + at, ins, (size_t)insLen);
    memcpy(d + at + insLen, c->data + at + len, (size_t)(c->len - at - len));
    c->data = d;
    c->len = (int16_t)(c->len - len + insLen);
    vm->patched[vm->npatched++] = d;
    return 1;
}

int vet_find(const VetVM *vm, const char *name)
{
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++)
        if (vm->nodes[i].name && izzy_stricmp(vm->nodes[i].name, name) == 0) return i;
    return -1;
}

uint16_t vet_set_busy(VetVM *vm, int set, uint16_t mask)
{   /* orig: 1018:6052 */
    vm->busyFlags = set ? (uint16_t)(vm->busyFlags | mask) : (uint16_t)(vm->busyFlags & ~mask);
    return vm->busyFlags;
}

uint16_t vet_get_busy(const VetVM *vm) { return vm->busyFlags; }   /* orig: 1018:6082 */

void vet_restart(VetVM *vm)
{   /* orig: 1018:6092 */
    vet_enter_node(vm, vm->startNode);
    vm->setDone = 0; vm->pc = 0; vm->running = 1;
}

static int find_exact(const VetVM *vm, const char *name)
{   /* the original binary-searches the sorted node names with String::Compare */
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < vm->nodeCount; i++) if (vm->nodes[i].name && strcmp(vm->nodes[i].name, name) == 0) return i;
    return -1;
}

int vet_jump_to(VetVM *vm, const char *name)
{   /* orig: 1018:60c6 */
    int n = find_exact(vm, name);
    if (n < 0) return 0;
    vet_enter_node(vm, n);
    vm->setDone = 0; vm->pc = 0; vm->running = 1;
    return 1;
}

int vet_set_by_name(VetVM *vm, const char *name, int16_t value, int16_t jitter)
{   /* orig: 1018:5c70 */
    int n = find_exact(vm, name);
    if (n < 0) return 0;
    vet_store_variable(vm, n, 0, value, jitter);
    return 1;
}

int16_t vet_read_named(VetVM *vm, const char *name, int index)
{   /* orig: 1018:5e14 - NAME[index] read with its jitter: value + rand()%(j+1) - rand()%(j+1) when j > 0 */
    int16_t v = 0, j = 0;
    int n = find_exact(vm, name);
    if (n < 0) return 0;
    if (vet_get_variable(vm, n, index, &v, &j) && j > 0) {
        int (*rnd)(void) = vm->rand ? vm->rand : vet_msvc_rand;
        int a = rnd() % (j + 1);
        int b = rnd() % (j + 1);
        v = (int16_t)(v + a - b);
    }
    return v;
}
void vet_bind_builtin(VetVM *vm, const char *name, VetBuiltinFn fn, void *ctx)
{   /* orig: 1018:10b8 vet_RegisterBuiltin */
    int i = vet_find(vm, name);
    if (i >= 0) { vm->nodes[i].builtinFn = fn; vm->nodes[i].builtinCtx = ctx; }
}

```

`src/vet.h`:

```h
/*
 * vet.h - the compiled VEP game-logic script (IZZY.VET) and its expression machine.
 *
 * File (orig: 1018:2e58 vet_ReadCompiled):
 *   u16 version(5); u16 nNodes; u16 startNode
 *   node: cstr name; i16 value, jitter, arrCount, type; i16 arr[arrCount]
 *     type 1: cstr desc; code code1; code code2; cstr s1; cstr s2; i16 flags, v16, v1a, v18
 *   code: u16 len; u8 tokens[len]
 *
 * Tokens: 81 k (statement kind) | 82, 85 (expression starts) | 83 n (variable/node n) | 84 v (literal)
 *         86 n (label) | 87 c f (operator) | 88 (end of statement) | 89 c c (punctuation ( ) [ ])
 * Values are 16-bit; booleans are 0 / 100.
 */
#ifndef IZZY_VET_H
#define IZZY_VET_H

#include "izzy_common.h"

typedef struct VetVM VetVM;

/* A built-in called from script as NAME(arg) (orig: node +0x18 fn / +0x1c ctx, set by vet_RegisterBuiltin). */
typedef int16_t (*VetBuiltinFn)(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, int node, int16_t arg);
/* Called after a statement changes a variable (orig: node +0x10 fn / +0x14 ctx). */
typedef void (*VetWatchFn)(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, int node);

typedef struct VetCode {
    const uint8_t *data;
    int16_t len;          /* current limit (code_SetLength) */
    int16_t pos;          /* read position (code_Seek / code_Tell) */
} VetCode;

typedef struct VetNode {
    char    *name;
    int16_t  type;        /* 1 = script node, 2 = variable / group */
    int16_t  value;       /* variable value (index < 1) */
    int16_t  jitter;      /* random range: reads add rand()%(j+1) - rand()%(j+1) */
    int16_t  arrCount;    /* int16 words in arr; elements = arrCount / 2 */
    int16_t *arr;         /* (value, jitter) pairs for NAME[i], i >= 1 */
    /* type 1 only */
    char    *desc, *s1, *s2;
    VetCode  code1, code2;   /* code1 = SET block (run once on entry), code2 = body (run one statement at a time) */
    int16_t  flags, v16, v1a;
    int16_t  next;           /* successor node when the body runs off its end (file field v18) */
    /* host bindings */
    VetBuiltinFn builtinFn; void *builtinCtx;
    VetWatchFn   watchFn;   void *watchCtx;
} VetNode;

struct VetVM {
    uint16_t version;
    int16_t  nodeCount, startNode;
    VetNode *nodes;
    int16_t  exprMode;    /* orig: VetVM +0xbe = (version > 4); 0 = flat precedence (logic 1, everything else 2) */
    int    (*rand)(void); /* defaults to vet_msvc_rand (the original MSVC LCG) */
    uint8_t *blob;        /* file bytes (code blocks point into it) */
    uint8_t *patched[8];  /* code blocks replaced by vet_replace_code (freed with the VM) */
    int      npatched;

    /* interpreter state (orig: VetVM +0x2e6 currentNode, +0x1dc pc, +0x1ec setDone, +0x2dc returning,
     * +0x2d6 running, +0x2d0 looping, +0x2b0/+0x2e8 GOSUB stack, +0x1ee depth, +0x2d2 last TIME tick) */
    int16_t  currentNode;
    int16_t  pc;          /* body position to resume at (set by RETURN) */
    int16_t  setDone;     /* SET block already run for this entry */
    int16_t  returning;   /* entered by RETURN: do not rerun SET */
    int16_t  running;
    int16_t  looping;     /* stopped on a LOOP statement */
    int16_t  stackPos[16], stackNode[16], depth;
    uint32_t lastTick;
    int16_t  timeVar;     /* node index of TIME, or -1 */
    uint16_t busyFlags;   /* +0x1da: bit0/1 description pending, bit2 music loading, bit3 sound loading (TAKE Done clears 0xD) */
    /* host hooks (all optional) */
    void   (*onError)(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, const char *msg);
    void   (*onEnter)(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, int node);   /* called when a node's SET block runs (port convenience) */
    /* the game's callbacks (orig: VetVM +0x92 / +0x9a / +0xa2 / +0xaa, installed by 1010:2d1a take_Player_ctor):
     * entering a node with a description or flag 2 calls onInterrupt(S2) (1010:b8d6 -> 1010:1c26: stop the
     * voice channels if narration is still pending); a node with flag 2 or 4 calls onJumpTake(S2) unless S2 is
     * "MIDI" (1010:b8ee vepfn_JumpTake; non-zero = a take started); description pages go to onNarrate
     * (1010:b928) or, when a page holds only a MID, to onMusic (1010:b996). */
    void   (*onInterrupt)(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, const char *s2);
    int    (*onJumpTake)(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, const char *s2);
    int    (*onNarrate)(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, const char *page, int first);
    int    (*onMusic)(void *ctx, VetVM *vm, const char *page, int first);
    /* narration state (orig: VetVM +0x82 page, +0x8e next offset, +0x2da started; DS:0a2a/0a2c/0a2e mask,
     * DS:0a34 active, DS:25a4 time of the last page) */
    char     narrPage[512];
    int16_t  narrNext;
    uint8_t  narrMask;        /* bit0 BMP, bit1 WAV, bit2 MID in the prepared page */
    int16_t  narrActive;
    uint32_t narrLast;
    int16_t  started;
    uint32_t lastRun;         /* DS:25a0 */
    void    *hookCtx;
};

/* Statement kinds (flag bits of "81 k"): */
enum { VK_EXPR = 1, VK_LABEL = 2, VK_IF = 4, VK_NOT = 8, VK_GOTO = 0x10, VK_GOSUB = 0x20,
       VK_RETURN = 0x40, VK_LOOP = 0x80, VK_INIT = 0x100 };

/* orig: 1018:4644 vet_EnterNode (+ the state resets done by its callers) */
void vet_enter_node(VetVM *vm, int node);
/* orig: 1018:5f2e "vet_Resume" (really: new game) - enter @START, clear all variables, TRUE/FALSE/TIME */
void vet_new_game(VetVM *vm, unsigned seed);
/* orig: 1018:4822 vet_RunCode - run the current node's SET block (EXPR / IF / INITIALIZE only) */
int  vet_run_set(VetVM *vm);
/* orig: 1018:4d7a vet_Step - one interpreter tick: advance TIME, run SET on entry, then body statements
 * until a GOTO / GOSUB / RETURN / LOOP / end of body. nowMs = current time, tickMs = TIME unit (orig default 6). */
int  vet_step(VetVM *vm, uint32_t nowMs, unsigned tickMs);

int  vet_load(VetVM *vm, const char *path);     /* 0 on success */
int  vet_load_buffer(VetVM *vm, uint8_t *blob, size_t len);   /* takes ownership of blob (malloc'ed) */
void vet_free(VetVM *vm);
/* Not in the original: replace `len` bytes at `at` in a node's code block with `ins` (for script fixes).
 * block 1 = SET, 2 = LOOP. Returns 1 on success. */
int  vet_replace_code(VetVM *vm, int node, int block, int at, int len, const uint8_t *ins, int insLen);
/* The byte offsets of the statements of a code block (each 81 kind ... 88); returns the count. */
int  vet_statements(const VetCode *c, int *starts, int max);
int  vet_find(const VetVM *vm, const char *name);   /* node index or -1 */
void vet_bind_builtin(VetVM *vm, const char *name, VetBuiltinFn fn, void *ctx);

/* orig: 1018:39ca vet_GetVariable / 1018:3a8a vet_StoreVariable */
int  vet_get_variable(const VetVM *vm, int node, int index, int16_t *value, int16_t *jitter);
int  vet_store_variable(VetVM *vm, int node, int index, int16_t value, int16_t jitter);

/* Evaluate one expression starting at code->pos (orig: 1018:3e62 vet_EvalExpression with min precedence 0). */
int  vet_eval(VetVM *vm, VetCode *code, int16_t *out);
/* Execute one assignment / expression statement at code->pos (orig: 1018:40ec vet_ExecStatement). */
int  vet_exec_statement(VetVM *vm, VetCode *code);

int  vet_msvc_rand(void);
/* orig: 1018:49c0 vet_FormatDescription - the next narration page of a description ("NAME.EXT ..."): at most one
 * BMP, WAV and MID, upper-cased, one per line in that order; a repeated type starts the next page.
 * Returns the offset of the following page (0 when this page is empty); *mask = 1 BMP | 2 WAV | 4 MID. */
int  vet_format_page(const char *desc, int start, char *page, size_t cap, uint8_t *mask);
/* orig: 1018:6052 vet_SetBusyFlags (set != 0: OR, else AND NOT) / 1018:6082 vet_GetBusyFlags */
uint16_t vet_set_busy(VetVM *vm, int set, uint16_t mask);
uint16_t vet_get_busy(const VetVM *vm);
/* orig: 1018:6092 vet_Restart - enter the start node and run from its top */
void vet_restart(VetVM *vm);
/* orig: 1018:60c6 vet_JumpToNode (the debug Jump dialog) - 1 if the node exists */
int  vet_jump_to(VetVM *vm, const char *name);
/* orig: 1018:5c70 vet_FindVariable (the debug Variables dialog) - set NAME = value, jitter; 1 if found */
int  vet_set_by_name(VetVM *vm, const char *name, int16_t value, int16_t jitter);
/* orig: 1018:5e14 - NAME[index] read with its jitter (value + rand()%(j+1) - rand()%(j+1) when j > 0) */
int16_t vet_read_named(VetVM *vm, const char *name, int index);
void vet_msvc_srand(unsigned seed);

#endif

```

`src/win/izzy_win.c`:

```c
/*
 * izzy_win.c - a native Windows front end for the reconstructed game (32-bit, Windows 7 and later).
 *
 * The original IZZY.EXE is a 16-bit Windows 3.1 program; 64-bit Windows cannot run it. This host runs the
 * portable C reconstruction (src/*.c) in an ordinary Win32 window:
 *   picture   the 640x480 8-bit stage, scaled up (integer zoom by default, any size when resized; F11 or
 *             Alt+Enter toggles full screen)
 *   input     mouse and keyboard to game_click / game_mouse_move / game_char; the original's two cursors
 *   sound     the wave mixer through waveOut (22050 Hz, 16-bit stereo)
 *   music     MCI "sequencer" for the loose .MID files (as the original did)
 *   video     the loose .AVI files, decoded here (avi.c + indeo3.c: Windows no longer has a working Indeo 3.2
 *             codec), drawn over the stage in the take's rectangle, with their own sound through a second waveOut
 *   menus     the original Game / Sound / About menu, the message boxes and the *.SGE file dialogs
 * winmm and comdlg32 are loaded at run time, so the build needs only kernel32 / user32 / gdi32.
 *
 * Usage: IzzyQuest.exe [game folder]   (default: the folder holding IzzyQuest.exe, or a parent of it).
 * (Not izzy.exe: that name would replace the original IZZY.EXE, whose resources the port reads.)
 */
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "../app.h"
#include "../resource.h"
#include "../config.h"
#include "../avi.h"
#include "../indeo3.h"
/* after the engine headers: windows.h defines VK_RETURN etc. as macros, which would break vet.h's enum */
#include <windows.h>

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ winmm / comdlg32 */

typedef struct { WORD wFormatTag, nChannels; DWORD nSamplesPerSec, nAvgBytesPerSec; WORD nBlockAlign, wBitsPerSample, cbSize; } XWAVEFORMATEX;
typedef struct XWAVEHDR { LPSTR lpData; DWORD dwBufferLength, dwBytesRecorded; DWORD_PTR dwUser; DWORD dwFlags, dwLoops; struct XWAVEHDR *lpNext; DWORD_PTR reserved; } XWAVEHDR;
#define XWHDR_DONE 1
typedef struct {
    DWORD lStructSize; HWND hwndOwner; HINSTANCE hInstance; LPCSTR lpstrFilter; LPSTR lpstrCustomFilter; DWORD nMaxCustFilter, nFilterIndex;
    LPSTR lpstrFile; DWORD nMaxFile; LPSTR lpstrFileTitle; DWORD nMaxFileTitle; LPCSTR lpstrInitialDir, lpstrTitle; DWORD Flags;
    WORD nFileOffset, nFileExtension; LPCSTR lpstrDefExt; LPARAM lCustData; void *lpfnHook; LPCSTR lpTemplateName;
} XOPENFILENAMEA;
#define XOFN_OVERWRITEPROMPT 0x2
#define XOFN_HIDEREADONLY 0x4
#define XOFN_PATHMUSTEXIST 0x800
#define XOFN_FILEMUSTEXIST 0x1000
#ifndef mmioFOURCC
#define mmioFOURCC(a, b, c, d) ((DWORD)(BYTE)(a) | ((DWORD)(BYTE)(b) << 8) | ((DWORD)(BYTE)(c) << 16) | ((DWORD)(BYTE)(d) << 24))
#endif
#define XMM_MCINOTIFY 0x3B9
#define XMCI_NOTIFY_SUCCESSFUL 1

static UINT  (WINAPI *p_waveOutOpen)(HANDLE *, UINT, const XWAVEFORMATEX *, DWORD_PTR, DWORD_PTR, DWORD);
static UINT  (WINAPI *p_waveOutPrepareHeader)(HANDLE, XWAVEHDR *, UINT);
static UINT  (WINAPI *p_waveOutUnprepareHeader)(HANDLE, XWAVEHDR *, UINT);
static UINT  (WINAPI *p_waveOutWrite)(HANDLE, XWAVEHDR *, UINT);
static UINT  (WINAPI *p_waveOutReset)(HANDLE);
static UINT  (WINAPI *p_waveOutClose)(HANDLE);
static UINT  (WINAPI *p_waveOutPause)(HANDLE);
static UINT  (WINAPI *p_waveOutRestart)(HANDLE);
static DWORD (WINAPI *p_mciSendStringA)(LPCSTR, LPSTR, UINT, HWND);
static UINT  (WINAPI *p_timeBeginPeriod)(UINT);
static BOOL  (WINAPI *p_GetOpenFileNameA)(XOPENFILENAMEA *);
static BOOL  (WINAPI *p_GetSaveFileNameA)(XOPENFILENAMEA *);

static void load_system_dlls(void)
{
    HMODULE mm = LoadLibraryA("winmm.dll"), cd = LoadLibraryA("comdlg32.dll"), u = GetModuleHandleA("user32.dll");
    BOOL (WINAPI *dpi)(void);
    if (mm) {
        *(FARPROC *)&p_waveOutOpen = GetProcAddress(mm, "waveOutOpen");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_waveOutPrepareHeader = GetProcAddress(mm, "waveOutPrepareHeader");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_waveOutUnprepareHeader = GetProcAddress(mm, "waveOutUnprepareHeader");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_waveOutWrite = GetProcAddress(mm, "waveOutWrite");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_waveOutReset = GetProcAddress(mm, "waveOutReset");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_waveOutClose = GetProcAddress(mm, "waveOutClose");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_waveOutPause = GetProcAddress(mm, "waveOutPause");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_waveOutRestart = GetProcAddress(mm, "waveOutRestart");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_mciSendStringA = GetProcAddress(mm, "mciSendStringA");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_timeBeginPeriod = GetProcAddress(mm, "timeBeginPeriod");
    }
    if (cd) {
        *(FARPROC *)&p_GetOpenFileNameA = GetProcAddress(cd, "GetOpenFileNameA");
        *(FARPROC *)&p_GetSaveFileNameA = GetProcAddress(cd, "GetSaveFileNameA");
    }
    if (u && (*(FARPROC *)&dpi = GetProcAddress(u, "SetProcessDPIAware")) != NULL) dpi();   /* crisp scaling */
}

/* test mode (environment): IZZY_TEST_LOG=file logs the system calls; IZZY_TEST_SECONDS=n quits after n seconds,
 * saving the window to IZZY_TEST_SHOT=file.bmp first; IZZY_TEST_AVI=NAME plays that AVI at start */
static FILE *g_log;
static void logf_(const char *fmt, ...)
{
    va_list ap;
    if (!g_log) return;
    va_start(ap, fmt); vfprintf(g_log, fmt, ap); va_end(ap);
    fputc('\n', g_log); fflush(g_log);
}

static DWORD mci(HWND w, const char *fmt, ...)
{
    char cmd[1200];
    va_list ap;
    DWORD r;
    if (!p_mciSendStringA) return 1;
    va_start(ap, fmt); vsnprintf(cmd, sizeof cmd, fmt, ap); va_end(ap);
    r = p_mciSendStringA(cmd, NULL, 0, w);
    logf_("mci \"%s\" -> %lu", cmd, (unsigned long)r);
    return r;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ state */

#define TITLE "Izzy's Quest for Olympic Gold"
#define AUDIO_BUFS 4
#define AUDIO_FRAMES 1024

static App      g_app;
static HWND     g_wnd;
static HMENU    g_menu;
static HCURSOR  g_cursor[3];
static char     g_dir[MAX_PATH], g_dataDir[MAX_PATH];
static int      g_fullscreen;
static WINDOWPLACEMENT g_place = { sizeof(WINDOWPLACEMENT) };
static LARGE_INTEGER g_freq, g_t0;
static int32_t  g_lastTick = -1;
static RECT     g_view;                        /* where the 640x480 picture goes in the client area */

static HANDLE   g_wave;
static XWAVEHDR g_hdr[AUDIO_BUFS];
static int16_t  g_pcm[AUDIO_BUFS][AUDIO_FRAMES * 2];

static int      g_midiOpen, g_midiLoop, g_midiPaused;
static char     g_midiPos[32];
/* the playing video (built-in AVI reader and Indeo 3.2 decoder; the original used MCI "avivideo") */
static int      g_aviOpen, g_aviRect[4], g_aviFrame, g_aviStride;
static Avi      g_avi;
static Indeo3   g_iv3;
static uint8_t *g_aviBgr;
static LARGE_INTEGER g_aviStart;
static HANDLE   g_aviWave;
static XWAVEHDR g_aviHdr;
static int      g_aviPaused;                   /* the video waits while the game is paused */
static LARGE_INTEGER g_aviPausedAt;
static int      g_focusPaused;                 /* we paused the game because the window lost the focus */
static int      g_testMode;                    /* IZZY_TEST_SECONDS: keep running without the focus */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ picture */

static void layout(void)
{   /* keep 4:3; integer zoom when it fits exactly enough */
    RECT r; int cw, ch, w, h;
    GetClientRect(g_wnd, &r);
    cw = r.right; ch = r.bottom;
    if (cw * 3 > ch * 4) { h = ch; w = ch * 4 / 3; } else { w = cw; h = cw * 3 / 4; }
    if (w >= 640) { int z = w / 640; if (z * 480 <= ch && w - z * 640 < 64) { w = 640 * z; h = 480 * z; } }
    g_view.left = (cw - w) / 2; g_view.top = (ch - h) / 2; g_view.right = g_view.left + w; g_view.bottom = g_view.top + h;
}

static void paint(HDC dc)
{
    static struct { BITMAPINFOHEADER h; RGBQUAD c[256]; } bi;
    const Screen *s = &g_app.game.screen;
    RECT r; HBRUSH black = (HBRUSH)GetStockObject(BLACK_BRUSH);
    int i;
    bi.h.biSize = sizeof bi.h; bi.h.biWidth = SCREEN_W; bi.h.biHeight = -SCREEN_H; bi.h.biPlanes = 1; bi.h.biBitCount = 8;
    bi.h.biCompression = BI_RGB; bi.h.biClrUsed = 256;
    for (i = 0; i < 256; i++) { bi.c[i].rgbRed = s->pal.r[i]; bi.c[i].rgbGreen = s->pal.g[i]; bi.c[i].rgbBlue = s->pal.b[i]; bi.c[i].rgbReserved = 0; }
    GetClientRect(g_wnd, &r);
    if (g_view.top > 0) { RECT b = { 0, 0, r.right, g_view.top }; FillRect(dc, &b, black); b.top = g_view.bottom; b.bottom = r.bottom; FillRect(dc, &b, black); }
    if (g_view.left > 0) { RECT b = { 0, 0, g_view.left, r.bottom }; FillRect(dc, &b, black); b.left = g_view.right; b.right = r.right; FillRect(dc, &b, black); }
    SetStretchBltMode(dc, COLORONCOLOR);
    StretchDIBits(dc, g_view.left, g_view.top, g_view.right - g_view.left, g_view.bottom - g_view.top,
                  0, 0, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, s->px, (BITMAPINFO *)&bi, DIB_RGB_COLORS, SRCCOPY);
    if (g_aviOpen && g_aviFrame >= 0) {                  /* the video on top, in the take's rectangle */
        BITMAPINFOHEADER vh;
        int w = g_view.right - g_view.left, h = g_view.bottom - g_view.top;
        memset(&vh, 0, sizeof vh);
        vh.biSize = sizeof vh; vh.biWidth = (LONG)g_avi.width; vh.biHeight = -(LONG)g_avi.height; vh.biPlanes = 1;
        vh.biBitCount = 24; vh.biCompression = BI_RGB;
        SetStretchBltMode(dc, HALFTONE); SetBrushOrgEx(dc, 0, 0, NULL);
        StretchDIBits(dc, g_view.left + g_aviRect[0] * w / SCREEN_W, g_view.top + g_aviRect[1] * h / SCREEN_H,
                      g_aviRect[2] * w / SCREEN_W, g_aviRect[3] * h / SCREEN_H, 0, 0, (int)g_avi.width, (int)g_avi.height,
                      g_aviBgr, (BITMAPINFO *)&vh, DIB_RGB_COLORS, SRCCOPY);
    }
}

static int to_game(LPARAM lp, int *x, int *y)
{
    int mx = (short)LOWORD(lp), my = (short)HIWORD(lp), w = g_view.right - g_view.left, h = g_view.bottom - g_view.top;
    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return 0;
    *x = (mx - g_view.left) * SCREEN_W / w; *y = (my - g_view.top) * SCREEN_H / h;
    return *x >= 0 && *y >= 0 && *x < SCREEN_W && *y < SCREEN_H;
}

static void toggle_fullscreen(void)
{
    LONG st = GetWindowLongA(g_wnd, GWL_STYLE);
    if (!g_fullscreen) {
        MONITORINFO mi = { sizeof mi };
        GetWindowPlacement(g_wnd, &g_place);
        GetMonitorInfoA(MonitorFromWindow(g_wnd, MONITOR_DEFAULTTOPRIMARY), &mi);
        SetWindowLongA(g_wnd, GWL_STYLE, st & ~WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW);
        SetMenu(g_wnd, NULL);
        SetWindowPos(g_wnd, HWND_TOP, mi.rcMonitor.left, mi.rcMonitor.top, mi.rcMonitor.right - mi.rcMonitor.left,
                     mi.rcMonitor.bottom - mi.rcMonitor.top, SWP_NOOWNERZORDER | SWP_FRAMECHANGED);
        g_fullscreen = 1;
    } else {
        SetWindowLongA(g_wnd, GWL_STYLE, st | WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW);
        SetMenu(g_wnd, g_menu);
        SetWindowPlacement(g_wnd, &g_place);
        SetWindowPos(g_wnd, NULL, 0, 0, 0, 0, SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOOWNERZORDER | SWP_FRAMECHANGED);
        g_fullscreen = 0;
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ sound */

static void audio_open(void)
{
    XWAVEFORMATEX f = { 1, 2, 22050, 22050 * 4, 4, 16, 0 };
    int i;
    UINT r = p_waveOutOpen ? p_waveOutOpen(&g_wave, (UINT)-1, &f, 0, 0, 0) : 99;
    logf_("waveOutOpen 22050 Hz 16-bit stereo -> %u", r);
    if (r != 0) { g_wave = NULL; return; }
    for (i = 0; i < AUDIO_BUFS; i++) {
        memset(&g_hdr[i], 0, sizeof g_hdr[i]);
        g_hdr[i].lpData = (LPSTR)g_pcm[i]; g_hdr[i].dwBufferLength = sizeof g_pcm[i];
        p_waveOutPrepareHeader(g_wave, &g_hdr[i], sizeof g_hdr[i]);
        g_hdr[i].dwFlags |= XWHDR_DONE;                       /* free to fill */
    }
}

static void audio_pump(void)
{   /* keep every finished buffer refilled from the game's mixer */
    int i;
    if (!g_wave) return;
    for (i = 0; i < AUDIO_BUFS; i++)
        if (g_hdr[i].dwFlags & XWHDR_DONE) {
            game_audio_mix(&g_app.game, g_pcm[i], AUDIO_FRAMES);
            g_hdr[i].dwFlags &= ~XWHDR_DONE;
            p_waveOutWrite(g_wave, &g_hdr[i], sizeof g_hdr[i]);
        }
}

static void audio_close(void)
{
    int i;
    if (!g_wave) return;
    p_waveOutReset(g_wave);
    for (i = 0; i < AUDIO_BUFS; i++) p_waveOutUnprepareHeader(g_wave, &g_hdr[i], sizeof g_hdr[i]);
    p_waveOutClose(g_wave);
    g_wave = NULL;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ MIDI and AVI (GameAudio) */

static void midi_path(const char *name, const uint8_t *data, size_t len, char *out, size_t cap)
{   /* the loose file in the game folder, or a temporary copy of the bytes the game read */
    FILE *f;
    snprintf(out, cap, "%s\\%s", g_dir, name);
    if ((f = fopen(out, "rb")) != NULL) { fclose(f); return; }
    if (!data) return;
    GetTempPathA((DWORD)cap, out);
    strncat(out, "izzy_music.mid", cap - strlen(out) - 1);
    if ((f = fopen(out, "wb")) != NULL) { fwrite(data, 1, len, f); fclose(f); }
}

static void host_midi(void *ctx, int op, const char *name, const uint8_t *data, size_t len)
{
    char path[MAX_PATH * 2];
    (void)ctx;
    logf_("midi op %d %s (game tick %ld, paused %d)", op, name ? name : "", (long)g_app.game.now, g_app.game.paused);
    switch (op) {
    case GAME_MIDI_PLAY:
        if (g_midiOpen) { mci(g_wnd, "stop izzymusic"); mci(g_wnd, "close izzymusic"); }
        midi_path(name, data, len, path, sizeof path);
        g_midiOpen = mci(g_wnd, "open \"%s\" type sequencer alias izzymusic", path) == 0;
        g_midiLoop = 0; g_midiPaused = 0;
        if (g_midiOpen) { mci(g_wnd, "set izzymusic time format milliseconds"); mci(g_wnd, "play izzymusic from 0 notify"); }
        break;
    case GAME_MIDI_STOP:
        if (g_midiOpen) { mci(g_wnd, "stop izzymusic"); mci(g_wnd, "close izzymusic"); }
        g_midiOpen = g_midiPaused = 0;
        break;
    case GAME_MIDI_PAUSE:
        if (g_midiOpen && !g_midiPaused) {
            g_midiPos[0] = 0;
            if (p_mciSendStringA) p_mciSendStringA("status izzymusic position", g_midiPos, sizeof g_midiPos, g_wnd);
            mci(g_wnd, "stop izzymusic");
            g_midiPaused = 1;
        }
        break;
    case GAME_MIDI_RESUME:
        if (g_midiOpen && g_midiPaused) { mci(g_wnd, "play izzymusic from %s notify", g_midiPos[0] ? g_midiPos : "0"); g_midiPaused = 0; }
        break;
    case GAME_MIDI_LOOP_ON: g_midiLoop = 1; break;
    case GAME_MIDI_LOOP_OFF: g_midiLoop = 0; break;
    }
}

static void video_stop(void)
{   /* the end of the video (or a click that skips it): release everything, then tell the game */
    if (!g_aviOpen) return;
    if (g_aviWave) {
        p_waveOutReset(g_aviWave);
        p_waveOutUnprepareHeader(g_aviWave, &g_aviHdr, sizeof g_aviHdr);
        p_waveOutClose(g_aviWave);
        g_aviWave = NULL;
    }
    iv3_free(&g_iv3);
    avi_close(&g_avi);
    free(g_aviBgr); g_aviBgr = NULL;
    g_aviOpen = 0;
    logf_("video finished");
    game_avi_finished(&g_app.game);
    if (g_wnd) InvalidateRect(g_wnd, NULL, FALSE);
}

static uint32_t video_ms(void)
{
    LARGE_INTEGER t;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&t);
    return (uint32_t)((t.QuadPart - g_aviStart.QuadPart) * 1000 / g_freq.QuadPart);
}

static int video_pump(void)
{   /* decode up to the frame due now (every frame: each one predicts from the previous); 1 = a new picture */
    uint32_t ms;
    int target, shown = 0;
    if (!g_aviOpen) return 0;
    if (g_app.game.paused != g_aviPaused) {             /* orig: UI +0xb4 / +0xb8 pause and resume the movie with the game */
        LARGE_INTEGER t;
        QueryPerformanceCounter(&t);
        g_aviPaused = g_app.game.paused;
        if (g_aviPaused) { g_aviPausedAt = t; if (g_aviWave && p_waveOutPause) p_waveOutPause(g_aviWave); }
        else { g_aviStart.QuadPart += t.QuadPart - g_aviPausedAt.QuadPart; if (g_aviWave && p_waveOutRestart) p_waveOutRestart(g_aviWave); }
    }
    if (g_aviPaused) return 0;
    ms = video_ms();
    if (ms >= avi_duration_ms(&g_avi)) { video_stop(); return 1; }
    target = avi_frame_at(&g_avi, ms);
    while (g_aviFrame < target) {
        const AviFrame *f = &g_avi.frames[++g_aviFrame];
        if (f->size && iv3_decode(&g_iv3, g_avi.data + f->offset, f->size) > 0) shown = 1;
    }
    if (shown) iv3_to_bgr(&g_iv3, g_aviBgr, g_aviStride);
    return shown;
}

static int host_play_avi(void *ctx, const char *path, int x, int y, int w, int h)
{   /* orig: 1008:7bae (MCI avivideo). A missing file plays nothing, as an MCI open failure did. */
    char p[MAX_PATH * 2], *c;
    (void)ctx;
    if (g_aviOpen) video_stop();
    strncpy(p, path, sizeof p - 1); p[sizeof p - 1] = 0;
    for (c = p; *c; c++) if (*c == '/') *c = '\\';
    if (!strchr(p + strlen(g_dir), '.')) strncat(p, ".AVI", sizeof p - strlen(p) - 1);
    if (avi_open(&g_avi, p) != 0) { logf_("video %s: cannot open", p); return 0; }
    if (g_avi.handler != 0x32335649u /* 'IV32' */ && g_avi.handler != 0x32337669u /* 'iv32' */) {
        logf_("video %s: codec %.4s not supported", p, (const char *)&g_avi.handler);
        avi_close(&g_avi);
        return 0;
    }
    g_aviStride = ((int)g_avi.width * 3 + 3) & ~3;
    g_aviBgr = (uint8_t *)calloc((size_t)g_aviStride * g_avi.height, 1);
    if (!g_aviBgr || iv3_init(&g_iv3, (int)g_avi.width, (int)g_avi.height) != 0) { free(g_aviBgr); g_aviBgr = NULL; avi_close(&g_avi); return 0; }
    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) { x = 0; y = 0; w = SCREEN_W; h = SCREEN_H; }
    g_aviRect[0] = x; g_aviRect[1] = y; g_aviRect[2] = w; g_aviRect[3] = h;
    g_aviFrame = -1;
    g_aviOpen = 1;
    g_aviPaused = 0;
    if (g_avi.audioLen && g_avi.sampleRate > 0 && p_waveOutOpen) {   /* its own sound, in its own format */
        XWAVEFORMATEX f;
        f.wFormatTag = 1; f.nChannels = (WORD)g_avi.channels; f.nSamplesPerSec = (DWORD)g_avi.sampleRate;
        f.wBitsPerSample = (WORD)g_avi.bits; f.nBlockAlign = (WORD)(g_avi.channels * g_avi.bits / 8);
        f.nAvgBytesPerSec = f.nSamplesPerSec * f.nBlockAlign; f.cbSize = 0;
        if (p_waveOutOpen(&g_aviWave, (UINT)-1, &f, 0, 0, 0) == 0) {
            memset(&g_aviHdr, 0, sizeof g_aviHdr);
            g_aviHdr.lpData = (LPSTR)g_avi.audio; g_aviHdr.dwBufferLength = (DWORD)g_avi.audioLen;
            p_waveOutPrepareHeader(g_aviWave, &g_aviHdr, sizeof g_aviHdr);
            p_waveOutWrite(g_aviWave, &g_aviHdr, sizeof g_aviHdr);
        } else g_aviWave = NULL;
    }
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&g_aviStart);
    video_pump();
    logf_("video %s: %ux%u, %d frames, %u ms, sound %s", p, g_avi.width, g_avi.height, g_avi.nframes, avi_duration_ms(&g_avi),
          g_aviWave ? "playing" : "none");
    return 1;
}

static void host_stop_avi(void *ctx) { (void)ctx; video_stop(); }

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ dialogs (AppHost) */

static int host_message(void *ctx, int id, const char *text, int style)
{
    (void)ctx; (void)id;
    return MessageBoxA(g_wnd, text ? text : "", TITLE, (UINT)style);
}

static int host_choose_file(void *ctx, int save, char *path, size_t cap)
{
    XOPENFILENAMEA o;
    (void)ctx;
    if (!(save ? p_GetSaveFileNameA : p_GetOpenFileNameA)) return 0;
    memset(&o, 0, sizeof o);
    o.lStructSize = sizeof o; o.hwndOwner = g_wnd;
    o.lpstrFilter = "Saved Games (*.SGE)\0*.SGE\0All Files (*.*)\0*.*\0";
    o.nFilterIndex = 1;
    o.lpstrFile = path; o.nMaxFile = (DWORD)cap; path[0] = 0;
    o.lpstrInitialDir = g_dataDir;
    o.lpstrTitle = save ? "Choose a Filename to Save your Game" : "Choose a Saved Game to Load";   /* 1008:38b6 / 389a */
    o.lpstrDefExt = "SGE";
    o.Flags = XOFN_HIDEREADONLY | XOFN_PATHMUSTEXIST | (save ? XOFN_OVERWRITEPROMPT : XOFN_FILEMUSTEXIST);
    return save ? p_GetSaveFileNameA(&o) : p_GetOpenFileNameA(&o);
}

static void host_about(void *ctx)
{
    (void)ctx;
    MessageBoxA(g_wnd, TITLE "\nVersion 1.0\n\nRunning on the reconstructed engine (IzzyQuest.exe for modern Windows).\n\n"
                "F11 or Alt+Enter: full screen", "About " TITLE, MB_OK | MB_ICONINFORMATION);
}

static void host_check_menu(void *ctx, int cmd, int on) { (void)ctx; if (g_menu) CheckMenuItem(g_menu, (UINT)cmd, MF_BYCOMMAND | (on ? MF_CHECKED : MF_UNCHECKED)); }
static void host_quit(void *ctx) { (void)ctx; if (g_wnd) DestroyWindow(g_wnd); }

static HMENU build_menu(void)
{   /* MENU 0x7c of IZZY.EXE */
    HMENU m = CreateMenu(), game = CreatePopupMenu(), sound = CreatePopupMenu();
    AppendMenuA(game, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_NEW_GAME, "&New Game");
    AppendMenuA(game, MF_SEPARATOR, 0, NULL);
    AppendMenuA(game, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_SAVE_GAME, "&Save Game");
    AppendMenuA(game, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_LOAD_GAME, "&Load Game");
    AppendMenuA(game, MF_SEPARATOR, 0, NULL);
    AppendMenuA(game, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_EXIT, "E&xit");
    AppendMenuA(sound, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_VOLUME_HIGH, "Volume High");
    AppendMenuA(sound, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_VOLUME_MED, "Volume Medium");
    AppendMenuA(sound, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_VOLUME_LOW, "Volume Low");
    AppendMenuA(sound, MF_SEPARATOR, 0, NULL);
    AppendMenuA(sound, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_MUSIC, "Music Playing");
    AppendMenuA(m, MF_POPUP, (UINT_PTR)game, "&Game");
    AppendMenuA(m, MF_POPUP, (UINT_PTR)sound, "&Sound");
    AppendMenuA(m, MF_STRING, APP_CMD_ABOUT, "&About");
    return m;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ cursors */

static HCURSOR exe_cursor(const NeResources *r, int groupId)
{   /* RT_GROUP_CURSOR -> the first RT_CURSOR (hotspot + DIB, the same layout Win32 uses) */
    size_t gl = 0, cl = 0;
    const uint8_t *grp = res_find(r, 12, groupId, &gl), *cur;
    if (!grp || gl < 6 + 14) return NULL;
    cur = res_find(r, 1, grp[6 + 12] | (grp[6 + 13] << 8), &cl);
    if (!cur) return NULL;
    return (HCURSOR)CreateIconFromResourceEx((PBYTE)cur, (DWORD)cl, FALSE, 0x00030000, 0, 0, 0);
}

static void load_cursors(void)
{   /* orig: ui_ctor 1008:61fe - IDC_ARROW, cursor 0x159 (kind 1), cursor 0x1b4 (kind 2) */
    g_cursor[0] = LoadCursorA(NULL, IDC_ARROW);
    g_cursor[1] = exe_cursor(&g_app.game.exe, 0x159);
    g_cursor[2] = exe_cursor(&g_app.game.exe, 0x1b4);
    logf_("cursors from IZZY.EXE: 0x159 %s, 0x1b4 %s", g_cursor[1] ? "ok" : "missing", g_cursor[2] ? "ok" : "missing");
    if (!g_cursor[1]) g_cursor[1] = LoadCursorA(NULL, IDC_HAND);
    if (!g_cursor[2]) g_cursor[2] = g_cursor[1];
}

static void save_window(const char *path)
{   /* what the screen shows: the client area copied with BitBlt (includes an AVI child window) */
    RECT r; HDC dc, mem; HBITMAP bm; BITMAPINFOHEADER h; BITMAPFILEHEADER fh; int w, hh; void *bits; FILE *f;
    GetClientRect(g_wnd, &r); w = r.right; hh = r.bottom;
    memset(&h, 0, sizeof h);
    h.biSize = sizeof h; h.biWidth = w; h.biHeight = hh; h.biPlanes = 1; h.biBitCount = 24; h.biCompression = BI_RGB;
    dc = GetDC(g_wnd); mem = CreateCompatibleDC(dc);
    bm = CreateDIBSection(dc, (BITMAPINFO *)&h, DIB_RGB_COLORS, &bits, NULL, 0);
    SelectObject(mem, bm);
    BitBlt(mem, 0, 0, w, hh, dc, 0, 0, SRCCOPY);
    GdiFlush();
    h.biSizeImage = (DWORD)(((w * 3 + 3) & ~3) * hh);
    memset(&fh, 0, sizeof fh);
    fh.bfType = 0x4d42; fh.bfOffBits = sizeof fh + sizeof h; fh.bfSize = fh.bfOffBits + h.biSizeImage;
    if ((f = fopen(path, "wb")) != NULL) { fwrite(&fh, sizeof fh, 1, f); fwrite(&h, sizeof h, 1, f); fwrite(bits, 1, h.biSizeImage, f); fclose(f); }
    DeleteDC(mem); DeleteObject(bm); ReleaseDC(g_wnd, dc);
    logf_("saved %dx%d window picture to %s", w, hh, path);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ the loop */

static int32_t game_ticks(void)
{
    LARGE_INTEGER t;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&t);
    return (int32_t)((t.QuadPart - g_t0.QuadPart) * 60 / g_freq.QuadPart);
}

static void step(void)
{
    int32_t now = game_ticks();
    audio_pump();
    if (video_pump() && g_wnd) { HDC dc = GetDC(g_wnd); paint(dc); ReleaseDC(g_wnd, dc); }
    if (now == g_lastTick) return;
    g_lastTick = now;
    if (!app_idle(&g_app, now)) return;                 /* the credits ended: the window is gone */
    app_idle(&g_app, now);
    audio_pump();
    if (g_wnd) { HDC dc = GetDC(g_wnd); paint(dc); ReleaseDC(g_wnd, dc); }
}

static LRESULT CALLBACK wndproc(HWND w, UINT msg, WPARAM wp, LPARAM lp)
{
    int x, y;
    switch (msg) {
    case WM_TIMER: step(); return 0;                    /* keeps running inside menus, dialogs and window moves */
    case WM_PAINT: { PAINTSTRUCT ps; HDC dc = BeginPaint(w, &ps); paint(dc); EndPaint(w, &ps); return 0; }
    case WM_ERASEBKGND: return 1;
    case WM_SIZE: layout(); InvalidateRect(w, NULL, FALSE); return 0;
    case WM_MOUSEMOVE:
        if (to_game(lp, &x, &y)) game_mouse_move(&g_app.game, x, y);
        return 0;
    case WM_SETCURSOR:
        if (LOWORD(lp) == HTCLIENT) { int k = g_app.game.ui.cursorKind; SetCursor(g_cursor[k >= 0 && k <= 2 ? k : 0]); return TRUE; }
        break;
    case WM_LBUTTONDOWN:
        if (to_game(lp, &x, &y)) game_click(&g_app.game, x, y);
        return 0;
    case WM_CHAR:
        if (wp >= 8 && wp < 256) game_char(&g_app.game, (int)wp);
        return 0;
    case WM_KEYDOWN:
        if (wp == VK_F11) { toggle_fullscreen(); return 0; }
        if (wp == VK_ESCAPE && g_fullscreen) { toggle_fullscreen(); return 0; }
        break;
    case WM_SYSKEYDOWN:
        if (wp == VK_RETURN && (lp & (1 << 29))) { toggle_fullscreen(); return 0; }
        break;
    case WM_COMMAND:
        if (app_command(&g_app, LOWORD(wp))) return 0;
        break;
    case XMM_MCINOTIFY:
        /* the MIDI song ended: start it again when it loops (the original polled "status music mode") */
        if (wp == XMCI_NOTIFY_SUCCESSFUL && g_midiOpen && g_midiLoop && !g_midiPaused) mci(w, "play izzymusic from 0 notify");
        return 0;
    case WM_KILLFOCUS:                                  /* orig: MAINWNDPROC WM_KILLFOCUS -> game_Pause */
        if (!g_app.game.paused && !g_testMode) { game_pause(&g_app.game); g_focusPaused = 1; }
        break;
    case WM_SETFOCUS:                                   /* orig: WM_SETFOCUS -> timer_Resume (only what we paused) */
        if (g_focusPaused) { g_focusPaused = 0; game_resume(&g_app.game); }
        break;
    case WM_CLOSE:
        app_request_quit(&g_app);                       /* asks, then plays the credits; closes after them */
        return 0;
    case WM_DESTROY:
        KillTimer(w, 1);
        g_wnd = NULL;
        PostQuitMessage(0);
        return 0;
    }
    return DefWindowProcA(w, msg, wp, lp);
}

static int is_game_dir(const char *d)
{
    char p[MAX_PATH * 2];
    DWORD a;
    snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s\\IZZY.DIR", d);
    a = GetFileAttributesA(p);
    return a != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES && !(a & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY);
}

static int find_game_dir(const char *arg)
{
    char exe[MAX_PATH], *s;
    int up;
    if (arg && *arg) {
        strncpy(g_dir, arg, sizeof g_dir - 1);
        if (g_dir[0] == '"') { memmove(g_dir, g_dir + 1, strlen(g_dir)); if ((s = strchr(g_dir, '"')) != NULL) *s = 0; }
        while (strlen(g_dir) > 3 && (g_dir[strlen(g_dir) - 1] == '\\' || g_dir[strlen(g_dir) - 1] == ' ')) g_dir[strlen(g_dir) - 1] = 0;
        return is_game_dir(g_dir);
    }
    GetModuleFileNameA(NULL, exe, sizeof exe);
    for (up = 0; up < 3; up++) {                        /* the exe's folder, then its parents */
        if ((s = strrchr(exe, '\\')) == NULL) break;
        *s = 0;
        if (is_game_dir(exe)) { strcpy(g_dir, exe); return 1; }
    }
    return 0;
}

static void pick_data_dir(void)
{   /* izzy.ini and saved games live with the game unless that folder is read-only (e.g. Program Files) */
    char p[MAX_PATH * 2];
    FILE *f;
    snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s\\izzy.ini", g_dir);
    if ((f = fopen(p, "a")) != NULL) { fclose(f); strcpy(g_dataDir, g_dir); return; }
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("APPDATA", g_dataDir, sizeof g_dataDir) == 0) { strcpy(g_dataDir, g_dir); return; }
    strncat(g_dataDir, "\\IzzysQuest", sizeof g_dataDir - strlen(g_dataDir) - 1);
    CreateDirectoryA(g_dataDir, NULL);
}

int WINAPI WinMain(HINSTANCE inst, HINSTANCE prev, LPSTR cmd, int show)
{
    WNDCLASSA wc;
    MSG m;
    RECT r, wa;
    int zoom;
    char env[MAX_PATH], shot[MAX_PATH] = "";
    double testSeconds = 0;
    (void)prev;
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("IZZY_TEST_LOG", env, sizeof env)) g_log = fopen(env, "w");
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("IZZY_TEST_SECONDS", env, sizeof env)) { testSeconds = atof(env); g_testMode = 1; }
    GetEnvironmentVariableA("IZZY_TEST_SHOT", shot, sizeof shot);
    load_system_dlls();
    logf_("winmm %s, comdlg32 %s", p_waveOutOpen ? "loaded" : "missing", p_GetOpenFileNameA ? "loaded" : "missing");
    if (!find_game_dir(cmd)) {
        MessageBoxA(NULL, "The game files were not found.\n\nPut IzzyQuest.exe in the folder that holds IZZY.DIR, IZZYCD1.DAT, "
                    "IZZY.VET and IZZY.EXE (the CD's files), or give that folder on the command line.", TITLE, MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR);
        return 1;
    }
    pick_data_dir();
    logf_("game folder %s, options and saves in %s", g_dir, g_dataDir);

    memset(&wc, 0, sizeof wc);
    wc.lpfnWndProc = wndproc; wc.hInstance = inst; wc.lpszClassName = "IzzyQuestWindow";
    wc.hCursor = LoadCursorA(NULL, IDC_ARROW); wc.hIcon = LoadIconA(NULL, IDI_APPLICATION);
    RegisterClassA(&wc);
    SystemParametersInfoA(SPI_GETWORKAREA, 0, &wa, 0);
    for (zoom = 4; zoom > 1; zoom--) if (640 * zoom + 40 <= wa.right - wa.left && 480 * zoom + 80 <= wa.bottom - wa.top) break;
    g_menu = build_menu();
    r.left = 0; r.top = 0; r.right = 640 * zoom; r.bottom = 480 * zoom;
    AdjustWindowRect(&r, WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_CLIPCHILDREN, TRUE);
    g_wnd = CreateWindowA(wc.lpszClassName, TITLE, WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_CLIPCHILDREN, CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT,
                          r.right - r.left, r.bottom - r.top, NULL, g_menu, inst, NULL);
    if (!g_wnd) return 1;

    g_app.host.ctx = NULL;
    g_app.host.message = host_message; g_app.host.choose_file = host_choose_file; g_app.host.about = host_about;
    g_app.host.check_menu = host_check_menu; g_app.host.quit = host_quit;
    if (app_open(&g_app, g_dir, (unsigned)GetTickCount()) != 0) {
        MessageBoxA(g_wnd, "Unable to run the StoryGame Engine.\n\nThe game files could not be read.", TITLE, MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR);
        return 1;
    }
    if (strcmp(g_dataDir, g_dir) != 0) {                /* move the options and high scores to the writable folder */
        snprintf(g_app.game.iniPath, sizeof g_app.game.iniPath, "%s\\izzy.ini", g_dataDir);
        g_app.game.ui.iniPath = g_app.game.iniPath;
        app_load_settings(&g_app.settings, g_app.game.iniPath);
        app_apply_settings(&g_app.game, &g_app.settings);
        ui_scores_load(&g_app.game.ui);
    }
    g_app.game.audio.ctx = NULL;
    g_app.game.audio.midi = host_midi; g_app.game.audio.playAvi = host_play_avi; g_app.game.audio.stopAvi = host_stop_avi;
    load_cursors();
    audio_open();
    if (p_timeBeginPeriod) p_timeBeginPeriod(1);
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&g_freq); QueryPerformanceCounter(&g_t0);
    layout();
    ShowWindow(g_wnd, show);
    UpdateWindow(g_wnd);
    SetTimer(g_wnd, 1, 8, NULL);
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("IZZY_TEST_MIDI", env, sizeof env)) {   /* IZZY_TEST_MIDI=NAME.MID plays it, looping */
        game_midi(&g_app.game, env, MIDI_CMD_PLAY);
        game_midi(&g_app.game, NULL, MIDI_CMD_LOOP_ON);
    }
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("IZZY_TEST_AVI", env, sizeof env)) {
        char p[MAX_PATH * 2];
        snprintf(p, sizeof p, "%s\\%s", g_dir, env);
        logf_("test AVI %s -> %s", p, host_play_avi(NULL, p, 0, 0, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H) ? "playing" : "failed");
    }

    for (;;) {
        while (PeekMessageA(&m, NULL, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) {
            if (m.message == WM_QUIT) goto done;
            TranslateMessage(&m);
            DispatchMessageA(&m);
        }
        step();
        if (testSeconds > 0 && g_wnd && game_ticks() >= (int32_t)(testSeconds * 60)) {
            logf_("test end at tick %ld: node %s, take %s, %ld takes, AVI %s, MIDI %s", (long)game_ticks(),
                  g_app.game.vm.currentNode >= 0 ? g_app.game.vm.nodes[g_app.game.vm.currentNode].name : "-",
                  g_app.game.takeName, g_app.game.takesStarted, g_aviOpen ? "open" : "closed", g_midiOpen ? "open" : "closed");
            if (shot[0]) save_window(shot);
            testSeconds = 0;
            DestroyWindow(g_wnd);
            continue;
        }
        MsgWaitForMultipleObjects(0, NULL, FALSE, 2, QS_ALLINPUT);
    }
done:
    if (g_aviOpen) video_stop();
    if (g_midiOpen) { mci(NULL, "close izzymusic"); g_midiOpen = 0; }
    audio_close();
    app_close(&g_app);
    return 0;
}

```
